# Siva Purana. Chapter 4

## Section 8 - Koṭirudra-Saṃhitā

## Chapter 1 - The greatness of Jyotirliṅgas and their Upaliṅgas

## \|\| 8.1.1 \|\|

*yo dhatte nijamāyayaiva bhuvanākāraṃ vikārojjhito \|*  
*yasyāhuḥ karuṇākaṭākṣavibhavau svargāpavarggābhidhau \|*  
*pratyagbodhasukhādvayaṃ hṛdi sadā paśyanti yaṃ yoginastasmai \|*  
*śailasutāñjitārddhavapuṣe śaśvannamastejase \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Obeisance for ever to that brilliance, half of whose body has been beautified by the daughter of the mountain; who, rid of all aberrations, assumes the shape of the worlds by means of his own Māyā; whose sympathetic glance, they say, brings about the wealth and glory in the form of heaven and salvation and whom the Yogins sec in their hearts as the one without a second and as the inner understanding and bliss.

## \|\| 8.1.2 \|\|

*kṛpālalitavīkṣaṇaṃ smitamanojñavaktrāmbujaṃ śaśāṃkakalayojjvalaṃ śamitaghoratāpatrayam \|*  
*karotu kimapi sphuratparamasaukhyasaccidvapurdharādharasutābhujodvalayitaṃ maho maṃgalam \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. May that mass of splendour, whatever it be, be auspicious, the splendour that has eyes gentle due to compassion; which has lotus-like face smiling charmly; which is brilliant with the digit of the moon; which subdues the terrible three-fold distresses; whose body of excessive bliss, existence and knowledge sheds brightness; and which is encircled by the arms of the daughter of the mountain, Pārvatī.

## \|\| 8.1.3 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*samyaguktaṃ tvayā sūta lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā \|*  
*śivāvatāramāhātmyaṃ nānākhyānasamanvitam \|\| 3 \|\|*

The sages said:—

3\. O Sūta, the greatness of the incarnations of Śiva inclusive of their narratives, has been well explained by you with a desire for the welfare of the world.

## \|\| 8.1.4 \|\|

*punaśca kathyatāṃ tāta śivamāhātmyamuttamam \|*  
*liṃgasambandhi suprītyā dhanyastvaṃ śaivasattamaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. O dear, may the excellent grandeur of Śiva be narrated with pleasure in connection with his phallic forms. You are blessed and the most excellent among the devotees of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.1.5 \|\|

*śṛṇvantastvanmukhāmbhojānna tṛptāssmo vayaṃ prabho \|*  
*śaivaṃ yaśo'mṛtaṃ ramyaṃ tadeva punarucyatām \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Listening from your lotus-like mouth, O holy lord, to the nectar-like glory of Śiva, we are never satiated. Let the same sweet narrative be repeated.

## \|\| 8.1.6-7 \|\|

*pṛthivyāṃ yāni yāni liṃgāni tīrthetīrthe śubhāni hi \|*  
*anyatra vā sthale yāni prasiddhāni sthitāni vai \|\| 6 \|\|*

*tāni tāni ca divyāni liṃgāni parameśituḥ \|*  
*vyāsaśiṣya samācakṣva lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā \|\| 7 \|\|*

6-7. O disciple of Vyāsa, with a desire for the welfare of the worlds,. please narrate in detail those divine phallic images of the great lord whether they be on the earth in the different holy centres or in other places glorified by men.

## \|\| 8.1.8 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*sādhupṛṣṭamṛṣiśreṣṭha lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā \|*  
*kathayāmi bhavatsnehāttāni saṃkṣepato dvijāḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

8\. O brahmins, O excellent sages, you have asked well. I shall explain them in brief with a desire for the welfare of the worlds and due to my affection for you.

## \|\| 8.1.9 \|\|

*sarveṣāṃ śivaliṃgānāṃ mune saṃkhyā na vidyate \|*  
*sarvaṃ liṃgamayī bhūmiḥ sarvaliṃgamayaṃ jagat \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. O sage, there is no limit to the number of phallic images. The entire earth, the entire universe is in the form of a phallus.

## \|\| 8.1.10 \|\|

*liṃgamayāni tīrthāni sarvaṃ liṃge pratiṣṭhitam \|*  
*saṃkhyā na vidyate teṣāṃ tāni kiṃcidbravīmyaham \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. The holy centres are equipped with phallic images. Everything is founded on these. There is no limit to their number. I shall mention a few of them.

## \|\| 8.1.11 \|\|

*yatkiṃciddṛśyate dṛśyaṃ varṇyate smaryate ca yat \|*  
*tatsarvaṃ śivarūpaṃ hi nānyadastīti kiṃcana \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Whatever is seen, described or remembered is Śiva’s form. Nothing other than that has real existence.

## \|\| 8.1.12-13 \|\|

*tathāpi śrūyatāmprītyā kathayāmi yathāśrutam \|*  
*liṃgāni ca ṛṣiśreṣṭhāḥ pṛthivyāṃ yāni tāni ha \|\| 12 \|\|*

*pātāle cāpi vartante svarge cāpi tathā bhuvi \|*  
*sarvatra pūjyate śambhuḥ sadevāsuramānuṣaiḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

12-13. Still I shall explain in the manner I have heard. Let it be heard with pleasure O excellent sages, whatever images are there on the earth in the Pātāla or in heaven, everywhere Śiva is worshipped by the gods, Asuras and human beings.

## \|\| 8.1.14 \|\|

*trijagacchambhunā vyāptaṃ sadevāsuramānuṣam \|*  
*anugrahāya lokānāṃ liṃgarūpeṇa sattamāḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. O excellent ones, the three worlds consisting of the gods, Asuras and human beings are pervaded by Śiva in the form of phallic image for the welfare of the worlds.

## \|\| 8.1.15 \|\|

*anugrahāya lokānāṃ liṃgāni ca maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*dadhāti vividhānyatra tīrthe cānyasthale tathā \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. In order to bless the worlds, lord Śiva assumes different phallic forms here in the holy centres as well as in other places.

## \|\| 8.1.16 \|\|

*yatrayatra yadā śaṃbhurbhaktyā bhaktaiśca saṃsmṛtaḥ \|*  
*tatratatrāvatīryātha kāryaṃ kṛtvā sthitastadā \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Śiva incarnates in those places wherever he is devoutly remembered by the devotees. After carrying out their task he stations himself there.

## \|\| 8.1.17 \|\|

*lokānāmupakārārthaṃ svaliṃgaṃ cāpyakalpayat \|*  
*talliṃgaṃ pūjayitvā tu siddhiṃ samadhigacchati \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. He has assigned his phallic form for the benefit of the worlds. The devotee attains perfection by worshipping that form.

## \|\| 8.1.18 \|\|

*pṛthibyāṃ yāni liṃgāni teṣāṃ saṃkhyā na vidyate \|*  
*tathāpi ca pradhānāni kathyate ca mayā dvijāḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. O noble ascetics, there is no limit to the number of his phallic images on the earth. Still I narrate the important ones.

## \|\| 8.1.19 \|\|

*pradhāneṣu ca yānīha mukhyāni pravadāmyaham \|*  
*yacchrutvā sarvapāpebhyo mucyate mānavaḥ kṣaṇāt \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Even among the important ones, I shall enumerate the principal ones, on hearing which a man instantly gets rid of his sins.

## \|\| 8.1.20 \|\|

*jyotirliṃgāni yānīha mukhyamukhyāni sattama \|*  
*tānyahaṃ kathayāmyadya śrutvā pāpaṃ vyapohati \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. O excellent one, I shall mention the most important of the Jyotirliṅgas on hearing which a person becomes sinless.

## \|\| 8.1.21-24 \|\|

*saurāṣṭre somanāthaṃ ca śrīśaile mallikārjunam \|*  
*ujjayinyāṃ mahākālamoṃkāre parameśvaram \|\| 21 \|\|*

*kedāraṃ himavatpṛṣṭhe ḍākinyāṃ bhīmaśaṃkaram \|*  
*vārāṇasyāṃ ca viśveśaṃ tryambakaṃ gautamītaṭe \|\| 22 \|\|*

*vaidyanāthaṃ citābhūmau nāgeśaṃ dārukāvane \|*  
*setubaṃdhe ca rāmeśaṃ ghuśmeśaṃ ca śivālaye \|\| 23 \|\|*

*dvādaśaitāni nāmāni prātarutthāya yaḥ paṭhet \|*  
*sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ sarvasiddhiphalaṃ labhet \|\| 24 \|\|*

21-24. He who reads or recites the twelve names early in the morning is freed from all sins and attains the fruits of all Siddhis. The twelve Jyotirliṅgas are—Somanātha in Saurāṣṭra, Mallikārjuna in Śrīśaila, Mahākāla in Ujjayinī Parameśvara in Oṃkāra, Kedāra on the Himavat, Bhīma-Śaṅkara in Dākinī (Ḍākinī?), Viśveśa in Vārāṇasī, Tryambaka on the bank of the Gautamī, Vaidyanātha in the cremation ground, Nāgeśa in the Dārukā forest, Rāmeśa at Setubandha and Ghuśmeśa at Śivālaya.

## \|\| 8.1.25 \|\|

*yaṃ yaṃ kāmamapekṣyaiva paṭhiṣyanti narottamāḥ \|*  
*prāpsyaṃti kāmaṃ taṃ taṃ hi paratreva munīśvarāḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. O great sages, such excellent men will attain those desires for which they recite the names.

## \|\| 8.1.26 \|\|

*ye niṣkāmatayā tāni paṭhiṣyanti śubhāśayāḥ \|*  
*teṣāṃ ca jananīgarbhe vāso naiva bhaviṣyati \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Those who recite them without any desire but with purity of mind will never have rebirth involving residence in the mother’s womb.

## \|\| 8.1.27 \|\|

*eteṣāṃ pūjanenaiva varṇānāṃ duḥkhanā śanam \|*  
*iha loke paratrāpi muktirbhavati niścitam \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. By worshipping these, people of all castes get their miseries destroyed in this world and become liberated hereafter, to be sure.

## \|\| 8.1.28 \|\|

*grāhyameṣāṃ ca naivedyaṃ bhojanīyaṃ prayatnataḥ \|*  
*tatkartuḥ sarvvapāpāni bhasmasād yānti vai kṣaṇāt \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. The food offerings of these Jyotirliṅgas shall be taken and others shall be fed with the same. Such acts reduce the sins to ashes instantaneously.

## \|\| 8.1.29 \|\|

*jyotiṣāṃ caiva liṃgānāṃ bahmādibhiralaṃ dvijāḥ \|*  
*viśeṣataḥ phalaṃ vaktuṃ śakyate na paraistathā \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. O brahmins, the results of the worship of Jyotirliṅgas cannot be described adequately by Brahmā and others.

## \|\| 8.1.30 \|\|

*ekaṃ ca pūjitaṃ yena ṣaṇmāsaṃ tannirantaram \|*  
*tasya duḥkhaṃ na jāyeta mātṛkukṣisamudbhavam \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Even if one of them is worshipped continuously for six months, the misery of rebirth in the mother’s womb will never recur.

## \|\| 8.1.31 \|\|

*hīnayonau yadā jāto jyotirliṃgaṃ ca paśyati \|*  
*tasya janma bhavettatra vimale satkule punaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Even if a person of base birth happens to see Jyotirliṅga, he will be born in a pure and good family.

## \|\| 8.1.32 \|\|

*satkule janma saṃprāpya dhanāḍhyo vedapāragaḥ \|*  
*śubhakarma tadā kṛtvā muktiṃ yātyanapāyinīm \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Being born in a good family he will become rich and master all the Vedas. Then by performing auspicious rites, he shall attain liberation without any obstacle.

## \|\| 8.1.33 \|\|

*mleccho vāpyantyajo vāpi ṣaṇḍho vāpi munīśvarāḥ \|*  
*dvijo bhūtvā bhavenmuktastasmāttaddarśanaṃ caret \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. O great sages, whoever he may be, an outcaste, a slave or an eunuch, he will be born as a brahmin and become liberated. Hence one shall view the Jyotirliṅga.

## \|\| 8.1.34 \|\|

*jyotiṣāṃ copaliṃgāni śrūyantāmṛṣisattamāḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. O excellent sages, a few of the fruits of the Jyotirliṅgas have been narrated by me. Let the Upaliṅgas (ancillary phallic forms) of these Jyotirliṅgas be listened to.

## \|\| 8.1.35 \|\|

*someśvarasya yalliṃgamantakeśamudāhṛtam \|*  
*mahyāssāgarasaṃyoge talliṃgamupaliṅgakam \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. The Upaliṅga of Someśvara is called Antakeśa and it is installed at the confluence of the river Mahī and the ocean.

## \|\| 8.1.36 \|\|

*mallikārjunasaṃbhūtamupaliṃgamudāhṛtam \|*  
*rudreśvaramiti khyātaṃ bhṛgukakṣe sukhāvaham \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. The Upaliṅga of Mallikārjuna is glorified as Rudreśvara. It is pleasing and is stationed in Bhṛgukakṣa.

## \|\| 8.1.37 \|\|

*mahākālabhavaṃ liṃgaṃ dugdheśamiti viśrutam \|*  
*narmadāyāṃ prasiddhaṃ tatsarvapāpaharaṃ smṛtam \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. The Upaliṅga of Mahākāla is well known as Dugdheśa. It is famous in the region of Narmadā and is remembered as destructive of all sins.

## \|\| 8.1.38 \|\|

*oṃkārajaṃ ca yalliṃgaṃ kardameśamiti śrutam \|*  
*prasiddhaṃ bindusarasi sarvakāmaphalapradam \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. The Upaliṅga of Oṃkāra is famous as Kardameśa in the Bindu lake and yields the fruits of all desires.

## \|\| 8.1.39 \|\|

*kedāreśvarasaṃjātaṃ bhūteśaṃ yamunā taṭe \|*  
*mahāpāpaharaṃ proktaṃ paśyatāmarcatāntathā \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. The Upaliṅga of Kedāreśvara is Bhūteśa on the banks of the Yamunā. It is destructive of sins of the visiting devotees.

## \|\| 8.1.40 \|\|

*bhīmaśaṃkarasaṃbhūtaṃ bhīmeśvaramiti smṛtam \|*  
*sahyācale prasiddhaṃ tanmahābalavivarddhanam \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. The Upaliṅga of Bhīmaśaṅkara is Bhīmeśvara. It is famous in the Sahya mountain and it causes the increase of great strength.

## \|\| 8.1.41 \|\|

*nāgeśvarasamudbhūtaṃ bhūteśvaramudāhṛtam \|*  
*mallikāsarasvatītīre darśanātpāpahārakam \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. The Upaliṅga of Nāgeśvara is Bhūteśvara. Installed on the banks of Mallikāsarasvatī, it quells sins by the very sight.

## \|\| 8.1.42 \|\|

*rāmeśvarācca yajjātaṃ gupteśvaramiti smṛtam \|*  
*ghuśmeśāccaiva yajjātaṃ vyāghreśvaramiti smṛtam \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. The Upaliṅga attached to Rāmeśvara is Gupteśvara. The Upaliṅga attached to Ghuśmeśa is Vyāghreśvara.

## \|\| 8.1.43 \|\|

*jyotirliṃgopaliṃgāni proktānīha mayā dvijāḥ \|*  
*darśanātpāpahārīṇi sarvakāmapradāni ca \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. O brahmins, thus I have mentioned the Upaliṅgas and the Jyotirliṅgas. They dispel sins by their sight and yield the fruit of all desires.

## \|\| 8.1.44 \|\|

*etāni supradhānāni mukhyatāṃ hi gatāni ca \|*  
*anyāni cāpi mukhyāni śrūyatāmṛṣisattamā \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. These are the most important ones. O excellent sages, let the other important ones also be heard.

## Chapter 2 - The greatness of Śivaliṅgas

## \|\| 8.2.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*gaṃgātīre suprasiddhā kāśī khalu vimuktidā \|*  
*sā hi liṃgamayī jñeyā śivavāsasthalī smṛtā \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. Kāśī on the banks of the Gaṅgā is very famous as the bestower of liberation. It is full of phallic images. It is the abode of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.2.2 \|\|

*liṃgaṃ tatraiva mukhyaṃ ca samproktamavimuktakam \|*  
*kṛttivāseśvaraḥ sākṣāttattulyo vṛddhabālakaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. The important phallic image there, is Avimuktaka. Kṛttivāseśvara is in the forms of an aged man and a boy.

## \|\| 8.2.3 \|\|

*tilabhāṇḍeśvaraścaiva daśāśvamedha eva ca \|*  
*gaṃgā sāgarasaṃyoge saṃgameśa iti smṛtaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Tilabhāṇḍeśvara is in Daśāśvamedha. Saṅgameśa is at the place where the Gaṅgā meets the sea.

## \|\| 8.2.4 \|\|

*bhūteśvaro yaḥ saṃprokto bhaktasarvārthadaḥ sadā \|*  
*nārīśvara iti khyātaḥ kauśikyāḥ sa samīpagaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. What is called as Bhūteśvara is the bestower of all to the devotees. What is famous as Nārīśvara stands near the Kauśikī river.

## \|\| 8.2.5 \|\|

*vartate gaṇḍakītīre baṭukeśvara eva saḥ \|*  
*pūreśvara iti khyātaḥ phalgutīre sukhapradaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Vaṭukeśvara is on the bank of the Gaṇḍakī river.\[2\] Pūreśvara stands on the bank of the Phalgu river and is the bestower of happiness.

## \|\| 8.2.6 \|\|

*siddhanātheśvaraścaiva darśanātsiddhido nṛṇām \|*  
*dūreśvara iti khyātaḥ pattane cottare tathā \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Siddhanātheśvara is the bestower of Siddhi at the very sight. What is famous as Dūreśvara is in the northern Patan.

## \|\| 8.2.7 \|\|

*śṛṃgeśvaraśca nāmnā vai vaidyanāthastathaiva ca \|*  
*japyeśvarastathā khyāto yo dadhī ciraṇasthale \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Śṛṅgeśvara as also Vaidyanātha are famous. What is famous as Japyeśvara stands at the battle-ground of Dadhīci.

## \|\| 8.2.8-10 \|\|

*gopeśvaraḥ samākhyātaḥ raṃgeśvara iti smṛtaḥ \|*  
*vāmeśvaraśca nāgeśaḥ kājeśo vimaleśvaraḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

*vyāseśvaraśca vikhyātaḥ sukeśaśca tathaiva hi \|*  
*bhāṇḍeśvarāśca vikhyāto huṃkāreśastathaiva ca \|\| 9 \|\|*

*surocanaśca vikhyāto bhūteśvara iti svayam \|*  
*saṃgameśastathā prokto mahāpātakanāśanaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

8-10. Gopeśvara, Raṅgeśvara, Vāmeśvara, Nāgeśa, Kāmeśa, Vimaleśvara, Vyāseśvara, Sukeśa, Bhāṇḍeśvara, Huṃkāreśa, Surocana-Bhūteśvara and Saṅgameśa are the destroyers of great sins.

## \|\| 8.2.11 \|\|

*tataśca taptakātīre kumāreśvara eva ca \|*  
*siddheśvaraśca vikhyātaḥ seneśaśca tathā smṛtaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Kumāreśvara is on the banks of the Taptakā river. Siddheśvara and Seneśa are famous.

## \|\| 8.2.12 \|\|

*rāmeśvara iti proktaḥ kuṃbheśaśca paro mataḥ \|*  
*nandīśvaraśca puṃjeśaḥ pūrṇāyāṃ pūrṇakastathā \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Rāmeśvara, Kumbheśa, Nandīśvara, Puñjeśa and Pūrṇaka in the city of Pūrṇā, (are all great shrines).

## \|\| 8.2.13 \|\|

*brahmeśvaraḥ prayoge ca brahmaṇā sthāpitaḥ purā \|*  
*daśāśvamedhatīrthe hi caturvargaphalapradaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Brahmeśvara was formerly installed at the holy centre of Daśāśvamedha at Prayāga by Brahmā. It is the bestower of the fourfold aims of life.

## \|\| 8.2.14 \|\|

*tathā someśvarastatra sarbvāpadvinivārakaḥ \|*  
*bhāradvājeśvaraścaiva brahmavarcaḥpravarddhakaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Someśvara wards off all adversities. Bhāradvājeśvara increases Brahmacarya.

## \|\| 8.2.15 \|\|

*śūlaṭaṃkeśvaraḥ sākṣātkāmanāprada īritaḥ \|*  
*mādhaveśaśca tatraiva bhaktarakṣāvidhāyakaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Śūlaṭaṅkeśvara is the bestower of desires. Mādhaveśa, there itself, protects devotees.

## \|\| 8.2.16 \|\|

*nāgeśākhyaḥ prasiddho hi sāketanagare dvijā \|*  
*sūryyavaṃśodbhavānāṃ ca viśeṣeṇa sukhapradaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. O noble sages, what is called Nāgeśa is installed in the city of Sāketa. The Shrine is particularly the bestower of happiness to those born of the solar race.

## \|\| 8.2.17 \|\|

*puruṣottamapuryāṃ tu bhuvaneśastu siddhidaḥ \|*  
*lokeśaśca mahāliṃgaḥ sarvānandapradāyakaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Bhuvaneśa in the city of Puruṣottama is the bestower of good Siddhis. Lokeśa and Mahāliṅga bestow all bliss.

## \|\| 8.2.18 \|\|

*kāmeśvaraḥ śaṃbhuliṃgo gaṃgeśaḥ paraśuddhikṛt \|*  
*śakreśvaraḥ śukrasiddho lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Kāmeśvara is also Śiva’s phallic image. Gaṅgeśa causes great purity. With a desire for the welfare of the people the shrines Śakreśvara and Śukrasiddha exist.

## \|\| 8.2.19 \|\|

*tathā vaṭeśvaraḥ khyātaḥ sarvakāmaphalapradaḥ \|*  
*sindhutīre kapāleśo vaktreśaḥ sarvapāpahā \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Vaṭeśvara is famous as the bestower of the fruits of all desires. Kapāleśa is on the shore of the ocean. Vaktreśa is the destroyer of all sins.

## \|\| 8.2.20 \|\|

*bhīmeśvara iti proktaḥ sūryeśvara iti smṛtaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Dhautapāpeśvara himself is partially the supreme lord. Bhīmeśvara, Sūryeśvara are great shrines.

## \|\| 8.2.21 \|\|

*nandeśvaraśca vijñeyo jñānado lokapūjitaḥ \|*  
*nākeśvaro mahāpuṇyastathā rāmeśvaraḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Nandeśvara is the bestower of knowledge and as such is worshipped by the world. Nākeśvara is highly meritorious. So also is Rāmeśvara.

## \|\| 8.2.22 \|\|

*vimaleśvaranāmā vai kaṃṭakeśvara eva ca \|*  
*pūrṇasāgarasaṃyoge dhartukeśastathaiva ca \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Kaṇṭakeśvara is named Vimaleśvara as well. So also is Dhartukeśa at the confluence of the Pūrṇā and the ocean.

## \|\| 8.2.23 \|\|

*candreśvaraśca vijñeyaścandrakāntiphalapradaḥ \|*  
*sarvakāma pradaścaiva siddheśvara iti smṛtaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Candreśvara should be known as the bestower of the lustre of the moon as fruit. Siddheśvara is the bestower of all desires.

## \|\| 8.2.24 \|\|

*bilveśvaraśca vikhyātaścāndhakeśastathaiva ca \|*  
*yatra vā hyandhako daityaḥ śaṃkareṇa hataḥ purā \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Bilveśvara is famous and so also is Andhakeśa where the Daitya Andhaka was formerly killed by Śiva.

## \|\| 8.2.25 \|\|

*ayaṃ svarūpamaṃśena dhṛtvā śaṃbhuḥ punaḥ sthitaḥ \|*  
*śaraṇeśvaravikhyāto lokānāṃ sukhadaḥ sadā \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Śiva assumed his form partially and became famous as Śaraṇeśvara. He is the bestower of happiness to all the worlds always.

## \|\| 8.2.26 \|\|

*kardameśaḥ paraḥ prokta koṭīśaścārbudācale \|*  
*acaleśaśca vikhyāto lokānāṃ sukhadaḥ sadā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Kardameśa is the greatest. Koṭīśa is on the mountain Arbuda. Acaleśa is famous as the bestower of happiness to all the people always.

## \|\| 8.2.27 \|\|

*nāgeśvarastu kauśikyāstīre tiṣṭhati nityaśaḥ \|*  
*ananteśvarasaṃjñaśca kalyāṇaśubhabhājanaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Nāgeśvara is stationed on the banks of the Kauśikī for ever. What is named Ananteśvara is the receptacle of all welfare and auspiciousness.

## \|\| 8.2.28 \|\|

*yogeśvaraśca vikhyāto vaidyanātheśvarastathā \|*  
*koṭīśvaraśca vijñeyaḥ sapteśvara iti smṛtaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Yogeśvara, Vaidyanātheśvara, Koṭīśvara and Sapteśvara are also famous.

## \|\| 8.2.29 \|\|

*bhadreśvaraśca vikhyāto bhadranāmā haraḥ svayam \|*  
*caṇḍīśvarastathā proktaḥ saṃgameśvara eva ca \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Bhadreśvara is very famous. Bhadra is Śiva himself. So also are Caṇḍīśvara and Saṅgameśvara.

## \|\| 8.2.30 \|\|

*pūrvasyāṃ diśi jātāni śivaliṃgāni yāni ca \|*  
*sāmānyānyapi cānyāni tānīha kathitāni te \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Thus the phallic forms of Śiva installed in the eastern quarter of ordinary and extraordinary efficacy have been narrated to you.

## \|\| 8.2.31 \|\|

*dakṣiṇasyāṃ diśi tathā śivaliṃgāni yāni ca \|*  
*saṃjātāni muniśreṣṭha tāni te kathayāmyaham \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. O great sage, I shall now narrate to you the phallic images of Śiva installed in the South.

## Chapter 3 - The penance of Anasūyā and Atri

## \|\| 8.3.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*brahmapuryāṃ citrakūṭaṃ liṃgaṃ mattagajendrakam \|*  
*brahmaṇā sthāpitaṃ pūrvaṃ sarvakāmasamṛddhidam \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. The Liṅga Mattagajendraka in Brahmapurī at Gitrakūṭa had been installed formerly by Brahmā. It bestows all desires lavishly.

## \|\| 8.3.2 \|\|

*tatpūrvadiśi koṭīśaṃ liṃgaṃ sarvavarapradam \|*  
*godāvaryyāḥ paścime talliṃgaṃ paśupatināmakam \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. To the east of it is the phallic image Koṭīśa bestowing all boons. It is installed to the west of Godāvarī and is famous as Paśupati.

## \|\| 8.3.3-4 \|\|

*dakṣiṇasyāṃ diśi kaścidatrīśvara iti svayam \|*  
*lokānāmupakārārthamanasūyāsukhāya ca \|\| 3 \|\|*

*prādurbhūtaḥ svayaṃ devo hyanāvṛṣṭyāmajīvayat \|*  
*sa eva śaṃkaraḥ sākṣādaṃśena svayameva hi \|\| 4 \|\|*

3-4. The lord himself appeared in the southern quarter in the name of Atrīśvara for helping the people and for the happiness of Anasūyā. He enlivened people at the time of draught. He is himself Śiva partially.

## \|\| 8.3.5 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*sūtasūta mahābhāga kathamatrīśvaro haraḥ \|*  
*utpannaḥ paramo divyastattvaṃ kathaya suvrata \|\| 5 \|\|*

The sages said:—

5\. O Sūta, O fortunate Sūta, how was the excessively divine Śiva born as Atrīśvara? O one of excellent rites, please narrate it.

## \|\| 8.3.6 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*sādhu pṛṣṭhamṛṣiśreṣṭhāḥ kathayāmi kathāṃ śubhām \|*  
*yāṃ kathāṃ satataṃ śrutvā pātakairmucyate dhruvam \|\| 6 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

6\. O great sages, you have put the question well. I shall narrate the story on hearing which constantly one is relieved of sins.

## \|\| 8.3.7-8 \|\|

*dakṣiṇasyāṃ diśi mahat kāmadaṃ nāma yadvanam \|*  
*citrakūṭasamīpesti tapasāṃ hitadaṃ satām \|\| 7 \|\|*

*tatra ca brahmaṇaḥ putro hyatrināmā ṛṣiḥ svayam \|*  
*tapastepe'ti kaṭhinamanasūyāsamanvitaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

7-8. There is a great forest Kāmada in the south near Citrakūṭa. It is benefìcient to the good in the performance of penance. There the son of Brahmā, the sage Atri, performed a severe penance accompanied by Anasūyā.

## \|\| 8.3.9 \|\|

*pūrvaṃ kadācittatraiva hyanāvṛṣṭirabhūnmune \|*  
*duḥkhadā prāṇināṃ daivādvikaṭā śatavārṣikī \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. O sage, formerly once, there was a great draught there, extending to a hundred years which put all living beings to distress.

## \|\| 8.3.10 \|\|

*vṛkṣāśśuṣkāstadā sarve pallavāni phalāni ca \|*  
*nityārthaṃ na jalaṃ kvāpi dṛṣṭamāsīnmunīśvarāḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. O great sages, the trees became dry then without sprouts or fruits. No water was seen anywhere even for daily routine work.

## \|\| 8.3.11 \|\|

*ārdrībhāvo na labhyeta kharā vātā diśo daśa \|*  
*hāhākāro mahānāsītpṛthivyāṃ duḥkhado'ti hi \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. There was no greenery anywhere. The winds blew dry and rough. There was a painful hue and cry all over the earth.

## \|\| 8.3.12 \|\|

*saṃvartaṃ caiva bhūtānāṃ dṛṣṭvātri gṛhiṇī priyā \|*  
*sādhvī caivābravīdatriṃ mayā duḥkhaṃ na sahyate \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. On seeing the dissolution of all living beings, the chaste lady, the beloved wife of Atri said to her husband. “I cannot bear this distress.”

## \|\| 8.3.13 \|\|

*samādhau ca vilīnobhūdāsane saṃsthitaḥ svayam \|*  
*prāṇāyāmaṃ trirāvṛttyā kṛtvā munivarastadā \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. The sage then sat in the yogic pose, repeated the Prāṇāyāma three times and sank into the yogic trance.

## \|\| 8.3.14 \|\|

*dhyāyati sma paraṃ jyotirātmasthamātmanā ca saḥ \|*  
*atrirmunivaro jñānī śaṃkaraṃ nirvikārakam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Atri, the great sage of perfect knowledge, meditated on Śiva devoid of aberrations, the great splendour stationed in the soul, through his own soul.

## \|\| 8.3.15 \|\|

*svāmini dhyānalīne ca śiṣyāste dūrato gatāḥ \|*  
*annaṃ vinā tadā te tu muktvā taṃ svaguruṃ munim \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. When the preceptor ank into meditation his disciples unable to get food deserted the sage, their preceptor, and went away.

## \|\| 8.3.16 \|\|

*ekākinī tadā jātā sānasūyā pativratā \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Anasūyā the chaste lady then was left all alone.

## \|\| 8.3.17-18 \|\|

*siṣeve sā ca satataṃ taṃ mudā munisattamam \|*  
*pārthivaṃ sundaraṃ kṛtvā maṃtreṇa vidhi pūrvakam \|\| 17 \|\|*

*mānasairupacāraiśca pūjayāmāsa śaṃkaram \|*  
*tuṣṭāva śaṃkaraṃ bhaktyā saṃsevitvā muhurmuhuḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

17-18. She joyously served the sage constantly. Making a beautiful clay image through mantras in accordance with the rules, she worshipped Śiva through mental modes of

service. After serving Śiva repeatedly she eulogised him with devotion.

## \|\| 8.3.19-21 \|\|

*baddhāñjalipuṭā bhūtvā prakramya svāminaṃ śivam \|*  
*daṇḍavatpraṇipātena pratiprakramaṇaṃ tadā \|\| 19 \|\|*

*cakāra sucaritrā sānasūyā munikāminī \|*  
*daityāśca dānavāḥ sarve dṛṣṭvā tu sundarīṃ tadā \|\| 20 \|\|*

*vihvalāścābhavaṃstatra tejasā dūrataḥ sthitāḥ \|*  
*agniṃ dṛṣṭvā yathā dūre vartante tadvadeva hi \|\| 21 \|\|*

19-21. Anasūyā the well behaved wife of the sage circumambulated her lord and Śiva. After prostration she went round anticlockwise too. On seeing that beautiful lady the Daityas and Dānavas were excessively agitated by her brilliance. They kept themselves away as people do from a blazing fire.

## \|\| 8.3.22 \|\|

*tathaināṃ ca tadā dṛṣṭvā nāyāntīha samīpagāḥ \|*  
*atreśca tapasaścaivānasūyā śivasevanam \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. O leading brahmins, Anasūyā’s service to Śiva performed sacredly through mind, speech and body surpassed even the penance of Atri.

## \|\| 8.3.23 \|\|

*viśiṣyate sma viprendrā manovākkāyasaṃskṛtama \|*  
*tāvatkālaṃ tu sā devī paricaryāṃ cakāra ha \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. As long as the sage was engrossed in Prāṇāyāma, the gentle lady continued her service.

## \|\| 8.3.24 \|\|

*yāvatkālaṃ munivaraḥ prāṇāyāmaparāyaṇaḥ \|*  
*tau dampatī tadā tatra svasva kāryaparāyaṇau \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. O excellent sage, the couple thus engaged in their activities remained there, alone.

## \|\| 8.3.25 \|\|

*saṃsthitau muniśārdūla nānyaḥ kaścitparaḥ sthitaḥ \|*  
*evaṃ jātaṃ tadā kāle hyatriśca ṛṣisattamaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Thus a long time elapsed. The excellent sage, sunk in great meditation did not wake up at all.

## \|\| 8.3.26 \|\|

*dhyāne ca parame līno na vyabudhyata kiṃcana \|*  
*anasūyāpi sā sādhvī svāminaṃ vai śivaṃ tathā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Anasūyā the chaste lady worshipped only her lord and Śiva. That chaste lady did not care to know anything else.

## \|\| 8.3.27-28 \|\|

*nānyatparaṃ kiṃcijjānīte sma ca sā satī \|*  
*tasyaiva tapasā sarve tasyāśca bhajanena ca \|\| 27 \|\|*

*devāśca ṛṣayaścaiva gaṃgādyāssaritastathā \|*  
*darśanārthaṃ tayoḥ sarvāḥ pare prītyā samāyayuḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

27-28. On account of his penance and her worship the gods and the sages, Gaṅgā and the rivers came there in order to sec them with pleasure.

## \|\| 8.3.29-30 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā ca tattapassevāṃ vismayaṃ paramaṃ yayuḥ \|*  
*tayostadadbhutaṃ dṛṣṭvā samūcurbhajanaṃ varam \|\| 29 \|\|*

*ubhayoḥ kiṃ viśiṣṭaṃ ca tapaso bhajanasya ca \|*  
*atreścaiva tapaḥ proktamanasūyānusevanam \|\| 30 \|\|*

29-30. On seeing his penance and her service they were surprised. On seeing their wonderful activity they asked one another. “Which of the two—the penance or the service, is the greater?” They themselves replied—“Arti’s penance is good but Anasūyā’s service is better”.

## \|\| 8.3.31 \|\|

*tatsarvamubhayordṛṣṭvā samūcurbhajanaṃ varam \|*  
*pūrvaiśca ṛṣibhiścaiva duṣkaraṃ tu tapaḥ kṛtam \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. On seeing their performance some said “Her service is better.” Others said “Difficult penances had been performed by sages of yore but nothing like Dadhīci’s penance was ever performed by any”.

## \|\| 8.3.32-33 \|\|

*etādṛśaṃ tu kenāpi kva kṛtaṃ naitadabruvan \|*  
*dhanyo'yaṃ ca munirdhanyā tatheyamanasūyikā \|\| 32 \|\|*

*yadaitābhyāṃ paraprītyā kriyate sutapaḥ punaḥ \|*  
*etādṛśaṃ śubhaṃ caitattapo duṣkaramuttamam \|\| 33 \|\|*

32-33. The sage is blessed. Anasūyā is blessed, since great penance is pursued by these with great pleasure. If there be a person performing such an auspicious, difficult and excellent penance anywhere in the three worlds, we do not know.

## \|\| 8.3.34 \|\|

*trilokyāṃ kriyate kena sāmprataṃ jñāyate na hi \|*  
*tayoreva praśaṃsāṃ ca kṛtvā te tu yathāgatam \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. After praising them thus, they went away but only Gaṅgā and Śiva were left.

## \|\| 8.3.35 \|\|

*gatāste ca tadā tatra gaṃgā na giriśaṃ vinā \|*  
*gaṃgā madbhajanaprītā sādhvī dharmavimohitā \|\| 35 \|\|*

Gaṅgā said:—

35\. “Delighted in worshipping us the chaste lady is too much engrossed in virtuous rites. I shall help her and then go.”

## \|\| 8.3.36 \|\|

*kṛtvopakārametasyā gamiṣyāmītyuvāca sā \|*  
*śivo'pi dhyānasambaddho muneratrermunīśvarāḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. O great sages, Śiva too, drawn by the meditation of Atri, stationed himself there in full digits. He did not go to Kailāsa.

## \|\| 8.3.37 \|\|

*pūrṇāṃśena sthitastatra kailāsaṃ taṃ jagāma ha \|*  
*paṃcāśacca tathā cātra catvāri ṛṣisattamāḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. O excellent sages, fifty-four years had elapsed. There was no rain at all.

## \|\| 8.3.38-39 \|\|

*varṣāṇi ca gatānyāsanvṛṣṭirnaivābhavattadā \|*  
*yāvaccāpyatriṇā hyevaṃ tapasā dhyānamāśritam \|\| 38 \|\|*

*anasūyā tadā naiva gṛhṇāmītīṣaṇā kṛtā \|*  
*evaṃ ca kriyamāṇe hi muninā tapasi sthite \|*  
*anasūyāsubhajane yajjātaṃ śrūyatāmiti \|\| 39 \|\|*

38-39. As long as the sage was in meditation and penance, Anasūyā decided pot to eat anything. Now listen to what happened when the sage was performing penance and Anasūyā continued her service.

## Chapter 4 - The greatness of Atrīśvara

## \|\| 8.4.1 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*kadācitsa ṛṣiśreṣṭho hyatrirbrahmavidāṃ varaḥ \|*  
*jāgṛtaśca jalaṃ dehi pratyuvāca priyāmiti \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. Once that great sage Atri, the foremost among the knowers of Brahman woke up and spoke to his wife “Give me water.”

## \|\| 8.4.2-3 \|\|

*sāpi sādhvī tvavaśyaṃ ca gṛhītvātha kamaṇḍalum \|*  
*jagāma vipine tatra jalaṃ me nīyate kutaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

*kiṃ karomi kva gacchāmi kuto nīyeta vai jalam \|*  
*iti vismayamāpannā tāṃ gaṃgāṃ hi dadarśa sā \|\| 3 \|\|*

2-3. Of course the chaste lady took the water pot and went into the forest. (But she began to muse) “Whence can I fetch water? What shall I do? Where shall I go? How can water be fetched?” Wondering thus she saw Gaṅgā.

## \|\| 8.4.4 \|\|

*tāmanuvrajatī yāvat sābravīcca sadā hi tām \|*  
*gaṃgā saridvarā devī bibhratī sundarāṃ tanum \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. By the time she went over to her, Gaṅgā, the most excellent river, assumed a beautiful form and spoke to her.

## \|\| 8.4.5 \|\|

*gaṃgovāca \|*  
*prasannāsmi ca te devi kutra yāsi vadādhunā \|*  
*dhanyā tvaṃ subhage satyaṃ tavājñāṃ ca karomyaham \|\| 5 \|\|*

Gaṅgā said:—

5\. O gentle lady, I am delighted with you. Where are you going? Tell me now. O fortunate lady, you are blessed. I shall truly carry out your behest.

## \|\| 8.4.6 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*tadvacaśca tadā śrutvā ṛṣipatnī tapasvinī \|*  
*pratyuvāca vacaḥ prītyā svayaṃ sucakitā dvijāḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

6\. On hearing her words the saintly woman, the wife of the sage, was greatly surprised O brahmins. She replied with great pleasure.

## \|\| 8.4.7 \|\|

*anasūyovāca \|*  
*kā tvaṃ kamalapatrākṣi kuto vā tvaṃ samāgatā \|*  
*tathyaṃ brūhi kṛpāṃ kṛtvā sādhvī supravadā satī \|\| 7 \|\|*

Anasūyā said:—

7\. O beautiful lady with eyes like lotus-petals, whence have you come? Please tell me the truth. A chaste lady speaks good words.

## \|\| 8.4.8 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityukte ca tayā tatra munipatnyā munīśvarāḥ \|*  
*saridvarā divyarūpā gaṃgā vākyamathābravīt \|\| 8 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

8\. O great sages, when the wife of the sage spoke thus, the divine formed Gaṅgā, the foremost of the rivers, said.

## \|\| 8.4.9 \|\|

*gaṃgovāca \|*  
*svāminaḥ sevanaṃ dṛṣṭvā śivasya ca parātmanaḥ \|*  
*sādhvi dharmaṃ ca te dṛṣṭvā sthitāsmi tava sannidhau \|\| 9 \|\|*

Gaṅgā said:—

9\. O chaste lady, on seeing the service rendered by you unto your lord and to Śiva the great soul and on taking view of your holy rites, I am staying near you.

## \|\| 8.4.10 \|\|

*ahaṃ gaṃgā samāyātā bhajanātte śucismite \|*  
*vaśībhūtā hyahaṃ jātā yadicchasi vṛṇīṣva tat \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. O lady of pure smiles, I am Gaṅgā come here attracted by your worship. Choose whatever you wish.

## \|\| 8.4.11 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityukte gaṃgayā sādhvī namaskṛtya puraḥ sthitā \|*  
*uvāceti jalaṃ dehi cetprasannā mamā'dhunā \|\| 11 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

11\. On hearing what Gaṅgā said, the chaste lady bowed to her and said—“If you are pleased madam, give me water now.”

## \|\| 8.4.12 \|\|

*ityetadvacanaṃ śrutvā gartaṃ kurṣviti sā'bravīt \|*  
*śīghraṃ cāyācca tatkṛtvā sthitā tatkṣaṇamātrataḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. On hearing her words Gaṅgā said—“Dig a pit.” She then made a pit in a trice, came and stood before her.

## \|\| 8.4.13 \|\|

*tatra sā ca praviṣṭā ca jalarūpamabhūttadā \|*  
*āścaryyaṃ paramaṃ gatvā gṛhītaṃ ca jalaṃ tayā \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Gaṅgā entered the pit which became full of water In surprise, Anasūyā took the water.

## \|\| 8.4.14 \|\|

*uvāca vacanaṃ caitallokānāṃ sukhahetave \|*  
*anasūyā muneḥ patnī divyarūpāṃ saridvarām \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Anasūyā the wife of the sage spoke for the welfare of the people to Gaṅgā, the excellent river of divine form.

## \|\| 8.4.15 \|\|

*anasūyovāca \|*  
*yadi tvaṃ suprasannā me vartase ca kṛpāmayi \|*  
*sthātavyaṃ ca tvayā tāvanmatsvāmī yāvadā vrajet \|\| 15 \|\|*

Anasūyā said:—

15\. If you are delighted, if you have sympathy for me you will please stay here till my husband comes.

## \|\| 8.4.16 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*iti śrutvānasūyāyā vacanaṃ sukhadaṃ satām \|*  
*gaṃgovāca prasannāti hyatrerdāsyasi me'naghe \|\| 16 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

16\. On hearing the words of Anasūyā pleasing to the good, the delighted Gaṅgā said—“O sinless lady, you will give my water to Atri.”

## \|\| 8.4.17 \|\|

*ityukte ca tayā tatra hyanapāyi kṛtantathā \|*  
*svāmine tajjalaṃ divyaṃ dattvā tatpurataḥ sthitā \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. When Gaṅgā said thus, Anasūyā acted accordingly. She gave the divine water to her lord and stood before him.

## \|\| 8.4.18 \|\|

*sa ṛṣiścāpi suprītyā svācamya vidhipūrvakam \|*  
*papau divyaṃ jalaṃ tacca pītvā sukhamavāpa ha \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. The sage performed the Ācamana duly with great pleasure and drank the divine water. On drinking it he derived great happiness.

## \|\| 8.4.19 \|\|

*aho nityaṃ jalaṃ yacca pīyate tajjalaṃ na hi \|*  
*vicāryeti ca tenāśu paritaścāvalokitam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. “O this is not the water usually drunk by me.” After thinking thus he suddenly looked all round.

## \|\| 8.4.20 \|\|

*śuṣkānvṛkṣānsamālokya diśo rūkṣatarāstathā \|*  
*uvāca tāmṛṣiśreṣṭho na jātaṃ varṣaṇaṃ punaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. On seeing the trees dry and withered and the quarters rough and arid, the excellent sage spoke to her “No rain has fallen?”

## \|\| 8.4.21 \|\|

*taduktaṃ tatsamākarṇya netineti priyāntadā \|*  
*tāmuvāca punaḥ so'pi jalaṃ nītaṃ kutastvayā \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. On hearing what he said, the beloved lady replied in the negative. He then asked her whence she brought that water for him.

## \|\| 8.4.22 \|\|

*ityukte tu tadā tena vismayaṃ paramaṃ gatā \|*  
*anasūyā svamanasi sacintā tu munīśvarāḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. When he said this, O great sages, Anasūyā was much surprised. She then thought within herself.

## \|\| 8.4.23 \|\|

*nivedyate mayā cedvai tadotkarṣo bhavenmama \|*  
*nivedyate yadā naiva vratabhaṅgo bhavenmama \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. “If I tell him the truth it will be the exhibition of my greatness. If I do not tell him, it will be a violation of my rites.

## \|\| 8.4.24 \|\|

*nobhayaṃ ca tathā syādvai nivedyaṃ tattathā mama \|*  
*iti yāvadvicāryeta tāvatpṛṣṭā punaḥ punaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. I shall tell him in a manner to avoid the contingencies. Even as she was musing thus, she was asked again and again.

## \|\| 8.4.25 \|\|

*athānugrahataḥ śaṃbhoḥ prāptabuddhiḥ pativratā \|*  
*uvāca śrūyatāṃ svāminyajjātaṃ kathayāmi te \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Then, thanks to the blessings of Śiva, an intelligent idea struck her mind.

“O lord, please hear me. I shall tell you what has happened.”

## \|\| 8.4.26 \|\|

*anasūyovāca \|*  
*śaṃkarasya pratāpācca tavaiva sukṛtaistathā \|*  
*gaṃgā samāgatātraiva tadīyaṃ salilantvidam \|\| 26 \|\|*

Anasūyā said:—

26\. “Thanks to the power of Śiva and you merits, Gaṅgā has come here herself. This is her water, to be sure.”

## \|\| 8.4.27 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*evaṃ vacastadā śrutvā munirvismayamānasaḥ \|*  
*priyāmuvāca suprītyā śaṃkaraṃ manasā smaran \|\| 27 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

27\. On hearing this, the sage was surprised in his mind. Remembering Śiva mentally he spoke to his beloved with great pleasure.

## \|\| 8.4.28 \|\|

*atriruvāca \|*  
*priye sundari tvaṃ satyamatha vācaṃ vyalīkakām \|*  
*bravīṣi ca yathārthaṃ tvaṃ na manye durlabhantvidam \|\| 28 \|\|*

Atri said:—

28\. O beloved, O beautiful lady, whether you speak truth or lie, I cannot believe it. This is something rare.

## \|\| 8.4.29 \|\|

*asādhyaṃ yogibhiryacca devairapi sadā śubhe \|*  
*taccaivādya kathaṃ jātaṃ vismayaḥ paramo mama \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. O auspicious lady, how did that happen which is impossible even to the yogins and the gods. I am extremely surprised.

## \|\| 8.4.30 \|\|

*yadyevaṃ dṛśyate cedvai tanmayehaṃ na cānyathā \|*  
*iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā pratyuvāca patipriyā \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. If I sec that myself I shall believe it, not otherwise. On hearing his words the beloved wife spoke to the husband:—

## \|\| 8.4.31 \|\|

*anasūyovāca \|*  
*āgamyatāṃ mayā sārddhaṃ tvayā nātha mahāmune \|*  
*saridvarāyā gaṃgāyā draṣṭumicchā bhavedyadi \|\| 31 \|\|*

Anasūyā said:—

31\. O lord, O great sage, please come with me if you wish to see Gaṅgā, the foremost among the rivers.

## \|\| 8.4.32 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā tu samādāya patiṃ taṃ sā pativratā \|*  
*gatā drutaṃ śivaṃ smṛtvā yatra gaṃgā saridvarā \|\| 32 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

32\. After saying this and remembering Śiva, the chaste lady took her husband to the place where Gaṅgā, the foremost among the rivers was present.

## \|\| 8.4.33 \|\|

*darśayāmāsa tāṃ tatra gaṃgāṃ patye pativratā \|*  
*garte ca saṃsthitāṃ tatra svayaṃ divyasvarūpiṇīm \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. There the chaste lady showed to her husband Gaṅgā of divine form stationed within the pit.

## \|\| 8.4.34 \|\|

*tatra gatvā ṛṣiśreṣṭho gartaṃ ca jalapūritam \|*  
*ākaṇṭhaṃ sundaraṃ dṛṣṭvā dhanyeyamiti cābravīt \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Going there and seeing the beautiful pit full to the brim with water, the excellent sage said—“She is blessed.”

## \|\| 8.4.35 \|\|

*kiṃ madīyaṃ tapaścaiva kimanyeṣāṃ punastadā \|*  
*ityukto muniśārdūlo bhaktyā tuṣṭāva tāṃ tadā \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. “Is this the fruit of my own penance or of somebody else?” After saying this the leading sage eulogised her with devotion.

## \|\| 8.4.36 \|\|

*tato hi sa munistatra susnātaḥ subhage jale \|*  
*ācamya punarevātra stutiṃ cakre punaḥ punaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Then the sage took a clean bath in that excellent water. After performing the Ācamana he eulogised her again and again.

## \|\| 8.4.37 \|\|

*anasūyāpi saṃsnātā sundare tajjale tadā \|*  
*nityaṃ cakre muniḥ karma sānasūyāpi suvratā \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Anasūyā too took her bath in that beautiful, clear water. The sage performed the routine rites. Anasūyā too, of good rites, performed likewise.

## \|\| 8.4.38 \|\|

*tatassovāca tāṃ gaṃgā gamyate svasthalaṃ mayā \|*  
*ityukte ca punaḥ sādhvī tāmuvāca saridvarām \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Then Gaṅgā told her “I am returning to my place.” When this was said, the chaste lady spoke to the leading river again.

## \|\| 8.4.39 \|\|

*anasūyovāca \|*  
*yadi prasannā deveśi yadyasti ca kṛpā mayi \|*  
*tvayā stheyaṃ niścalatvādasmindevi tapovane \|\| 39 \|\|*

Anasūyā said:—

39\. O goddess if you are delighted, if you are kind to me, you will stay in this penance-grove for ever.

## \|\| 8.4.40 \|\|

*mahatāṃ ca svabhāvaśca nāṃgīkṛtya parityajet \|*  
*ityuktā ca karau baddhvā tāṃ tuṣṭāva punaḥpunaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. This is the nature of the great that once they accept a request they will never forego it. After expressing her desire with joined palms, she eulogised her again and again.

## \|\| 8.4.41 \|\|

*ṛṣiścāpi tathovāca tvayā stheyaṃ saridvare \|*  
*sānukūlā bhava tvaṃ hi sanāthāndevi naḥ kuru \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. The sage said likewise—“O leading river, you shall stay. O goddess, be favourable to us and bless us with your support.”

## \|\| 8.4.42 \|\|

*tadīyaṃ tadvacaḥ śrutvā ramyaṃ gaṃgā saridvarā \|*  
*prasannamānasā gaṃgā'nasūyāṃ vākyamabravīt \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. On hearing his pleasing words, the leading river Gaṅgā was delighted in her mind and spoke to Anasūyā.

## \|\| 8.4.43 \|\|

*gaṃgovāca \|*  
*śaṃkarārcanasaṃbhūtaphalaṃ varṣasya yacchasi \|*  
*svāminaśca tadā sthāsye devānāmupakāraṇāta \|\| 43 \|\|*

Gaṅgā said:—

43\. I shall stay back for helping the gods if you pass on to me a year’s merit accruing to you by worshipping lord Śiva.

## \|\| 8.4.44 \|\|

*tathā dānairna me tuṣṭistīrthasnānaistathā ca vai \|*  
*yajñaistathātha vā yogairyathā pātivratena ca \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. I am not so much satisfied with charitable gifts, or ceremonial ablutions in the holy centres or sacrifices or the practice of Yogic rites as I am satisfied with chastity.

## \|\| 8.4.45 \|\|

*pativratāṃ yathā dṛṣṭvā manasaḥ prīṇanaṃ bhavet \|*  
*tathā nānyairupāyaiśca satyaṃ me vyāhṛtaṃ sati \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. O chaste lady, this is the truth I speak that the mind is not so pleased by any other means as it is pleased at the sight of a chaste lady.

## \|\| 8.4.46 \|\|

*pativratāṃ striyaṃ dṛṣṭvā pāpanāśo bhavenmama \|*  
*śuddhā jātā viśeṣeṇa gaurītulyā pativratā \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. On seeing a chaste lady, all my sins have perished. I have become purified and like Pārvatī have become chaste.

## \|\| 8.4.47 \|\|

*tasmācca yadi lokasya hitāya tatprayacchasi \|*  
*tarhyahaṃ sthiratāṃ yāsye yadi kalyāṇamicchasi \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. If you seek for welfare, you give up that merit for the benefit of the world, then I shall become steady and stable here.

## \|\| 8.4.48 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityevaṃ vacanaṃ śrutvā'nasūyā sā pativratā \|*  
*gaṃgāyai pradadau puṇyaṃ sarvaṃ tadvarṣasaṃbhavam \|\| 48 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

48\. On hearing these words, the chaste Anasūyā passed on a year’s merit to Gaṅgā.

## \|\| 8.4.49 \|\|

*mahatāṃ ca svabhāvo hi pareṣāṃ hitamāvahet \|*  
*suvarṇaṃ candanaṃ cekṣurasastatra nidarśanam \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. It is the nature of the great that they do what is beneficial to others. The instance of gold-sandal and sugarcane juice may be cited.

## \|\| 8.4.50 \|\|

*etaddṛṣṭvānasūyaṃ tatkarma pātivrataṃ mahat \|*  
*prasannobhūnmahādevaḥ pārthivādāvirāśu vai \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. On seeing this performance of Anasūya, a chaste woman, Lord Śiva was delighted and appeared out of his earthern phallic form.

## \|\| 8.4.51 \|\|

*śaṃbhuruvāca \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā te karma sādhvyetat prasanno'smi pativrate \|*  
*varaṃ brūhi priye matto yataḥ priyatarāsi me \|\| 51 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

51\. O chaste lady, on seeing this activity of yours I am delighted. Mention the boon you wish to choose, since I am very fond of you.

## \|\| 8.4.52 \|\|

*atha tau dampatī śaṃbhumabhūtāṃ sundarākṛtim \|*  
*pañcavaktrādisaṃyuktaṃ haraṃ prekṣya suvismitau \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Then the couple glanced at the wonderfully beautiful form of Śiva. On seeing five-faced Śiva they were much surprised.

## \|\| 8.4.53 \|\|

*natvā stutvā karau baddhvā mahābhaktisamanvitau \|*  
*avocetāṃ samabhyarcya śaṃkaraṃ lokaśaṃkaram \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. Bowing to and eulogising Śiva, the benefactor of the worlds, the couple endowed with great devotion spoke to him after worshipping him.

## \|\| 8.4.54 \|\|

*dampatī ūcatuḥ \|*  
*yadi prasanno deveśa prasannā jagadambikā \|*  
*asmiṃstapovane tiṣṭha lokānāṃ sukhado bhava \|\| 54 \|\|*

The couple said:—

54\. O lord of gods, if you are delighted, if the mother of the universe is also delighted, please stay in this penance-grove and be the bestower of happiness to the worlds.

## \|\| 8.4.55 \|\|

*prasannā ca tadā gaṃgā prasannaśca śivastadā \|*  
*ubhau tau ca sthitau tatra yatrāsīdṛṣisattamaḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Then the river Gaṅgā was delighted. Śiva too was pleased. They stayed at the hermitage of the excellent sage.

## \|\| 8.4.56 \|\|

*atrīśvaraśca nāmnāsīdīśvaraḥ paraduḥkhahā \|*  
*gaṃgā sāpi sthitā tatra tadā gartetha māyayā \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. Lord Śiva, the destroyer of miseries was known as Atrīśvara. Gaṅgā too stayed in the pit by her magical power.

## \|\| 8.4.57 \|\|

*taddinaṃ hi samārabhya tatrākṣayyajalaṃ sadā \|*  
*hastamātre hi tadgarte gaṃgā mandākinī hyabhūt \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Thence onwards there is a perennial supply of water in that pit deep only by a hand’s length. Gaṅgā flows gently there.

## \|\| 8.4.58 \|\|

*tatraiva ṛṣayo divyāḥ samājagmussahāṃganāḥ \|*  
*tīrthāttīrthācca te sarve te purā nirgatā dvijāḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. Divine sages came there with their womenfolk and the sages who had formerly gone out of the various holy centres returned.

## \|\| 8.4.59 \|\|

*yavāśca vrīhayaścaiva yajñayāgaparāyaṇāḥ \|*  
*yuktā ṛṣivaraistaiśca homaṃ cakruśca te janāḥ \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. Barleys and food-grains were plenty. People interested in sacrifices performed these accompanied by the sages.

## \|\| 8.4.60 \|\|

*karmabhistaiśca saṃtuṣṭā vṛṣṭiṃ cakrurghanāstadā \|*  
*ānandaḥ paramo loke babhūvātimunīśvarāḥ \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. O great sages, delighted with these rites, clouds showered in plenty. There was great bliss in the world.

## \|\| 8.4.61 \|\|

*atrīśvarasya māhātmyamityuktaṃ vaḥ sukhāvaham \|*  
*bhuktimuktipradaṃ sarvakāmadaṃ bhakti varddhanam \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. Thus the greatness of Atrīśvara has been explained to you. It is pleasing and conducive to worldly pleasures and salvation. It yields all desires and enhances devotion.

## Chapter 5 - The death of the Brahmin lady and the greatness of Nandikeśvara

## \|\| 8.5.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*kālaṃjare girau divye nīlakaṇṭho maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*liṃgarūpassadā caiva bhaktānandapradaḥ sadā \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. Nīlakaṇṭha, the great god, stands in the phallic form on the divine mountain, Kālañjara. He is the bestower of bliss to the devotees always

## \|\| 8.5.2 \|\|

*mahimā tasya divyosti śrutismṛtiprakītitaḥ \|*  
*tīrthaṃ tadākhyayā tatra snānātpātakanāśakṛt \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. His greatness is divine as glorified in Śrutis and Smṛtis. The holy centre there is of that name. By taking ceremonial ablution there, sins are quelled.

## \|\| 8.5.3 \|\|

*revātīre yāni santi śivaliṃgāni suvratāḥ \|*  
*sarvasaukhyakarāṇīha teṣāṃ saṃkhyā na vidyate \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. O great ones of good rites, there is no limit to the phallic forms of Śiva on the bank of Revā that bestow all sorts of happiness.

## \|\| 8.5.4 \|\|

*sā ca rudrasvarūpā hi darśanātpāpahārikā \|*  
*tasyāṃ sthitāśca ye kecitpāṣāṇāḥ śivarūpiṇaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. The river itself is in the form of Rudra that quells sins by mere sight. Any stone or pebble within it is in the form of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.5.5 \|\|

*tathāpi ca pravakṣyāmi yathānyāni munīśvarāḥ \|*  
*pradhānaśivaliṃgāni bhuktimuktipradāni ca \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. O great sages, still I shall enumerate and explain the important phallic forms that yield worldly pleasures here and salvation hereafter.

## \|\| 8.5.6 \|\|

*ārteśvarasunāmā hi vartate pāpahārakaḥ \|*  
*parameśvara iti khyātaḥ siṃheśvara iti smṛtaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. One with the good name of Ārteśvara is the dispeller of sins. What is famous as Parameśvara and what is remembered as Siṃheśvara (are good shrines).

## \|\| 8.5.7 \|\|

*śarmeśaśca tathā cātra kumāreśvara eva ca \|*  
*puṇḍarīkeśvaraḥ khyāto maṇḍapeśvara eva ca \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Śarmeśa, Kumāreśvara, Puṇḍarīkeśvara and Maṇḍapeśvara are all well known.

## \|\| 8.5.8 \|\|

*tīkṣṇeśanāmā tatrāsīddarśanātpāpahārakaḥ \|*  
*dhuṃdhureśvaranāmāsītpāpahā narmadātaṭe \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Tīksṇeśa is there which quells sins at the very sight. What is called Dhundhureśvara is on the banks of the Narmadā river. It quells sins.

## \|\| 8.5.9 \|\|

*śūleśvara iti khyātastathā kuṃbheśvaraḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*kubereśvaranāmāpi tathā someśvaraḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Śūleśvara, Kumbheśvara, Kubereśvara and Someśvara are very famous.

## \|\| 8.5.10 \|\|

*nīlakaṇṭho maṃgaleśo maṃgalāyatano mahān \|*  
*mahākapīśvaro devaḥ sthāpito hi hanūmatā \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Maṅgaleśa is blue-necked and is a great bestower of auspiciousness. Lord Mahākapīśvara, was installed by Hanūmat.

## \|\| 8.5.11 \|\|

*tataśca naṃdiko devo hatyākoṭinivārakaḥ \|*  
*sarvakāmārthadaścaiva mokṣado hi prakīrtita \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Lord Nandika prevents the sins of a crore of murders. He is glorified as the bestower of the fruits of desire and salvation too.

## \|\| 8.5.12 \|\|

*nandīkeśaṃ ca yaścaiva pūjayetparayā mudā \|*  
*nityaṃ tasyākhilā siddhirbhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. If one worships Nandikeśa with great devotion one will certainly achieve all Siddhis.

## \|\| 8.5.13 \|\|

*tatra tīre ca yaḥ snāti revāyāṃ munisattamāḥ \|*  
*tasya kāmāśca sidhyanti sarvaṃ pāpaṃ vinaśyati \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. O excellent sages, he who takes bath in the Revā or on its banks, realises all desires and gets rid of sins.

## \|\| 8.5.14 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*evaṃ tasya ca māhātmyaṃ kathaṃ tatra mahāmate \|*  
*nandikeśasya kṛpayā kathyatāṃ ca tvayādhunā \|\| 14 \|\|*

The sages said:—

14\. O highly intelligent one, how is the greatness of Nandikeśa there. Let it be kindly mentioned by you now.

## \|\| 8.5.15 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*samyakpṛṣṭaṃ bhavadbhiśca kathayāmi yathāśrutam \|*  
*śaunakādyāśca munayaḥ sarve hi śṛṇutādarāt \|\| 15 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

15\. O sages, Śaunaka and others, you have well put the question. I shall narrate in the manner I have heard. Listen respectfully.

## \|\| 8.5.16 \|\|

*purā yudhiṣṭhireṇaiva pṛṣṭaśca ṛṣisattamaḥ \|*  
*yathovāca tathā vacmi bhavatsnehānusārataḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Formerly the excellent sage was asked by Yudhiṣṭhira. Out of affection for you, I shall tell you as he had mentioned then.

## \|\| 8.5.17 \|\|

*revāyāḥ paścime tīre karṇikī nāma vai purī \|*  
*virājate suśobhāḍhyā caturvarṇasamākulā \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. The city named Karṇikī of great splendour shines on the western bank of the Revā. People of four castes reside there in plenty.

## \|\| 8.5.18 \|\|

*tatra dvijavaraḥ kaściduttasya kulasambhavaḥ \|*  
*kāśyāṃ gataśca putrābhyāmarpayitvā svapatnikām \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. A certain noble brahmin there, born of the family of Utta, entrusted his wife to the care of his two sons and went to Kāśī.

## \|\| 8.5.19 \|\|

*tatraiva sa mṛto vipraḥ putrābhyāṃ ca śrutantadā \|*  
*tadīyaṃ caiva tatkṛtyaṃ cakrāte putrakāvubhau \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. The brahmin died there itself. On getting the news the two sons performed the obsequies.

## \|\| 8.5.20 \|\|

*patnī ca pālayāmāsa putrau putrahitaiṣiṇī \|*  
*putrau ca varjayitvā ca vibhaktaṃ vai dhanaṃ tayā \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. The wife looked after the sons, seeking their welfare. After bringing up the sons she divided the assets.

## \|\| 8.5.21-22 \|\|

*svīyaṃ ca rakṣitaṃ kiṃciddhanaṃ maraṇahetave \|*  
*tataśca dvijapatnī hi kiyatkālaṃ mṛtā ca sā \|\| 21 \|\|*

*kadācitkriyamāṇā sā vividhaṃ puṇyamācarat \|*  
*na mṛtā daivayogena dvijapatnī ca sā dvijāḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

21-22. She reserved some amount for her funeral expenses. After sometime when she was going to die, she made pious gifts of various sorts. O brahmins, providentially she did not die.

## \|\| 8.5.23 \|\|

*yadā prāṇānna mumuce mātā daivāttayośca sā \|*  
*taddṛṣṭvā jananīkaṣṭaṃ putrakāvūcatustadā \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. When she did not die providentially, the son observing her ailments said.

## \|\| 8.5.24 \|\|

*putrāvūcatuḥ \|*  
*kiṃ nyūnaṃ vidyate mātaḥ kaṣṭaṃ yadvidyate mahat \|*  
*vriyatāṃ tadṛtaṃ prītyā tadāvāṃ karavāvahe \|\| 24 \|\|*

The sons said:—

24\. O mother, what is amiss that you suffer so much. Please tell us so that we shall carry it out with pleasure.

## \|\| 8.5.25 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*tacchrutvoktaṃ tayā tatra nyūnaṃ tu vidyate bahu \|*  
*tadeva kriyate cedvai sukhena maraṇaṃ bhavet \|\| 25 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

25\. On hearing these words she said—“There is much that is amiss. If you do the same I shall die happily.”

## \|\| 8.5.26 \|\|

*jyeṣṭhaputraśca yastasyāstenoktaṃ kathyatāntvayā \|*  
*kariṣyāmi tadetaddhi tayā ca kathitantadā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. The elder son replied—“Please do mention. I shall do the same.” She then replied.

## \|\| 8.5.27 \|\|

*dvijapatnyuvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu putra vacaḥ prītyā purāsīnme manaḥ spṛhā \|*  
*kāśyāṃ gaṃtuṃ tathā nāsīdidānīṃ mriyate punaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

The brahmin lady said:—

27\. O son, hear with attention. I had a great desire to go to Kāśī. That was not to be. Now I am dying here.

## \|\| 8.5.28 \|\|

*mamāsthīni tvayā putra kṣepaṇīyānyatandritam \|*  
*gaṃgājale śubhaṃ tedya bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Dear son, my bones shall be cast off into the waters of the Gaṅgā assiduously. That pious act will bless you, to be sure.

## \|\| 8.5.29 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityukte ca tayā mātrā sa jyeṣṭhatanayobravīt \|*  
*mātaraṃ mātṛbhaktistu suvratāṃ maraṇonmukhīm \|\| 29 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

29\. When she of good rites and on the verge of death, said thus, the affectionate elder son replied to her.

## \|\| 8.5.30 \|\|

*\|\| putra uvāca \|*  
*mātastvayā sukhenaiva prāṇāstyājyā na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*tava kāryaṃ purā kṛtvā paścātkāryaṃ madīyakam \|\| 30 \|\|*

The son said:—

30\. “O mother, you shall die peacefully and with confidence. I shall carry out your task first and then attend to mine.”

## \|\| 8.5.31 \|\|

*iti haste jalaṃ dattvā yāvatputro gṛhaṃ gataḥ \|*  
*tāvatsā ca mṛtā tatra harasmaraṇatatparā \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. After saying this, he handed water to her and went to his house. Remembering Śiva, she died in the meantime.

## \|\| 8.5.32 \|\|

*tasyāścaiva tu yatkṛtyaṃ tatsarvaṃ saṃvidhāya saḥ \|*  
*māsikaṃ karma kṛtvā tu gamanāya pracakrame \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. After performing her obsequies and the monthly Śrāddha he started on his journey.

## \|\| 8.5.33 \|\|

*dvayoḥ śreṣṭhatamo yo vai suvādo nāma viśrutaḥ \|*  
*tadasthīni samādāya nissṛtastīrthakāmyayā \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. The elder son who was famous as Suvāda took her bones and started on his pilgrimage.

## \|\| 8.5.34-35 \|\|

*saṃgṛhya sevakaṃ kaṃcittenaiva sahitastadā \|*  
*āśvāsya bhāryyāputrāṃśca mātuḥ priyacikīrṣayā \|\| 34 \|\|*

*śrāddhadānādikaṃ bhojyaṃ kṛtvā vidhimanuttamam \|*  
*maṃgalasmaraṇaṃ kṛtvā nirjagāma gṛhāddvijaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

34-35. He engaged a servant to accompany him. He consoled his wife and sons. He performed Śrāddha, Dāna and other pious rites. He then set out from his house after Maṅgala Smaraṇa accompanied by the servant.

## \|\| 8.5.36 \|\|

*taddine yojanaṃ gatvā viṃśati grāmake śubhe \|*  
*uvāsāstaṃ gate bhānau gṛhe viprasya kasyacit \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. He walked about twelve kilometres that day and when the sun set, he halted at the house of a certain Brahmin in the auspicious village of Viṃśatigrāma.131

## \|\| 8.5.37 \|\|

*cakre sandhyādisatkarma sa dvijo vidhipūrvakam \|*  
*stavādi kṛtavāṃstatra śaṃbhoradbhutakarmaṇaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. He performed Sandhyā and other rites duly. He recited the hymns of prayer to Śiva of wonderful activities.

## \|\| 8.5.38-39 \|\|

*sevakena tadā yukto brāhmaṇaḥ saṃsthitastadā \|*  
*yāminī ca gatā tatra muhūrtadvayasaṃmitā \|\| 38 \|\|*

*etasminnaṃtare tatraikamāścaryyamabhūttadā \|*  
*śṛṇutādaratastacca munayo vo vadāmyaham \|\| 39 \|\|*

38-39. The brahmin stayed there accompanied by the servant. The night had passed by two muhūrtas when something surprising happened. O sages, listen attentively. I shall tell you everything.

## Chapter 6 - The Brahmin lady attains heaven

## \|\| 8.6.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*gauścaikāpyabhavattatra hyaṃgaṇe baṃdhitā śubhā \|*  
*tadaiva brāhmaṇo rātrāvājagāma bahirgataḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. There was a fine cow tied up in the courtyard. The brahmin of the house who had gone out returned after nightfall.

## \|\| 8.6.2 \|\|

*sa uvāca priyāṃ svīyāṃ dṛṣṭvā gāmaṃgaṇe sthitām \|*  
*adugdhāṃ khedanirviṇṇo dogdhukāmo munīśvarāḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. O great sages, on seeing the cow, tethered in the courtyard, not yet milked, the distressed brahmin desirous of milking it told his wife.

## \|\| 8.6.3 \|\|

*gauḥ priye naiva dugdhā te setyuktā vatsamānayat \|*  
*dohanārthaṃ samāhūya striyaṃ śīghrataraṃ tadā \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. In order to milk the cow he called his wife quickly and said “O beloved, the cow is not yet milked.” Thus urged she brought the calf.

## \|\| 8.6.4 \|\|

*vatsaṃ kīle svayaṃ baddhuṃ yatnaṃ caivākarottadā \|*  
*brāhmaṇassa gṛhasvāmī munayo dugdhalālasaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. O sages, the owner of the house, the brahmin, desirous of milk tried to tie up the calf to the peg.

## \|\| 8.6.5 \|\|

*vatsopi karṣamāṇaśca pāde vai pādapīḍanam \|*  
*cakāra brāhmaṇaścaiva kaṣṭaṃ prāptaśca suvratāḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Dragged by the leg the calf kicked. O great one of good rites, the brahmin was put to pain.

## \|\| 8.6.6 \|\|

*tena pādaprahāreṇa sa dvijaḥ krodhamūrchitaḥ \|*  
*vatsaṃ ca tāḍayāmāsa kūpairdṛḍhataraṃ tadā \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Infuriated by the kick of the calf, he hit the calf severely with the pieces of firewood.

## \|\| 8.6.7 \|\|

*vatsopi pīḍitastena śrāṃtaścaivābhavattadā \|*  
*dugdhā gaurmocito vatso na krodhena dvijanmanā \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Hit by him the calf became utterly exhausted. Due to anger neither the cow was milked nor the calf was untied by the brahmin.

## \|\| 8.6.8 \|\|

*gaurdogdhuṃ mahatprītyā rodanaṃ cākarottadā \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā ca rodanaṃ tasyā vatso vākyamathābravīt \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. For the pleasure of getting milked, the cow mooed loudly. On hearing the mooing sound of the cow, the calf said.

## \|\| 8.6.9 \|\|

*vatsa uvāca \|*  
*kathaṃ ca rudyate mātaḥ kinte duḥkhamupasthitam \|*  
*tannivedaya me prītyā tacchrutvā gauravocata \|\| 9 \|\|*

The calf said:—

9\. “O mother, why do you cry? What is the misery that has befallen you? Please tell me lovingly.” On hearing this, the cow said.

## \|\| 8.6.10 \|\|

*śrūyatāṃ putra me duḥkhaṃ vaktuṃ śaknomyahaṃ na hi \|*  
*duṣṭena tāḍitatvaṃ ca tena duḥkhaṃ mamāpyabhūt \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. “O son, listen to me. Although I am unable to express my grief adequately, you have been hit by the wicked man. Hence I am distressed.”

## \|\| 8.6.11 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*svamāturvacanaṃ śrutvā sa vatsaḥ pratyabodhayat \|*  
*pratyuvāca svajananīṃ prārabdhapariniṣṭhitaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

11\. On hearing his mother’s words, the calf taking his stand by the principle of previous action replied enlightening his mother.

## \|\| 8.6.12 \|\|

*kiṃ karttavyaṃ kva gaṃtavyaṃ karmabaddhā vayaṃ yataḥ \|*  
*kṛtaṃ caiva yathāpūrvaṃ bhujyate ca tathādhunā \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. “What should be done? Where should we go? Since the results are bound by previous actions, we experience joy or sorrow accordingly.

## \|\| 8.6.13 \|\|

*hasatā kriyate karma rudatā paribhujyate \|*  
*duḥkhadātā na kopyasti sukhadātā na kaścana \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Activities are pursued with pleasure and fruits are derived sorrowfully. There is none who gives misery and none who gives happiness.

## \|\| 8.6.14 \|\|

*sukhaduḥkhe paro datta ityeṣā kumatirmatā \|*  
*ahaṃ cāpi karomyatra mithyājñānaṃ tadocyate \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. This is a wrong notion that another man gives us sorrow or pleasure. This is also an illusory concept that it is I who do this or that.

## \|\| 8.6.15 \|\|

*svakarmaṇā bhaveddukhaṃ sukhaṃ tenaiva karmaṇā \|*  
*tasmācca pūjyate karma sarvaṃ karmaṇi saṃsthitama \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Misery or happiness results from our own actions. Hence action is prominent and everything is founded on action.

## \|\| 8.6.16 \|\|

*tvaṃ caivāhaṃ ca jananī ime jīvādayaśca ye \|*  
*te sarve karmaṇā baddhā na śocyāḥ karhicittvayā \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. You the mother, I the child and all the living beings are bound by action. They are not to be bewailed by you.

## \|\| 8.6.17 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*evaṃ śrutvā svaputrasya vacanaṃ jñānagarbhitam \|*  
*putraśokānvitā dīnā sā ca gaurabravīdidam \|\| 17 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

17\. On hearing these words of her calf full of wisdom, the distressed cow sorrowing for her calf, said thus.

## \|\| 8.6.18 \|\|

*gauruvāca \|*  
*vatsa sarvaṃ vijānāmi karmādhīnāḥ prajā iti \|*  
*tathāpi māyayā grastā duḥkhaṃ prāpnomyahaṃ punaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

The cow said:—

18\. “O dear son, I know fully well that all the people are subservient to their actions. Still, seized by Māyā I feel much distressed.

## \|\| 8.6.19 \|\|

*rodanaṃ ca kṛtaṃ bhūri duḥkhaśāntirbhavennahi \|*  
*ityetadvacanaṃ śrutvā prasūṃ vatso'bravīdidam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. I have cried a lot but the sorrow has not subsided.” On hearing these words, the calf spoke to her again.

## \|\| 8.6.20 \|\|

*\|\| vatsa uvāca \|*  
*yadyevaṃ ca vijānāmi punaśca rudanaṃ kutaḥ \|*  
*kṛtvā ca sādhyate kiñcittasmādduḥkhaṃ tyajādhunā \|\| 20 \|\|*

The calf said:—

20\. If you know thus, can anything be gained by crying? Hence leave off your sorrow.

## \|\| 8.6.21 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*evaṃ putravacaḥ śrutvā tanmātā duḥkhasaṃyutā \|*  
*niḥśvasyāti tadā dhenurvatsaṃ vacanamabravīt \|\| 21 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

21\. On hearing the words of her son, the distressed cow heaved a deep sigh and spoke to the calf.

## \|\| 8.6.22 \|\|

*gauruvāca \|*  
*mama duḥkhaṃ tadā gacchedyathā dukhaṃ tathāvidham \|*  
*bhaveddhi brāhmaṇasyāpi satyametadbravīmyaham \|\| 22 \|\|*

The cow said:—

22\. My distress will disappear only when a similar misery befalls the brahmin. I am telling you the truth.

## \|\| 8.6.23 \|\|

*prātaścaiva mayā putra śṛṃgābhyāṃ hi haniṣyate \|*  
*hatasya jīvitaṃ sadyo yāsyatyasya na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Tomorrow morning I shall hit him by my horns. No doubt, when hit he will lose his life.

## \|\| 8.6.24 \|\|

*vatsa uvāca \|*  
*prathamaṃ yatkṛtaṃ karma tatphalaṃ bhujyate'dhunā \|*  
*asyāśca brahmahatyāyā mātaḥ kiṃ phalamāpsyase \|\| 24 \|\|*

The calf said:—

24\. The fruit of previous actions wc experience now. What is the fruit you are goiṇg to reap as a result of this brahmin-slaughter?

## \|\| 8.6.25 \|\|

*samābhyāṃ puṇyapāpābhyāṃ bhavejjanma ca bhārate \|*  
*tayoḥ kṣaye ca bhogena mātarmuktiravāpyate \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. When merits and demerits are on a par one takes birth in Bharata. O mother, when they are wiped off by enjoyment or suffering, liberation is achieved.

## \|\| 8.6.26 \|\|

*kadāpi karmaṇo nāśaḥ kadā bhogaḥ prajāyate \|*  
*tasmācca punarevaṃ tvaṃ karma mā kartumudyatā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Some time the action is destroyed, some time the action results. That is why you have begun to perform this misdeed.

## \|\| 8.6.27 \|\|

*ahaṃ kutaste putrodya tvaṃ mātā kuta eva ca \|*  
*vṛthābhimānaḥ putratve mātṛtve ca vicāryyatām \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Whence am I your son? Whence are you my mother? This fond consciousness of sonhood and motherhood is futile. Ponder.

## \|\| 8.6.28 \|\|

*kva mātā kva pitā viddhi kva svāmī kva kalatrakam \|*  
*na ko'pi kasya cāstīha sarvepi svakṛtaṃ bhujaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Where is mother? Where is father? Where is husband? Where is wife? Know that none belongs to any one here. AH reap the fruits of their own actions.

## \|\| 8.6.29 \|\|

*evaṃ jñātvā tvayā mātarduḥkhaṃ tyājyaṃ suyatnataḥ \|*  
*subhagācaraṇaṃ kāryaṃ paralokasukhepsayā \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. O mother, knowing thus you cast off your misery strenuously. If there is a desire for pleasure hereafter you shall do good deeds here.

## \|\| 8.6.30 \|\|

*gauruvāca \|*  
*evaṃ jānāmyahaṃ putra māyā māṃ na jahātyasau \|*  
*tvadduḥkhena suduḥkhaṃ me tasmai dāsye tadeva hi \|\| 30 \|\|*

The Cow said:—

30\. O son, I know this but delusion does not forsake me. I am much distressed by your misery. I shall return tit for tat.

## \|\| 8.6.31 \|\|

*punaśca brahmahatyāyā nāśo yatra bhavediha \|*  
*tatsthalaṃ ca mayā dṛṣṭaṃ hatyā me hi gamiṣyati \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. I know the place that dispels the sin of brahmin-slaughter. Going there I can get rid of the sin of Brahmin-slaughter.

## \|\| 8.6.32 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityetadvacanaṃ śrutvā svamāturgordvijottamāḥ \|*  
*maunatvaṃ svīkṛtaṃ tatra vatsenoktaṃ na kiñcana \|\| 32 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

32\. O excellent brahmins, on hearing these words of the mother cow, the calf observed silence. It did not say anything more.

## \|\| 8.6.33 \|\|

*tayostadadbhutaṃ vṛttaṃ śrutvā pāntho dvijastadā \|*  
*hṛdā vicārayāmāsa vismito hi munīśvarāḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. O great sages, on hearing this wonderful dialogue between the two, the pilgrim brahmin was surprised and he thought within himself.

## \|\| 8.6.34 \|\|

*idamatyadbhutaṃ vṛttaṃ dṛṣṭvā prātarmayā khalu \|*  
*gaṃtavyaṃ punarevāto gaṃtavyaṃ tatsthalaṃ punaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. “I shall leave in the morning only after seeing the wonderful sequel. I shall go to that holy spot myself.”

## \|\| 8.6.35 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*vicāryeti hṛdā vipraḥ sa dvijāḥ sevakena ca \|*  
*suṣvāpa tatra jananībhaktaḥ paramavismitaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. O brahmins, after thinking thus the pilgrim-brahmin, devoted to his mother, became surprised. He and his servant went to sleep.

## \|\| 8.6.36 \|\|

*prātaḥkāle tadā jāte gṛhasvāmī samutthitaḥ \|*  
*bodhayāmāsa taṃ pānthaṃ vacanaṃ cedamabravīt \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. When the day dawned, the owner of the house got up. Awakening the traveller he spoke these words.

## \|\| 8.6.37 \|\|

*dvija uvāca \|*  
*svapiṣi tvaṃ kimarthaṃ hi prātaḥkālo bhavatyalam \|*  
*svayātrāṃ kuru taṃ deśaṃ gamanecchā ca yatra ha \|\| 37 \|\|*

The brahmin said:—

37\. “Why do you sleep? It is already day-break. Go on your journey to the place where you desire to go.”

## \|\| 8.6.38 \|\|

*tenoktaṃ śrūyatāmbrahmañccharīre sevakasya me \|*  
*vartate hi vyathā sthitvā muhūrtaṃ gamyate tataḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. He replied—“Please listen, O brahmin, there is an acute pain in the stomach of my servant. We will stay a little longer and go thereafter.”

## \|\| 8.6.39 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityevaṃ ca miṣaṃ kṛtvā suṣvāpa puruṣastadā \|*  
*tadvṛttamakhilaṃ jñātumadbhutaṃ vismayāvaham \|\| 39 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

39\. Creating such a pretext the man continued to sleep, desiring to know the details of the wonderful incident that had surprised him much.

## \|\| 8.6.40 \|\|

*dohanasya tadā kāle brāhmaṇaḥ svasutaṃ prati \|*  
*uvāca gaṃtukāmaśca kāryārthaṃ kutracicca saḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. At the time of milking, the brahmin who wanted to go somewhere on some business spoke to his son.

## \|\| 8.6.41 \|\|

*pitovāca \|*  
*mayā tu gamyate putra kāryārthaṃ kutracitpunaḥ \|*  
*dhenurdohyā tvayā vatsa sāvadhānādiyaṃ nijā \|\| 41 \|\|*

The father said:—

41\. O son, I am going out on some business. Dear, you shall carefully milk our cow.

## \|\| 8.6.42 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā brāhmaṇavarassa jagāma ca kutracit \|*  
*putraḥ samutthitastatra vatsaṃ ca muktavāṃstadā \|\| 42 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

42\. After saying this, the brahmin left the house. Thereafter the son got up and untied the calf.

## \|\| 8.6.43-44 \|\|

*mātā ca tasya dohārthamājagāma svayantadā \|*  
*dvijaputrastadā vatsaṃ khinnaṃ kīlena tāḍitam \|\| 43 \|\|*

*baṃdhanārthaṃ hi goḥ pārśvamanayaddugdhalālasaḥ \|*  
*punargauśca tadā kruddhā śṛṃgenātāḍayacca tam \|\| 44 \|\|*

43-44. The cow approached him herself for being milked. The brahmin’s son, eager for milk, took the distressed calf hit by his father near the cow in order to tie it up. The infuriated cow hit him with her horn.

## \|\| 8.6.45-46 \|\|

*papāta mūrcchāṃ saṃprāpya sopi marmaṇi tāḍitaḥ \|*  
*lokāśca militāstatra gavā bālo vihiṃsitaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

*jalaṃ jalaṃ vadantaste pitrādyā yatra saṃsthitāḥ \|*  
*yatnaśca kriyate yāvattāvadbālo mṛtastadā \|\| 46 \|\|*

45-46. The boy, hit in a vulnerable point, fell unconscious. People gathered there, saying—“O, the boy is struck by the cow. Fetch water? fetch water?” By the time they attempted to do something, the boy died.

## \|\| 8.6.47 \|\|

*mṛte ca bālake tatra hāhākāro mahānabhūt \|*  
*tanmātā duḥkhitā hyāsīdruroda ca punaḥ punaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. When the boy died, there was a great hue and cry. His mother was struck with grief. She cried again and again.

## \|\| 8.6.48 \|\|

*kiṃ karomi kva gacchāmi ko me duḥkhaṃ vyapohati \|*  
*ruditveti tadā gāṃ ca tāḍayitvā vyamocayat \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. “What shall I do? Where shall I go? Who can remove my grief?” After lamenting thus she struck the cow and let it off.

## \|\| 8.6.49 \|\|

*śvetavarṇā tadā sā gaurdrutaṃ śyāmā vyadṛśyata \|*  
*aho ca dṛśyatāṃ lokāścukruśuśca parasparam \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. The cow which had been white in colour turned black immediately. People cried to one another “O see O see.”

## \|\| 8.6.50 \|\|

*brāhmaṇaśca tadā pānrthau dṛṣṭvāścaryaṃ vinirgataḥ \|*  
*yatra gauśca gatastatra tāmanu brāhmaṇo gataḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. On seeing this surprising event the brahmin traveller set out and followed the cow as it ran away.

## \|\| 8.6.51-52 \|\|

*ūrdhvapucchaṃ tadā kṛtvā śīghraṃ gaurnarmadāṃ prati \|*  
*āgatya nandikasyāsya samīpe narmadājale \|\| 51 \|\|*

*saṃnimajya trivāraṃ tu śvetatvaṃ ca gatā hi sā \|*  
*yathāgataṃ gatā sā ca brāhmaṇo vismayaṃ gataḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

51-52. Lifting up its tail, the cow ran off to the river Narmadā. Coming near the Nandikeśa shrine, it dipped itself into the waters of the Narmadā thrice. It regained its white colour and went away the way it came. The brahmin was surprised.

## \|\| 8.6.53 \|\|

*aho dhanyatamaṃ tīrthaṃ brahmahatyānivāraṇam \|*  
*svayaṃ mamajja tatrāsau brāhmaṇassevakastathā \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. Ah, blessed indeed is this holy centre that removes the sin of Brahmin-slaughter. The brahmin and the servant took their baths there.

## \|\| 8.6.54 \|\|

*nimajjya hi gatau tau ca praśaṃsantau nadī ca tām \|*  
*mārge ca militā kācitsundarī bhūṣaṇānvitā \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. After the holy dip they started on their journey praising the river. On the way they met a beautiful maiden fully bedecked in ornaments.

## \|\| 8.6.55 \|\|

*tayoktaṃ taṃ ca bhoḥ pāṃtha kuto yāsi suvismitaḥ \|*  
*satyaṃ brūhi cchalaṃ tyaktvā vipravarya mamāgrataḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

She said:—

55\. “O brahmin-traveller where do you go in such bewilderment? Eschewing all deceit tell me the truth here.

## \|\| 8.6.56 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*evaṃ vacastadā śrutvā dvijenoktaṃ yathātatham \|*  
*punaścāyaṃ dvijastatra striyoktaḥ sthīyatāṃ tvayā \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. On hearing these words, the brahmin narrated everything truthfully. The brahmin was again addressed by the women, “Stay here”.

## \|\| 8.6.57 \|\|

*tayoktaṃ ca samākarṇya sthitasya brāhmaṇastataḥ \|*  
*pratyuvāca vinītātmā kathyate kiṃ vadeti ca \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. On hearing her words, the brahmin stopped and replied humbly—“Speak out what you desire to say.

## \|\| 8.6.58 \|\|

*sā cāha punarevātra tvayā dṛṣṭaṃ sthalaṃ ca yat \|*  
*tatrādhunā kṣipāsthīni mātuḥ kiṃ gamyate'nyataḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. She said again “In the holy spot that you have just seen, cast off the bones of your mother. Why shall you go elsewhere?

## \|\| 8.6.59 \|\|

*tava mātā pānthavaryya sākṣāddivyamayaṃ varam \|*  
*dehaṃ dhṛtvā drutaṃ sākṣācchaṃbhoryāsyati sadgatim \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. O excellent traveller, your mother will assume a divine form and will immediately attain the good goal of Siva.

## \|\| 8.6.60 \|\|

*vaiśākhe caiva saṃprāpte saptamyāśca dine śubhe \|*  
*sitepakṣe sadā gaṃgā hyāyāti dvijasattama \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. O excellent brahmin, in the month of Vaiśākha on the auspicious seventh day in the bright half, Gaṅgā comes here always.

## \|\| 8.6.61 \|\|

*adyaiva saptamī yā sā gaṃgārūpāsti tatra vai \|*  
*ityuktvāntardadhe devī sā gaṃgā munisattamāḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. Today is that seventh day. The river is in the form of Gaṅgā there.” Saying so the lady vanished. O excellent sages, she was Gaṅgā herself.

## \|\| 8.6.62 \|\|

*nivṛttaśca dvijaḥ sopi mātrasthyarddhaṃ svavastrataḥ \|*  
*kṣipedyāvattatra tīrthe tāvaccitramabhūttadā \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. The brahmin returned. As soon as he cast off half the bones of his mother from his bundle into the holy river, a wanderful incident happened.

## \|\| 8.6.63-64 \|\|

*divyadehatvamāpannā svamātā ca vyadṛśyata \|*  
*dhanyosi kṛtakṛtyosi pavitraṃ ca kulaṃ tvayā \|\| 63 \|\|*

*dhanaṃ dhānyaṃ tathā cāyurvaṃśo vai varddhatāṃ tava \|*  
*ityāśiṣaṃ muhurdattvā svaputrāya divaṃ gatā \|\| 64 \|\|*

63-64. He saw his mother endowed with a divine form. She said—“You are blessed. You have fulfilled your duty. The family has been sanctified by you. May your wealth, food-grains, longevity and line flourish.” Blessing her son frequently, she went to heaven.

## \|\| 8.6.65 \|\|

*tatra bhuktvā sukhaṃ bhūri cirakālaṃ mahottamam \|*  
*śaṃkarasya prasādena gatā sā hyuttamāṃ gatim \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. After enjoying excellent happiness there for a long time, she attained the excellent goal, thanks to the grace of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.6.66 \|\|

*brāhmaṇaśca sutastasyāḥ kṣiptvāsthīni punastataḥ \|*  
*prasannamānaso'bhūtsa śuddhātmā svagṛhaṃ gataḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. Her son, the brahmin, cast off the remaining bones too. He was much delighted in his mind. With contented soul he returned to his abode.

## Chapter 7 - The greatness of Nandikeśvara

## \|\| 8.7.1 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*kathaṃ gaṃgā samāyātā vaiśākhe saptamīdine \|*  
*narmadāyāṃ viśeṣeṇa sūtaitadvarṇaya prabho \|\| 1 \|\|*

The sages said:—

1\. O holy lord, Sūta, narrate in detail how Gaṅgā came to Narmada on the seventh day in Vaiśākha.

## \|\| 8.7.2 \|\|

*īśvaraśca kathaṃ jāto nandikeśo hi nāmataḥ \|*  
*vṛttaṃ tadapi suprītyā kathaya tvaṃ mahāmate \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. How did the lord come to be known by the name Nandikeśa? O intelligent one, narrate that too with pleasure.

## \|\| 8.7.3 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*sādhu pṛṣṭamṛṣiśreṣṭhā nandikeśāśritaṃ vacaḥ \|*  
*tadahaṃ kathayāmyadya śravaṇātpuṇyavarddhanam \|\| 3 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

3\. O excellent sages, you have put a very good question concerning Nandikeśa. I shall narrate it as it enhances merit.

## \|\| 8.7.4 \|\|

*brāhmaṇī ṛṣikā nāmnā kasyacicca dvijanmanaḥ \|*  
*sutā vivāhitā kasmaiciddvijāya vidhānataḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. A brahmin lady Ṛṣīkā, the daughter of a certain brahmin, was duly married to a certain brahmin youth.

## \|\| 8.7.5 \|\|

*pūrvakarmaprabhāvena patnī sā hi dvijanmanaḥ \|*  
*suvratāpi ca viprendrā bālavaidhavyamāgatā \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. O leading brahmins, although she observed holy rites, the brahmin lady became a widow at an early age as a result of previous actions.

## \|\| 8.7.6 \|\|

*atha sā dvijapatnī hi brahmacaryyavratānvitā \|*  
*pārthivārcanapūrvaṃ hi tapastepe sudāruṇam \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Then the brahmin widow, observing the rite of celibacy performed a severe penance by worshipping Śiva’s earthen phallic image.

## \|\| 8.7.7 \|\|

*tasminnavasare duṣṭo mūḍhanāmā'suro balī \|*  
*yayau tatra mahāmāyī kāmabāṇena tāḍitaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. In the meantime, a wicked and very powerful Asura named Mūḍha came there. He, the wielder of great Māyā, was struck by the arrows of the cupid.

## \|\| 8.7.8 \|\|

*tapantīṃ tāṃ samālokya sundarīmatikāminīm \|*  
*tayā bhogaṃ yayāce sa nānālobhaṃ pradarśayan \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. On seeing that charming beautiful lady performing penance, he offered her many temptations and requested for sexual pleasure with her.

## \|\| 8.7.9 \|\|

*atha sā suvratā nārī śivadhyānaparāyaṇā \|*  
*tasmindṛṣṭiṃ dadhau naiva kāmadṛṣṭyā munīśvarāḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. O great sages, the lady obeserving holy rites and engrossed in Śiva’s meditation did not cast a loving glance at him.

## \|\| 8.7.10 \|\|

*na mānitavatī taṃ ca brāhmaṇī sā taporatā \|*  
*atīva hi taponiṣṭhāsīcchivadhyānamāśritā \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. She did not honour him. Engaged in penance she remained meditating upon Śiva and strictly continued her penance.

## \|\| 8.7.11 \|\|

*atha mūḍhaḥ sa daityendraḥ tayā tanvyā tiraskṛtaḥ \|*  
*cukrodha vikaṭaṃ tasyai paścādrūpamadarśayat \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. That leading Daitya, Mūḍha, when slighted by that lady, became angry and showed his hideous form.

## \|\| 8.7.12 \|\|

*atha provāca duṣṭātmā durvaco bhayakārakam \|*  
*trāsayāmāsa bahuśastāṃ ca patnīṃ dvijanmanaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. The wicked-minded demon spoke harsh words terrifying her. He threatened the brahmin lady in many ways,

## \|\| 8.7.13 \|\|

*tadā sā bhayasaṃtrastā bahuvāraṃ śiveti ca \|*  
*babhāṣe snehatastanvī dvijapatnī śivāśrayā \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Then the frightened brahmin lady, lovingly resorting to Śiva, repeated the name Śiva many times.

## \|\| 8.7.14 \|\|

*vihvalātīva sā nārī śivanāmaprabhāṣiṇī \|*  
*jagāma śaraṇaṃ śambhoḥ svadharmāvanahetave \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. The agitated lady repeated the names of Śiva, sought refuge in him for the protection of her sacred virtue.

## \|\| 8.7.15 \|\|

*śaraṇāgatarakṣārthaṃ kartuṃ sadvṛttamāhitam \|*  
*ānandārthaṃ hi tasyāstu śiva āvirbabhūva ha \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. To protect one who sought refuge in him and to uphold good conduct, Śiva manifested himself, as well as to delight her.

## \|\| 8.7.16 \|\|

*atha taṃ mūḍhanāmānaṃ daityendraṃ kāma vihvalam \|*  
*cakāra bhasmasātsadyaḥ śaṃkaro bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Śiva, favourably disposed towards his devotees, immediately reduced to ashes that lusty leading demon named Mūḍha.

## \|\| 8.7.17 \|\|

*tataśca parameśāno kṛpādṛṣṭyā vilokya tām \|*  
*varaṃ brūhīti covāca bhaktarakṣaṇadakṣadhīḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Looking at her with a compassionate look, lord Śiva efficient in and mindful of protecting the devotees told her, “Mention the boon you wish to have”.

## \|\| 8.7.18 \|\|

*śrutvā maheśavacanaṃ sā sādhvī dvijakāminī \|*  
*dadarśa śāṃkaraṃ rūpamānandajanakaṃ śubham \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. On hearing the words of lord Śiva that chaste lady the wife of the brahmin, saw the auspicious and delightful form of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.7.19 \|\|

*tataḥ praṇamya taṃ śaṃbhuṃ parameśasukhāvaham \|*  
*tuṣṭāva sāñjaliḥ sādhvī nataskandhā śubhāśayā \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Then bowing to Śiva the beautiful supreme lord, the chaste lady of pure mind eulogised him with palms joined together and shoulders drooping down.

## \|\| 8.7.20 \|\|

*ṛṣikovāca \|*  
*devadeva mahādeva śaraṇāgatavatsala \|*  
*dīnabandhustvamīśāno bhaktarakṣākaraḥ sadā \|\| 20 \|\|*

Ṛṣīkā said:—

20\. O great God, lord of the gods, favourably disposed towards those who seek refuge, you are the kinsman of the distressed. You are lord Śiva, protecting your devotees always.

## \|\| 8.7.21 \|\|

*tvayā me rakṣito dharmo mūḍhanāmno'surādiha \|*  
*yadayaṃ nihato duṣṭo jagadrakṣā kṛtā tvayā \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. My virtue has been saved by you from the Asura Mūḍha. By killing that wicked person the whole universe has been saved by you.

## \|\| 8.7.22 \|\|

*svapādayoḥ parāṃ bhaktiṃ dehi me hyanapāyinīm \|*  
*ayameva varo nātha kimanyadadhikaṃ hyataḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. O lord, grant me the greatest devotion to your feet which will never decay. This alone is the boon I ask of you. Nothing more than this.

## \|\| 8.7.23 \|\|

*anyadākarṇaya vibho prārthanāṃ me maheśvara \|*  
*lokānāmupakārārthamiha tvaṃ saṃsthito bhava \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. O great lord, please listen to another request of mine. Please stay here permanently for the benefit of the people.

## \|\| 8.7.24 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*
