*iti stutvā mahādevamṛṣikā sā śubhavratā \|*  
*tūṣṇīmāsātha giriśaḥ provāca karuṇākaraḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

24\. After eulogising lord Śiva thus, Ṛṣīkā of auspicious rites remained silent. Then the merciful lord Śiva spoke.

## \|\| 8.7.25 \|\|

*giriśa uvāca \|*  
*ṛṣike sucaritrā tvaṃ mama bhaktā viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*dattā varāśca te sarve tubhyaṃ yeye hi yācitāḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

Lord Śiva said:—

25\. O Ṛṣīkā, you are well-behaved. You are a special devotee of mine. Whatever boons you have asked for have been granted to you.

## \|\| 8.7.26 \|\|

*etasminnaṃtare tatra haribrahmādayaḥ surāḥ \|*  
*śivāvirbhāvamājñāya yayurharṣasamanvitāḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. In the meantime, coming to know of the manifestation of Śiva, Viṣṇu, Brahmā and other gods came there with great delight.

## \|\| 8.7.27 \|\|

*śivaṃ praṇamya suprītyā samānarcuśca te'khilāḥ \|*  
*tuṣṭuvurnatakā viprāḥ karau baddhvā sucetasaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. O brahmins, bowing down with great pleasure to śiva they all worshipped him. Joining their palms and bending down they eulogised him attentively.

## \|\| 8.7.28 \|\|

*etasminsamaye gaṃgā sādhvī tāṃ svardhunī jagau \|*  
*ṛṣikāṃ suprasannātmā praśaṃsanto ca tīdvidhim \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. In the meantime, Gaṅgā the celestial river, with delighted mind, praised her rites and spoke to that chaste lady Ṛṣīkā.

## \|\| 8.7.29 \|\|

*gaṃgovāca \|*  
*mamārthe caiva vaiśākhe māsi deyaṃ tvayā vacaḥ \|*  
*sthityarthaṃ dinamekaṃ me sāmīpyaṃ kāryyameva hi \|\| 29 \|\|*

Gaṅgā said:—

29\. For my sake you should make a verbal promise You should stay with me for a day in the month of Vaiśākha.

## \|\| 8.7.30 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*gaṃgāvacanamākarṇya sā sādhvī prāha suvratā \|*  
*tathāstviti vacaḥ prītyā lokānāṃ hitahetave \|\| 30 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

30\. On hearing the words of Gaṅgā, the chaste lady of good rices consented with pleasure for the benefit of the world.

## \|\| 8.7.31 \|\|

*ānandārthaṃ śivastasyāḥ suprasannaśca pārthive \|*  
*tasmiṃlliṃge layaṃ yātaḥ pūrṇāṃśena tayā haraḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. For her bliss, the delighted Śiva, vanished into that earthen phallic image along with her.

## \|\| 8.7.32 \|\|

*devaḥ sarve suprasannāḥ praśaṃsaṃti śivaṃ ca tām \|*  
*svaṃsvaṃ dhāma yayurviṣṇubrahmādyā api svarṇadī \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Praising Śiva and the brahmin lady, the delighted Brahmā, Viṣṇu and other gods and the celestial river Gaṅgā returned to their abodes.

## \|\| 8.7.33 \|\|

*taddinātpāvanaṃ tīrthamāsīdīdṛśamuttamam \|*  
*nandikeśaḥ śivaḥ khyātaḥ sarvapāpavināśanaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. From that day onwards this became a holy and excellent centre. Śiva became famous as Nandikeśa, the destroyer of all sins.

## \|\| 8.7.34 \|\|

*gaṃgāpi prativarṣaṃ taddine yāti śubhecchayā \|*  
*kṣālanārthaṃ svapāpasya yadgrahītaṃ nṛṇāṃ dvijāḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. O brahmin, in order to wipe off her sins which she takes from others, Gaṅgā comes here on that day every year out of her own good will.

## \|\| 8.7.35 \|\|

*tatra snāto naraḥ samyaṅ naṃdikeśaṃ samarcya ca \|*  
*brahmahatyādibhiḥ pāpairmucyate hyakhilairapi \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. The man who takes bath there and worships Nandikeśa with devotion becomes freed from all sins such as the slaughter of a Brahmin etc.

## Chapter 8 - The greatness of Mahābala

## \|\| 8.8.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*dvijāḥ śṛṇuta sadbhaktyā śivaliṃgāni tāni ca \|*  
*paścimāyāṃ diśāyāṃ vai yāni khyātāni bhūtale \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. O brahmins, listen with devotion to the phallic forms of Śiva that exist in the western quarter on this earth.

## \|\| 8.8.2 \|\|

*kapilāyāṃ nagaryāṃ tu kālarāmeśvarābhidhe \|*  
*śivaliṃge mahādivye darśanātpāpahārake \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. In the city of Kapilā are the two divine phallic images of Śiva named Kāla and Rāmeśvara. By their very sight they dispel sins.

## \|\| 8.8.3 \|\|

*paścime sāgare caiva mahāsiddheśvaraḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*dharmārthakāmadaścaiva tathā mokṣaprado'pi hi \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Mahāsiddheśvara is on the shore of the western ocean and is famous as the bestower of virtue, wealth, love and salvation.

## \|\| 8.8.4 \|\|

*paścimāmbudhitīrasthaṃ gokarṇaṃ kṣetramuttamam \|*  
*brahmahatyādipāpaghnaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Gokarṇa the excellent shrine, situated on the shore of the western ocean, destroys the sin of brahmin’s slaughter and other sins and yields the fruits of all desires.

## \|\| 8.8.5 \|\|

*gokarṇe śivaliṃgāni vidyante koṭikoṭiśaḥ \|*  
*asaṃkhyātāni tīrthāni tiṣṭhanti ca padepade \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. At Gokarṇa there are crores and crores of Śiva’s phallic images. At every step there are innumerable holy centres.

## \|\| 8.8.6 \|\|

*bahunātra kimuktena gokarṇasthāni sarvaśaḥ \|*  
*śivapratyakṣaliṃgāni tīrthānyambhāṃsi sarvaśaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Of what avail is much talk. Everything in Gokarṇa is Śiva’s visible phallic image. All the waters there are holy Tīrthas.

## \|\| 8.8.7 \|\|

*gokarṇe śivaliṃgānāṃ tīrthānāmapi sarvaśaḥ \|*  
*varṇyate mahimā tāta purāṇeṣu maharṣibhiḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. O dear, the greatness of Śiva’s holy images and the Tīrthas at Gokarṇa is explained and described in the Purāṇas by great sages.

## \|\| 8.8.8 \|\|

*kṛteyuge sa hi śvetastretāyāṃ sotilohitaḥ \|*  
*dvāpare pītavarṇaśca kalau śyāmo bhaviṣyati \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. In the Kṛta Yuga it is white; in the Tretā it is deep-red; in the Dvāpara it is yellow and in the Kali age it is dark in colour.

## \|\| 8.8.9 \|\|

*ākrāntasaptapātālakuharopi mahābalaḥ \|*  
*prāpte kaliyuge ghore mṛdutāmupayāsyati \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Mahābala, though it has pervaded the regions as far below as the seven nether worlds, will become soft when the terrible age of Kali arrives.

## \|\| 8.8.10 \|\|

*mahāpātakinaścātra samabhyarcya mahābalam \|*  
*śivaliṃgaṃ ca gokarṇe prayātāśśāṃkarampadam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Persons committing great sins have attained Śiva’s region after worshipping Mahābala, the phallic image of Śiva at Gokarṇa.

## \|\| 8.8.11 \|\|

*gokarṇe tatra munayo gatvā puṇyarkṣavāsare \|*  
*ye'rcayanti ca taṃ bhaktyā te rudrāḥ syurna saṃśaya \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. O sages, those who worship the lord with devotion after going to Gokarṇa on a holy day with sacred constellation will undoubtedly become Rudras themselves.

## \|\| 8.8.12 \|\|

*yadā kadācidgokarṇe yo vā ko vāpi mānavaḥ \|*  
*pūjayecchivaliṃgaṃ tatsa gacchedbrahmaṇaḥ padam \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. A devotee worshipping Śiva’s image at any time in Gokarṇa attains the region of Brahmā.

## \|\| 8.8.13 \|\|

*brahmaviṣṇvādidevānāṃ śaṃkaro hita kāmyayā \|*  
*mahābalābhidhānena devaḥ saṃnihitassadā \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. With a desire for the welfare of Brahmā, Viṣṇu and other gods, the lord is always present there in the name of Mahābala.

## \|\| 8.8.14 \|\|

*ghoreṇa tapasā labdhaṃ rāvaṇākhyena rakṣasā \|*  
*talliṃgaṃ sthāpayāmāsa gokarṇa gaṇanāyakaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. That phallic image secured by the Rākṣasa Rāvaṇa as a result of severe penance was installed by Gaṇanāyaka at Gokarṇa.

## \|\| 8.8.15-16 \|\|

*viṣṇurbrahmā mahendraśca viśvadevo marudgaṇāḥ \|*  
*ādityā vasavo dasrau śaśāṃkaśca satārakaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

*ete vimānagatayo devāśca saha pārṣadaiḥ \|*  
*pūrvadvāraṃ niṣevante tasya vai prītikāraṇāt \|\| 16 \|\|*

15-16. The following deities resort to its eastern gate out of devotion for it—Visṇu, Brahmā, Mahendra, Viśvedevas, Marudgaṇas, Ādityas, Vasus, Dasras, the moon with the stars and the gods with their attendants. They go there in their aerial chariots.

## \|\| 8.8.17 \|\|

*yamo mṛtyuḥ svayaṃ sākṣāccitraguptaśca pāvakaḥ \|*  
*pitṛbhiḥ saha rudraiśca dakṣiṇadvāramāśritaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Yama, the god of death himself, Citragupta, the fire-god, the manes and the Rudras resort to the southern gate.

## \|\| 8.8.18 \|\|

*varuṇaḥ saritāṃ nātho gaṃgādisaritā gaṇaiḥ \|*  
*mahābalaṃ ca sevante paścimadvāramāśritāḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Varuṇa, the lord of all rivers, along with the rivers Gaṅgā etc. resort to the western gate and serve Mahābala.

## \|\| 8.8.19 \|\|

*tathā vāyuḥ kuberaśca deveśī bhadrakālikā \|*  
*mātṛbhiścaṇḍikādyābhiruttaradvāramāśritāḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Similarly the wind god, Kubera, Bhadrakālikā, the goddess of the gods and Caṇḍikā and other Mothers resort to the northern gate.

## \|\| 8.8.20-22 \|\|

*sarve devāssagandharvāḥ pitaraḥ siddhacāraṇāḥ \|*  
*vidyādharāḥ kiṃpuruṣāḥ kinnarā guhyakāḥ khagāḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

*nānāpiśācā vetālā daiteyāśca mahābalāḥ \|*  
*nāgāśśeṣādayassarve siddhāśca munayo'khilāḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

*praṇuvanti ca taṃ devaṃ praṇamanti mahābalam \|*  
*labhanta īpsitānkāmānramante ca yathāsukham \|\| 22 \|\|*

20-22. All the gods, the Gandharvas, the Pitṛs, Siddhas, Gāraṇas, Vidyādharas, Kiṃpuruṣas, Kinnaras, Guhyakas, Khagas, different types of Piśācas, Vetālas, the powerful Daiteyas, Śeṣa and other serpents, the Siddhas and the sages, eulogise and bow to lord Mahābala. They get their cherished desires and sport about happily.

## \|\| 8.8.23 \|\|

*bahubhistatra sutapastaptaṃ sampūjya taṃ vibhum \|*  
*labdhā hi paramā siddhirihāmutrāpi saukhyadā \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Good penance has been performed by many after worshipping the lord. Great achievements pleasing here and hereafter have been secured by them.

## \|\| 8.8.24 \|\|

*gokarṇe śivaliṃgaṃ tu mokṣadvāra udāhṛtaḥ \|*  
*mahābalābhidhāno'sau pūjitaḥ saṃstuto dvijāḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. O brahmins, the Mahābala image of Śiva at Gokarṇa worshipped and eulogised is the doorway to salvation.

## \|\| 8.8.25 \|\|

*māghāsitacaturdaśyāṃ mahābalasamarcanam \|*  
*vimuktidaṃ viśeṣeṇa sarveṣāṃ pāpināmapi \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. The worship of Mahābala on the fourteenth day in the dark half of the month of Māgha yields salvation particularly even to the sinners.

## \|\| 8.8.26 \|\|

*asyāṃ śivatithau sarve mahotsavadidṛkṣavaḥ \|*  
*āyāṃti sarvadeśebhyaścāturvarṇyamahājanāḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. On this festive day of Śiva, people of all the four castes come here from all countries, desirous of witnessing the great festivals.

## \|\| 8.8.27 \|\|

*striyo vṛddhāśca bālāśca caturāśramavāsinaḥ \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā tatretya deveśaṃ lebhire kṛtakṛtyatām \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Women, old men, children and people of the four stages of life come there, see the lord of gods and obtain contentment.

## \|\| 8.8.28 \|\|

*mahābalaprabhāvāttu tacca liṃgaṃ śivasya tu \|*  
*sampūjyaikātha cāṇḍālī śivalokaṃ gatā drutam \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. It is by the power of Mahābala that a Cāṇḍālī attained Śivaloka after worshipping that image of Śiva.

## Chapter 9 - The attainment of good goal by the outcaste woman

## \|\| 8.9.1 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*sūtasūta mahābhāga dhanyastvaṃ śaivasattamaḥ \|*  
*cāṇḍālī kā samākhyātā tatkathāṃ kathaya prabho \|\| 1 \|\|*

The sages said:

1\. O Sūta, O fortunate Sūta, you are blessed, you are the most excellent among the devotees of Śiva. O holy lord, please narrate the story of the outcaste woman you just referred to.

## \|\| 8.9.2 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*dvijāḥ śṛṇuta sadbhaktyā tāṃ kathāṃ paramādbhutām \|*  
*śivaprabhāvasaṃmiśrāṃ śṛṇvatāṃ bhaktivarddhinīm \|\| 2 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

2\. O brahmins, listen with great devotion to that wonderful story illustrative of Śiva’s power. It enhances the devotional feelings of the listeners.

## \|\| 8.9.3 \|\|

*cāṃḍālī sā pūrvabhare'bhavadbrāhmaṇakanyakā \|*  
*sauminī nāma candrāsyā sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutā \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. The outcaste woman was a brahmin girl in her previous birth. She was named Sauminī. She was endowed with all characteristic signs. Her face was bright like the moon.

## \|\| 8.9.4 \|\|

*atha sā samaye kanyā yuvatiḥ sauminī dvijāḥ \|*  
*pitrā dattā ca kasmaicidvidhinā dvijasūnave \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. O brahmins, that youthful maiden Sauminī was married to a certain brahmin boy duly by her father.

## \|\| 8.9.5 \|\|

*sā bhartāramanuprāpya kiṃcitkālaṃ śubhavratā \|*  
*reme tena dvijaśreṣṭhā navayauvanaśālinī \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. After getting a husband, O great brahmins, she in her fresh blooming youth indulged in sexual dalliance with him and maintained her auspicious rites.

## \|\| 8.9.6 \|\|

*atha tasyāḥ patirviprastaruṇassurujārditaḥ \|*  
*sauminyāḥ kālayogāttu pañcatvamagamaddvijāḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. O brahmins, then her youthful husband, the brahmin boy became distressed with a great ailment. Due to the misfortune of Sauminī he died.

## \|\| 8.9.7 \|\|

*mṛte bhartari sā nārī dukhitātiviṣaṇṇadhīḥ \|*  
*kiṃcitkālaṃ śubhācārā suśīlovāsa sadmani \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. When the husband passed away, the woman was much distressed, dejected and despondent. For some time she maintained her pure conduct. She remained in the house, a well-behaved lady.

## \|\| 8.9.8 \|\|

*tatassā manmathāviṣṭahṛdayā vidhavāpi ca \|*  
*yuvāvasthāviśeṣeṇa babhūva vyabhicāriṇī \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Thereafter, though a widow, her heart became defiled by lustful feelings because she was still in her prime of youth. She then transgressed the limits of decency.

## \|\| 8.9.9 \|\|

*tajjñātvā gotriṇastasyā duṣkarma kuladūṣaṇam \|*  
*sametāstatyaju dūraṃ nītvā tāṃ sakacagrahām \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Coming to know of her misdeeds defiling the family her kinsmen gathered together, caught hold of her hair, took her far off and abandoned her.

## \|\| 8.9.10 \|\|

*kaścicchūdravarastāṃ vai vicarantīṃ nijecchayā \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā vane striyaṃ cakre nināya svagṛhaṃ tata \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. A leading Śūdra saw her roaming about in the forest, took her to his house and made her his wife.

## \|\| 8.9.11 \|\|

*atha sā piśitāhārā nityamāpītavāruṇī \|*  
*ajījanatsutāntena śūdreṇa suratapriyā \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. She then became very fond of sexual intercourse, began to take in meat and indulged in drinking wine. She bore that Śūdra a daughter too.

## \|\| 8.9.12 \|\|

*kadācidbhartari kvāpi yāte pītasurātha sā \|*  
*iyeṣa piśitāhāraṃ sauminī vyabhicāriṇī \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Once while the husband had gone somewhere, Sauminī the erring woman drank wine and badly wanted meat.

## \|\| 8.9.13 \|\|

*tato meṣeṣu baddheṣu gobhissaha bahirvraje \|*  
*niśāmukhe tamo'ndhe hi khaḍgamādāya sā yayau \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. In her cattleshed outside, goats and sheep had been tethered along with cows. It was dusk when nothing could be visible due to darkness. She then took a sword and went out.

## \|\| 8.9.14 \|\|

*avimṛśya madāveśānmeṣabudyā''miṣa priyā \|*  
*ekaṃ jaghāna govatsaṃ krośaṃtamatidurbhagā \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Inebriated that she was, the foul woman fond of flesh diet killed a calf taking it to be a goat without much consideration.

## \|\| 8.9.15 \|\|

*hataṃ taṃ gṛhamānīya jñātvā govatsamaṃganā \|*  
*bhītā śivaśivetyāha kenacitpuṇyakarmaṇā \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. After taking it within she realised that it was a calf. The terrified woman blurted out “Śiva, Śiva”, thanks to some previous merit.

## \|\| 8.9.16 \|\|

*sā muhūrtaṃ śivaṃ dhyātvāmiṣabhojanalālasā \|*  
*chittvā tameva govatsaṃ cakārāhāramīpsitam \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. She meditated on Śiva for a short while. But the desire for meat overwhelmed her. She minced the meat of the calf and ate it up with great relish.

## \|\| 8.9.17 \|\|

*evaṃ bahutithe kāle gate sā sauminī dvijāḥ \|*  
*kālasya vaśamāpannā jagāma yamasaṃkṣayam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. O brahmins, when a long time elapsed thus, Sauminī died and went to Yama’s abode.

## \|\| 8.9.18 \|\|

*yamo'pi dharmamālokya tasyāḥ karma ca paurvikam \|*  
*nivartya nirayāvāsāccakre cāṇḍālajātikām \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Yama went through her antecedents and taking into consideration her merits and demerits he sent her back from hell to be born in an outcaste family.

## \|\| 8.9.19 \|\|

*sātha bhraṣṭā yamapurāccāṇḍālīgarbhamāśritā \|*  
*tato babhūva janmāndhā praśāṃtāṃgāramecakā \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Fallen from Yama’s city she was born as a congenitally blind girl out of the womb of a Cāṇḍāla woman. Her complexion was as dark as the dying embers.

## \|\| 8.9.20 \|\|

*janmāndhā sātha bālye'pi vidhvastapitṛmātṛkā \|*  
*ūḍhā na kenacidduṣṭā mahākuṣṭharujārditā \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. She was born blind. Even during her infancy her father and mother died. She was not married to anyone. She became infected with leprosy and was utterly defiled.

## \|\| 8.9.21 \|\|

*tataḥ kṣudhārditā dīnā yaṣṭipāṇirgatekṣaṇā \|*  
*cāṇḍālocchiṣṭapiṃḍena jaṭharāgnimataparyat \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Emaciated and distressed with hunger, blind and moving about with a staff in her hand, she somehow subdued her gastric fire by the leavings of food of the Cāṇḍālas nearby.

## \|\| 8.9.22 \|\|

*evaṃ kṛcchreṇa mahatā nītvā svavipulaṃ vayaḥ \|*  
*jarayāgrastasavārṅgī duḥkhamāpa duratyayam \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. With great difficulty and sufferings she passed a major portion of her life. When old age affected her limbs she was subjected to unending grief and distress.

## \|\| 8.9.23 \|\|

*kadācitsātha cāṃḍālī gokarṇaṃ taṃ mahājanān \|*  
*āyāsyaṃtyāṃ śivatithau gacchato bubudhe'nvagān \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Once she came to know of travellers going to visit Mahābala and Gokarṇa on the Śiva festival day.

## \|\| 8.9.24 \|\|

*athāsāvapi cāṃḍālī vasanāsanatṛṣṇayā \|*  
*mahājanān yācayituṃ saṃcacāra śanaiḥ śanaiḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Then prompted by a desire for clothes and meals she walked slowly to beg of the general public.

## \|\| 8.9.25 \|\|

*gatvā tatrātha cāṃḍālī prārthayantī mahājanān \|*  
*yatra tatra cacārāsau dīnavākprasṛtāñjaliḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. She moved about here and there begging of the people imploring them with piteous words and outstretched hands after arriving at that place.

## \|\| 8.9.26 \|\|

*evamabhyarthayaṃtyāstu cāṃḍālyāḥ prasṛtāñjalau \|*  
*ekaḥ puṇyatamaḥ pānthaḥ prākṣipadbilvamaṃjarīm \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. In the outstretched hands of the suppliant woman, a meritorious traveller hurled a bunch of Bilva leaves.

## \|\| 8.9.27 \|\|

*tāmaṃjalau nipatitā sā vimṛśya punaḥ punaḥ \|*  
*abhakṣyamiti matvātha dūre prākṣipadāturā \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. When that bunch fell into her hands she felt it again and again and realising that it was not edible she cast it off dejectedly.

## \|\| 8.9.28 \|\|

*tasyāḥ karādvinirmuktā rātrau sā bilvamaṃjarī \|*  
*papāta kasyaciddiṣṭyā śivaliṃgasya mastake \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. That bunch of Bilva leaves cast off from her hands during the night, fortunately fell on the top of a Śiva’s phallic image.

## \|\| 8.9.29 \|\|

*saivaṃ śivacaturdaśyāṃ rātrau pānthajanānmuhuḥ \|*  
*yācamānāpi yatkiṃcinna lebhe daivayogataḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Thus on the Śiva Caturdaśī night though she begged of the travellers again and again she did not get anything through the intercession of fate.

## \|\| 8.9.30 \|\|

*evaṃ śivacaturdaśyā vrataṃ jātaṃ ca nirmalam \|*  
*ajñānato jāgaraṇaṃ paramānandadāyakam \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Thus, unwittingly she observed the rites of Śivacaturdaśī and kept awake during the night and obtained great bliss.

## \|\| 8.9.31 \|\|

*tataḥ prabhāte sā nārī śokena mahatā vṛtā \|*  
*śanairnivavṛte dīnā svadeśāyaiva kevalam \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Then in the next morning, overcome with great grief the dejected woman returned to her native place alone slowly.

## \|\| 8.9.32 \|\|

*śrāṃtā ciropavāsena nipataṃtī padepade \|*  
*atītya tāvatīṃ bhūmiṃ nipapāta vicetanā \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Exhausted by long starvation, and faltering and staggering at every step she somehow covered the long distance but fell down unconscious.

## \|\| 8.9.33 \|\|

*atha sā śaṃbhukṛpayā jagāma paramaṃ padam \|*  
*āruhya suvimānaṃ ca nītaṃ śivagaṇairdrutam \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Seated in an aerial chariot brought immediately by Śiva’s Gaṇas, thanks to Śiva’s mercy, she went to Śiva’s region.

## \|\| 8.9.34 \|\|

*ādau yadeṣā śivanāma nārī pramādato vāpyasatī jagāda \|*  
*teneha bhūyaḥ sukṛtena viprā mahābalasthānamavāpa divyam \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. O brahmins, since at the outset the unchaste woman had cried out Śiva’s name unwittingly, she acquired a great merit whereby she reached the divine abode of Mahābala.

## \|\| 8.9.35 \|\|

*śrīgokarṇe śivatithāvupoṣya śivamastake \|*  
*kṛtvā jāgaraṇaṃ sā hi cakre bilvārcanaṃ niśi \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. At Gokarṇa on Śiva’s Tithi during the night she had kept awake and observed fast. She had performed the worship of Śiva’s image with Bilva bunch.

## \|\| 8.9.36 \|\|

*akāmataḥ kṛtasyāsya puṇyasyaiva ca tatphalam \|*  
*bhunaktyadyāpi sā caiva mahābalaprasādataḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. That was the fruit of a meritorious action performed unconsciously. She was liberated thanks to the grace of Mahābala.

## \|\| 8.9.37 \|\|

*evaṃvidhaṃ mahāliṃgaṃ śaṃkarasya mahābalam \|*  
*sarvapāpaharaṃ sadyaḥ paramānandadāyakam \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Thus is the great image of Mahābala, destructive of sins and the bestower of the highest bliss instantaneously.

## \|\| 8.9.38 \|\|

*evaṃ vaḥ kathitaṃ viprā māhātmyaṃ paramaṃ mayā \|*  
*mahābalābhidhānasya śivaliṃgavarasya hi \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. O brahmins thus the greatness of the excellent Śiva image Mahābala has been mentioned to you by me.

## \|\| 8.9.39 \|\|

*athānyadapi vakṣyāmi māhātmyaṃ tasya cādbhutam \|*  
*śrutamātreṇa yenāśu śive bhaktiḥ prajāyate \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. I shall now mention another wonderful glory of the same. By merely listening to it, the devotion for Śiva is generated.

## Chapter 10 - The greatness and glory of Mahābala

## \|\| 8.10.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*śrīmatīkṣvākuvaṃśe hi rājā paramadhārmikaḥ \|*  
*āsīnmitrasaho nāma śreṣṭhassarvadhanuṣmatām \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. There was a highly virtuous king Mitrasaha in the glorious family of Ikṣvāku. He was the greatest of skilled archers.

## \|\| 8.10.2 \|\|

*tasya rājñaḥ sudharmiṣṭhā madayantī priyā śubhā \|*  
*damayantī nalasyeva babhūva viditā satī \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. The virtuous and auspicious-natured lady Madayantī was his beloved wife, like Damayantī of Nala. She was known as a chaste lady.

## \|\| 8.10.3 \|\|

*sa ekadā hi mṛgayāsnehī mitrasaho nṛpaḥ \|*  
*mahadbalena saṃyukto jagāma gahanaṃ vanam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Fond of hunting, once that king Mitrasaha went to a thick forest accompanied by a huge army.

## \|\| 8.10.4 \|\|

*viharaṃstatra sa nṛpaḥ kamaṭhāhvaṃ niśācaram \|*  
*nijaghāna mahāduṣṭaṃ sādhupīḍākaraṃ khalam \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Sporting about there, the king killed a wicked demon Kamaṭha who used to harass good men.

## \|\| 8.10.5 \|\|

*atha tasyānujaḥ pāpī jayeyaṃ chadmanaiva tam \|*  
*matvā jagāma nṛpaterantika cchadmakārakaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The demon’s younger brother, a deceitful sinner thought “I shall conquer him by deceitful means” and with this evil intention approached the king.

## \|\| 8.10.6 \|\|

*taṃ vinamrākṛtiṃ dṛṣṭvā bhṛtyatāṃ kartumāgatam \|*  
*cakre mahānasādhyakṣamajñānātsa mahīpatiḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. On seeing him humble in behaviour in seeking service under him, the king made him the chief cook without knowing his real nature.

## \|\| 8.10.7 \|\|

*atha tasminvane rājā kiyatkālaṃ vihṛtya saḥ \|*  
*nivṛtto mṛgayāṃ hitvā svapurīmāyayau mudā \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. After indulging in hunting sports for sometime in the forest, the king abandoned the game and joyously returned to his capital.

## \|\| 8.10.8 \|\|

*pituḥ kṣayāhe samprāpte nimaṃtrya svaguruṃ nṛpaḥ \|*  
*vasiṣṭhaṃ gṛhamāninye bhojayāmāsa bhaktitaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. On the Śrāddha day of his father the king invited his preceptor Vasiṣṭha to his palace and fed him devoutly.

## \|\| 8.10.9 \|\|

*rakṣasā sūdarūpeṇa saṃmiśritanarāmiṣam \|*  
*śākāmiṣaṃ puraḥ kṣiptaṃ dṛṣṭvā gururathābravīt \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. On seeing the vegetarian and non-vegetarian dish in which the demon in the guise of a cook had mixed human flesh, the preceptor said.

## \|\| 8.10.10 \|\|

*gururuvāca \|*  
*dhik tvāṃ narāmiṣaṃ rājaṃstvayaitacchadmakāriṇā \|*  
*khalenopahṛtaṃ mahyaṃ tato rakṣo bhaviṣyasi \|\| 10 \|\|*

The preceptor said:—

10\. O wicked king, fie upon you. Human flesh has been deceitfully offered to me by you. Hence you will become a demon.

## \|\| 8.10.11 \|\|

*rakṣaḥkṛtaṃ ca vijñāya tadaivaṃ sa gurustadā \|*  
*punarvimṛśya taṃ śāpaṃ cakāra dvādaśābdikam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. On realising that it had been perpetrated by the demon, the preceptor pondered over and prescribed a timelimit of twelve years for the curse.

## \|\| 8.10.12 \|\|

*sa rājānucitaṃ śāpaṃ vijñāya krodhamūrchitaḥ \|*  
*jalāṃjaliṃ samādāya guruṃ śaptuṃ samudyataḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Thinking that the curse was unjustified, the infuriated king was about to return the curse with a handful of water.

## \|\| 8.10.13 \|\|

*tadā ca tatpriyā sādhvī madayantī sudharmiṇī \|*  
*patitvā pādayostasya śāpaṃ taṃ hi nyavārayat \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Then the chaste and virtuous queen Madayantī fell at his feet and entreated him to spare the curse.

## \|\| 8.10.14 \|\|

*tato nivṛttaśāpastu tasyā vacanagauravāt \|*  
*tatyāja pādayoraṃbhaḥ pādau kalmaṣatāṃ gatau \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Out of deference for her entreaties the king desisted from cursing, but dropped the handful of water on his own feet which became deformed.

## \|\| 8.10.15 \|\|

*tataḥprabhṛti rājābhūtsa lokesminmunīśvarāḥ \|*  
*kalmaṣāṃghririti khyātaḥ prabhāvāttajjalasya hi \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. O great sages, from that day onwards the king became famous in the world as Kalmāṣāṅghri by the virtue of that water.

## \|\| 8.10.16 \|\|

*rājā mitrasahaḥ śāpādguro ṛṣivarasya hi \|*  
*babhūva rākṣaso ghoro hiṃsako vanagocaraḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. By the curse of his preceptor the leading sage, the king Mitrasaha became a terrible violent Rākṣasa, roaming in the forest.

## \|\| 8.10.17 \|\|

*sa bibhradrākṣasaṃ rūpaṃ kālāntakayamopamam \|*  
*cakhāda vividhāñjaṃtūnmānuṣādīnvanecaraḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Transformed as Rākṣasa and resembling Yama, the destroyer of the world, he roamed about in the forest devouring various living beings, men and others.

## \|\| 8.10.18 \|\|

*sa kadācidvane kvāpi ramamāṇau kiśorakau \|*  
*apaśyadantakākāro navoḍhau munidampatī \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Once, somewhere in the forest, the king who was as hideous as the god of death saw a newly married couple—a young sage and his young wife indulging in sexual dalliance.

## \|\| 8.10.19 \|\|

*rākṣasaḥ sa narāhāraḥ kiśoraṃ muninandanam \|*  
*jagdhuṃ jagrāha śāpārtto vyāghro mṛgaśiśuṃ yathā \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. The demon habituated to eat human flesh in his distressed state due to the curse caught hold of the young sage in order to eat him like a tiger seizing a fawn.

## \|\| 8.10.20 \|\|

*kukṣau gṛhītaṃ bhartāraṃ dṛṣṭvā bhītā ca tatpriyā \|*  
*sā cakre prārthanaṃ tasmai vadaṃtī karuṇaṃ vacaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. The young woman, on seeing her husband seized by the demon, was much frightened and implored him with piteous cries.

## \|\| 8.10.21 \|\|

*prārthyamāno'pi bahuśaḥ puruṣādaḥ sa nirghṛṇaḥ \|*  
*cakhāda śira utkṛtya viprasūnordurāśayaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. In spite of repeated importunities the man-eating ruthless wicked demon cut off the head of the brahmin sage and devoured it.

## \|\| 8.10.22 \|\|

*atha sādhvī ca sā dīnā vilapya bhṛśaduḥkhitā \|*  
*āhṛtya bharturasthīni citāṃ cakre kilolbaṇām \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. The distressed, grief-stricken chaste lady lamented much. She gathered the bones of her husband and lighted a funeral pyre.

## \|\| 8.10.23 \|\|

*bhartāramanugacchantī saṃviśaṃtī hutāśanam \|*  
*rājānaṃ rākṣasākāraṃ sā śaśāpa dvijāṅganā \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. The brahmin lady desirous of entering the pyre in order to follow her husband cursed the Rākṣasa king.

## \|\| 8.10.24 \|\|

*adyaprabhṛti nārīṣu yadā tvaṃ saṃgato bhaveḥ \|*  
*tadā mṛtistavetyuktvā viveśa jvalanaṃ satī \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. The chaste lady entered fire after proclaiming “From now onwards if you become united with any woman in sexual embrace you will die.”

## \|\| 8.10.25 \|\|

*sopi rājā gurośśāpamanubhūya kṛtāvadhim \|*  
*punaḥ svarūpamāsthāya svagṛhaṃ mudito yayau \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. After undergoing the results of his preceptor’s curse to which there was a time-limit, the king regained his original form and joyously returned to his palace.

## \|\| 8.10.26 \|\|

*jñātvā viprasatīśāpaṃ madayantī ratipriyam \|*  
*patiṃ nivārayāmāsa vaidhavyādatibibhyatī \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Madayantī who knew of the curse of the chaste brahmin lady prevented her husband who evinced a desire for sexual embrace. She was much frightened of widowhood.

## \|\| 8.10.27 \|\|

*anapatyo vinirviṇṇo rājyabhogeṣu pārthivaḥ \|*  
*visṛjya sakalāṃ lakṣmīṃ vanameva jagāma ha \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. The issueless king became disgusted with kingly pleasures. Eschewing all riches he went to the forest.

## \|\| 8.10.28 \|\|

*svapṛṣṭhataḥ samāyāntīṃ brahmahatyāṃ suduḥkhadām \|*  
*dadarśa vikaṭākārāṃ tarjayantī muhurmuhuḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. He saw the hideous form of Brahmahatyā closely following him, threatening him again and again and tormenting him.

## \|\| 8.10.29 \|\|

*tasyā nirbhadramanvicchan rājā nirviṇṇamānasaḥ \|*  
*cakāra nānopāyānsa japavratamakhādikān \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Dejected in mind the king wanted to get rid of Brahmahatyā. He tried japas, holy rites, sacrifices and various other means.

## \|\| 8.10.30 \|\|

*nānopāyairyadā rājñastīrthasnānādibhirdvijāḥ \|*  
*na nivṛttā brahmahatyā mithilāṃ sa yayau tadā \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. O brahmins, when Brahmahatyā did not leave him inspite of the means like the ceremonial ablution in holy centres, the king went to Mithilā.

## \|\| 8.10.31 \|\|

*bāhyodyānagatastasyāścitayā parayārditaḥ \|*  
*dadarśa munimāyāntaṃ gautamaṃ pārthivaśca saḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Distressed and worried by anxiety the king reached a park at the outskirts of the city. There he saw the sage Gautama approaching him.

## \|\| 8.10.32 \|\|

*abhisṛtya sa rājendro gautamaṃ vimalāśayam \|*  
*taddarśanāptakiṃcitkaḥ praṇanāma muhurmuhuḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. The king advanced towards that sage of pure mind. He felt satiated on seeing him. He bowed to him again and again.

## \|\| 8.10.33 \|\|

*atha tatpṛṣṭakuśalo dīrghamuṣṇaṃ ca niśvasan \|*  
*tatkṛpādṛṣṭisaṃprāptasukha provāca taṃ nṛpaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. When the sage enquired of his welfare, the king heaved a sigh of grief and distress. Viewed mercifully by him the king felt pleased and contented. He said thus.

## \|\| 8.10.34 \|\|

*rājovāca \|*  
*mune māṃ bādhate hyeṣā brahmahatyā duratyayā \|*  
*alakṣitā paraistāta tarjayaṃtī padepade \|\| 34 \|\|*

The king said:—

34\. O sage, this endless Brahmahatyā harasses me. O dear, it is imperceptible to others but it threatens me at every step.

## \|\| 8.10.35 \|\|

*yanmayā śāpadagdhena vipraputraśca bhakṣitaḥ \|*  
*tatpāpasya na śāntirhi prāyaścittasahasrakaiḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. A brahmin boy had been devoured by me in the clutches of a curse. That sin cannot be suppressed even by thousands of expiatory rites.

## \|\| 8.10.36 \|\|

*nānopāyāḥ kṛtā me hi tacchāntyai bhramatā mune \|*  
*na nivṛttā brahmahatyā mama pāpātmanaḥ kimu \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. O sage, I roamed here and there and tried various means to quell it. But it did not leave me off. Is it not due to my being a sinning soul?

## \|\| 8.10.37 \|\|

*adya me janmasāphalyaṃ saṃprāptamiva lakṣaye \|*  
*yatastvaddarśanādeva mamānandabharo'bhavat \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Now it seems to me that I have attained the fruits of my birth because merely by your sight my heart is filled with bliss.

## \|\| 8.10.38 \|\|

*adya me tavapādābja śaraṇasya kṛtainasaḥ \|*  
*śāṃtiṃ kuru mahābhāga yenāhaṃ sukhamāpnuyām \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. O fortunate one, I, the sinner, seek refuge in your lotus-like feet. Grant me peace, whereby I can be happy.

## \|\| 8.10.39 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*iti rājñā samādiṣṭo gautamaḥ karuṇārdradhīḥ \|*  
*samādideśa ghorāṇāmaghānāṃ sādhu niṣkṛtim \|\| 39 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

39\. Implored thus by the king, the kind-hearted Gautama instructed him in the expiatory means for terrible sins.

## \|\| 8.10.40 \|\|

*gautama uvāca \|*  
*sādhu rājendra dhanyosi mahāghebhyo bhayantyaja \|*  
*śive śāstari bhaktānāṃ kva bhayaṃ śaraṇaiṣiṇām \|\| 40 \|\|*

Gautama said:—

40\. O excellent king, well done You are blessed. Eschew all fear from sins. As long as Śiva is the ruler, there can be no terror to devotees who seek refuge in him.

## \|\| 8.10.41 \|\|

*śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga kṣetramanyatpratiṣṭhitam \|*  
*mahāpātakasaṃhāri gokarṇākhyaṃ śivālayam \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. O fortunate king, listen. There is another shrine well consecrated. There is a Śiva’s temple named Gokarṇa. It destroys all great sins.

## \|\| 8.10.42 \|\|

*tatra sthitirna pāpānāṃ mahadbhyo mahatāmapi \|*  
*mahābalābhidhānena śivaḥ saṃnihitaḥ svayam \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Sins greater than the greatest cannot stay there. Śiva himself is present there in the name of Mahābala.

## \|\| 8.10.43 \|\|

*sarveṣāṃ śivaliṃgānāṃ sārvabhaumo mahābalaḥ \|*  
*caturyuge caturvarṇassarvapāpāpahārakaḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Mahābala is the emperor of all phallic images. It assumes four different colours in the four Yugas. It dispels all sins.

## \|\| 8.10.44 \|\|

*paścimāmbudhitīrasthaṃ gokarṇaṃ tīrthamuttamam \|*  
*tatrāsti śivaliṃgaṃ tanmahāpātakanāśakam \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The excellent holy centre of Gokarṇa is on the shore of western ocean. There is a phallic image of Śiva, destructive of great sins.

## \|\| 8.10.45 \|\|

*tatra gatvā mahāpāpāḥ snātvā tīrtheṣu bhūriśaḥ \|*  
*mahābalaṃ ca saṃpūjya prayātāśśāṃkarampadam \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Great sinners go there, take their ceremonial ablutions many times in the sacred waters and worship Mahābala. They have all attained Śiva’s region.

## \|\| 8.10.46 \|\|

*tathā tvamapi rājendra gokarṇa giriśālayam \|*  
*gatvā sampūjya talliṃgaṃ kṛtakṛtyatvamāpnuyāḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. O king, you also go to Gokarṇa, the temple of Śiva. After going there and worshipping Śiva attain contentment.

## \|\| 8.10.47 \|\|

*tatra sarveṣu tīrtheṣu snātvābhyarcya mahābalam \|*  
*sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ śivalokantvamāpnuyāḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Take your holy dip in the sacred waters there. Worship Mahābala. Thus you will be rid of sins and attain Śivaloka.

## \|\| 8.10.48 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityādiṣṭaḥ sa muninā gautamena mahātmanā \|*  
*mahāhṛṣṭamanā rājā gokarṇaṃ pratyapadyata \|\| 48 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

48\. Advised thus by the noble-souled Gautama, the king highly delighted in the mind reached Gokarṇa.

## \|\| 8.10.49 \|\|

*tatra tīrtheṣu susnātvā samabhyarcya mahābalam \|*  
*nirdhūtāśeṣapāpaugho'labhacchaṃbhoḥ parampadam \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. He took his dip in the holy ponds and worshipped Mahābala. His sins were wiped off without the least vestige. He attained the great region of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.10.50 \|\|

*ya imāṃ śṛṇuyānnityaṃ mahābalakathāṃ priyām \|*  
*trisaptakulajaissārddhaṃ śivaloke vrajatyasau \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. He who listens to this pleasing narrative of Mahābala goes to Śiyaloka along with the members of his family upto twenty-one generations.

## \|\| 8.10.51 \|\|

*iti vaśca samākhyātaṃ māhātmyaṃ paramādbhutam \|*  
*mahābalasya giriśaliṃgasya nikhilāghahṛt \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Thus the extremely wonderful glory of Mahābala, the phallic image of Śiva, has been narrated to you. It dispels all sins.

## Chapter 11 - The greatness of the moon-crested Paśupatinātha

## \|\| 8.11.1 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*sūtasūta mahābhāga dhanyastvaṃ śivasaktadhīḥ \|*  
*mahābalasya liṃgasya śrāviteyaṃ kathādbhutā \|\| 1 \|\|*

The sages said:—

1\. O Sūta, O fortunate Sūta, you are blessed with your mind attached to Śiva. This wonderful story of Mahābala has been narrated to you.

## \|\| 8.11.2 \|\|

*uttarasyāṃ diśāyāṃ ca śivaliṃgāni yāni ca \|*  
*teṣāṃ māhātmyamanagha vada tvaṃ pāpanāśakam \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Please narrate the sacred glory oí the sin-destroying phallic images that are in the northern quarter.

## \|\| 8.11.3 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇutādarato viprā auttarāṇāṃ viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*māhātmyaṃ śivaliṃgānāṃ pravadāmi samāsataḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

3\. O brahmins, listen with respect. I shall succinctly explain the particular glory of the northern phallic images of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.11.4 \|\|

*gokarṇaṃ kṣetramaparaṃ mahāpātakanāśanam \|*  
*mahāvanaṃ ca tatrāsti pavitramativistaram \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. There is another shrine Gokarṇa destructive of great sins. There is an extensive holy forest there.

## \|\| 8.11.5 \|\|

*tatrāsti candrabhālākhyaṃ śivaliṃgamanuttamam \|*  
*rāvaṇena samānītaṃ sadbhaktyā sarvasiddhidam \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The moon-crested Śiva’s phallic image, excellent of all, is situated there. It was installed by Rāvaṇa with great devotion and it confers all accomplishments.

## \|\| 8.11.6 \|\|

*tasya tatra sthitirvaidyanāthasyeva munīśvarāḥ \|*  
*sarvalokahitārthāya karuṇāsāgarasya ca \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. O great sages, its situation there, is for the benefit of the people as that of Vaidyanātha, the ocean of mercy.

## \|\| 8.11.7 \|\|

*snānaṃ kṛtvā tu gokarṇe candrabhālaṃ samarcya ca \|*  
*śivalokamavāpnoti satyaṃsatyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Taking the ceremonial ablution in Gokarṇa and worshipping the moon-crested Śiva, the devotee attains Śívaloka. True. It is true undoubtedly.

## \|\| 8.11.8 \|\|

*candrabhālasya liṃgasya mahimā paramādbhutaḥ \|*  
*na śakyo varṇituṃ vyāsādbhaktasnehitarasya hi \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. It is impossible to describe in detail the excessively wonderful glory of the moon-crested lord Śiva who has a great affection for his devotees.

## \|\| 8.11.9 \|\|

*candrabhālamahādeva liṃgasya mahimā mahān \|*  
*yathākathaṃcitsaṃproktaḥ paraliṃgasya vai śṛṇu \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Somehow the extreme grandeur of the phallic Śiva has been explained to you. Now listen to that of the other phallic image.

## \|\| 8.11.10 \|\|

*dādhīcaṃ śivaliṃgaṃ tu miśrarṣivaratīrthake \|*  
*dadhīcinā munīśena suprītyā ca pratiṣṭhitam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. In the excellent holy centre of the sage Miśra, the phallic image of Śiva had been installed by the great sage Dadhīci.

## \|\| 8.11.11 \|\|

*tatra gatvā ca tattīrthe snātvā samyagvidhānataḥ \|*  
*śivaliṃgaṃ samarcedvai dādhīceśvaramādarāt \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. After going there and taking the due ceremonial ablution in the sacred pond, the devotee shall worship this image reverentially.

## \|\| 8.11.12 \|\|

*dādhīcamūrtistatraiva samarcyā vidhipūrvakam \|*  
*śivaprītyarthamevāśu tīrthayātrā phalārthibhiḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. The idol of Dadhīca should be duly worshipped for the propitiation of Śiva by those who seek the fruit of the pilgrimage.

## \|\| 8.11.13 \|\|

*evaṃ kṛte muniśreṣṭhāḥ kṛtakṛtyo bhavennaraḥ \|*  
*iha sarvasukhaṃ bhuktvā paratra gatimāpnuyāt \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. O excellent sages, if this is duly performed, the devotee will feel contented. After enjoying all pleasure here he will attain the supreme goal hereafter.

## \|\| 8.11.14 \|\|

*naimiṣāraṇyatīrthe tu nikhilarṣipratiṣṭhitam \|*  
*ṛṣīśvaramiti khyātaṃ śivaliṃgaṃ sukhapradam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. The phallic image of Śiva known as Ṛṣīśvara installed by the sages in the holy centre of Naimiṣa forest, yields happiness.

## \|\| 8.11.15 \|\|

*taddarśanātpūjanācca janānāṃ pāpināmapi \|*  
*bhuktimuktiśca teṣāṃ tu paratreha munīśvarāḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. O great sages, enjoyment of worldly pleasures and salvation here and hereafter will fall to the lot of even sinful persons who visit and worship it,

## \|\| 8.11.16 \|\|

*hatyāharaṇatīrthe tu śivaliṃgamaghāpaham \|*  
*pūjanīyaṃ viśeṣeṇa hatyākoṭivināśanam \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The phallic image destructive of sins, in the holy centre of Hatyāharaṇa should be particularly worshipped. It destroys even the sins of several slaughters.

## \|\| 8.11.17 \|\|

*devaprayāgatīrthe tu laliteśvaranāmakam \|*  
*śivaliṃgaṃ sadā pūjyaṃ naraissarvāghanāśanam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Laliteśvara, the phallic image of Śiva in the holy centre Devaprayāga should be worshipped by men. It is destructive of all sins.

## \|\| 8.11.18 \|\|

*nayapālākhyapuryyāṃ tu prasiddhāyāṃ mahītale \|*  
*liṃgaṃ paśupatīśākhyaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. The phallic image Paśupatīśa in the Nayapāla town famous on the earth is the bestower of the fruits of all desires.

## \|\| 8.11.19 \|\|

*śirobhāgasvarūpeṇa śivaliṃgaṃ tadasti hi \|*  
*tatkathāṃ varṇayiṣyāmi kedāreśvaravarṇane \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. That phallic image is in the form of the crest. I shall narrate the story in the description of Kedāreśvara.

## \|\| 8.11.20 \|\|

*tadārānmuktināthākhyaṃ śivaliṃgaṃ mahādbhutam \|*  
*darśanādarcanāttasya bhuktirmuktiśca labhyate \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Close to it lies the wonderful phallīc image Muktinātha. Its very sight and worship bestows worldly enjoyment here and liberation hereafter.

## \|\| 8.11.21 \|\|

*iti vaśca samākhyātaṃ liṃgavarṇanamuttamam \|*  
*caturdikṣu muniśreṣṭhāḥ kimanyacchrotumicchatha \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. O great sages, the excellent phallic images in the four quarters have been thus described to you all. What else do you wish to hear.

## Chapter 12 - The reason for Śiva’s assuming the phallic form (liṅga)

## \|\| 8.12.1 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*sūta jānāsi sakalaṃ vastu vyāsaprasādataḥ \|*  
*tavājñātaṃ na vidyeta tasmātpṛcchāmahe vayam \|\| 1 \|\|*

The sages said:—

1\. O Sūta, you know every thing by the grace of Vyāsa. There is nothing not known to you. Hence we approach you with a query.

## \|\| 8.12.2 \|\|

*liṃgaṃ ca pūjyate loke tattvayā kathitaṃ ca yat \|*  
*tattathaiva na cānyadvā kāraṇaṃ vidyate tviha \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. The phallic form (liṅga) of Śiva is worshipped throughout the world. You have said so. Is there any special reason for the same?

## \|\| 8.12.3 \|\|

*bāṇarūpā śrutā loke pārvatī śivavallabhā \|*  
*etatkiṃ kāraṇaṃ sūta kathaya tvaṃ yathāśrutam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Pārvatī, the beloved of Śiva, is heard in the world in the form of an arrow. O Sūta, what is the reason for this? Please satisfy our curiosity in this respect by giving the account you have heard.

## \|\| 8.12.4 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*kalpabhedakathā caiva śrutā vyāsānmayā dvijāḥ \|*  
*tāmeva kathayāmyadya śrūyatāmṛṣisattamāḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

Sūtasaid:—

4\. O brahmins, the story of a different Kalpa was heard from Vyāsa by me. O excellent sages, I shall narrate the same. Please listen.

## \|\| 8.12.5 \|\|

*purā dāruvane jātaṃ yadvṛttaṃ tu dvijanmanām \|*  
*tadeva śrūyatāṃ samyak kathayāmi kathāśrutam \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. What happened formerly among the brahmins in the Dāruvana forest may kindly be heard. I shall narrate the same in the manner I have heard.

## \|\| 8.12.6 \|\|

*dārunāmavanaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ tatrāsannṛṣisattamāḥ \|*  
*śivabhaktāssadā nityaṃ śivadhyānaparāyaṇāḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. There is an excellent forest Dāruvana. There were many excellent sages there, great devotees of Śiva who were always engrossed in meditation on Siva.

## \|\| 8.12.7 \|\|

*trikālaṃ śivapūjāṃ ca kurvaṃti sma nirantaram \|*  
*nānāvidhaiḥ stavairdivyaistuṣṭuvuste munīśvarāḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. O great sages, they performed the worship of Śiva incessantly thrice a day. They eulogised Śiva with different devotional hymns.

## \|\| 8.12.8 \|\|

*te kadācidvane yātāssamidhāharaṇāya ca \|*  
*sarve dvijarṣabhāśśaivāśśivadhyānaparāyaṇāḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Once the leading brahmin devotees of Śiva engrossed in the meditation of Śiva went into the forest for bringing sacrificial twigs.

## \|\| 8.12.9 \|\|

*etasminnaṃtare sākṣācchaṃkaro nīla lohitaḥ \|*  
*virūpaṃ ca samāsthāya parīkṣārthaṃ samāgataḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. In the meantime Śiva himself assuming a very hideous form came there in order to test their devotion.

## \|\| 8.12.10 \|\|

*digambaro'titejasvī bhūtibhūṣaṇabhūṣitaḥ \|*  
*sa ceṣṭāmakarodduṣṭāṃ haste liṃgaṃ vidhārayan \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. He was very brilliant but stark naked. He had smeared ashes all over his body as the sole ornament. Standing there and holding his penis he began to show all sorts of vicious tricks.

## \|\| 8.12.11 \|\|

*manasā ca priyaṃ teṣāṃ kartuṃ vai vanavāsinām \|*  
*jagāma tadvanaṃ prītyā bhaktaprīto haraḥ svayam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. It was with a mind to do something pleasing to the forest-dwellers that Śiva, favourite of the devotees, came to the forest at his will.

## \|\| 8.12.12 \|\|

*taṃ dṛṣṭvā ṛṣipatnyastāḥ paraṃ trāsamupāgatāḥ \|*  
*vihvalā vismitāścānyāssamājagmustathā punaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. The wives of the sages were extremely frightened at this sight. The other women excited and surprised approached the lord.

## \|\| 8.12.13 \|\|

*aliliṃgustathā cānyāḥ karaṃ dhṛtyā tathāparāḥ \|*  
*parasparaṃ tu saṃgharṣātsaṃmagnāstāḥ striyastadā \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Some embraced him. Others held his hands. The women were engrossed in struggling with one another.

## \|\| 8.12.14 \|\|

*etasminneva samaye ṛṣivaryāḥ samāgaman \|*  
*viruddhaṃ taṃ ca te dṛṣṭvā duḥkhitāḥ krodhamūrcchitāḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Meanwhile the great sages came there. On seeing him engaged in perverse activities they were pained and infuriated.

## \|\| 8.12.15 \|\|

*tadā duḥkhamanuprāptāḥ koyaṃ koyaṃ tathā'buvan \|*  
*samastā ṛṣayaste vai śivamāyāvimohitāḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. The sages, deluded by Śiva’s Māyā and plunged in grief, began to say—“Who is this? Who is this?”

## \|\| 8.12.16 \|\|

*yadā ca noktavānkiṃcitsovadhūto digambaraḥ \|*  
*ūcustaṃ puruṣaṃ bhīmaṃ tadā te paramarṣayaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. When the naked sage did not reply, the great sages told that terrible Puruṣa.

## \|\| 8.12.17 \|\|

*tvayā viruddhaṃ kriyate vedamārga vilopi yat \|*  
*tatastvadīyaṃ talliṃgaṃ patatāṃ pṛthivītale \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. “You are acting pervertedly. This violates the Vedic path. Hence let your penis fall on the ground.”

## \|\| 8.12.18 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityukte tu tadā taiśca liṃgaṃ ca patitaṃ kṣaṇāt \|*  
*avadhūtasya tasyāśu śivasyādbhutarūpiṇaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

18\. When they said thus, the penis of that Avadhūta, who was Śiva of wonderful form, fell down instantly.

## \|\| 8.12.19 \|\|

*talliṃgaṃ cāgnivatsarvaṃ yaddadāha purā sthitam \|*  
*yatrayatra ca tadyāti tatratatra dahetpunaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. That penis burnt everything in front; wherever it went it began to burn everything there.

## \|\| 8.12.20 \|\|

*pātāle ca gataṃ taśca svarge cāpi tathaiva ca \|*  
*bhūmau sarvatra tadyātaṃ na kutrāpi sthiraṃ hi tat \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. It went to Pātāla; it went to heaven; it went all over the earth; it never remained steady anywhere.

## \|\| 8.12.21 \|\|

*lokāśca vyākulā jātā ṛṣayastetiduḥkhitāḥ \|*  
*na śarma lebhire keciddevāśca ṛṣayastathā \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. All the worlds and the people were distressed. The sages became grief-stricken. Whether gods or sages no one had any peace or joy.

## \|\| 8.12.22 \|\|

*na jñātastu śivo yaistu te sarve ca surarṣayaḥ \|*  
*duḥkhitā militāśśīghraṃ brahmāṇaṃ śaraṇaṃ yayuḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. All the gods and sages who did not recognize Śiva became sad. They assembled together and hastened to Brahmā and sought refuge in him.

## \|\| 8.12.23 \|\|

*tatra gatvā ca te sarve natvā stutvā vidhiṃ dvijāḥ \|*  
*tatsarvamavadanvṛttaṃ brahmaṇe sṛṣṭikāriṇe \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. O brahmins, after going there, they bowed to and eulogised Brahma. They narrated what had happened to Brahmā the Creator.

## \|\| 8.12.24 \|\|

*brahmā tadvacanaṃ śrutvā śivamāyāvimohitān \|*  
*jñātvā tāñcchaṃkaraṃ natvā provāca ṛṣisattamān \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Brahmā heard them. He realised that they had been deluded by Śiva’s Māyā. After bowing to Śiva, he told the excellent sages.

## \|\| 8.12.25 \|\|

*\|\| brahmovāca \|*  
*jñātāraśca bhavanto vai kurvate garhitaṃ dvijāḥ \|*  
*ajñātāro yadā kuryuḥ kiṃ punaḥ kathyate punaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

Brahmā said:—

25\. O brahmins, you though wise do such despicable things. Then why complain against the ignorant who act likewise?

## \|\| 8.12.26-27 \|\|

*viruddhyaivaṃ śivaṃ devaṃ kuśalaṃ kassamīhate \|*  
*madhyāhnasamaye yo vai nātithiṃ ca parāmṛśet \|\| 26 \|\|*

*tasyaiva sukṛtaṃ nītvā svīyaṃ ca duṣkṛtaṃ punaḥ \|*  
*saṃsthāpya cātithiryāti kiṃ punaḥ śivameva vā \|\| 27 \|\|*

26-27. Who can wish for happiness after offending and antagonising Śiva thus? If a person does not welcome and serve a guest at midday, his virtue is taken away by the guest who in return deposits his sin in him. What then of Śiva himself as a guest?

## \|\| 8.12.28 \|\|

*yāvalliṃgaṃ sthiraṃ naiva jagatāṃ tritaye śubham \|*  
*jāyate na tadā kvāpi satyametadvadāmyaham \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. As long as the penis does not become stationary there cannot be anything good in the three worlds. I am telling you the truth.

## \|\| 8.12.29 \|\|

*bhavadbhiśca tathā kāryaṃ yathā svāsthyaṃ bhavediha \|*  
*śivaliṃgasya ṛṣayo manasā saṃvicāryyatām \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. O sages, you must do such things as will make the penis of Śiva steady. Please ponder over this in your minds.

## \|\| 8.12.30 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktāste praṇamyocurbrahmāṇamṛṣayaśca vai \|*  
*kimasmābhirvidhe kāryaṃ tatkāryaṃ tvaṃ samādiśa \|\| 30 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

30\. Thus urged, the sages bowed to Brahmā and said—“O Brahmā, what shall be done by us? Please guide us in that task.”

## \|\| 8.12.31 \|\|

*ityuktaśca munīśaistaissarvalokapitāmahaḥ \|*  
*munīśāṃstāṃstadā brahmā svayaṃ provāca vai tadā \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. When the great sages asked him thus, Brahmā, the grandfather of the worlds, spoke to them.

## \|\| 8.12.32 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*ārādhya girijāṃ devīṃ prārthayantu surāśśivam \|*  
*yonirūpā bhaveccedvai tadā tatsthiratāṃ vrajet \|\| 32 \|\|*

Brahmā said:—

32\. Let the gods propitiate goddess Pārvatī and pray. If she can assume the form of the vaginal passage that penis will become steady.

## \|\| 8.12.33 \|\|

*tadvidhimpravadāmyadya sarve śṛṇuta sattamāḥ \|*  
*tāmeva kuruta premṇā prasannā sā bhaviṣyati \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. O excellent sages, listen. I shall tell you the mode of procedure. Act accordingly with love and devotion. She will be thus pleased.

## \|\| 8.12.34 \|\|

*kumbhamekaṃ ca saṃsthāpya kṛtvāṣṭadalamuttamam \|*  
*dūrvāyavāṃkuraistīrthodakamāpūrayettataḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Make an eight-petalled mystic diagram of lotus and place a pot over it. Water from holy centres shall be poured into the pot along with the sprouts of Dūrvā and barley.

## \|\| 8.12.35 \|\|

*vedamaṃtraistatastaṃ vai kuṃbhaṃ caivābhimaṃtrayet \|*  
*śrutyuktavidhinā tasya pūjāṃ kṛtvā śivaṃ smaran \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. The pot shall be invoked with Vedic mantras. It shall be worshipped according to the Vedic rituals after remembering Śiva.

## \|\| 8.12.36 \|\|

*talliṃgaṃ tajjalenābhiṣecayetparamarṣayaḥ \|*  
*śatarudriyamaṃtraistu prokṣitaṃ śāṃtimāpnuyāt \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. The penis shall be drenched with that water, O great sages. When the sprinkling is made with Śatarudrīya mantras it will become stable.

## \|\| 8.12.37 \|\|

*girijāṃ yonirūpāṃ ca bāṇaṃ sthāpya śubhaṃ punaḥ \|*  
*tatra liṃgaṃ ca tatsthāpyaṃ punaścaivābhimaṃtrayet \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Pārvatī in the form of the Vaginal passage and an auspicions arrow shall form as the pedestal wherein the phallus shall be installed in accompaniment of the Vedic mantras.

## \|\| 8.12.38 \|\|

*sugandhaiścandanaiścaiva puṣpadhūpādibhistathā \|*  
*naivedyādikapūjābhistoṣayetparameśvaram \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Lord Śiva shall be propitiated with the offerings of sweet scents, sandal paste, fragrant flowers, incense and other things as well as by food offerings and other forms of worship.

## \|\| 8.12.39 \|\|

*praṇipātaiḥ stavaiḥ puṇyairvādyairgānaistathā punaḥ \|*  
*tataḥ svastyayanaṃ kṛtvā jayeti vyāharettathā \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. With prostrations, holy hymns, musical instruments and songs he shall be propitiated further. Svastyayana mantras shall be repeated and the devotees shall proclaim the victory of the lord.

## \|\| 8.12.40 \|\|

*prasanno bhava deveśa jagadāhlādakāraka \|*  
*kartā pālayitā tvañca saṃhartā tvaṃ nirakṣaraḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. The following prayer shall be recited—“O lord of the gods, be pleased, O delighter of the universe. You are the creator, protector and the annihilator. You are imperishable.

## \|\| 8.12.41 \|\|

*jagadādirjagadyonirjagadantargatopi ca \|*  
*śānto bhava maheśāna sarvāṃllokāṃśca pālaya \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. You are the beginning of the universe, the origin of the universe within the universe. “O lord Śiva, be quiet. Protect all the worlds.”

## \|\| 8.12.42 \|\|

*evaṃ kṛte vidhau svāsthyaṃ bhaviṣyati na saṃśaya \|*  
*vikāro na trilokesminbhaviṣyati sukhaṃ sadā \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. If this is performed there will be stability, to be sure. There will not be any aberration in the three worlds. There will be happiness always.

## \|\| 8.12.43 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktāste dvijā devāḥ praṇipatya pitāmaham \|*  
*śivaṃ taṃ śaraṇaṃ prāptassarvalokasukhepsayā \|\| 43 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

43\. O brahmins, thus advised, the gods bowed to Brahmā and then sought refuge in Śiva with a desire for the happiness of the worlds.

## \|\| 8.12.44 \|\|

*pūjitaḥ parayā bhaktyā prārthitaḥ śaṃkarastadā \|*  
*suprasannastato bhūtvā tānuvāca maheśvaraḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. When worshipped with great devotion and implored, lord Śiva became delighted and spoke to them.

## \|\| 8.12.45 \|\|

*maheśvara uvāca \|*  
*he devā ṛṣayaḥ sarve madvacaḥ śṛṇutādarāt \|*  
*yonirūpeṇa malliṃgaṃ dhṛtaṃ cetsyāttadā sukham \|\| 45 \|\|*

Lord Śiva said:—

45\. O gods, O sages, you listen to my words with reverence. If my penis is supported in a vaginal passage there will be happiness.

## \|\| 8.12.46 \|\|

*pārvatīṃ ca vinā nānyā liṃgaṃ dhārayituṃ kṣamā \|*  
*tayā dhṛtaṃ ca malliṃgaṃ drutaṃ śāntiṃ gamiṣyati \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Except Pārvatī, no other woman can hold my penis. Held by her my penis will immediately become quiet.

## \|\| 8.12.47 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*tacchrutvā ṛṣibhirdevaissuprasannairmunīśvarāḥ \|*  
*gṛhītvā caiva brahmāṇaṃ girijā prārthitā tadā \|\| 47 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

47\. O great sages, on hearing those words the delighted sages and the gods took Brahmā with them and prayed to Pārvatī.

## \|\| 8.12.48 \|\|

*prasannāṃ girijāṃ kṛtvā vṛṣabhadhvajameva ca \|*  
*pūrvoktaṃ ca vidhiṃ kṛtvā sthāpitaṃ liṃgamuttamam \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. After propitiating Pārvatī and the bull-bannered lord and performing the rites mentioned before, the excellent penis became static.

## \|\| 8.12.49 \|\|

*maṃtroktena vidhānena devāśca ṛṣayastathā \|*  
*cakruḥ prasannāṃ girijāṃ śivaṃ ca dharmahetave \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. The gods and the sages propitiated Pārvatī and Śiva by the mode of procedure laid down in the Vedas for the sake of virtue.

## \|\| 8.12.50 \|\|

*samānarcurviśeṣeṇa sarve devarṣayaḥ śivam \|*  
*brahmā viṣṇuḥ pare caiva trailokyaṃ sacarācaram \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Brahmā, Viṣṇu and other gods, sages and the three worlds including the mobile and immobile beings worshipped Śiva particularly.

## \|\| 8.12.51 \|\|

*suprasannaḥ śivo jātaḥ śivā ca jagadambikā \|*  
*dhṛtaṃ tayā ca talliṃgaṃ tena rūpeṇa vai tadā \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Śiva became delighted and so also Pārvatī, the mother of the universe. That phallus was held by her in that form then.

## \|\| 8.12.52 \|\|

*lokānāṃ sthāpite liṃge kalyāṇaṃ cābhavattadā \|*  
*prasiddhaṃ caiva talliṃgaṃ trilokyāmabhavaddvijāḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. When the phallus was stabilised, there was welfare throughout the worlds. O brahmins, that phallus became famous in the three worlds.

## \|\| 8.12.53 \|\|

*hāṭakeśamiti khyātaṃ tacchivāśivamityapi \|*  
*pūjanāttasya lokānāṃ sukhaṃ bhavati sarvathā \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. The phallus is known as “Hāṭeśa” as well as “Śivā-Śiva.” By worshipping it, all the people become happy in every respect.

## \|\| 8.12.54 \|\|

*iha sarvasamṛddhiḥ syānnānāsukhavahādhikā \|*  
*paratra paramā muktirnātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. Here everything will flourish in plenty conferring supreme happiness and bliss. Hereafter it will bestow final liberation. No doubt need be entertained in this respect.

## Chapter 13 - The origin of Vaṭuka

## \|\| 8.13.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*yathābhavalliṃgarūpaḥ saṃpūjyastribhave śivaḥ \|*  
*tathoktaṃ vā dvijāḥ prītyā kimanyacchrotumicchatha \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. O brahmins, it has been narrated to you how Śiva came to be worshipped in the three worlds in the form of a phallic image. What else do you wish to hear?

## \|\| 8.13.2 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ andhakeśvaraliṃgasya mahimānaṃ vada prabho \|*  
*tathānyacchivaliṃgānāṃ prītyā vaktumihārhasi \|\| 2 \|\|*

The sages said:—

2\. O holy lord, please narrate the glory of the phallic image known as Andhakeśvara. Similarly it behoves you to mention the glory of other phallic images willingly.

## \|\| 8.13.3 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*purābdhigartamāśritya vasandaityo'ndhakāsuraḥ \|*  
*svavaśaṃ kārayāmāsa trailokyaṃ surasūdanaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

3\. Formerly the Asura Andhaka resorted to the deep ditch of the ocean and kept the three worlds under his control. He harassed the gods.

## \|\| 8.13.4 \|\|

*tasmādgartācca nissṛtya pīḍayitvā punaḥ prajāḥ \|*  
*prāviśacca tadā daityastaṃ gartaṃ suparākramaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. He used to come out of the ditch and harrass the people. Then he of great exploit re-entered the ditch.

## \|\| 8.13.5 \|\|

*devāśca duḥkhitaḥ sarve śivaṃ prārthya punaḥpunaḥ \|*  
*sarvaṃ nivedayāmāsussvaduḥkhaṃ ca munīśvarāḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. O great sages, the gods were grief-stricken. Imploring Śiva again and again, they mentioned their miseries to him.

## \|\| 8.13.6 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*tadākarṇya vacasteṣāṃ devānāṃ parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*pratyuvāca prasannātmā duṣṭahaṃtā satāṃ gatiḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

6\. On hearing the words of the gods, the delighted lord Śiva, the slayer of the wicked and the goal of the good replied.

## \|\| 8.13.7 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*ghātayiṣyāmi taṃ daityamandhakaṃ surasūdanam \|*  
*sainyaṃ ca nīyatāndevā hyāyāmi ca gaṇaissaha \|\| 7 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

7\. I shall slay that Asura Andhaka, the murderer of the gods. O gods, proceed with the army. I too shall come along with the Gaṇas.

## \|\| 8.13.8 \|\|

*tasmādgartādaṃdhake hi devarṣidruhi bhīkare \|*  
*nissṛte ca tadā tasmindevā gartamupāśritāḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. When that terrible antagonist of the gods and the sages came out of the ditch, the gods went and occupied it.

## \|\| 8.13.9 \|\|

*daityāśca devatāścaiva yuddhaṃ cakruḥ sudāruṇam \|*  
*śivānugrahato devāḥ prabalāścābhavaṃstadā \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. The Asuras and the gods fought a terrible battle. By the blessings of Śiva, the gods became very strong.

## \|\| 8.13.10 \|\|

*devaiśca pīḍitaḥ sopi yāvadgartamupāgataḥ \|*  
*tāvacchūlena saṃprotaḥ śivena paramātmanā \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Attacked and tormented by the gods, the Asura returned to the ditch when he was pierced through with the trident by Śiva, the supreme soul.

## \|\| 8.13.11 \|\|

*tatratyaśca tadā śaṃbhuṃ dhyātvā saṃprārthayattadā \|*  
*antakāle ca tvāṃ dṛṣṭvā tādṛśo bhavati kṣaṇāt \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Remaining there he meditated on Śiva and prayed—“On seeing you at the hour of death, one becomes identical with you instantaneously.”

## \|\| 8.13.12 \|\|

*ityevaṃ saṃstutassopi prasannaḥ śaṃkarastadā \|*  
*uvāca vacanaṃ tatra varaṃ brūhi dadāmi te \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Thus eulogised, the delighted Śiva spoke to him—“Mention the boon you wish to have. I shall grant it to you.”

## \|\| 8.13.13 \|\|

*ityevaṃ vacanaṃ śrutvā sa daityaḥ punarabravīt \|*  
*supraṇamya śivaṃ stutvā sattvabhāvamupāśritaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. On hearing these words, the Asura said again after bowing to and eulogising Śiva with pious feelings.

## \|\| 8.13.14 \|\|

*andhaka uvāca \|*  
*yadi prasanno deveśa svabhaktiṃ dehi me śubhām \|*  
*kṛpāṃ kṛtvā viśeṣeṇa saṃsthito bhava ceha vai \|\| 14 \|\|*

Andhaka said:—

14\. O lord of gods, if you are delighted grant me the auspicious devotion to you. Taking unusual compassion on me, be stationed here.

## \|\| 8.13.15 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktastena daityaṃ taṃ tadgarte cākṣipaddharaḥ \|*  
*svayaṃ tatra sthito liṃgarūpo'sau lokakāmyayā \|\| 15 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

15\. Requested thus, Śiva hurled that Asura into that ditch. He himself stood there in the form of a phallic image with a desire to help the world.

## \|\| 8.13.16 \|\|

*andhakeśaṃ ca talliṃgaṃ nityaṃ yaḥ pūjayennaraḥ \|*  
*ṣaṇmāsājjāyate tasya vāṃchāsiddhirna saṃśayaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. If a man worships Andhakeśa every day, his desires are fulfilled within six months.

## \|\| 8.13.17-19 \|\|

*vṛttyarthaṃ pūjayelliṃgaṃ lokasya hitakārakam \|*  
*ṣaṇmāsaṃ yo dvijaścaiva sa vai devalakaḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

*yathā devalakaścaiva sa bhavediha vai tadā \|*  
*devalakaśca yaḥ prokto nādhikāro dvijasya hi \|\| 18 \|\|*

*\|\| ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*devalakaśca kaḥ proktaḥ kiṃ kāryaṃ tasya vidyate \|*  
*tattvaṃ vada mahāprājña lokānāṃ hitahetave \|\| 19 \|\|*

17-19. The brahmin who worships the beneficent phallic image for his livelihood for six months becomes a Devalaka, the worshipper of idol for subsistence. He is not authorised in ordinary rites of brahmins.

## \|\| 8.13.20 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*dadhīcirnāma vipro yo dharmiṣṭho vedapāragaḥ \|*  
*śivabhaktirato nityaṃ śivaśāstraparāyaṇaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

The sages said:—

Who is a Devalaka? What is his function? O intelligent one, mention that for the guidance of the people.

Sūta said:—

20\. There was a brahmin Dadhīci who was well-versed in the Vedas and highly virtuous. He was engrossed in his devotion to Śiva and was interested in Śiva’s lore.

## \|\| 8.13.21 \|\|

*tasya putrastathā hyāsītsmṛto nāmnā sudarśanaḥ \|*  
*tasya bhāryā dukūlā ca nāmnā duṣṭakulodbhavā \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. His son was known as Sudarśana whose wife was Dukūlā. She was born of a defiled family.

## \|\| 8.13.22 \|\|

*tadvaśe sa ca bhartāsīttasya putracatuṣṭayam \|*  
*so'pi nityaṃ śivasyaiva pūjāṃ ca sma karotyasau \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. The husband was always under her control. He had four sons. Of course he used to worship Śiva every day.

## \|\| 8.13.23 \|\|

*dadhīcestu tadā hyāsīdgrāmāntaraniveśanam \|*  
*jñātisaṃyogataścaiva jñātibhirna sa mocitaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Then it happened that Dadhīci had to go and live in another village along with his kinsmen. The kinsmen did not let him go back.

## \|\| 8.13.24 \|\|

*kathayitvā ca putraṃ sa śivabhaktirato bhava \|*  
*ityuktvā sa gato mukto dādhīciśśaivasattamaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. While leaving home, Dadhīci, the excellent devotee, had already instructed his son, “Be devoted to Śiva.”

## \|\| 8.13.25 \|\|

*sudarśanastatputro'pi śivapūjāṃ cakāra ha \|*  
*evaṃ cirataraḥ kālo vyatīyāya munīśvarāḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Sudarśana’s son also worshipped Śiva. O great sages, thus a long time elapsed.

## \|\| 8.13.26-28 \|\|

*evaṃ ca śivarātriśca samāyātā kadācana \|*  
*tasyāṃ copoṣitāssarve svayaṃ saṃyogatastadā \|\| 26 \|\|*

*pūjāṃ kṛtvā gatasso'pi sudarśana iti smṛtaḥ \|*  
*strīsaṃgaṃ śivarātrau tuṃ kṛtvā punarihāgataḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

*na snānaṃ tena ca kṛtaṃ tadrātryāṃ śivapūjanam \|*  
*tena tatkarmapākena kruddhaḥ provāca śaṅkaraḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

26-28. So, once it happened that on a Śivarātri occasion when all observed fast, Sudarśana had his sexual intercourse with his wife and then performed the worship. But before he performed the worship he abstained from taking bath. For this misdeed Śiva was infuriated and said.

## \|\| 8.13.29 \|\|

*maheśvara uvāca \|*  
*śivarātryāṃ tvayā duṣṭa sevanaṃ ca striyāḥ kṛtam \|*  
*asnātena madīyā ca kṛtā pūjāvivekinā \|\| 29 \|\|*

Lord Śiva said:—

29\. O knave, you had sexual intercourse with your wife on the occasion of Śivarātri. Without taking bath you performed the worship. You are an indiscreet fellow.

## \|\| 8.13.30 \|\|

*jñātvā caivaṃ kṛtaṃ yasmāttasmāttvaṃ jaḍatāṃ vraja \|*  
*mamāspṛśyo bhava tvaṃ ca dūrato darśanaṃ kuru \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Because you have done this consciously, be sluggish and insensible. You are an untouchable person for me. Avoid touching me.

## \|\| 8.13.31 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*iti śapto maheśena dādhīcissa sudarśanaḥ \|*  
*jaḍatvaṃ prāptavānsadyaśśivamāyāvimohitaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

31\. Cursed thus by lord Śiva, Sudarśana, the son of Dadhīci, attained insensibility immediately, deluded by Śiva’s. Māyā.

## \|\| 8.13.32 \|\|

*etasminsamaye viprā dadhīciḥ śaivasattamaḥ \|*  
*grāmāntarātsamāyāto vṛttāntaṃ śrutavāṃśca saḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. O brahmins, meanwhile Dadhīci, the excellent devotee of Śiva, returned from the other village and heard the details.

## \|\| 8.13.33 \|\|

*śivena bhartsitaḥ so'pi duḥkhito'bhūdatīva hi \|*  
*ruroda hā hato'śmīti duḥkhena sutakarmaṇā \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. He too was rebuked by Śiva. Hence he felt much distressed. He cried—“Oh I am doomed by this foul deed of my son.”

## \|\| 8.13.34 \|\|

*punaḥpunaruvāceti sa dadhīcissatāṃ mataḥ \|*  
*anenedaṃ kuputreṇa hataṃ me kulamuttamam \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Dadhīci, honoured amongst the good, grumbled again and again—“My excellent family is damned by the misbehaviour of my vicious son.

## \|\| 8.13.35 \|\|

*sa putro'pi hato bhāryāṃ puṃścalīṃ kṛtavāndrutam \|*  
*paścāttāpamanuprāpya svapitrā paribhartsitaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. That son too is doomed. He has taken a vicious slut to his wife.” Thus the son was rebuked by his father who repented.

## \|\| 8.13.36 \|\|

*tatpitrā girijā tatra pūjitā vidhibhirvaraiḥ \|*  
*suyatnato mahābhaktyā svaputrasukhahetave \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. The father then worshipped Pārvatī with excellent rites, sincere devotion and perseverance for the happiness of his son.

## \|\| 8.13.37 \|\|

*sudarśano'pi girijāṃ pūjayāmāsa ca svayam \|*  
*caṇḍīpūjanamārgeṇa mahābhaktyā śubhaiḥ stavaiḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Sudarśana himself performed the worship of Pārvatī with great devotion adopting the path of Caṇḍī worship and with auspicious hymns.

## \|\| 8.13.38 \|\|

*evaṃ tau pitṛputrau hi nānopāyaiḥ subhaktitaḥ \|*  
*prasannāṃ cakraturdevīṃ girijāṃ bhaktavatsalām \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Thus by various means and with sincere devotion the father and the son propitiated the goddess Pārvatī who is favourably disposed towards her devotees.

## \|\| 8.13.39 \|\|

*tayoḥ sevāprabhāveṇa prasannā caṇḍikā tadā \|*  
*sudarśanaṃ ca putratve cakāra girijā mune \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. O sage, by the devotion of their service Caṇḍikā was delighted. She adopted Sudarśana as her son.

## \|\| 8.13.40 \|\|

*śivaṃ prasādayāmāsa putrārthe caṇḍikā svayam \|*  
*kruddhā'kruddhā punaścaṇḍī tatputrasya prasannadhīḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. For the sake of her adopted son she herself implored Śiva. Śiva who was angry had become quite calm. The son also was delighted.

## \|\| 8.13.41 \|\|

*athājñāya prasannaṃ taṃ maheśaṃ vṛṣabhadhvajam \|*  
*namaskṛtya svayaṃ tasya hyutsaṃge taṃ nyaveśayat \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Then realising that lord Śiva the bull-bannered deity was delighted, she bowed to him herself and placed the son on his lap.

## \|\| 8.13.42-43 \|\|

*ghṛtasnānaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā putrasya girijā svayam \|*  
*trirāvṛttopavītaṃ ca granthinaikena saṃyutam \|\| 42 \|\|*

*sudarśanāya putrāya dadau prītyā tadāmbikā \|*  
*uddiśya śivagāyatrīṃ ṣoḍaśākṣarasaṃyutām \|\| 43 \|\|*

42-43. Making the ablution of the son with ghee, Pārvatī handed over to her son Sudarśana the three-stringed sacred thread with a single knot and imparted instruction in Śivagāyatrī consisting of sixteen syllables.

## \|\| 8.13.44 \|\|

*tadoṃnamaḥ śivāyeti śrīśabda pūrvakāya ca \|*  
*vārānṣoḍaśa saṃkalpapūjāṃ kuryādayaṃ baṭuḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Then the brahmin boy performed the Saṃkalpapūjā (worship by mental conception) sixteen times with the five-syllabled mantra with the word Śrī prefixed to Śiva.

## \|\| 8.13.45-46 \|\|

*āsnānādipraṇāmāntaṃ pūjayanvṛṣabhadhvajam \|*  
*maṃtravāditrapūjābhissarṣīṇāṃ sannidhau tathā \|\| 45 \|\|*

*nāmamaṃtrānanekāṃśca pāṭhayāmāsa vai tadā \|*  
*uvāca suprasannātmā caṇḍikā ca śivastathā \|\| 46 \|\|*

45-46. Beginning with ceremonial ablution and ending with the concluding obeisance he worshipped the bull-bannered deity by means of devotional mantras, musical instruments and adorations in the presence of the sages. He recited various names of Śiva. Then the delighted Śiva and

## \|\| 8.13.47 \|\|

*madarpitaṃ ca yatkiṃciddhanadhānyādikantathā \|*  
*tatsarvaṃ ca tvayā grāhyaṃ na doṣāya bhaviṣyati \|\| 47 \|\|*

Caṇḍikā said:—

47\. “Wealth, foodgrain etc. whatever is offered to me shall be taken by you. There is no sin in this action.

## \|\| 8.13.48 \|\|

*mama kṛtye bhavānmukhyo devīkṛtye viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*ghṛtatailādikaṃ sarvaṃ tvayā grāhyaṃ madarpitam \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. In the rites concerning me you will officiate as the chief, especially so in the rites concerning the goddess. Ghee, oil etc., everything offered to me shall be taken by you.

## \|\| 8.13.49 \|\|

*prājāpatyaṃ bhavedyarhiṃ tarhyeko hi bhavānbhavet \|*  
*tadā pūjā ca sampūrṇānyathā sarvā ca niṣphalā \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. When the Prājāpatya rite is performed only one of you shall be there. Then alone is the worship complete. Or everything becomes fruitless.

## \|\| 8.13.50 \|\|

*tilakaṃ vartulaṃ kāryaṃ snānaṃ kāryaṃ sadā tvayā \|*  
*śivasandhyā ca kartavyā gāyatrī ca tadīyikā \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. The Tilaka must be made round. Then you shall take bath. Sandhyā prayers to Śiva shall be offered and his Gāyatrī repeated.

## \|\| 8.13.51 \|\|

*matsevāṃ prathamaṃ kṛtvā kāryamanyatkulocitam \|*  
*evaṃ kṛte'khile bhadraṃ doṣāḥ kṣāntā mayā tava \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. After rendering service to me at the outset, the other rites shall be performed according to the family tradition. When everything is thus performed there shall be welfare. Your blemishes have been excused by me.

## \|\| 8.13.52 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā tasya putrāśca catvāro baṭukāstadā \|*  
*abhiṣiktāścaturdikṣu śivena paramātmanā \|\| 52 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

52\. After saying this, the supreme soul Śiva consecrated his four sons as Vaṭukas in the four quarters.

## \|\| 8.13.53 \|\|

*caṇḍī caivātmanikaṭe putraṃ sthāpya sudarśanam \|*  
*tatputrānprerayāmāsa varāndattvā hyanekaśaḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. After keeping Sudarśana as her son with herself she granted boons to his sons and blessed them in several ways.

## \|\| 8.13.54 \|\|

*devyuvāca \|*  
*ubhayoryuvayormadhye vaṭuko yo bhavenmama \|*  
*tasya syādvijayo nityaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|\| 54 \|\|*

The Goddess said:—

54\. Between you two whoever becomes my Vaṭuka shall be victorious. No doubt need be entertained in this respect.

## \|\| 8.13.55 \|\|

*bhavāṃśca pūjito yena tenaivāhaṃ prapūjitā \|*  
*kartavyaṃ hi bhavadbhiśca svīyaṃ karma sadā suta \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. He who worships you has worshipped me too. You shall perform your duties always, O son.

## \|\| 8.13.56 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*evaṃ tasmai varāndattāssaputrāya mahātmane \|*  
*sudarśanāya kṛpayā śivābhyāṃ jagatāṃ kṛte \|\| 56 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

56\. Thus boons were granted to the noble Sudarśana and his sons by Śiva and Śivā out of pity for the welfare of the worlds.

## \|\| 8.13.57 \|\|

*śivābhyāṃ sthāpitā yasmāttasmāttaṃ vaṭukāḥ smṛtāḥ \|*  
*tapobhraṣṭāśca ye jātāḥ smṛtāstasmāttapodhamāḥ \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Because they had been established by Śiva and Śivā they are known as Vaṭukas. Those who neglect penance are known as Tapodhamas.

## \|\| 8.13.58 \|\|

*śivayoḥ kṛpayā sarve vistāraṃ bahudhā gatāḥ \|*  
*teṣāṃ ca prathamā pūjā mahāpūjā mahātmanaḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. Thanks to the mercy of Śiva and Śivā they expanded in various ways. Their worship at the outset is the great worship of Śiva, the supreme soul.

## \|\| 8.13.59 \|\|

*tena yāvatkṛtā naiva pūjā vai śaṃkarasya ca \|*  
*tāvatpūjā na karttavyā kṛtā cenna śubhāpi sā \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. No worship shall be performed by any person as long as he has not performed Śiva’s worship. If it is performed it does not turn out to be auspicious.

## \|\| 8.13.60 \|\|

*śubhaṃ vāpyaśubhaṃ vāpi baṭukaṃ na parityajet \|*  
*prājāpatye ca bhojye vai vaṭureko viśiṣyate \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. Whether auspicious or inauspicious, the Vaṭuka is not to be eschewed. In the Prājāpatya rite and at the feast a single Vaṭu is considered excellent.

## \|\| 8.13.61 \|\|

*śivayośca tathā kārye viśeṣo'tra pradṛśyate \|*  
*tadeva śṛṇu suprājña yathāhaṃ vacmi te'nagha \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. In the rites of Śiva and Śivā this difference is marked. O intelligent one, hear the same. O faultless one, I shall explain it now.

## \|\| 8.13.62-63 \|\|

*tasyaiva nagare rājño bhadrasya nityabhojane \|*  
*prājāpatyasya niyame hyandhakeśasamīpataḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

*yajjātamadbhutaṃ vṛttaṃ śivānugrahakāraṇāt \|*  
*śrūyatāṃ tacca suprītyā kathayāmi yathāśrutam \|\| 63 \|\|*

62-63. I shall explain in the manner I have heard, the event that happened in the city of king Bhadra near the shrine of Andhakeśa during the daily rite of feasting in the Prājāpatya rite.

## \|\| 8.13.64 \|\|

*dhvaja ekaśca tadrājñe dattastuṣṭena śaṃbhunā \|*  
*proktaśca kṛpayā rājā devadevena tena saḥ \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. A certain banner was given to that king by Śiva who was satisfied with his devotion. The king was addressed too by the lord mercifully.

## \|\| 8.13.65-66 \|\|

*prātaśca vardhyatāṃ rājandhvajo rātrau patiṣyati \|*  
*mama tvevaṃ ca sampūrṇe prājāpatye tathā punaḥ \|\| 65 \|\|*

*anyathāyaṃ dhvajo me hi rātrāvapi sthiro bhavet \|*  
*ityuktvāntarhitaśśaṃbhū rājñe tuṣṭaḥ kṛpānidhiḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

65-66. The banner will be raised and bound in the morning. It will fall down in the night. This will happen when the Prājāpatya rite is full and complete. Otherwise this banner will remain steady even during the night. After saying this to the king, the delighted Śiva, the storehouse of mercy, vanished.

## \|\| 8.13.67 \|\|

*tatheti niyamaścāsīttasya rājño mahāmune \|*  
*prājāpatyaṃ kṛtaṃ nityaṃ śivapūjāvidhānata \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. O great sage, the king adopted the procedure accordingly. The Prājāpatya rite was performed every day in accordance with the rules of Śiva-worship.

## \|\| 8.13.68 \|\|

*svayaṃ prātarvivarde्dheta dhvajaḥ sāyaṃ patediti \|*  
*yadi kāryaṃ ca sampūrṇaṃ jātaṃ caiva bhavediha \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. If the rites became full and complete, the banner rose up itself in the morning and fell down during the night.

## \|\| 8.13.69 \|\|

*ekasminsamaye cātra baṭoḥ kāryaṃ purā hyabhūt \|*  
*dhvajaḥ sa patito vai hi brahmabhojaṃ vināpi hi \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. On one occasion the function of the Vaṭu took place in the beginning. The banner then fell even before the feast.

## \|\| 8.13.70 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā tacca tadā tatra pṛṣṭā rājñā ca paṇḍitāḥ \|*  
*bhuñjate brāhmaṇā hyatra notthito vai dhvajastviti \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. On seeing it there, the learned men were asked by the king. The brahmins are at their feast. Why is the banner not flying?

## \|\| 8.13.71 \|\|

*kathaṃ ca patitaḥ so'tra brāhmaṇā brūta satyataḥ \|*  
*te pṛṣṭāśca tadā procurbrāhmaṇāḥ paṇḍitottamāḥ \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. How did it fall down? O brahmins, tell me the truth. Thus asked the brahmins, the excellent scholars, said.

## \|\| 8.13.72 \|\|

*brahmabhoje mahārāja vaṭuko bhojitaḥ purā \|*  
*caṇḍīputraśśivastuṣṭastasmācca patito dhvajaḥ \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. “O great king, while feasting the brahmins Vaṭuka the son of Caṇḍī was fed at the outset. Śiva was satisfied. Hence the banner fell down.”

## \|\| 8.13.73 \|\|

*tacchrutvā nṛpatisso'tha janāścānye 'pi sarvaśaḥ \|*  
*abhavanvismitāstatra praśaṃsāṃ cakrire tataḥ \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. On hearing it, the king and the people were surprised. They praised them.

## \|\| 8.13.74 \|\|

*evaṃ ca mahimā teṣāṃ varddhitaḥ śaṅkareṇa hi \|*  
*tasmācca vaṭukāḥ śreṣṭhāḥ purā vidbhiḥ prakīrtitāḥ \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. Thus the greatness of the Vaṭukas was enhanced by Śiva. The Vaṭukas are glorified as excellent ritualists by those who know things of yore.

## \|\| 8.13.75 \|\|

*śivapūjā tu taiḥ pūrvamuttāryyā nānyathā punaḥ \|*  
*anyeṣāṃ nādhikāro'sti śivasya vacanādiha \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. At first Śiva’s worship shall be performed by the Baṭukas alone. Not otherwise. None else is entitled to the worship as Śiva has said.

## \|\| 8.13.76 \|\|

*uttāraṇaṃ ca kāryyaṃ vai pūjā pūrṇā bhavatviti \|*  
*etāvadeva teṣāṃ tu śṛṇu nānyattathaiva ca \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. They shall conclude the worship with the remarks, “Let the worship be complete.” This alone is their work. Nothing else.

## \|\| 8.13.77 \|\|

*etatsarvaṃ samākhyātaṃ yatpṛṣṭaṃ ca munīśvarāḥ \|*  
*yacchrutvā śivapūjāyāḥ phalaṃ prāpnoti vai naraḥ \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. O great sages, thus whatever was asked has been narrated to you, on hearing which a man obtains the fruit of Śiva’s worship.

## Chapter 14 - The origin of the Jyotirliṅga Somanātha

## \|\| 8.14.1 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|\| \|*  
*jyotiṣāṃ caiva liṃgānāṃ māhātmyaṃ kathayādhunā \|*  
*utpattiṃ ca tathā teṣāṃ brūhi sarvaṃ yathāśrutam \|\| 1 \|\|*

The sages said:—

1\. Please narrate the greatness of the Jyotirliṅgas. Please mention their origin and everything else in the manner you have heard.

## \|\| 8.14.2 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇvantu viprā vakṣyāmi tanmāhātmyaṃ janiṃ tathā \|*  
*saṃkṣepato yathābuddhi sadgurośca mayā śrutam \|\| 2 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

2\. O brahmins, please listen, I shall narrate its greatness and origin succinctly in the manner I have heard from my good preceptor, to the extent of my intellect.

## \|\| 8.14.3 \|\|

*eteṣāṃ caiva māhātmyaṃ vaktuṃ varṣaśatairapi \|*  
*śakyate na muniśreṣṭhāstathāpi kathayāmi vaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. The first among them is glorified as Somanātha. O sage, at the outset, listen to its greatness with attention.

## \|\| 8.14.4 \|\|

*somanāthaśca teṣāṃ vai prathamaḥ parikīrtitaḥ \|*  
*tanmāhātmyaṃ śṛṇu mune prathamaṃ sāvadhānataḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. O great sages, the greatness of Jyotirliṅgas cannot be adequately described even in hundreds of years. Still I shall tell you.

## \|\| 8.14.5 \|\|

*saptaviṃśanmitāḥ kanyā dakṣeṇa ca mahātmanā \|*  
*tena candramase dattā aśvinyādyā munīśvarāḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. O great sages, his daughters, twenty-seven in number, Aśvinī and others, were given in marriage to the moon by Dakṣa, the great soul.

## \|\| 8.14.6 \|\|

*candraṃ ca svāminaṃ prāpya śobhamānā viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*candro'pi caiva tāḥ prāpya śobhate sma nirantaram \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. On securing the moon as their lord, those daughters shone extraordinarily. The moon too, on securing them shone incessantly.

## \|\| 8.14.7 \|\|

*hemnā caiva maṇirbhāti maṇinā hema caiva hi \|*  
*evaṃ ca samaye tasya yajjātaṃ śrūyatāmiti \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. The jewel shines with gold and the gold shines with the jewel. Now listen to what happened in due course.

## \|\| 8.14.8 \|\|

*sarvāsvapi ca patnīṣu rohiṇīnāma yā smṛtā \|*  
*yathaikā sā priyā cāsīttathānyā na kadācana \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Out of his twenty-seven wives he did not love any one so much at anytime as he loved Rohiṇī.

## \|\| 8.14.9 \|\|

*anyāśca duḥkhamāpannāḥ pitaraṃ śaraṇaṃ yayuḥ \|*  
*gatvā tasmai ca yadduḥkhaṃ tathā tābhirniveditam \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. The others became distressed and sought refuge in their father. After going to him they mentioned their misery to him.

## \|\| 8.14.10 \|\|

*dakṣassa ca tathā śrutvā duḥkhaṃ ca prāptāvāṃstadā \|*  
*samāgatya dvijāścandraṃ śāntyāvocadvacastadā \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. On hearing it, Dakṣa too was struck with grief. O brahmins, approaching the moon he calmly spoke to him thus.

## \|\| 8.14.11 \|\|

*dakṣa uvāca \|*  
*vimale ca kule tvaṃ hi samutpannaḥ kalānidhe \|*  
*āśriteṣu ca sarveṣu nyūnādhikyaṃ kathaṃ tava \|\| 11 \|\|*

Dakṣa said:—

11\. O storehouse of digits, you are born of a noble family. How can you cherish oddity in your affection towards your dependents?

## \|\| 8.14.12 \|\|

*kṛtaṃ cettakṛtaṃ tacca na kartavyaṃ tvayā punaḥ \|*  
*vartanaṃ viṣamatvena narakapradamīritam \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. If you have done so, stop with it. This is enough. You shall not repeat it. Oddity in behaviour is said to be conducive to hell.

## \|\| 8.14.13 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*dakṣaścaiva ca saṃprārthya candraṃ jāmātaraṃ svayam \|*  
*jagāma mandiraṃ svaṃ vai niścayaṃ paramaṃ gataḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

13\. After requesting the moon, his son-in-law, thus, Dakṣa returned to his abode fully assured.

## \|\| 8.14.14 \|\|

*caṃdro'pi vacanaṃ tasya na cakāra vimohitaḥ \|*
