*śivamāyāprabhāveṇa yayā saṃmohitaṃ jagat \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. The moon did not act according to his advice being deluded by Śiva’s Māyā which deludes the universe.

## \|\| 8.14.15 \|\|

*śubhaṃ bhāvi yadā yasya śubhaṃ bhavati tasya vai \|*  
*aśubhaṃ ca yadā bhāvi kathaṃ tasya śubhaṃ bhavet \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Welfare befalls him who is destined to have it. How can one destined to court woe have a weal?

## \|\| 8.14.16 \|\|

*candro'pi balavadbhāvivaśānmene na tadvacaḥ \|*  
*rohiṇyāṃ ca samāsakto nānyāṃ mene kadācana \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Being subjected to the force of destiny the moon did not accept his advice. Infatuated of Rohiṇī he neglected others.

## \|\| 8.14.17 \|\|

*tacchrutvā punarāgatya svayaṃ duḥkhasamanvitaḥ \|*  
*prārthayāmāsa candraṃ sa dakṣo dakṣassunītitaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. On hearing it the skilful Dakṣa returned to the moon excessively distressed. He politely asked the moon to mend his ways.

## \|\| 8.14.18 \|\|

*dakṣa uvāca \|*  
*śrūyatāṃ candra yatpūrvaṃ prārthito bahudhā mayā \|*  
*na mānitaṃ tvayā yasmāttasmāttvaṃ ca kṣayī bhava \|\| 18 \|\|*

Dakṣa said:—

18\. O moon, listen. Although requested repeatedly you did not pay heed to my entreaties. Hence, fall a prey to the wasting disease.

## \|\| 8.14.19 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityukte tena candro vai kṣayī jātaḥ kṣaṇādiha \|*  
*hāhākāro mahānāsīttadendau kṣīṇatāṃ gate \|\| 19 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

19\. Just as he cursed him, the moon contracted consumption within a trice. When he was wasted away there was a great hue and cry.

## \|\| 8.14.20 \|\|

*devarṣayastadā sarve kiṃ kāryyaṃ hā kathaṃ bhavet \|*  
*iti duḥkhaṃ samāpannā vihvalā hyabhavanmune \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. O sage, the gods and the sages became extremely agitated and felt miserable—“What shall be done? What will happen now?”

## \|\| 8.14.21 \|\|

*vijñāpitāśca candreṇa sarve śakrādayassurāḥ \|*  
*ṛṣayaśca vasiṣṭhādyā brahmāṇaṃ śaraṇaṃ yayu \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Informed and implored by the moon, Indra and other gods, Vasiṣṭha and other sages sought refuge in Brahmā.

## \|\| 8.14.22 \|\|

*gatvāpi tu tadā procustadvṛttaṃ nikhilaṃ mune \|*  
*brahmaṇe ṛṣayo devā natvā nutvātivihvalāḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. O sage, after approaching him in their great agitation they bowed to and eulogised Brahmā and mentioned everything to him.

## \|\| 8.14.23 \|\|

*brahmāpi tadvacaḥ śrutvā vismayaṃ paramaṃ yayau \|*  
*śivamāyāṃ supraśasya śrāvayaṃstānuvāca ha \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. On hearing their words Brahmā was much surprised. Praising the Māyā of Śiva within their hearing he said.

## \|\| 8.14.24 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*aho kaṣṭaṃ mahajjātaṃ sarvalokasya duḥkhadam \|*  
*candrastu sarvadā duṣṭo dakṣaśca śaptavānamum \|\| 24 \|\|*

Brahmā said:—

24\. O what a pity? This is distressing to the entire world. The moon is always wicked and defiled. Now Dakṣa has cursed him.

## \|\| 8.14.25 \|\|

*sarvaṃ duṣṭena candreṇa kṛtaṃ karmāpyanekaśaḥ \|*  
*śrūyatāmṛṣayo devāścandrakṛtyaṃ purātanam \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Many unholy deeds have been committed by the wicked moon. O sages, O gods, may the early misdeeds of the moon be heard.

## \|\| 8.14.26 \|\|

*bṛhaspatergṛhaṃ gatvā tārā duṣṭena vai hṛtā \|*  
*tasya bhāryā punaścaiva sa daityānsamupasthitaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. The wicked fellow went to Bṛhaspati’s house and abducted his wife Tārā. He then allied himself with the Daityas.

## \|\| 8.14.27 \|\|

*samāśritastadā daityānyuddhaṃ devaiścakāra ha \|*  
*mayā'triṇā niṣiddhaśca tasmai tārāṃ dadau śaśī \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Having sought refuge in the Daityas he fought against the god though forbidden by me and Atri. Then the moon restored Tārā to Bṛhaspati.

## \|\| 8.14.28 \|\|

*tāṃ ca garbhavatīṃ dṛṣṭvā na gṛhṇāmīti so'bravīt \|*  
*asmābhirvārito jīvaḥ kṛcchrājjagrāha tāṃ tadā \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Seeing her pregnant, Bṛhaspati refused to take her back. Induced by us he accepted her unwillingly.

## \|\| 8.14.29-30 \|\|

*yadi garbhaṃ jahātīha gṛhṇāmītyabravītpunaḥ \|*  
*garbhe mayā punastatra tyājite munisattamāḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

*kasyāyaṃ ca punargarbhassomasyeti ca sā'bravīt \|*  
*paścāttena gṛhītā sā mayā ca vāritena vai \|\| 30 \|\|*

29-30. But he said, “If she casts off the foetus I shall accept her.” When they asked her whose child she bore in the womb, she replied that the foetus grew out of the seed of the moon. O excellent sages, I then removed the foetus and urged by me, she was accepted by him.

## \|\| 8.14.31 \|\|

*evaṃvidhāni candrasya duścāritrāṇyanekaśaḥ \|*  
*varṇyaṃte kiṃ punastāni so'dyāpi kurute katham \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. What avails the narration of his previous countless vicious deeds? He still continues to do similar things.

## \|\| 8.14.32 \|\|

*yajjātaṃ tatsusaṃjātaṃ nānyathā bhavati dhruvam \|*  
*ataḥ paramupāyaṃ vo vakṣyāmi śṛṇutādarāt \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. What has happened cannot be reversed. I shall tell you the means to be adopted now. Listen attentively.

## \|\| 8.14.33 \|\|

*prabhāsake śubhe kṣetre vrajeścandrassadaivataiḥ \|*  
*śivamārādhayettatra mṛtyuñjayavidhānataḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Let the moon go to the auspicious shrine at Prabhāsa along with the gods. Let him propitiate Śiva there according to Mṛtyuñjaya rites.

## \|\| 8.14.34 \|\|

*nidhāyeśaṃ purastatra candrastapatu nityaśaḥ \|*  
*prasannaśca śivaḥ paścādakṣayaṃ taṃ kariṣyati \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Let him perform the penance incessantly sitting before the lord. The delighted Śiva will cure him from consumption afterwards.

## \|\| 8.14.35 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*iti śrutvā vacastasya brahmaṇaste surarṣayaḥ \|*  
*saṃnivṛtyāyayussarve yatra dakṣavidhū tataḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

35\. On hearing the words of Brahmā, the gods and sages returned to the place where Dakṣa and the moon were present.

## \|\| 8.14.36-37 \|\|

*gṛhītvā te tataścandraṃ dakṣaṃ cāśvāsya nirjarāḥ \|*  
*prabhāse ṛṣayaścakrustatra gatvākhilāśca vai \|\| 36 \|\|*

*āvāhya tīrthavaryāṇi sarasvatyādikāni ca \|*  
*pārthivena tadā pūjāṃ mṛtyuñjayavidhānataḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

36-37. The gods and the sages consoled Dakṣa and took the moon to Prabhāsa. They invoked the sacred waters of Sarasvatī and other rivers and performed the worship of the earthen phallic image in accordance with the Mṛtyuñjaya rites.

## \|\| 8.14.38 \|\|

*te devāśca tadā sarve ṛṣayo nirmalāśayāḥ \|*  
*sthāpya candraṃ prabhāse ca svaṃsvaṃ dhāma yayurmudā \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. The gods and the sages of pious mind left the moon at Prabhāsa anḍ joyously returned to their abodes.

## \|\| 8.14.39 \|\|

*candreṇa ca tapastaptaṃ paṇmāsaṃ ca niraṃtaram \|*  
*mṛtyuṃjayena maṃtreṇa pūjito vṛṣabhadhvajaḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. The moon performed the penance incessantly for six months. He worshipped the bull-bannered deity with the Mṛtyuñjaya mantra.

## \|\| 8.14.40 \|\|

*daśakoṭimitaṃ mantraṃ samāvṛtya śaśī ca tam \|\| \|*  
*dhyātvā mṛtyuñjayaṃ mantraṃ tasthau niścalamānasaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. The moon repeated the Mṛtyuñjaya mantra a hundred million times. He meditated on the lord Mṛtyuñjaya with his mind unruffled.

## \|\| 8.14.41 \|\|

*taṃ dṛṣṭvā śaṃkaro devaḥ prasanno'bhūttataḥ prabhuḥ \|*  
*āvirbhūya vidhuṃ prāha svabhaktaṃ bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Lord Śiva, who is favourably disposed towards his devotees, was pleased by his devotion and manifesting himself there spoke to him thus.

## \|\| 8.14.42 \|\|

*śaṃkara uvāca \|*  
*varaṃ vṛṇīṣva bhadraṃ te manasā yatsamīpsitam \|*  
*prasanno'haṃ śaśinsarvaṃ dāsye varamanuttamam \|\| 42 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

42\. O moon, may there be welfare to you. Choose your boon, whatever you desire in your mind. I am pleased with you. I shall grant you an excellent boon.

## \|\| 8.14.43 \|\|

*caṃdra uvāca \|*  
*yadi prasanno deveśa kimasādhyaṃ bhavenmama \|*  
*tathāpi me śarīrasya kṣayaṃ vāraya śaṃkara \|\| 43 \|\|*

The moon said:—

43\. O lord of gods, if you are delighted, what is not attainable to me? Still, O lord, let not my body decay by consumption.

## \|\| 8.14.44 \|\|

*kṣaṃtavyo me'parādhaśca kalyāṇaṃ kuru sarvadā \|*  
*ityukte ca tadā tena śivo vacanamabravīt \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Pardon my faults. Remain benevolent to me always. When he had said thus Śiva spoke to him again.

## \|\| 8.14.45 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*pakṣe ca kṣīyatāṃ candra kalā te ca dinedine \|*  
*punaśca varddhatāṃ pakṣe sā kalā ca niraṃtaram \|\| 45 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

45\. O moon, let your digit decline day by day in one fortnight and increase steadily in another.

## \|\| 8.14.46 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*evaṃ sati tadā devā harṣanirbharamānasāḥ \|*  
*ṛṣayaśca tathā sarve samājagmurdrutaṃ dvijāḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

46\. O brahmins, when this happened the delighted gods and the sages came there quickly.

## \|\| 8.14.47 \|\|

*āgatya ca tadā sarve candrāyāśiṣamabruvan \|*  
*śivaṃ natvā karau baddhvā prārthayāmāsurādarāt \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. After coming there they offered benedictory blessings to the moon. They joined their palms in reverence and bowed to Śiva. They prayed to him devotionally.

## \|\| 8.14.48 \|\|

*devāḥ ūcuḥ \|*  
*devadeva mahādeva parameśa namo'stu te \|*  
*umayā sahitaśśaṃbho svāminnatra sthiro bhava \|\| 48 \|\|*

The gods said:—

48\. O great god, O lord of gods, obeisance be to you. O Śiva, O lord, be stable here along with Umā.

## \|\| 8.14.49 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*tataścandreṇa sadbhaktyā saṃstutaśśaṃkaraḥ purā \|*  
*nirākāraśca sākāraḥ punaścaivābhavatprabhuḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

49\. Then the moon eulogised the lord with good devotion first in the Nirguṇa and then in the Saguṇa form.

## \|\| 8.14.50-51 \|\|

*prasannaśca sa devānāṃ kṣetramāhātmyahetave \|*  
*candrasya yaśase tatra nāmnā candrasya śaṃkaraḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

*someśvaraśca nāmāsīdvikhyāto bhuvana traye \|*  
*kṣayakuṣṭhādirogāṇāṃ nāśakaḥ pūjanāddvijāḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

50-51. Śiva was delighted with the gods. For increasing the glory and greatness of the region and the moon, Śiva stayed there in the name- of Someśvara. He became famous in the three worlds. O brahmins, by worship Śiva becomes the destroyer of ailments such as consumption, leprosy etc.

## \|\| 8.14.52 \|\|

*dhanyo'yaṃ kṛtakṛtyoyaṃ yannāmnā śaṃkarassvayam \|*  
*sthitaśca jagatāṃ nāthaḥ pāvayañjagatītalam \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Blessed indeed and contented is he in whose name Śiva the lord of the worlds, himself stayed there sanctifying the entire world.

## \|\| 8.14.53 \|\|

*tatkuṃḍaṃ taiśca tatraiva sarvairdevaiḥ pratiṣṭhitam \|*  
*śivena brahmaṇā tatra hyavibhaktaṃ tu tatpunaḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. A pond was established there itself by all the gods. That was shared by Śiva and Brahmā together.

## \|\| 8.14.54 \|\|

*candrakuṇḍaṃ prasiddhaṃ ca pṛthivyāṃ pāpanāśanam \|*  
*tatra snāti naro yassa sarvaiḥ pāpaiḥ pramucyate \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. It is famous as the moon’s pond on the earth. It is destructive of sins. The man who takes bath there gets rid of sins here.

## \|\| 8.14.55 \|\|

*rogāssarve kṣayādyāśca hyasādhyā ye bhavaṃti vai \|*  
*te sarve ca kṣayaṃ yānti ṣaṇmāsaṃ snānamātrataḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Incurable diseases like consumption are entirely eradicated if a person takes ablutions there for six months.

## \|\| 8.14.56 \|\|

*prabhāsaṃ ca parikramya pṛthivīkramasaṃbhavam \|*  
*phalaṃ prāpnoti śuddhātmā mṛtaḥ svarge mahīyate \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. A pious man attains the fruit of circumambulating the earth, if he circumambulates Prabhāsa. After death he is honoured in heaven by the gods.

## \|\| 8.14.57 \|\|

*somaliṃgaṃ naro dṛṣṭvā sarvapāpātpramucyate \|*  
*labdhvā phalaṃ manobhīṣṭaṃ mṛtassvargaṃ samīhate \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. On seeing this Somaliṅga a person is relieved of all sins. After enjoying the fruits as desired, he attains heaven after death.

## \|\| 8.14.58 \|\|

*yadyatphalaṃ samuddiśya kurute tīrthamuttamam \|*  
*tattatphalamavāpnoti sarvathā nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. He who makes pilgrimage to this place attains the fruits of whatever he might have had in view. There is no doubt about this.

## \|\| 8.14.59-60 \|\|

*iti te ṛṣayo devāḥ phalaṃ dṛṣṭvā tathāvidham \|*  
*mudā śivaṃ namaskṛtya gṛhītvā candramakṣayam \|\| 59 \|\|*

*parikramya ca tattīrthaṃ praśaṃsantaśca te yayuḥ \|*  
*caṃdraścāpi svakīyaṃ ca kāryyaṃ cakre purātanam \|\| 60 \|\|*

59-60. Thus seeing the fruit of that high order, the sages and the gods joyously bowed to Śiva and took the moon now freed from wastage. Circumambulating and praising that holy centre they returned to their abodes. The moon resumed his former activity.

## \|\| 8.14.61 \|\|

*iti sarvaḥ samākhyātaḥ someśasya samudbhavaḥ \|*  
*evaṃ someśvaraṃ liṃgaṃ samutpannaṃ munīśvarāḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. O great sages, thus the details of the origin of Someśa have been narrated to you. Someśvara has originated thus.

## \|\| 8.14.62 \|\|

*yaḥ śṛṇoti tadutpattiṃ śrāvayedvā parānnaraḥ \|*  
*sarvānkāmānavāpnoti sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. He who listens to this origin or narrates it to others attains all desires and becomes rid of all sins.

## Chapter 15 - The origin of the Second Mallikārjuna Jyotirliṅga

## \|\| 8.15.1 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi mallikārjunasaṃbhavam \|*  
*yaḥ śrutvā bhaktimāndhīmānsarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. Hereafter I shall explain the origin of Mallikārjuna on hearing which the devotee becomes intelligent and rid of all sins.

## \|\| 8.15.2 \|\|

*pūrvaṃ cā kathitaṃ yacca tatpunaḥ kathayāmyaham \|*  
*kumāracaritaṃ divyaṃ sarvapāpavināśanam \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. The story of Kumāra has already been mentioned by me. I shall explain it again. It is divine and destructive of all sins.

## \|\| 8.15.3-4 \|\|

*yadā pṛthvīṃ samākramya kailāsaṃ punarāgataḥ \|*  
*kumārassa śivāputrastārakārirmahābalaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

*tadā surarṣirāgatya sarvaṃ vṛttaṃ jagāda ha \|*  
*gaṇeśvaravivāhādi bhrāmayaṃstaṃ svabuddhitaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

3-4. When Kumāra, son of Śivā and the powerful enemy of Tāraka returned to Kailāsa, the celestial sage Nāradn came there and told him all the news such as the marriage of Gaṇeśvara and corrupted his mind.

## \|\| 8.15.5 \|\|

*tacchutvā sa kumāro hi praṇamya pitarau ca tau \|*  
*jagāma parvataṃ kraucaṃ pitṛbhyāṃ vārito'pi hi \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. On hearing it, Kumāra bowed to his parents and went to the mountain Krauñca though forbidden by his parents.

## \|\| 8.15.6 \|\|

*kumārasya viyogena tanmātā girijā yadā \|*  
*duḥkhitāsīttadā śaṃbhustāmuvāca subodhakṛt \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Due to separation from her son, Pārvatī was distressed. Then Śiva enlightened her.

## \|\| 8.15.7 \|\|

*kathaṃ priye duḥkhitāsi na duḥkhaṃ kuru pārvati \|*  
*āyāsyati sutaḥ subhrūstyajyatāṃ duḥkhamutkaṭam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. O beloved, why are you distressed ? O Pārvatī, do not be grieved. O beautiful lady, our son will certainly return. Let not your excessive grief overpower you.

## \|\| 8.15.8 \|\|

*sā yadā ca na tanmene pārvatī duḥkhitā bhṛśam \|*  
*tadā ca preṣitāstatra śaṃkareṇa surarṣayaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. When the distressed Pārvatī did not pay heed to his advice, the celestial sages were sent to Kārttika by Śiva.

## \|\| 8.15.9 \|\|

*devāśca ṛṣayassarve sagaṇā hi mudānvitāḥ \|*  
*kumārānayanārthaṃ vai tatra jagmuḥ subuddhayaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. The gods and the sages, accompanied by their attendants went there joyously to bring back Kumāra.

## \|\| 8.15.10 \|\|

*tatra gatvā ca te sarve kumāraṃ supraṇamya ca \|*  
*vijñāpya bahudhāpyenaṃ prārthanāṃ cakrurādarāt \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. They went there and bowed to Kumāra, implored him in many ways and requested him humbly and respectfully.

## \|\| 8.15.11 \|\|

*devādiprārthanāṃ tāṃ ca śivājñāsaṃkulāṃ guruḥ \|*  
*na mene sa kumāro hi mahāhaṃkāravihvalaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Agitated by great pride Kumāra did not care for the importunities of the gods and sages, intermingled with Śiva’s behest,

## \|\| 8.15.12 \|\|

*tataśca punarāvṛtya sarve te hi śivāṃtikam \|*  
*svaṃsvaṃ sthānaṃ gatā natvā prāpya śaṃkaraśāsanam \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Then they returned again to Śiva. After bowing to him and taking leave of him they returned to their abodes.

## \|\| 8.15.13 \|\|

*tadā ca girijādevī virahaṃ putrasaṃbhavam \|*  
*śaṃbhuśca paramaṃ duḥkhaṃ prāpa tasminnanāgate \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. When he did not return, goddess Pārvatī felt severely the pangs of separation from her son. Śiva too was extremely miserable.

## \|\| 8.15.14 \|\|

*atho suduḥkhitau dīnau lokācārakarau tadā \|*  
*jagmatustatra susnehātsvaputro yatra saṃsthitaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Grief-stricken and distressed following the conventions of the world, out of affection they went to the place where their son was.

## \|\| 8.15.15 \|\|

*sa putraśca kumārākhyaḥ pitrorāgamanaṃ gireḥ \|*  
*jñātvā dūraṃ gato'snehādyojanatrayameva ca \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Coming to know of the arrival of his parents, from the top of the mountain Kumāra went thirty-six kilometres further out of disregard for their love.

## \|\| 8.15.16 \|\|

*krauṃce ca parvate dūraṃ gate tasminsvaputrake \|*  
*tau ca tatra samāsīnau jyo tīrūpaṃ samāśritau \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. When he went further on the Krauñca hill, Śiva and Pārvatī stayed behind assuming the fiery form.

## \|\| 8.15.17 \|\|

*putrasnehāturau tau vai śivau parvaṇiparvaṇi \|*  
*darśanārthaṃ kumārasya svaputrasya hi gacchataḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Out of filial affection they used to visit Kumāra on the concluding day of every fortnight.

## \|\| 8.15.18 \|\|

*amāvāsyādine śaṃbhussvayaṃ gacchati tatra ha \|*  
*paurṇamāsīdine tatra pārvatī gacchati dhruvam \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Śiva goes there on Amāvasyā day and Pārvatī on the Full Moon day.

## \|\| 8.15.19 \|\|

*taddinaṃ hi samārabhya mallikārjunasaṃbhavam \|*  
*liṃgaṃ caiva śivasyaikaṃ prasiddhaṃ bhuvanatraye \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. From that day onwards: that phallic image of Śiva born of Mallikārjuna became famous in the three worlds.

## \|\| 8.15.20 \|\|

*talliṃgaṃ yaḥ samīkṣeta sa sarvaiḥ kilbiṣairapi \|*  
*mucyate nātra sandehaḥ sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. He who sees that phallic image becomes rid of all sins and attains all desires. There is no doubt about it.

## \|\| 8.15.21 \|\|

*duḥkhaṃ ca dūrato yāti sukhamātyaṃtikaṃ labhet \|*  
*jananīgarbhasaṃbhūtaṃ kaṣṭaṃ nāpnoti vai punaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. His misery disappears. He obtains supreme happiness. He never suffers from the distress of rebirth in the mother’s womb.

## \|\| 8.15.22 \|\|

*dhanadhānyasamṛddhiśca pratiṣṭhārogyameva ca \|*  
*abhīṣṭaphalasiddhiśca jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. There is no doubt that he obtains flourishing wealth, food-grains, fame, health and the fruits of cherished desires.

## \|\| 8.15.23 \|\|

*jyotirliṃgaṃ dvitīyaṃ ca proktaṃ mallikasaṃjñitam \|*  
*darśanātsarvasukhadaṃ kathitaṃ lokahetave \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Thus the second Jyotirliṅga Mallika has been narrated. It is the bestower of happiness and the welfare to the world.

## Chapter 16 - The greatness of the Jyotirliṅga Mahākāla

## \|\| 8.16.1 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*sūta sarvaṃ vijānāsi vastu vyāsa prasādataḥ \|*  
*jyotiṣāṃ ca kathāṃ śrutvā tṛptirnaiva prajāyate \|\| 1 \|\|*

The sages said:—

1\. O Sūta, you know everything by Vyāsa’s favour. We are never satiated by hearing about the Jyotirliṅgas.

## \|\| 8.16.2 \|\|

*tasmāttvaṃ hi viśeṣeṇa kṛpāṃ kṛtvātulāṃ prabho \|*  
*jyotirliṃgaṃ tṛtīyaṃ ca kathaya tvaṃ hi no'dhunā \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Hence O holy lord, be kind to us and narrate the third Jyotirliṅga to us now.

## \|\| 8.16.3 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*dhanyo'haṃ kṛtakṛtyo'haṃ śrīmatāṃ bhavatāṃ yadi \|*  
*gataśca saṃgamaṃ viprā dhanyā vai sādhusaṃgatiḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

3\. O brahmins, if I remain in the company of you all, the glorious ones, I am blessed and contented. Blessed indeed is the association of saintly men.

## \|\| 8.16.4 \|\|

*ato matvā svabhāgyaṃ hi kathayiṣyāmi pāvanīm \|*  
*pāpapraṇāśinīṃ divyāṃ kathāṃ ca śṛṇutādarāt \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Hence, considering it my good fortune, I shall narrate the sanctifying sin-destroying divine story. Please listen to this story respectfully.

## \|\| 8.16.5 \|\|

*avaṃtī nagarī ramyā muktidā sarvadehinām \|*  
*śivapriyā mahāpuṇyā vartate lokapāvanī \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. There is a beautiful city Avanti yielding liberation to all souls. It is a favourite of Śiva, highly meritorious and sanctifying.

## \|\| 8.16.6 \|\|

*tatrāsīdbāhmaṇaśreṣṭhaśśubhakarmaparāyaṇaḥ \|*  
*vedādhyayanakarttā ca vedakarmaratassadā \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. An excellent brahmin engrossed in the performance of auspicious rites lived there. He studied the Vedas and performed the Vedic rites.

## \|\| 8.16.7 \|\|

*agnyādhānasamāyuktaśśivapūjāratassadā \|*  
*pārthivīṃ pratyahaṃ mūrtiṃ pūjayāmāsa vai dvijaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. The brahmin maintained sacrificial fires. He was the devotee of Śiva. Everyday he worshipped the idol of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.16.8 \|\|

*sarvakarmaphalaṃ prāpya dvijo vedapriyassadā \|*  
*satāṃ gatiṃ samālebhe samyagjñānaparāyaṇaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. This brahmin Vedapriya was always interested in acquiring perfect knowledge. After securing the fruits of holy rites he attained the goal of the good.

## \|\| 8.16.9 \|\|

*tatputrāstādṛśāścāsaṃścatvāro munisattamāḥ \|*  
*śivapūjāratā nityaṃ pitroranavamāssadā \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. O excellent sages, he had four sons like him. They were not inferior to their parents and they were ever engaged in the worship of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.16.10 \|\|

*devapriyaśca tajjyeṣṭhaḥ priyamedhāstataḥ param \|*  
*tṛtīyastu kṛto nāma dharmavāhī ca suvrataḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. The eldest was Devapriya The next was Priyamedhas. The third was Sukṛta and the fourth was Dharmavāhin of good rites.

## \|\| 8.16.11 \|\|

*teṣāṃ puṇyapratāpācca pṛthivyāṃ sukhamaidhata \|*  
*śuklapakṣe yathā candro varddhate ca niraṃtaram \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Thanks to the power of their merit, happiness increased on the earth even as the moon waxes incessantly in the bright half of the month.

## \|\| 8.16.12 \|\|

*tathā teṣāṃ guṇāstatra varddhante sma sukhāvahāḥ \|*  
*brahmatejomayī sā vai nagarī cābhavattadā \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Their good qualities too flourished delighting and pleasing all. That city was enveloped in the brahminical splendour.

## \|\| 8.16.13 \|\|

*etasminnantare tatra yajjātaṃ vṛttamuttamam \|*  
*śrūyatāṃ taddvijaśreṣṭhāḥ kathayāmi yathāśrutam \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. O excellent brahmins, let the excellent event that happened in the meantime be heard. I shall narrate it in the manner I have heard.

## \|\| 8.16.14 \|\|

*parvate ratnamāle ca dūṣaṇākhyo mahāsuraḥ \|*  
*balavāndaityarājaśca dharmadveṣī nirantaram \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. There was a great Asura Dūṣaṇa on the Ratnamāla hill. He was very powerful. He was the king of the Daityas incessantly engaged in hating virtue and virtuous rites.

## \|\| 8.16.15 \|\|

*brahmaṇo varadānācca jagatucchīcakāra ha \|*  
*devā parājitāstena sthānānnissāritāstathā \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Thanks to the grant of boons by Brahmā he considered the whole universe insignificant. The gods were defeated by him and ousted from their places and positions.

## \|\| 8.16.16 \|\|

*pṛthivyāṃ vedadharmāśca smṛtidharmāśca sarvaśaḥ \|*  
*sphoṭitāstena duṣṭena siṃheneva śaśā khalu \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Vedic rites and rites according to Smṛtis all over the earth were smothered by that wicked fellow like rabbits by a lion.

## \|\| 8.16.17 \|\|

*yāvaṃto vedadharmāśca tāvaṃto dūrataḥ kṛtāḥ \|*  
*tīrthetīrthe tathā kṣetre dharmo nītaśca dūrataḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Whatever Vedic rites were there in the shrines and holy centres were stopped by him. Dharma was exiled to a distant place.

## \|\| 8.16.18 \|\|

*avaṃtī nagarī ramyā tatraikā dṛśyate punaḥ \|*  
*itthaṃ vicārya tenaiva yatkṛtaṃ śrūyatāṃ hi tat \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. “There is a beautiful city Avanti.” Thinking that what the Asura did, hear.

## \|\| 8.16.19 \|\|

*bahusainyasamāyukto dūṣaṇassa mahāsuraḥ \|*  
*tatrasthānbrahmaṇānsarvānuddiśya samupāyayau \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Accompanied by a vast army, the great Asura Dūṣaṇa went there desiring to harm the brahmins staying in the city.

## \|\| 8.16.20 \|\|

*tatrāgatya sa daityendraścaturo daityasattamān \|*  
*provācāhūya vacanaṃ vipra drohī mahākhalaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. After reaching the place, that great knave, that hater of brahmins called four hefty Daityas to him and said these words.

## \|\| 8.16.21 \|\|

*daitya uvāca \|*  
*kimete brāhmaṇā duṣṭā na kurvaṃti vaco mama \|*  
*vedadharmaratā ete sarve daṃḍyā mate mama \|\| 21 \|\|*

The Daitya said:—

21\. Why don’t the wicked brahmins do as I say? All these, engaged in Vedic rites and rituals are worthy of being punished by me.

## \|\| 8.16.22 \|\|

*sarve devā mayā loke rājānaśca parājitāḥ \|*  
*vaśe kiṃ brāhmaṇāśśakyā na kartuṃ daityasattamāḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. O excellent Daityas, the gods and the kings in the world have been defeated by me. Why should not the brahmins be kept in my control?

## \|\| 8.16.23 \|\|

*yadi jīvitumicchā syāttadā dharmaṃ śivasya ca \|*  
*vedānāṃ paramaṃ dharmaṃ tyaktvā sukhasubhāginaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. If they wish to live happily let them abandon the rites of Śiva and the rituals of the Vedas.

## \|\| 8.16.24 \|\|

*anyathā jīvane teṣāṃ saṃśayaśca bhaviṣyati \|*  
*iti satyaṃ mayā proktaṃ tatkurudhvaṃ viśaṃkitāḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Otherwise it is doubtful that they will continue to live. I have said this truthfully. Hence, do that unhesitatingly.

## \|\| 8.16.25 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*iti niścitya te daityāścatvāraḥ pāvakā iva \|*  
*caturdikṣu tadā jātāḥ pralaye ca yathā purā \|\| 25 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

25\. On hearing this and receiving the instruction the four Daityas besieged the four quarters of the city like the fires of dissolution.

## \|\| 8.16.26 \|\|

*te brāhmaṇāstathā śrutvā daityānāmudyamaṃ tadā \|*  
*na duḥkhaṃ lebhire tatra śivadhyāna parāyaṇāḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. On coming to know of the intent of the Daityas, the brahmins were not at all distressed because they were engaged in meditating on Śiva.

## \|\| 8.16.27 \|\|

*dhairyaṃ samāśritāste ca rekhāmātraṃ tadā dvijāḥ \|*  
*na celuḥ paramadhyānādvarākāḥ ke śivāgrataḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Resorting to courage, the brahmins did not move even a line’s breadth from meditation. With Śiva to help who can remain in distress?

## \|\| 8.16.28 \|\|

*etasminnantare taistu vyāptāsīnnagarī śubhā \|*  
*lokāśca pīḍitāstaistu brāhmaṇānsamupāyayuḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. In the meantime, the whole of that auspicious city was overrun by them. The people were harassed and they rushed against the brahmins.

## \|\| 8.16.29 \|\|

*lokā ūcuḥ \|*  
*svāminaḥ kiṃ ca karttavyaṃ duṣṭāśca samupāgatāḥ \|*  
*hiṃsitā bahavo lokā āgatāśca samīpataḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

The people said:—

29\. O holy lords, what shall be done? The wicked Asuras have come. People are tortured. They have come very near.

## \|\| 8.16.30 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*teṣāmiti vacaśśrutvā vedapriyasutāśca te \|*  
*samūcurbrāhmaṇāstānvai viśvastāśśaṃkare sadā \|\| 30 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

30\. On hearing their words, the brahmins, the sons of Vedapriya, placing their faith in Śiva for ever, spoke to them.

## \|\| 8.16.31 \|\|

*brāhmaṇā ūcuḥ \|*  
*śrūyatāṃ vidyate naiva balaṃ duṣṭabhayāvaham \|*  
*na śastrāṇi tathā saṃti yacca te vimukhāḥ punaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

The Brahmins said:—

31\. Listen, we do not have an army to frighten the wicked Asuras. We do not have weapons to drive them back.

## \|\| 8.16.32 \|\|

*sāmānyasyāpamāno no hyāśrayasya bhavediha \|*  
*punaśca kiṃ samarthasya śivasyeha bhaviṣyati \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. The general dishonour and insult reflects on him who supports us. Moreover, what can happen to Śiva who is omnipotent?

## \|\| 8.16.33 \|\|

*śivo rakṣāṃ karotvadyāsurāṇāṃ bhayataḥ prabhuḥ \|*  
*nānyathā śaraṇaṃ loke bhaktavatsalataśśivāt \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Let lord Śiva protect us from the terror of the Asuras. Except Śiva, favourably disposed to his devotees there is no other refuge.

## \|\| 8.16.34-35 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*iti dhairyaṃ samāsthāya samarcāṃ pārthivasya ca \|*  
*kṛtvā te ca dvijāḥ samyaksthitā dhyānaparāyaṇāḥ \|*  
*dṛṣṭā daityena tāvacca te viprāssabalena hi \|\| 34 \|\|*

*dūṣaṇena vacaḥ proktaṃ hanyatāṃ vadhyatāmiti \|*  
*tacchrutaṃ taistadā naiva daityaproktaṃ vaco dvijaiḥ \|*  
*vedapriyasutaiśśaṃbhordhyānamārgaparāyaṇaiḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

34-35. Emboldened thus they performed the worship of Śiva’ś earthern idol. They sat firmly engaged in meditation. On seeing them thus the mighty Dūṣāṇa shouted “Let them be killed. Let them be bound.” But the brahmins, the sons of Vedapriya engrossed in meditation on Śiva did not hear the shouts.

## \|\| 8.16.36 \|\|

*atha yāvatsa duṣṭātmā hantumaicchaddvijāṃśca tān \|*  
*tāvacca prārthivasthāne garttaṃ āsītsaśabdakaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. When the wicked Daitya desired to kill the brahmins, the spot near the Pārthiva idol caved in with a loud report.,

## \|\| 8.16.37 \|\|

*gartāttatassamutpannaḥ śivo vikaṭarūpadhṛk \|*  
*mahākāla iti khyāto duṣṭahaṃtā satāṃ gatiḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. From the chasm thus created, Śiva rose up assuming a hideous form. He became famous as Mahākāla, the slayer of the wicked and the goal of the good.

## \|\| 8.16.38 \|\|

*mahākālassamutpanno duṣṭānāṃ tvādṛśāmaham \|*  
*khala tvaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ hi samīpāddūrato vraja \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. “I am Mahākāla (the great god of death) risen up against the wicked like you. O sinful one, go away, leave the presence of these brahmins.”

## \|\| 8.16.39 \|\|

*ityuktvā huṃkṛtenaiva bhasmasātkṛtavāṃstadā \|*  
*dūṣaṇaṃ ca mahākālaḥ śaṃkarassabalaṃ drutam \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. After saying this Śiva reduced Dūṣaṇa and his army, to ashes immediately with a mere sound of Hum.

## \|\| 8.16.40 \|\|

*kiyatsainyaṃ hataṃ tena kiṃcitsainyaṃ palāyitam \|*  
*dūṣaṇaśca hatastena śiveneha parātmanā \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Dūṣaṇa and a portion of his army too were killed by Śiva the great soul. A part of the army fled.

## \|\| 8.16.41 \|\|

*sūryaṃ dṛṣṭvā yathā yāti saṃkṣayaṃ sarvaśastamaḥ \|*  
*tathaiva ca śivaṃ dṛṣṭvā tatsainyaṃ vinanāśa ha \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Just as darkness is dispelled on seeing the sun, so also that army was destroyed on seeing Śiva.

## \|\| 8.16.42 \|\|

*devadundubhayo neduḥ puṣpavṛṣṭiḥ papāta ha \|*  
*devāssamāyayussarve haribrahmādayastathā \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Divine drums were sounded. Shower of flowers fell. Viṣṇu, Brahmā and other gods came there.

## \|\| 8.16.43 \|\|

*bhaktyā praṇamya taṃ devaṃ śaṃkaraṃ lokaśaṃkaram \|*  
*tuṣṭuvurvividhaiḥ stotraiḥ kṛtāṃjalipuṭā dvijāḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. After bowing to the benefactor of the worlds, the brahmins joined their palms in reverence and eulogised him with different hymns

## \|\| 8.16.44 \|\|

*brāhmaṇāṃśca samāśvāsya suprasannaśśivassvayam \|*  
*varaṃ brūteti covāca mahākālo maheśvaraḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. After consoling the brahmins the delighted Śiva asked the Brahmins to choose the boon they wished to have.

## \|\| 8.16.45 \|\|

*tacchrutvā te dvijāssarve kṛtāñjalipuṭāstadā \|*  
*supraṇamya śivaṃ bhaktyā procussaṃnatamastakāḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. On hearing it, the devout brahmins with palms joined in reverence and heads bent down bowed to Śiva and said.

## \|\| 8.16.46 \|\|

*\|\| dvijā ūcuḥ \|*  
*mahākāla mahādeva duṣṭadaṇḍakara prabho \|*  
*muktiṃ prayaccha naśśaṃbho saṃsārāṃbudhitaśśiva \|\| 46 \|\|*

The brahmins said:—

46\. O lord, the chastiser of the wicked, O Śiva, grant us liberation from the ocean of worldly existence. O Śiva.

## \|\| 8.16.47 \|\|

*atraiva lokarakṣārthaṃ sthātavyaṃ hi tvayā śiva \|*  
*svadarśakānnarāñchambho tāraya tvaṃ sadā prabho \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. O Śiva, you shall stay here itself for the protection of the people. O lord Śiva, save those who see you.

## \|\| 8.16.48 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktastaiśśivastatra tasthau garte suśobhane \|*  
*bhaktānāṃ caiva rakṣārthaṃ dattvā tebhyaśca sadgatim \|\| 48 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

48\. Requested thus, Śiva remained in that auspicious chasm for the protection of his devotees after granting them the goal of the good.

## \|\| 8.16.49 \|\|

*dvijāste muktimāpannāścaturddikṣu śivāspadam \|*  
*krośamātraṃ tadā jātaṃ liṃgarūpiṇa eva ca \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. The brahmins attained salvation. The supporting base of Śiva in the form of Liṅga extended to 3 kilometres in each of the four directions.

## \|\| 8.16.50 \|\|

*mahākāleśvaro nāma śivaḥ khyātaśca bhūtale \|*  
*taṃ dṛṣṭvā na bhavetsvapne kiṃcidduḥkhamapi dvijāḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. O brahmins, Śiva became famous as Mahākāleśvara. On seeing this image no one will have misery even in dreams.

## \|\| 8.16.51 \|\|

*yaṃyaṃ kāmamapekṣyaiva talliṃgaṃ bhajate tu yaḥ \|*  
*taṃtaṃ kāmamavāpnoti labhenmokṣaṃ paratra ca \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. A man worshipping this image with a cherished desire in his mind will attain the same here and salvation hereafter.

## \|\| 8.16.52 \|\|

*etatsarvaṃ samākhyātaṃ mahākālasya suvratāḥ \|*  
*samudbhavaśca māhātmyaṃ kimanyacchrotumicchatha \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. O great ones with good rites, the origin and the greatness of Mahākāla has thus been narrated to you. What else do you wish to hear?

## Chapter 17 - The greatness of Jyotirliṅga Mahākāla

## \|\| 8.17.1 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*mahākālasamāhvasthajyotirliṃgasya rakṣiṇaḥ \|*  
*bhaktānāṃ mahimānaṃ ca punarbrūhi mahāmate \|\| 1 \|\|*

The sages said:—

1\. O most intelligent one, please mention again the greatness of the Jyotirliṅga Mahākāla, the protector of his devotees.

## \|\| 8.17.2 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇutādarato vipro bhaktarakṣāvidhāyinaḥ \|*  
*mahākālasya liṃgasya māhātmyaṃ bhaktivarddhanam \|\| 2 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

2\. O brahmins, listen with interest to the devotion enhancing greatness of Mahākāla, the protector of the devotees.

## \|\| 8.17.3 \|\|

*ujjayinyāmabhūdrājā candrasenāhvayo mahān \|*  
*sarvaśāstrārthatattvajñaśśivabhakto jitendriyaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. In Ujjayinī there was a king Candrasena, a devotee of Śiva, who had conquered his sense-organs and who knew the principles of all Śāstras.

## \|\| 8.17.4 \|\|

*tasyābhavatsakhā rājño maṇibhadro gaṇo dvijāḥ \|*  
*girīśagaṇamukhyaśca sarvalokanamaskṛtaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. O brahmins, the Gaṇa Maṇibhadra, honoured by the people, the chief of the Gaṇas of Śiva, was a friend of that king.

## \|\| 8.17.5 \|\|

*ekadā sa gaṇendro hi prasannāsyo mahāmaṇim \|*  
*maṇibhadro dadau tasmai ciṃtāmaṇimudāradhīḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Once the liberal-minded Gaṇa-chief Maṇibhadra, gave him the great jewel Cintāmaṇi, out of pleasure.

## \|\| 8.17.6 \|\|

*sa vai maṇiḥ kaustubhavaddyotamānorkasannibhaḥ \|*  
*dhyāto dṛṣṭaḥ śruto vāpi maṃgalaṃ yacchati dhruvam \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. The jewel was as brilliant as the sun. It shone like Kaustubha. It blessed auspiciously when meditated upon, heard of or sighted.

## \|\| 8.17.7 \|\|

*tasya kāṃtitalaspṛṣṭaṃ kāṃsyaṃ tāmramayaṃ trapu \|*  
*pāṣāṇādikamanyadvā drutaṃ bhavati hāṭakam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Any thing made of bell-metal, copper, tin or stone touched with its brilliant surface turned into gold.

## \|\| 8.17.8 \|\|

*sa tu cintāmaṇiṃ kaṃṭhe bibhradrājā śivāśrayaḥ \|*  
*candraseno rarājāti devamadhyeva bhānumān \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Wearing that gem round his neck the king Candrasena, dependent on Śiva shone well like the sun in the midst of gods.

## \|\| 8.17.9 \|\|

*śrutvā cintāmaṇigrīvaṃ candrasenaṃ nṛpottamam \|*  
*nikhilāḥ kṣitirājānastṛṣṇākṣubdhahṛdo'bhavan \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. On hearing that the excellent king Candrasena had the Cintāmaṇi round his neck, the kings on the earth were agitated in their hearts due to greed.

## \|\| 8.17.10 \|\|

*nṛpā matsariṇassarve taṃ maṇiṃ candrasenataḥ \|*  
*nānopāyairayācaṃta devalabdhamabuddhayaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. The kings ignorantly trying to rival with him, begged of Candrasena, that jewel secured from the god. They used various means to get the jewel.

## \|\| 8.17.11 \|\|

*sarveṣāṃ bhūbhṛtāṃ yāñcā candrasenena tena vai \|*  
*vyarthīkṛtā mahākāladṛḍhabhaktena bhūsurāḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. O brahmins, the importunity of the kings was rendered futile by Cmdrasena, a staunch devotee of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.17.12 \|\|

*te kadarthīkṛtāssarve candrasenena bhūbhṛtā \|*  
*rājānassarvadeśānāṃ saṃrambhaṃ cakrire tadā \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Disappointed and offended thus by him the kings of all the lands became wrathful against him.

## \|\| 8.17.13 \|\|

*atha te sarvarājānaścaturaṃgabalānvitāḥ \|*  
*candrasenaṃ raṇe jetuṃ saṃbabhūvuḥ kilodyatāḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Then the kings fully equipped with the four types of armies attempted to conquer Candrasena in the battle.

## \|\| 8.17.14 \|\|

*te tu sarve sametā vai kṛtasaṃketasaṃvidaḥ \|*  
*ujjayinyāścaturdvāraṃ rurudhurbahusainikāḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. They joined together, consulted and conspired with one another. With a vast army they laid siege to the four main gates of Ujjayinī.

## \|\| 8.17.15 \|\|

*saṃrudhyamānāṃ svapurīṃ dṛṣṭvā nikhila rājabhiḥ \|*  
*tameva śaraṇaṃ rājā mahākāleśvaraṃ yayau \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. On seeing his city thus attacked by the kings, the king Candrasena sought refuge in Mahākāleśvara.

## \|\| 8.17.16 \|\|

*nirvikalpo nirāhārassa nṛpo dṛḍhaniścayaḥ \|*  
*samānarca mahākālaṃ divā naktamananyadhīḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Without doubts and hesitations, without taking in any food that king of steady resolve worshipped Mahākāla day and night without turning his mind to anything else.

## \|\| 8.17.17 \|\|

*tatassa bhagavāñchaṃbhurmahākālaḥ prasannadhīḥ \|*  
*taṃ rakṣitumupāyaṃ vai cakre taṃ śṛṇutādarāt \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Then the lord Śiva, delighted in his mind, concocted a means in order to save him. Listen to that with attention.

## \|\| 8.17.18 \|\|

*tadaiva samaye gopi kācittatra purottame \|*  
*caraṃtī saśiśurviprā mahākālāṃtikaṃ yayau \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. O brahmins, at that very time a certain cowherdess roaming here and there in that excellent city accompanied by her child came near Mahākāla.

## \|\| 8.17.19 \|\|

*pañcābdavayasaṃ bālaṃ vahantī gatabhartṛkā \|*  
*rājñā kṛtāṃ mahākālapūjāṃ sāpaśyadādarāt \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. She had lost her husband. She carried her babe five years old. With great devotion she watched the Mahākāla worship performed by the monarch.

## \|\| 8.17.20 \|\|

*sā dṛṣṭvā sumahāścaryāṃ śivapūjāṃ ca tatkṛtām \|*  
*praṇipatya svaśiviraṃ punarevābhyapadyata \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. After witnessing the wonderful Śiva-worship performed by him and bowing down she returned to her camp.

## \|\| 8.17.21 \|\|

*tatsarvamaśeṣeṇa sa dṛṣṭvā ballavīsutaḥ \|*  
*kutūhalena tāṃ karttuṃ śivapūjāṃ manodadhe \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. The son of that cowherdess who had watched everything out of curiosity, thought of performing Śiva’s worship similarly.

## \|\| 8.17.22-23 \|\|

*ānīya hṛdyaṃ pāṣāṇaṃ śūnye tu śivirāṃtare \|*  
*avidūre svaśibirācchivaligaṃ sa bhaktitaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

*gandhālaṃkāravāsobhirdhūpadīpākṣatādibhiḥ \|*  
*vidhāya kṛtrimairdravyairnaivedyaṃ cāpyakalpayat \|\| 23 \|\|*

22-23. He brought a fine pebble from somewhere and considered it his Śivaliṅga. He placed it in a vacant place not far from his camp. Various articles were conceived by him as sweet scents, ornaments, cloth, incense, lamp, rice grains and foodgrains in the course of his worship.

## \|\| 8.17.24 \|\|

*bhūyobhūyassamabhyarcya patraiḥ puṣpairmanoramaiḥ \|*  
*nṛtyaṃ ca vividhaṃ kṛtvā praṇanāma punaḥpunaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Worshipping again and again with delightful leaves and flowers he danced in various ways and made obeisance again and again.

## \|\| 8.17.25 \|\|

*etasminsamaye putraṃ śivāsaktasucetasam \|*  
*praṇayādgopikā sā taṃ bhojanāya samāhvayat \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. As his mind was engrossed in the worship of Śiva the cowherdess, his mother called him to take his meals.

## \|\| 8.17.26 \|\|

*yadāhūto'pi bahuśaśśivapūjāktamānasaḥ \|*  
*bālaśca bhojanaṃ naicchattadā tatra yayau prasūḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. When the son engrossed in the worship did not like to take meals though called many times, the mother went there.

## \|\| 8.17.27 \|\|

*taṃ vilokya śivasyāgre niṣaṇṇaṃ mīlitekṣaṇam \|*  
*cakarṣa pāṇiṃ saṃgṛhya kopena samatāḍayat \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. On seeing him seated in front of Śiva with eyes closed she angrily caught hold of his hand, dragged him and beat him.

## \|\| 8.17.28 \|\|

*ākṛṣṭastāḍitaścāpi nāgacchatsvasuto yadā \|*  
*tāṃ pūjāṃ nāśayāmāsa kṣiptvā liṃgaṃ ca dūrataḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. When the son did not come away even after being dragged and struck she threw the image far off and spoiled his worship.

## \|\| 8.17.29 \|\|

*hāheti dūyamānaṃ taṃ nirbhartsya svasutaṃ ca sā \|*  
*punarviveśa svagṛhaṃ gopī krodhasamanvitā \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Rebuking her son who was lamenting piteously the infuriated cowherdess entered her house again.

## \|\| 8.17.30 \|\|

*mātrā vināśitāṃ pūjāṃ dṛṣṭvā devasya śūlinaḥ \|*  
*devadeveti cukrośa nipapāta sa bālakaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. On seeing his worship spoilt by his mother, the boy fell down and lamented, “O lord, O lord.”

## \|\| 8.17.31 \|\|

*pranaṣṭasaṃjñaḥ sahasā sa babhūva śucākulaḥ \|*  
*labdhasaṃjño muhūrtena cakṣuṣī udamīlayat \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. In his excessive grief he became unconscious suddenly. Regaining consciousness after a while he opened his eyes.

## \|\| 8.17.32 \|\|

*tadaiva jātaṃ śibiraṃ mahākālasya sundaram \|*  
*dadarśa sa śiśustatra śivānugrahato'cirāt \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Immediately the camp became a beautiful temple of Mahākāla. Thanks to the blessings of Śiva, that child saw all these.

## \|\| 8.17.33 \|\|

*hiraṇmayabṛhaddvāraṃ kapāṭavaratoraṇam \|*  
*mahārhanīlavimalavajravedīvirājitam \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. The doorway was made of gold. There were excellent festoons at the doorstep. The temple had a shining dais set with costly and pure blue diamonds.

## \|\| 8.17.34 \|\|

*saṃtaptahemakalaśairvicitrairbahubhiryutam \|*  
*prodbhāsitamaṇistaṃbhairbaddhasphaṭikabhūtalaiḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. The temple was equipped with many golden potlike domes, shining jewel-bedecked columns and the floor-paved with crystal bricks.

## \|\| 8.17.35 \|\|

*tanmadhye ratnaliṃgaṃ hi śaṃkarasya kṛpānidhe \|*  
*svakṛtārcanasaṃyuktamapaśyadgopikāsutaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. In the midst, the cowherd’s son saw a jewel-bedecked liṅga of Śiva, the storehouse of mercy, along with the articles he used for worshipping.

## \|\| 8.17.36 \|\|

*sa dṛṣṭvā sahasotthāya śiśurvismitamānasaḥ \|*  
*saṃnimagna ivāsīdvai paramānaṃdasāgare \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. On seeing these, the boy was much surprised in his mind. He got up immediately. It seemed as if he was immersed in the ocean of great bliss.

## \|\| 8.17.37 \|\|

*tataḥ stutvā sa giriśaṃ bhūyobhūyaḥ praṇamya ca \|*  
*sūrye cāstaṃ gate bālo nirjagāma śivālayāt \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. He eulogised and bowed to Śiva again and again. When the sun set the boy came out of the Śiva temple.

## \|\| 8.17.38 \|\|

*athāpaśyatsvaśibiraṃ puraṃdarapuropamam \|*  
*sadyo hiraṇmayībhūtaṃ vicitraṃ paramojjvalam \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Then he saw his own camp as beautiful as the city of Indra. It had been suddenly transformed into one of gold, of variegated nature and shining much.

## \|\| 8.17.39 \|\|

*sontarviveśa bhavanaṃ sarvaśobhāsamanvitam \|*  
*maṇihemagaṇākīrṇa modamāno niśāmukhe \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. He entered the house equipped with everything brilliant and glittering in the night. Jewels and gold pieces were scattered everywhere. He was joyous.

## \|\| 8.17.40 \|\|

*tatrāpaśyatsvajananīṃ svapaṃtīṃ divyalakṣaṇām \|*  
*ratnālaṃkāradīptāṃgīṃ sākṣātsuravadhūmiva \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. There he saw his mother sleeping. She was like a celestial lady with all divine characteristics. Her limbs shone and glittered with ornamental jewelry.

## \|\| 8.17.41 \|\|

*atho sa tanayo viprāśśivānugrahabhājanam \|*  
*javenotthāpayāmāsa mātaraṃ sukhavihvalaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. O brahmins, then that son, the special object of Śiva’s blessings, excited due to happiness awakened his mother immediately.

## \|\| 8.17.42 \|\|

*sotthitādbhutamālakṣyāpūrvaṃ sarvamivābhavat \|*  
*mahānaṃdasumagnā hi sasvaje svasutaṃ ca tam \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Getting up and seeing everything unprecedentedly wonderful, she was as it were immersed in great bliss. She embraced her son.

## \|\| 8.17.43 \|\|

*śrutvā putramukhātsarvaṃ prasādaṃ girijāpateḥ \|*  
*prabhuṃ vijñāpayāmāsa yo bhajatyaniśaṃ śivam \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Hearing everything of the benign favour of the lord of Pārvatī from her son she sent word of it to the monarch who was worshipping Śiva constantly.

## \|\| 8.17.44 \|\|

*sa rājā sahasāgatya samāptaniyamo niśi \|*  
*dadarśa gopikāsūnoḥ prabhāvaṃ śivatoṣaṇam \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The king who had concluded the observance of rites during the night came there immediately and saw the refulgence of the cowherd’s son in propitiating Śiva.

## \|\| 8.17.45 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā mahīpatissarvaṃ tatsāmātyapurohitaḥ \|*  
*āsīnnimagno vidhṛtiḥ paramānaṃdasāgare \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. On seeing everything in the company of his ministers and chief priest the king was immersed in the ocean of great bliss and emboldened.

## \|\| 8.17.46 \|\|

*premṇā vāṣpajalaṃ muñcañcandraseno nṛpo hi saḥ \|*  
*śivanāmoccaranprītyā parirebhe tamarbhakam \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Shedding tears of love and repeating the names of Śiva with pleasure, the king Candrasena embraced the boy.

## \|\| 8.17.47 \|\|

*mahāmahotsavastatra prababhūvādbhuto dvijāḥ \|*  
*maheśakīrtanaṃ cakrussarve ca sukhavihvalāḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. O brahmins, there was a great and wonderful jubilation. Excited with happiness they sang the glorious songs of lord Śiva.

## \|\| 8.17.48 \|\|

*evamatyadbhutācārācchivamāhātmyadarśanāt \|*  
*paurāṇāṃ sambhramāccaiva sā rātriḥ kṣaṇatāmagāt \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. On account of this wonderful event, this manifestation of the greatness of Śiva and the flutter in the midst of the citizens, the night elapsed as though it was only a moment.

## \|\| 8.17.49 \|\|

*atha prabhāte yuddhāya puraṃ saṃrudhya saṃsthitāḥ \|*  
*rājānaścāravaktrebhyaśśuśruvuścaritaṃ ca tat \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. The kings who had besieged the city for an attack heard of this event in the morning through their spies.

## \|\| 8.17.50 \|\|

*te sametāśca rājānaḥ sarve yeye samāgatāḥ \|*  
*parasparamiti procustacchrutvā cakita ati \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Extremely surprised on hearing it the kings who had come there met together and consulted one another.

## \|\| 8.17.51 \|\|

*rājāna ūcuḥ \|*  
*ayaṃ rājā candrasenaśśivabhaktoti durjayaḥ \|*  
*ujjayinyā mahākālapuryāḥ patiranākulaḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

The kings said:—

51\. This king Candrasena is a devotee of Śiva and hence invincible. The king of Ujjayinī, the city of Mahākāla, is never distressed.

## \|\| 8.17.52 \|\|

*īdṛśāśśiśavo yasya puryyāṃ saṃti śivavratāḥ \|*  
*sa rājā candrasenastu mahāśaṃkarasevakaḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. The king Candrasena is a great devotee of Śiva inasmuch as even children in his city observe Śiva’s rites.

## \|\| 8.17.53 \|\|

*nūnamasya virodhena śivaḥ krodhaṃ kariṣyati \|*  
*tatkrodhāddhi vayaṃ sarve bhaviṣyāmo vinaṣṭakāḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. Certainly Śiva will be furious if we offend him. We will be doomed if Śiva is furious.

## \|\| 8.17.54 \|\|

*tasmādanena rājñā vai milāpaḥ kārya eva hi \|*  
*evaṃ sati maheśānaḥ kariṣyati kṛpāṃ parām \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. Hence we shall make an alliance with him. In that case lord Śiva will be compassionate to us.

## \|\| 8.17.55 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*iti niścitya te bhūpāstyaktavairāssadāśayāḥ \|*  
*sarve babhūvussuprītā nyastaśastrāstrapāṇayaḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

55\. Thus deciding, the kings abandoned their enmity. They regained purity of mind. They were pleased. They abandoned their weapons and missiles.

## \|\| 8.17.56 \|\|

*viviśuste purīṃ ramyāṃ mahākālasya bhūbhṛtaḥ \|*  
*mahākālaṃ samānarcuścaṃdrasenānumoditāḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. Permitted and encouraged by Candrasena they entered Ujjayinī, the beautiful city of Mahākāla and worshipped him.

## \|\| 8.17.57 \|\|

*tataste gopavanitā gehaṃ jagmurmahībhṛtaḥ \|*  
*prasaṃśaṃtaśca tadbhāgyaṃ sarve divyamahodayam \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Then they went to the house of the cowherdess. They praised her good fortune with divine blessings.

## \|\| 8.17.58 \|\|

*te tatra candrasenena pratyudgamyābhipūjitāḥ \|*  
*mahārhaviṣṭaragatāḥ pratyanaṃdansuvismitāḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. They were welcomed and honoured by Candrasena there. Seated on a very worthy seat they congratulated him and were surprised.

## \|\| 8.17.59 \|\|

*gopasūnoḥ prasādāttatprādurbhūtaṃ śivālayam \|*  
*saṃvīkṣya śivaliṃgaṃ ca śive cakuḥ parāṃ matim \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. Seeing the shrine of Śiva and the Śiva-Liṅga that rose up, thanks to the power of the son of the cowherdess they fixed their mind in Siva.

## \|\| 8.17.60 \|\|

*tataste gopaśiśave prītā nikhilabhūbhujaḥ \|*  
*dadurbahūni vastūni tasmai śivakṛpārthinaḥ \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. The delighted kings, sought the sympathy of Śiva and presented several valuable things to the cowherd boy.

## \|\| 8.17.61 \|\|

*yeye sarveṣu deśeṣu gopāstiṣṭhaṃti bhūriśaḥ \|*  
*teṣāṃ tameva rājānaṃ cakrire sarvapārthivāḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. The kings made him the chieftain of cowherds in all their different lands.

## \|\| 8.17.62 \|\|

*athāsminnantare sarvaistridaśairabhipūjitaḥ \|*  
*prādurbabhūva tejasvī hanūmānvānareśvaraḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. In the meantime Hanumat, the brilliant lord of monkeys, worshipped by the gods manifested himself there.

## \|\| 8.17.63 \|\|

*te tasyābhigamādeva rājāno jātasaṃbhramāḥ \|*  
*pratyutthāya namaścakurbhaktinamrātmamūrtayaḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. The kings were bewildered at his arrival. They rose up in reverence and made obeisance to him perfectly humble in their devotion.

## \|\| 8.17.64 \|\|

*teṣāṃ madhye samāsīnaḥ pūjitaḥ plavageśvaraḥ \|*  
*gopātmajaṃ tamāliṃgya rājño vīkṣyedamabravīt \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. Worshipped by them and seated in their midst the lord of monkeys embraced the cowherd boy and glanced at the kings and said.

## \|\| 8.17.65 \|\|

*hanūmānuvāca \|*  
*sarve śṛṇvantu bhadraṃ vo rājāno ye ca dehinaḥ \|*  
*ṛte śivaṃ nānyatamo gatirasti śarīriṇām \|\| 65 \|\|*

Hanūmat said:—

65\. Weal unto ye all, O kings and all the souls here, listen. Except Śiva there is no other goal to human beings.

## \|\| 8.17.66 \|\|

*evaṃ gopasuto diṣṭyā śivapūjāṃ vilokya ca \|*  
*amaṃtreṇāpi saṃpūjya śivaṃ śivamavāptavān \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. This cowherd boy fortunately saw the worship of Śiva. Without the use of mantras he worshipped Śiva and attained happiness.

## \|\| 8.17.67 \|\|

*eṣa bhaktavaraśśaṃbhorgopānāṃ kīrtivarddhanaḥ \|*  
*iha bhuktvākhilānbhogānaṃte mokṣamavāpsyati \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. This boy, the most excellent devotee of Śiva, the enhancer of the glory of cowherds, shall enjoy all pleasures here and attain salvation hereafter.

## \|\| 8.17.68 \|\|

*asya vaṃśe'ṣṭamo bhāvī nando nāma mahāyaśāḥ \|*  
*prāpsyate tasya putratvaṃ kṛṣṇo nārāyaṇassvayam \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. In his race, in the eighth generation, there will be a famous cowherd Nanda. Viṣṇu himself will be born as his son, Kṛṣna.

## \|\| 8.17.69 \|\|

*adyaprabhṛti lokesminneṣa gopa kumārakaḥ \|*  
*nāmnā śrīkara ityuccairlokakhyātiṃ gamiṣyati \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. Henceforth this cowherd boy will attain glory all over the world in the name of Śrīkara.

## \|\| 8.17.70 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*evamuktvāñjanīsūnuḥ śivarūpo harīśvaraḥ \|*  
*sarvānrājñaścandrasenaṃ kṛpādṛṣṭyā dadarśa ha \|\| 70 \|\|*

Sūla said:—

70\. After saying this, the son of Añjanā the lord of monkeys in the form of Śiva mercifully glanced at the kings and Candrasena.

## \|\| 8.17.71 \|\|

*atha tasmai śrīkarāya gopaputrāya dhīmate \|*  
*upādideśa suprītyā śivācāraṃ śivapriyam \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. He then initiated the intelligent cowherd child Śrīkara with pleasure in the rites of Śiva pleasing to the lord.

## \|\| 8.17.72 \|\|

*hanūmānatha suprītaḥ sarveṣāṃ paśyatāṃ dvijaḥ \|*  
*candrasenaṃ śrīkaraṃ ca tatraivāntaradhī yata \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. O brahmins even as all of them were glancing at Candrasena and Śrīkara, the delighted Hanūmat vanished there itself.

## \|\| 8.17.73 \|\|

*taṃ sarve ca mahīpālāssaṃhṛṣṭāḥ pratipūjitāḥ \|*  
*candrasenaṃ samāmaṃtrya pratijagmuryathāgatam \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. The delighted kings who were duly honoured took leave of Candrasena and returned the way they had come.

## \|\| 8.17.74 \|\|

*śrīkaropi mahātejā upadiṣṭo hanūmatā \|*  
*brāhmaṇaissahadharmajñaiścakre śambhossamarhaṇam \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. The brilliant Śrīkara, initiated by Hanūmat, propitiated Śiva along with brahmins well-versed in sacred rites.

## \|\| 8.17.75 \|\|

*candraseno mahārājaḥ śrīkaro gopabālakaḥ \|*  
*ubhāvapi paraprītyā mahākālaṃ ca bhejatuḥ \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. The king Candrasena and Śrīkara, the cowherd boy, worshipped Mahākāla with great devotion and pleasure.

## \|\| 8.17.76 \|\|

*kālena śrīkarassopi candrasenaśca bhūpatiḥ \|*  
*samārādhya mahākālaṃ bhejatuḥ paramaṃ padam \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. In due course, Śrīkara and Candrasena propitiating Mahākāla attained the great region of lord Śiva.

## \|\| 8.17.77 \|\|

*evaṃvidho mahākālaśśivaliṃgassatāṃ gatiḥ \|*  
*sarvathā duṣṭahaṃtā ca śaṃkaro bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. Such is the phallic form of Śiva Mahākāla, the goal of the good, the slayer of the wicked in every respect who is favourably disposed towards his devotees.

## \|\| 8.17.78 \|\|

*idaṃ pavitraṃ paramaṃ rahasyaṃ sarvasaukhyadam \|*  
*ākhyānaṃ kathitaṃ svargyaṃ śivabhaktivivarddhanam \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. Thus the great secret, the sanctifying narrative that bestows all happiness, conducive to heaven and enhancing devotion to Śiva has been narrated to you.147

## Chapter 18 - The greatness of the Jyotirliṅga Oṃkāreśvara

## \|\| 8.18.1 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*tvayā sūta mahābhāga śrāvitā hyadbhutā kathā \|*  
*mahākālākhyaliṃgasya nijabhaktasurakṣikaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

The sage said:—

1\. O Sūta, O fortunate Sūta, a wonderful story has been narrated of Mahākāla, the phallic image of Śiva that protects his devotees.

## \|\| 8.18.2 \|\|

*jyotirliṃgaṃ caturthaṃ ca kṛpayā vada vittama \|*  
*oṃkāraṃ parameśasya sarvapātakahāriṇaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. O foremost of the wise, please describe the fourth Jyotirliṅga of the great lord, the destroyer of all sins, in Oṃkāra.

## \|\| 8.18.3 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*oṃkāre parameśākhyaṃ liṃgamāsīdyathā dvijāḥ \|*  
*tathā vakṣyāmi vaḥ prītyā śrūyatāṃ paramarṣayaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

3\. O brahmins, O great sages, I shall describe to you with pleasure how the phallic image of lord Śiva named Parameśa manifested from Oṃkāra. Let it be heard.

## \|\| 8.18.4 \|\|

*kasmiṃścitsamaye cāña nārado bhagavānmuniḥ \|*  
*gokarṇākhyaṃ śivaṃ gatvā siṣeve parabhaktimān \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Once, the holy sage Nārada served with great devotion the phallic form of Śiva named Gokarṇa after going there.

## \|\| 8.18.5 \|\|

*tatassa āgato vindhyaṃ nageśaṃ munisattamaḥ \|*  
*tatraiva pūjitastena bahumānapurassaram \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. From there the excellent sage came to Vindhya, the lord of mountains. He was duly worshipped by the mountain with great honour.

## \|\| 8.18.6 \|\|

*mayi sarvaṃ vidyate ca na nyūnaṃ hi kadācana \|*  
*iti bhāvaṃ samāsthāya saṃsthito nāradāgrataḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. “Every thing is present in me. I am not deficient in anything.” It was with this obsession that he sat in front of Nārada.

## \|\| 8.18.7 \|\|

*tanmānaṃ tattadā śrutvā nārado mānahā tataḥ \|*  
*niśśvasya saṃsthitastatra śrutvāvindhyo'bravīdidam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. On knowing his arrogance, Nārada the dispeller of pride heaved a sigh and stayed there. On hearing that Vindhya spoke again.

## \|\| 8.18.8 \|\|

*vindhya uvāca \|*  
*kiṃ nyūnaṃ ca tvayā dṛṣṭaṃ mayi niśśvāsakāraṇam \|*  
*tacchrutvā nārado vākyamabravītsa mahāmuniḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

Vindhya said:—

8\. “What deficiency do you observe in me that you heave this deep breath?” On hearing these words, the great sage Nārada spoke again.

## \|\| 8.18.9 \|\|

*\|\| nārada uvāca \|*  
*vidyate tvayi sarvaṃ hi meruruccataraḥ punaḥ \|*  
*deveṣvapi vibhāgo'sya na tavāsti kadācana \|\| 9 \|\|*

Nārada said:—

9\. Everything is present in you. But Meru is loftier. He is classified among gods. But you, never,

## \|\| 8.18.10 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā nāradastasmājjagāma ca yathāgatam \|*  
*vindhyaśca paritapto vai dhigvai me jīvitādikam \|\| 10 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

10\. After saying this Nārada left the place and went in the manner he had come. Vindhya was distressed. “O fie upon my life”, he exclaimed.

## \|\| 8.18.11 \|\|

*viśveśvaraṃ tathā śaṃbhumārādhya ca tapāmyaham \|*  
*iti niścitya manasā śaṃkara śaraṇaṃ gataḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. “I shall propitiate Śiva, the lord of the universe and perform penance.” Deciding thus in Ms mind he sought refuge in Śiva.

## \|\| 8.18.12 \|\|

*jagāma tatra suprītyā hyoṃkāro yatra vai svayam \|*  
*cakāra ca punastatra śivamūrtiśca pārthivīm \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. With great pleasure he went to the place where Oṃkāra himself was present. There he made an earthen idol of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.18.13 \|\|

*ārādhya ca tadā śaṃbhuṃ ṣaṇmāsaṃ sa nirantaram \|*  
*na cacāla tapasthānācchivadhyānaparāyaṇaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Continuously for six months he propitiated Śiva. Engaged in meditation on Śiva he never moved from the place of penance.

## \|\| 8.18.14 \|\|

*evaṃ viṃdhyatapo dṛṣṭvā prasannaḥ pārvatīpatiḥ \|*  
*svarūpaṃ darśayāmāsa durllabhaṃ yogināmapi \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. On seeing the penance of Vindhya, Śiva, the delighted consort of Pārvatī, revealed to him his real form inaccessible even to the Yogins.

## \|\| 8.18.15 \|\|

*prasannassa tadovāca brūhi tvaṃ manasepsitam \|*  
*tapasā te prasannosmi bhaktānāmīpsitapradaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Delightedly he said—“Tell me what you wish in your mind. I am delighted with your penance. I fulfil the desires of my devotees.”

## \|\| 8.18.16 \|\|

*vindhya uvāca \|*  
*yadi prasanno deveśa buddhiṃ dehi yathepsitām \|*  
*svakāryasādhinīṃ śaṃbho tvaṃ sadā bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

Vindhya said:—

16\. O lord of gods, if you are delighted, give me such intellect as I may achieve my object. O Śiva, you are always favourably disposed towards your devotees.

## \|\| 8.18.17 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*tacchrutvā bhagavāñchaṃbhuściceta hṛdaye ciram \|*  
*paropatāpadaṃ vindhyo varamicchati mūḍhadhīḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

17\. On hearing that, lord Śiva thought within himself—“This Vindhya of deluded intellect desires for a boon capable of harassing others.

## \|\| 8.18.18 \|\|

*kiṃ karomi yadetasmai varadānaṃ bhavecchubham \|*  
*maddattaṃ paraduḥkhāya varadānaṃ yathā nahi \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. What shall I do? I shall grant him that boor which being auspicious for him may not harass others?”

## \|\| 8.18.19 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*tathāpi dattavāñ śaṃbhustasmai tadvaramuttamam \|*  
*vidhyaparvatarāja tvaṃ yathecchasi tathā kuru \|\| 19 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

19\. Still Śiva granted him that excellent boon “O Vindhya, king of mountains, do as you please.”

## \|\| 8.18.20 \|\|

*evaṃ ca samaye devā ṛpayaścāmalāśayāḥ \|*  
*saṃpūjya śaṃkaraṃ tatra sthātavyamiti cābuvan \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. At this time the gods and the sages worshipped Śiva and said—“You must stay here.”

## \|\| 8.18.21 \|\|

*tacchutvā devavacanaṃ prasannaḥ parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*tathaiva kṛtavānprītyā lokānāṃ sukhahetave \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. On hearing the words of the gods, the delighted lord Śiva did so with pleasure for the happiness of the worlds.

## \|\| 8.18.22 \|\|

*oṃ kāraṃ caiva yalliṃgamekaṃ tacca dvidhā gatam \|*  
*praṇave caiva oṃkāranāmāsītsa sadāśivaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. The phallus image of Śiva known as Oṃkāra is twofold. In the Praṇava, Sadāśiva came to be known as Oṃkāra.

## \|\| 8.18.23 \|\|

*pārthive caiva yajjātaṃ tadāsītparameśvaraḥ \|*  
*bhaktābhīṣṭapradau cobhau bhuktimuktipradau dvijāḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. What is born of the earthen idol became Parameśvara. Both of them yield the desires of the devotees. O brahmins, they yield worldly pleasures and salvation.

## \|\| 8.18.24 \|\|

*tatpūjāṃ ca tadā cakrurdevāśca ṛṣayastathā \|*  
*prāpurvarānanekāṃśca saṃtoṣya vṛṣabhadhvajam \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. The gods and the sages performed his worship then. After propitiating the bull-bannered deity they attained many boons.

## \|\| 8.18.25 \|\|

*svasvasthānaṃ yayurdevā vindhyopi mudito'dhikam \|*  
*kāryyaṃ sādhitavānsvīyaṃ paritāpaṃ jahau dvijāḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. O brahmins, the gods returned to their abodes. Vindhya was much delighted. He realised his object and eschewed his despair.

## \|\| 8.18.26 \|\|

*ya evaṃ pūjayecchaṃbhuṃ mātṛgarbhaṃ vasenna hi \|*  
*yadabhīṣṭaphalaṃ tacca prāpnuyānnātra saṃśaya \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. He who worships Śiva in this manner will not stay in the mother’s womb. He will obtain all the fruits desired. There is no doubt in this.

## \|\| 8.18.27 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*etatte sarvamākhyātamoṃkāraprabhave phalam \|*  
*ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi kedāraṃ liṃgamuttamam \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Thus I have narrated to you the fruit of worshipping Oṃkāra. Hereafter I shall mention the glory of Kedāra, the excellent phallic form of Śiva.

## Chapter 19 - The greatness of the Jyotirliṅga Kedāreśvara

## \|\| 8.19.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*naranārāyaṇākhyau yāvavatārau harerdvijāḥ \|*  
*tepāte bhārate khaṇḍe badaryāśrama eva hi \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. O brahmins, the incarnations of Viṣṇu—Nara and Nārāyaṇa performed their penance in the Bhārata Khaṇḍa in the hermitage of Badarikāśrama.

## \|\| 8.19.2 \|\|

*tābhyāṃ saṃprārthitaśśaṃbhuḥ pārthive pūjanāya vai \|*  
*āyāti nityaṃ talliṃge bhaktādhīnatayā śiva \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Requested by them for their worship, Śiva, being subservient to the devotees comes every day to that earthen phallic image of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.19.3 \|\|

*evaṃ pūjayatośśaṃbhuṃ tayorviṣṇvavatārayoḥ \|*  
*cirakālo vyatītāya śaivayordharmaputrayoḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. A long time elapsed while these two incarnations of Viṣṇu, the spiritual sons of Śiva, performed the worship of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.19.4 \|\|

*ekasminsamaye tatra prasannaḥ parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*pratyuvāca prasannosmi varo me vriyatāmiti \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. On one occasion the delighted Śiva told them—“I am delighted. Choose your boon from me.”

## \|\| 8.19.5 \|\|

*ityukte ca tadā \|*  
*tena naro nārāyaṇassvayam \|*  
*ūcaturvacanaṃ tatra lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. When he said this, Nara and Nārāyaṇa spoke these words keeping in mind the welfare of the world.

## \|\| 8.19.6 \|\|

*naranārāyaṇāvūcatuḥ \|*  
*yadi prasanno deveśa yadi deyo varastvayā \|*  
*sthīyatāṃ svena rūpeṇa pūjārthaṃ śaṃkarassvayam \|\| 6 \|\|*

Nara and Nārāyaṇa said:—

6\. O lord of gods, if you are delighted, if the boon is to be granted by you, O Śiva, stay here in your own form and accept the devotion of your devotees.

## \|\| 8.19.7 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktastu tadā tābhyāṃ kedāre himasaṃśraye \|*  
*svayaṃ ca śaṃkarastasthau jyotīrūpo maheśvaraḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

7\. Thus requested, lord Śiva himself stayed in Kedāra on the Himavat in the form of Jyotirliṅga.

## \|\| 8.19.8 \|\|

*tābhyāṃ ca pūjitaścaiva sarvaduḥkhabhayāpahaḥ \|*  
*lokānāmupakārārthaṃ bhaktānāṃ darśanāya vai \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. He was worshipped by them for helping the worlds and for appearing in the presence of the devotees. He destroys their miseries and terrors.

## \|\| 8.19.9 \|\|

*svayaṃ sthitastadā śaṃbhuḥ kedāreśvarasaṃjñakaḥ \|*  
*bhaktābhīṣṭaprado nityaṃ darśanādarcanādapi \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Then Śiva himself stayed there in the name of Kedāreśvara. By his sight and worship he bestows the desires of the devotees.

## \|\| 8.19.10 \|\|

*devāśca pūjayaṃtīha ṛṣayaśca purātanāḥ \|*  
*manobhīṣṭa phalaṃ tete suprasannānmaheśvarāt \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. The gods and the sages of yore worship here. They get the fruit of their desire from the delighted lord Śiva.

## \|\| 8.19.11 \|\|

*bhavasya pūjanānnityaṃ badaryāśramavāsinaḥ \|*  
*prāpnuvanti yataḥ so'sau bhaktābhī ṣṭapradaḥ sadā \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Since the residents of Badarikāśrama derive their desires every day by worshipping him, he is called the bestower of the desires of his devotees ever.

## \|\| 8.19.12 \|\|

*taddinaṃ hi samārabhya kedāreśvara eva ca \|*  
*pūjito yena bhaktyā vai duḥkhaṃ svapne'ti durlabham \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. From that day onwards, if anyone worships Kedāreśvara with devotion he will not suffer distress even in dreams.

## \|\| 8.19.13 \|\|

*yo vai hi pāṇḍavāndṛṣṭvā māhiṣaṃ rūpamāsthitaḥ \|*  
*māyāmāsthāya tatraiva palāyanaparo'bhavat \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. It was he who on seeing Pāṇḍavas assumed the form of a buffalo, having recourse to his magical skill and began to run away.

## \|\| 8.19.14 \|\|

*dhṛtaśca pāṇḍavaistatra hyavāṅmukhatayā sthitaḥ \|*  
*puccha caiva dhṛtaṃ taistu prārthitaśca punaḥpunaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. When he was caught by the Pāṇḍavas he stood with his face bent down. They held his tail and implored him again and again.

## \|\| 8.19.15 \|\|

*tadrūpeṇa sthitastatra bhaktavatsalanāmabhāk \|*  
*nayapāle śirobhāgo gatastadrūpataḥ sthitaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. He remained in that form in the name of Bhaktavatsala. His head portion went and remained fixed in the city of Nayapāla. The lord stood in that form there.

## \|\| 8.19.16 \|\|

*sa vai va pūjanānnityamājñāṃ caivāpyadāttathā \|*  
*pūjitaśca svayaṃ śaṃbhustatra tasthau varānadāt \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. He asked them to worship him in that trunkless form. Worshipped by them, Śiva remained there and granted boons.

## \|\| 8.19.17 \|\|

*pūjayitvā gatāste tu pāṇḍavā muditāstadā \|*  
*labdhvā cittepsitaṃ sarvaṃ vimuktāssarvaduḥkhataḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. The Pāṇḍavas went away with joy after worshipping him. After obtaining what they desired in their minds, they were rid of all their miseries.

## \|\| 8.19.18 \|\|

*tatra nityaṃ hassākṣātkṣetre kedārasaṃjñake \|*  
*bhāratībhiḥ prajābhiśca tatheva paripūjyate \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. There in the shrine of Kedāra, Śiva is directly worshipped by the Indian people.

## \|\| 8.19.19-20 \|\|

*tatratyaṃ valayaṃ yo vai dadāti haravallabhaḥ \|*  
*hararūpāṃtikaṃ tacca hararūpasamanvitam \|\| 19 \|\|*

*tathaiva rūpaṃ dṛṣṭvā ca sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate \|*  
*jīvanmukto bhavetsopi yo gato badarīvane \|\| 20 \|\|*

19-20. He who makes a gift of a ring or a bracelet after going there becomes a beloved of Śiva. He comes very near the form of Śiva. He is endowed with the form of Śiva. On seeing that form of Śiva, a person gets rid of sins. By going to Badarī forest he becomes a living liberated soul.

## \|\| 8.19.21 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvā rūpaṃ narasyaiva tathā nārāyaṇasya hi \|*  
*kedāreśvaraśaṃbhośca muktabhāgī na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. On seeing the forms of Nara, Nārāyaṇa and Kedāreśvara, undoubtedly he can achieve liberation.

## \|\| 8.19.22 \|\|

*kedāreśasya bhaktā ye mārgasthāstasya vai mṛtā \|*  
*gate'pi muktā bhavaṃtyeva nātra kāryyā vicāraṇā \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. The devotees of Kedāreśa who die on the way are released from rebirth. No doubt need be entertained in this respect.

## \|\| 8.19.23 \|\|

*gatvā tatra prītiyuktaḥ kedāreśaṃ prapūjya ca \|*  
*tatratyamudakaṃ pītvā puna rjanma na vindati \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Going there, with pleasure, worshipping Kedāreśa and drinking the water there a person is released from re-birth.

## \|\| 8.19.24 \|\|

*khaṇḍesminbhārate viprā naranārāyaṇeśvaraḥ \|*  
*kedāreśaḥ prapūjyaśca sarvairjīvaissubhaktitaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. O brahmins, in this Bhārata country people should worship with devotion Nara-Nārayaṇeśvara (Nara-Nārāyaṇeśvara?) and Kedāreśa.

## \|\| 8.19.25 \|\|

*asya khaṇḍasya sa svāmī sarveśopi viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*sarvakāmapradaśśaṃbhuḥ kedārākhyo na saṃśaya \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Although he is the lord of the universe still he is particularly the lord of Bharata. There is no doubt that Śiva Kedāra is the bestower of all desires.

## \|\| 8.19.26 \|\|

*etadvacassamākhyātaṃ yatpṛṣṭamṛṣisattamāḥ \|*  
*śrutvā pāpaṃ haretsarvaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. O excellent sages, I have narrated to you what you have asked for. On hearing this narrative the sins disappear at once. No doubt need be entertained in this regard.

## Chapter 20 - The greatness of the Jyotirliṅga Bhīmeśvara

## \|\| 8.20.1 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi māhātmyaṃ bhaimaśaṃkaram \|*  
*yasya śravaṇamātreṇa sarvābhīṣṭaṃ labhennaraḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

Summary: The greatness of the Jyotirliṅga Bhīmeśvara and the havoc perpetrated by Bhīmāsura.

Sūta said:—

1\. Hereafter I shall explain the greatness of Bhimeśvara on hearing which alone a man obtains all desires.

## \|\| 8.20.2 \|\|

*kāmarūpābhidhe deśe śaṃkaro lokakāmyayā \|*  
*avatīrṇaḥ svayaṃ sākṣātkalyāṇasukhabhājanam \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. With a desire to bless the worlds Śiva himself, the receptacle of welfare and happiness, took his incarnation in Kāmarūpa.

## \|\| 8.20.3 \|\|

*yadarthamavatīrṇosau śaṃkaro lokaśaṃkaraḥ \|*  
*śṛṇutādaratastacca kathayāmi munīśvarāḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. O great sages, I shall tell you why Śiva, the benefactor of the people, took the incarnation. Please listen with respect.

## \|\| 8.20.4 \|\|

*bhīmonāma mahovīryo rākṣaso'bhūtpurā dvijāḥ \|*  
*duḥkhadassarvabhūtānāṃ dharmadhvaṃsakarassadā \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. O brahmins, formerly there was a Rākṣasa of great virility named Bhīma who harassed all living beings and who spoiled virtuous rites always.

## \|\| 8.20.5 \|\|

*kuṃbhakarṇātsamutpannaḥ karkaṭyāṃ sumahābalaḥ \|*  
*sahye ca parvate so'pi mātrā vāsaṃ cakāra ha \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. This powerful demon was born of Karkaṭī from Kumbhakarṇa. He took up residence on the Sahya mountain along with his mother.

## \|\| 8.20.6 \|\|

*kuṃbhakarṇe ca rāmeṇa hate lokabhayaṃkare \|*  
*rākṣasī putrasaṃyuktā sahye'tiṣṭhatsvayaṃ tadā \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. When Kumbhakarṇa, the terror of the worlds, was killed by Rāma, the ogress remained on the Sahya alone in the company of her son.

## \|\| 8.20.7 \|\|

*sa bāla ekadā bhīmaḥ karkaṭīṃ mātaraṃ dvijāḥ \|*  
*papraccha ca khalo lokaduḥkhado bhīmavikramaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. O brahmins, once Bhīma, the wicked tormentor of the worlds, of terrible exploits, while yet a boy asked his mother Karkaṭī.

## \|\| 8.20.8 \|\|

*bhīma uvāca \|*  
*mātarme kaḥ pitā kutra kathaṃ vaikākinī sthitā \|*  
*jñātumicchāmi tatsarvaṃ yathārthaṃ tvaṃ vadādhunā \|\| 8 \|\|*

Bhīma said:—

8\. O mother, who is my father? Where is he? How is it that you are living alone? I wish to know everything. Please tell me the truth.

## \|\| 8.20.9 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*evaṃ pṛṣṭā tadā tena putreṇa rākṣasī ca sā \|*  
*uvāca putraṃ sā duṣṭā śrūyatāṃ kathayāmyaham \|\| 9 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

9\. Asked thus by her son the defiled ogress spoke to her son. Listen, I shall tell you.

## \|\| 8.20.10 \|\|

*karkaṭyuvāca \|*  
*pitā te kumbhakarṇaśca rāvaṇānuja eva ca \|*  
*rāmeṇa māritassoyaṃ bhrātrā saha mahābalaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

Karkaṭī said:—

10\. Your father was Kumbhakarṇa the younger brother of Rāvaṇa. That powerful demon was killed by Rāma along with his brother.

## \|\| 8.20.11 \|\|

*atrāgataḥ kadācidvai kumbhakarṇasya rākṣasaḥ \|*  
*madbhogaṃ kṛtavāṃstāta prasahya balavānpurā \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Once that powerful demon Kumbhakarṇa had been here. He caught me forcefully and enjoyed me.

## \|\| 8.20.12 \|\|

*laṃkāṃ sa gatavānmāṃ ca tyaktvātraiva mahābalaḥ \|*  
*mayā na dṛṣṭvā sā laṃkā hyatraiva nivasāmyaham \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. That powerful demon left me here itself and returned to Laṅkā. That Laṅkā was not visited by me. I have been staying here alone.

## \|\| 8.20.13 \|\|

*pitā me karkaṭo nāma mātā me puṣkasī matā \|*  
*bhartā mama virādho hi rāmeṇa nihataḥ purā \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. My father was Karkaṭa and my mother Puṣkasī. My husband Virādha was killed by Rāma.

## \|\| 8.20.14 \|\|

*pitroḥ pārśve sthitā cāhaṃ nihate svāmini priye \|*  
*pitarau me mṛtau cātra ṛṣiṇā bhasmasātkṛtau \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. When my husband was killed I stayed with my parents. My parents are dead now, reduced to ashes by a certain sage.

## \|\| 8.20.15 \|\|

*bhakṣaṇārthaṃ gatau tatra kuddhena sumahātmanā \|*  
*sutīkṣṇena sutapasā'gastyaśiṣyeṇa vai tadā \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. When they had been there for food, the infuriated disciple of Agastya, the noble Sutīkṣṇa of good penance reduced them to ashes.

## \|\| 8.20.16 \|\|

*sā'hamekākinī jātā duḥkhitā parvate purā \|*  
*nivasāmi sma duḥkhārtā nirālaṃbā nirāśrayā \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. That was why I happened to be alone on this mountain, miserable and helpless.

## \|\| 8.20.17 \|\|

*etasminsamaye hyatra rākṣaso rāvaṇānujaḥ \|*  
*āgatya kṛtavānsaṃgaṃ māṃ vihāya gato hi saḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. It was during this period that Kumbhakarṇa the younger brother of Rāvaṇa came here and had his sexual intercourse with me. He left me alone and went away.

## \|\| 8.20.18 \|\|

*tatastvaṃ ca samutpanno mahābalaparākramaḥ \|*  
*avalaṃbya punastvāṃ ca kālakṣepaṃ karomyaham \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. You were born after that. You have great strength and exploit. Depending on you I spend my days here.

## \|\| 8.20.19 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*iti śrutvā vacastasyā bhīmo bhīmaparākramaḥ \|*  
*kuddhaśca ciṃtayāmāsa kiṃ karomi hariṃ prati \|\| 19 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

19\. On hearing this, Bhīma of terrible exploits was angry and thought “What am I to do against Viṣṇu?

## \|\| 8.20.20 \|\|

*pitānena hato me hi tathā mātāmaho hyapi \|*  
*virādhaśca hato'nena duḥkhaṃ bahutaraṃ kṛtam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. My father was killed by him and so my grand parents. Virādha too was killed by him. Many miseries have been inflicted on us.

## \|\| 8.20.21 \|\|

*tatputrohaṃ bhaveyaṃ ceddhariṃ taṃ pīḍayāmyaham \|*  
*iti kṛtvā matiṃ bhīmastapastaptuṃ mahadyayau \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. If I am the true son of my mother I shall surely harass Viṣṇu. Thinking thus Bhīma went to perform a great penance.

## \|\| 8.20.22 \|\|

*brahmāṇāṃ ca samuddiśya varṣāṇāṃ ca sahasrakam \|*  
*manasā dhyānamāśritya tapaścakre mahattadā \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. With Brahmā as the goal of his meditation he performed a great penance for a thousand years.

## \|\| 8.20.23 \|\|

*ūrdhvabāhuścaikapādassūryye dṛṣṭiṃ dadhatpurā \|*  
*saṃsthitassa babhūvātha bhīmo rākṣasaputrakaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. He kept his arms lifted up; he stood on a single foot. He fixed his vision at the sun. Bhīma the son of Kumbhakarṇa remained like this.

## \|\| 8.20.24 \|\|

*śirasastasya saṃjātaṃ tejaḥ paramadāruṇam \|*  
*tena dagdhāstadā devā brahmāṇaṃ śaraṇaṃ yayuḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. A terrible fiery splendour arose from his head. Scorched by it the gods sought refuge in Brahmā.

## \|\| 8.20.25 \|\|

*praṇamya vedhasaṃ bhaktyā tuṣṭuvurvividhaiḥ stavaiḥ \|*  
*duḥkhaṃ nivedayāṃcakurbrahmaṇe te savāsavāḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. The gods including Indra bowed to Brahmā with devotion and eulogised him with different hymns. They mentioned their miserable plight to him.

## \|\| 8.20.26 \|\|

*devā ūcuḥ \|*  
*brahmanvai rakṣasastejo lokānpīḍitumudyatam \|*  
*yatprārthyate ca duṣṭena tattvaṃ dehi varaṃ vidhe \|\| 26 \|\|*

The gods said:—

26\. O Brahmā, the fiery splendour of the demon has risen to harass the worlds. O Brahmā, grant the boon to this wicked Rākṣasa.

## \|\| 8.20.27 \|\|

*nocedadya vayaṃ dagdhāstīvratattejasā punaḥ \|*  
*yāsyāmassaṃkṣayaṃ sarve tasmāttaṃ dehi prārthitam \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. If not, we shall be scorched by his terrible fiery splendour and court ruin. Hence grant him his prayer.

## \|\| 8.20.28 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*iti teṣāṃ vacaśśrutvā brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ \|*  
*jagāma ca varaṃ dātuṃ vacanaṃ cedamabravīt \|\| 28 \|\|*

Sūta said:-

28\. On hearing their words, Brahmā the grandfather of the universe went there to grant him the boon and spoke thus.

## \|\| 8.20.29 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*prasanno'smi varaṃ brūhi yatte manasi vartate \|*  
*iti śrutvā vidhervākyamabravīdrākṣaso hi saḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

Brahmā said:—

29\. “I am delighted with you. Mention the boon you desire in your mind.” On hearing the words of Brahmā the Rākṣasa said.

## \|\| 8.20.30 \|\|

*bhīma uvāca \|*  
*yadi prasanno deveśa yadi deyo vara stvayā \|*  
*atulaṃ ca balaṃ me'dya dehi tvaṃ kamalāsana \|\| 30 \|\|*

Brahmā said:—

30\. O lord of gods, if you are delighted, if a boon is to be granted by you, O Lotus-seated one, grant me unequalled strength.

## \|\| 8.20.31 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā tu namaścakre brahmaṇe sa hi rākṣasaḥ \|*  
*brahmā cāpi tadā tasmai varaṃ dattvā gṛhaṃ yayau \|\| 31 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

31\. After saying this, the Rākṣasa made obeisance to Brahmā. After granting him the boon, Brahmā returned to his abode.

## \|\| 8.20.32 \|\|

*rākṣaso gṛhamāgatya brahmāptātibalastadā \|*  
*mātaraṃ praṇipatyāśu sa bhīmaḥ prāha garvavān \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. The Rākṣasa Bhīma who acquired great strength from Brahmā returned to his house puffed up with pride. After bowing to his mother he said.

## \|\| 8.20.33 \|\|

*bhīma uvāca \|*  
*paśya mātarbalaṃ me'dya karomi pralayaṃ mahat \|*  
*devānāṃ śakramukhyānāṃ harervai tatsahāyinaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

Brahmā said:—

33\. O mother, observe my strength. I shall work up a havoc among the gods including their chief Indra and also Viṣṇu who goes to help them.

## \|\| 8.20.34 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā prathamaṃ bhīmo jigye devānsavāsavān \|*  
*sthānānnissārayāmāsa svātsvāttānbhīmavikramaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. After saying this, Bhīma of terrible exploit conquered the gods including Indra at the outset and ousted them from their respective positions.

## \|\| 8.20.35 \|\|

*tato jigye hariṃ yuddhe prārthitaṃ nirjarairapi \|*  
*tato jetuṃ rasāṃ daityaḥ prāraṃbhaṃ kṛtavānmudā \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Then in a battle he conquered Viṣṇu respected even by the gods. Then joyously the Rākṣasa began to conquer the earth.

## \|\| 8.20.36 \|\|

*purā sudakṣiṇāṃ tatra kāmarūpeśvaraṃ prabhum \|*  
*jetuṃ gatastatastena yuddhamāsīdbhayaṃkaram \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. At first he went to conquer Sudakṣiṇa, the king of Kāmarūpa. Then he fought a terrible battle with him.

## \|\| 8.20.37 \|\|

*bhīmo'tha taṃ mahārājaṃ prabhāvādbrahmaṇo'suraḥ \|*  
*jigye varaprabhāveṇa mahāvīraṃ śivāśrayam \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Thanks to the powers of Brahmā and that of the boon, the Asura Bhīma conquered that great king though he was a great hero and had the support of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.20.38 \|\|

*sa hi jitvā tatastaṃ ca kāmarūpeśvaraṃ prabhum \|*  
*babaṃdha tāḍayāmāsa bhīmo bhīmaparākramaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. After conquering the lord of Kāmarūpa, Bhīma of terrible exploit bound him and struck him.

## \|\| 8.20.39 \|\|

*gṛhītaṃ tasya sarvasvaṃ rājyaṃ sopaskaraṃ dvijāḥ \|*  
*tena bhīmena duṣṭena śivadāsasya bhūpateḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. O brahmins, all that belonged to that king who was a servant of Śiva, including his kingdom and its adjuncts was seized by that wicked Bhīma.

## \|\| 8.20.40 \|\|

*rājā cāpi sudharmiṣṭhaḥ priyadharmo harapriyaḥ \|*  
*gṛhīto nigaḍaistena hyekāṃte sthāpitaśca saḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. The virtuous king, fond of sacred rites, a favourite of Śiva, was bound with fetters and imprisoned in an isolated cell.

## \|\| 8.20.41 \|\|

*tatra tena tadā kṛtvā pārthivīṃ mūrtimuttamām \|*  
*bhajanaṃ ca śivasyaiva prārabdhī priyakāmyayā \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. There the captive monarch made an excellent earthen idol of Śiva and started its worship with a desire for pleasant results.

## \|\| 8.20.42 \|\|

*gaṃgāyāstavanaṃ tena bahudhā ca tadā kṛtam \|*  
*mānasaṃ snānakarmādi kṛtvā śaṃkarapūjanam \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. He eulogised Gaṅgā in various ways. He performed the rites of ablutions and the worship of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.20.43 \|\|

*pārthivena vidhānena cakāra nṛpasattamaḥ \|*  
*taddhyānaṃ ca yathā syādvai kṛtvā ca vidhipūrvakam \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. He performed meditation duly in accordance with the rules prescribed for the worship of earthen idols.

## \|\| 8.20.44 \|\|

*praṇipātaistathā stotrairmudrāsana purassaram \|*  
*kṛtvā hi sakalaṃ tacca sa bheje śaṃkaraṃ mudā \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. He joyously worshipped Śiva after performing obeisances, prayers and the like with the mystic signs and poses.

## \|\| 8.20.45 \|\|
