*paṃcākṣaramayīṃ vidyāṃ jajāpa praṇavānvitām \|*  
*nānyatkāryaṃ sa vai kartuṃ labdhavānantaraṃ tadā \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. He repeated the japas of the five-syllabled mantra with the Praṇava prefixed. He did not get any leisure to do any thing else.

## \|\| 8.20.46 \|\|

*tatpatnī ca tadā sādhvī dakṣiṇā nāma viśrutā \|*  
*nidhānaṃ pārthivaṃ prītyā cakāra nṛpavallabhā \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. The king’s beloved wife, the chaste lady, famous as Dakṣiṇā performed the worship of the earthen idol with pleasure.

## \|\| 8.20.47 \|\|

*daṃpatī tvekabhāvena śaṃkaraṃ bhaktaśaṃkaram \|*  
*bhejāte tatra tau nityaṃ śivārādhanatatparau \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. The couple, devoutly engaged in the propitiation of Śiva worshipped the deity, the benefactor of the devotees with single-mindedness.

## \|\| 8.20.48 \|\|

*rākṣaso yajñakarmādi varadarpa vimohitaḥ \|*  
*lopayāmāsa tatsarvaṃ mahyaṃ vai dīyatāmiti \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. But the Rākṣasa deluded by the arrogance due to the boon spoiled all sacrificial rites saying, “Let the offerings be made to me.”

## \|\| 8.20.49 \|\|

*bahusainyasamāyukto rākṣasānāṃ durātmanāma \|*  
*cakāra vasudhāṃ sarvāṃ svavaśe carṣisattamāḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. O excellent sages, with a huge army of wicked Rākṣasas he put the entire earth under his control.

## \|\| 8.20.50 \|\|

*vedadharmaṃ śāstradharmaṃ smṛtidharmaṃ purāṇajam \|*  
*lopayitvā ca tatsarvaṃ bubhuje svayamūrjitaḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Defiling the Vedic rites and the sacred rituals of Śāstras, Smṛtis and Purāṇas, the powerful demon enjoyed everything himself.

## \|\| 8.20.51 \|\|

*devāśca pīḍitāstena saśakrā ṛṣayastathā \|*  
*atyantaṃ duḥkhamāpannā lokānnissāritā dvijāḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. The gods including Indra were harassed by him. The sages were subjected to misery, Brahmins were ousted from the world.

## \|\| 8.20.52 \|\|

*te tato vikalāssarve savāsavasurarṣayaḥ \|*  
*brahmaviṣṇū purodhāya śaṃkaraṃ śaraṇaṃ yayuḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Then the dejected gods and sages including Indra, with Brahmā and Viṣṇu at their head, sought refuge in Śiva.

## \|\| 8.20.53 \|\|

*stutvā stotrairanekaiśca śaṃkaraṃ loka śaṃkaram \|*  
*prasannaṃ kṛtavaṃtaste mahākośyāstaṭe śubhe \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. On the auspicious banks of the Mahākośī they delighted Śiva, the benefactor of the worlds after eulogising him with various prayers.

## \|\| 8.20.54 \|\|

*kṛtvā ca pārthivīṃ mūrtiṃ pūjayitvā vidhānataḥ \|*  
*tuṣṭuvurvividhaiḥ stotrairnamaskārādibhiḥ kramāt \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. They made the earthen idol and performed worship in accordance with the rules and eulogised him with different prayers and obeisances duly.

## \|\| 8.20.55 \|\|

*evaṃ stutastadā śaṃbhurdevānāṃ stavanādibhiḥ \|*  
*suprasannataro bhūtvā tānsurānidamabravīt \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Thus eulogised by means of prayers, by the gods, Śiva was much delighted and spoke to the gods.

## \|\| 8.20.56 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*he hare he vidhe devā ṛṣayaścākhilā aham \|*  
*prasannosmi varaṃ brūta kiṃ kāryaṃ karavāṇi vaḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

56\. O Viṣṇu, O Brahmā, O gods, O sages, I am delighted, mention the boon you wish to have. What shall I do for you?

## \|\| 8.20.57 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityukte ca tadā tena śivena vacane dvijāḥ \|*  
*supraṇamya karau baddhvā devaḥ ūcuśśivaṃ tadā \|\| 57 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

57\. O brahmins, when these words were mentioned by Śiva, the gods spoke to Śiva after bowing to him with palms joined in reverence.

## \|\| 8.20.58 \|\|

*devā ūcuḥ \|*  
*sarvaṃ jānāsi deveśa sarveṣāṃ manasi sthitam \|*  
*antaryāmī ca sarvasya nājñātaṃ vidyate tava \|\| 58 \|\|*

The gods said:—

58\. O lord of gods, you know everything in the mind of others. You are the immanent soul. There is nothing unknown to you.

## \|\| 8.20.59 \|\|

*tathāpi śrūyatāṃ nātha svaduḥkhaṃ brūmahe vayam \|*  
*tvadājñayā mahādeva kṛpādṛṣṭyā vilokaya \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. Still at your behest, O lord, we shall mention our grief. Be pleased to hear. O lord, look at us with your merciful eye.

## \|\| 8.20.60 \|\|

*rākṣasaḥ karkaṭīputraḥ kuṃbhakarṇodbhavo balī \|*  
*pīḍayatyaniśaṃ devānbrahmadattavarorjitaḥ \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. The Rākṣasa, the powerful son of Karkaṭī, begot by Kumbhakarṇa, harasses the gods always, with his strength enhanced by the boon granted by Brahmā.

## \|\| 8.20.61 \|\|

*tamimaṃ jahi bhīmāhvaṃ rākṣasaṃ duḥkhadāyakam \|*  
*kṛpāṃ kuru maheśāna vilaṃbaṃ na kuru prabho \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. Please kill this Rākṣasa who causes much misery. O great lord, be merciful. O lord, do not delay.

## \|\| 8.20.62 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktastu suraissarvaiśśaṃbhuveṃ bhaktavatsalaḥ \|*  
*vadhaṃ tasya kariṣyāmītyuktvā devāṃstato'bravīt \|\| 62 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

62\. Thus requested by the gods, Śiva favourably disposed to his devotees said—“I shall slay him.” He then spoke to the gods.

## \|\| 8.20.63 \|\|

*śaṃbhuruvāca \|*  
*kāmarūpeśvaro rājā madīyo bhakta uttamaḥ \|*  
*tasmai brūteti vai devāḥ kāryyaṃ śīghraṃ bhaviṣyati \|\| 63 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

63\. The king of Kāmarūpa is my excellent devotee. O gods, tell him that his task will soon be accomplished by me.

## \|\| 8.20.64 \|\|

*sudakṣiṇa mahārāja kāma rūpeśvara prabho \|*  
*madbhaktastvaṃ viśeṣeṇa kuru madbhajanaṃ rateḥ \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. The great king Sudakṣiṇa, lord of Kāmarūpa, is a special devotee of mine. Let him continue my worship interestedly.

## \|\| 8.20.65 \|\|

*daityaṃ bhīmāhvayaṃ duṣṭaṃ brahmaprāptavarorjitam \|*  
*haniṣyāmi na saṃdehastvattiraskārakāriṇam \|\| 65 \|\|*

65.. I shall kill Bhīma who is wicked, whose power is enhanced by the boon granted by Brahmā and who has insulted you.

## \|\| 8.20.66 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*atha te nirjarāssarve tatra gatvā mudānvitāḥ \|*  
*tasmai mahānṛpāyocuryaduktaṃ śaṃbhunā ca tat \|\| 66 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

66\. Then the gods went there. The delighted gods informed the great king what lord Śiva had said.

## \|\| 8.20.67 \|\|

*tamityuktvā ca vai devā ānaṃdaṃ paramaṃ gatāḥ \|*  
*maharṣayaśca te sarve yayuśśīpraṃ nijāśramān \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. After informing him thus, the gods were highly delighted. The sages then returned to their respective abodes.

## Chapter 21 - The origin of the Jyotirliṅga Bhīmeśvara

## \|\| 8.21.1 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*śivo'pi ca gaṇaissārddhaṃ jagāma hitakāmyayā \|*  
*svabhaktanikaṭaṃ guptastasthau rakṣārthamādarāt \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. With a desire to accord benefit to him Śiva stayed with his devotee along with his Gaṇas. In order to protect him he remained concealed there.

## \|\| 8.21.2 \|\|

*etasminnantare tatra kāmarūpeśvareṇa ca \|*  
*atyaṃtaṃ dhyānamārabdhaṃ pārthivasya purastadā \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. In the meantime a great meditation was started by the lord of Kāmarūpa in front of the earthen idol of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.21.3 \|\|

*kenacittatra gatvā ca rākṣasāya niveditam \|*  
*rājā kiṃcitkarotyevaṃ tvadarthaṃ hyābhicārikam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Then somebody informed the Rākṣasa, “The king is performing a black magic against you.”

## \|\| 8.21.4 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*rākṣasassa ca tacchutvā kruddhastaddhananecchayā \|*  
*gṛhītvā karavālaṃ ca jagāma nṛpatiṃ prati \|\| 4 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

4\. On hearing it the Rākṣasa was infuriated. With a desire to kill him he seized a sword and rushed at the king.

## \|\| 8.21.5-6 \|\|

*taddṛṣṭvā rākṣasastatra pārthivādi sthitaṃ ca yat \|*  
*tadarthaṃ tatsvarūpaṃ ca dṛṣṭvā kiṃcitkarotyasau \|\| 5 \|\|*

*ata enaṃ balādadya hanmi sopaskaraṃ nṛpam \|*  
*vicāryeti mahākruddho rākṣasaḥ prāha taṃ nṛpam \|\| 6 \|\|*

5-6. On seeing the earthen idol of Śiva, the Rākṣasa thought “Contemplating on this idol he is doing something. Hence I shall kill him along with his adjuncts.” Thinking thus the extremely infuriated Rākṣasa spoke thus.

## \|\| 8.21.7 \|\|

*bhīma uvāca \|*  
*rere pārthiva duṣṭātmankriyate kiṃ tvayādhunā \|*  
*satyaṃ vada na hanyāṃ tvāmanyathā hanmi niścitam \|\| 7 \|\|*

Bhīma said:—

7\. O wicked king, what are you doing here? Tell me the truth. I shall not kill you then. Otherwise I will certainly kill you.

## \|\| 8.21.8 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*iti śrutvā vacastasya kāmarūpeśvaraśca saḥ \|*  
*manasīti cicintāśu śivaviśvāsapūritaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

8\. On hearing his words, the lord of Kāmarūpa full of faith in Śiva thought within his mind thus.

## \|\| 8.21.9 \|\|

*bhaviṣyaṃ yadbhavatyeva nāsti tasya nivartakaḥ \|*  
*prārabdhādhīnamevātra prārabdhassa śivaḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. What is destined to happen will happen. There is nothing to prevent it. Everything is determined by our own previous action and Śiva is the dispenser of the result.

## \|\| 8.21.10 \|\|

*kṛpāluśśaṃkaraścātra pārthive vartate dhruvam \|*  
*madarthaṃ na karotīha kutaḥ koyaṃ ca rākṣasaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. The merciful lord is certainly present in this earthen idol. He is carrying out my task. Who is this Rākṣasa? Whence has he come?

## \|\| 8.21.11 \|\|

*svānurūpāṃ pratijñāṃ sa satyaṃ caiva kariṣyati \|*  
*satyapratijño bhagavāñchivaśceti śrutau śrutaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Lord Śiva is reputed in the Vedas as true to his promise. He carries out his promises in the interest of his devotees.

## \|\| 8.21.12 \|\|

*mama bhaktaṃ yadā kaścitpīḍayatyatidāruṇaḥ \|*  
*tadāhaṃ tasya rakṣārthaṃ duṣṭaṃ hanmi na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. The promise is “If and when any terrible person harasses my devotee I shall kill that wicked person in order to save him. There is no doubt in this.”

## \|\| 8.21.13 \|\|

*evaṃ dhairyyaṃ samālaṃbya dhyātvā devaṃ ca śaṃkaram \|*  
*prārthayāmāsa sadbhaktyā manasaiva raseśvaraḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Thus emboldened and meditating on lord Śiva the king reminded him of the promise, mentally and devotedly.

## \|\| 8.21.14 \|\|

*tvadīyo'smi mahārāja yathecchasi tathā kuru \|*  
*satyaṃ ca vacanaṃ hyatra bravīmi kuru me hitam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. “O lord, I am yours. Do as you wish. I will tell him the truth. Do what is beneficial to me,”

## \|\| 8.21.15 \|\|

*evaṃ manasi sa dhyātvā satyapāśena maṃtritaḥ \|*  
*prāha satyaṃ vaco rājā rākṣasaṃ cāvamānayan \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Thus meditating in his mind, the king bound by the noose of truth spoke the truth slighting the Rākṣasa.

## \|\| 8.21.16 \|\|

*nṛpa uvāca \|*  
*bhajāmi śaṃkaraṃ devaṃ svabhaktaparipālakam \|*  
*carācarāṇāṃ sarveṣāmīśvaraṃ nirvikārakam \|\| 16 \|\|*

The king said:—

16\. I am worshipping lord Śiva who protects his devotees, who is the lord of all beings—mobile or immobile and who is free from aberrations.

## \|\| 8.21.17 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*iti tasya vacaḥ śrutvā kāmarūpeśvarasya saḥ \|*  
*krodhena pracaladgātro bhīmo vacanamabravīt \|\| 17 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

17\. On hearing these words of the king of Kāmarūpa, Bhīma spoke thunderingly, his body shaking with rage.

## \|\| 8.21.18 \|\|

*\|\| bhīma uvāca \|*  
*śaṃkaraste mayā jñātaḥ kiṃ kariṣyati vai mama \|*  
*yo me pitṛvyakenaiva sthāpitaḥ kiṃkaro yathā \|\| 18 \|\|*

Bhīma said:—

18\. Your Śiva is known to me? What harm can he do to me? He was kept as a servant by my uncle.

## \|\| 8.21.19 \|\|

*tadbalaṃ hi samāśritya vijetuṃ tvaṃ samīhase \|*  
*tarhi tvayā jitaṃ sarvaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. You wish to conquer me depending on his strength. Then you have conquered everything, indeed. No need for any hesitation.

## \|\| 8.21.20 \|\|

*yāvanmayā na dṛṣṭo hi śaṃkarastvatprapālakaḥ \|*  
*tāvattvaṃ svāminaṃ matvā sevase nānyathā kvacit \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. As long as Śiva your protector is not seen by me you can serve him as your lord and not otherwise.

## \|\| 8.21.21 \|\|

*mayā dṛṣṭe ca tatsarvaṃ sphuṭaṃ syātsarvathā nṛpa \|*  
*tasmāttvaṃ vai śivasyedaṃ rūpaṃ dūrataraṃ kuru \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. If he is seen by me, O king, everything will be clear in every respect. Hence you remove all these idols of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.21.22 \|\|

*anyathā hi bhayaṃ te'dya bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*svāminaste karaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ dāsye'haṃ bhīmavikramaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Otherwise great terror will befall you. There is no doubt about it. I am of terrible exploit and I shall lay my hands heavily on your lord.

## \|\| 8.21.23 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā kāmarūpeśvaro nṛpaḥ \|*  
*dṛḍhaṃ śaṃkaraviśvāso drutaṃ vākyamuvāca tam \|\| 23 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

23\. On hearing his words, the king of Kāmarūpa who had steady faith in Śiva immediately spoke to the Rākṣasa.

## \|\| 8.21.24 \|\|

*rājovāca \|*  
*ahaṃ ca pāmaro duṣṭo na mokṣye śaṃkaraṃ punaḥ \|*  
*sarvotkṛṣṭaśca me svāmī na māṃ muṃcati karhicit \|\| 24 \|\|*

The king said:—

24\. I am a wicked ignorant brute in your eyes but I shall never forsake Śiva. My lord who is the most excellent of all will never forsake me.

## \|\| 8.21.25 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*evaṃ vacastadā śrutvā tasya rājñaśśivātmanaḥ \|*  
*taṃ prahasya drutaṃ bhīmo bhūpatiṃ rākṣaso'bravīt \|\| 25 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

25\. On hearing the words of the king with his soul dedicated to Śiva, the Rākṣasa laughed and spoke to the king immediately.

## \|\| 8.21.26-27 \|\|

*\|\| bhīma uvāca \|*  
*matto bhikṣayate nityaṃ sa kiṃ jānāti svākṛtim \|*  
*yogināṃ kā ca niṣṭhā vai bhaktānāṃ pratipālane \|\| 26 \|\|*

*iti kṛtvā matiṃ tvaṃ ca dūrato bhava sarvathā \|*  
*ahaṃ ca tava sa svāmī yuddhaṃ vai karavāvahe \|\| 27 \|\|*

Bhīma said:—

26-27. “He is intoxicated. He begs everyday. What does he know about his features? What steadfastness can a Yogi have in affording protection to his devotees?” Think thus and keep aloof in every respect. Your lord and I shall proceed with our fight.

## \|\| 8.21.28 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktasya nṛpaśreṣṭhaśśaṃbhubhakto dṛḍhavrataḥ \|*  
*pratyuvācābhayo bhīmaṃ duḥkhadaṃ jagatāṃ sadā \|\| 28 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

28\. Thus ridiculed, the excellent king, a devotee of Śiva, steadfast in his rites, spoke fearlessly to Bhīma who caused misery to the people.

## \|\| 8.21.29 \|\|

*rājovāca \|*  
*śṛṇu rākṣasa duṣṭātmanmayā kartuṃ na śakyate \|*  
*tvayā vikriyate tarhi kutastvaṃ śaktimānasi \|\| 29 \|\|*

The king said:—

29\. O wicked Rākṣasa, listen. I shall not comply with your threats. If you go astray wherefore are you powerful?

## \|\| 8.21.30 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktassainyamādāya rājānaṃ paribhartsya tam \|*  
*karālaṃ karavālaṃ ca pārthive prākṣipattadā \|\| 30 \|\|*

The Sūta said:—

30\. Thus addressed by the king, the demon rebuked him and hurled his terrible sword at the earthen idol.

## \|\| 8.21.31 \|\|

*paśya tvaṃ svāmino'dyaiva balaṃ bhaktasukhāvaham \|*  
*ityuvāca vihasyaiva rākṣasaissa mahābalaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. “See now itself your lord’s strength pleasing to the devotees.” Saying this, the powerful demon laughed in the company of the Rākṣasas.

## \|\| 8.21.32 \|\|

*karavālaḥ pārthivaṃ ca yāvatspṛśati no dvijāḥ \|*  
*yāvacca pārthivāttasmādāvirāsītsvayaṃ haraḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. O brahmins, no sooner did the sword touch the earthen idol than Śiva manifested himself from the earthen idol.

## \|\| 8.21.33 \|\|

*paśya bhīmeśvarohaṃ ca rakṣārthaṃ prakaṭo'bhavam \|*  
*mama pūrvavrataṃ hyetadrakṣyo bhakto mayā sadā \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. “See, I am Bhīmeśvara. I have revealed myself to protect my devotee. This is my old rite that the devotee should be protected by me always.

## \|\| 8.21.34 \|\|

*etasmātpaśya me śīghraṃ balaṃ bhaktasukhāvaham \|*  
*ityuktvā sa pinākena karavālo dvidhā kṛtaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. From this alone see soon my strength that is pleasing to the devotees.” After saying this he split the sword into two with his Pināka.

## \|\| 8.21.35 \|\|

*punaścaiva triśūlaṃ svaṃ cikṣipe tena rakṣasā \|*  
*tacchūlaṃ śatadhā nītamapi duṣṭasya śaṃbhunā \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. His spear was then hurled by the Rākṣasa. That spear too of the wicked Rākṣasa was split into a hundred pieces by Śiva.

## \|\| 8.21.36 \|\|

*punaśśaktiśca niḥkṣiptā tena śaṃbhūpari dvijāḥ \|*  
*śaṃbhunā sāpi bāṇaissvairlakṣadhā ca kṛtā drutam \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. O brahmins, then a lance was hurled at Śiva by him. It too was split into a hundred-thousand pieces with his arrows by Śiva.

## \|\| 8.21.37 \|\|

*paṭṭiśaśca tatastena niḥkṣipto hi śivopari \|*  
*śivena sa triśūlena tilaśaśca kṛtaṃ kṣaṇāt \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Then a huge iron club was hurled at Śiva by him. Within a trice, it too was reduced to small pieces like gingelly seeds by Śiva with his trident.

## \|\| 8.21.38 \|\|

*tataśśivagaṇānāṃ ca rākṣasānāṃ parasparam \|*  
*yuddhamāsīttadā ghoraṃ paśyatāṃ duḥkhakāvaham \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Then a terrible fight ensued between the attendants of Śiva and the Rākṣasa. It frightened all the onlookers with distress.

## \|\| 8.21.39 \|\|

*tataśca pṛthivī sarvā vyākulā cābhavatkṣaṇāt \|*  
*samudrāśca tadā sarve cukṣubhussamahīdharāḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Within a trice, the entire earth became agitated. The oceans and the mountains became excited.

## \|\| 8.21.40 \|\|

*devāśca ṛṣayassarve babhūvurvikalā ati \|*  
*ūcuḥ parasparaṃ ceti vyarthaṃ vai prārthitaśśivaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. The god and the sages were much distressed. They said to one another. “In vain did we beseech Śiva.”

## \|\| 8.21.41 \|\|

*nāradaśca samāgatya śaṃkaraṃ duḥkhadāhakam \|*  
*prārthayāmāsa tatraiva sāṃjalirnatamastakaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Nārada came there and requested Śiva, the destroyer of miseries with palms joined in reverence and head bent down.

## \|\| 8.21.42 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*kṣamyatāṃ kṣamyatāṃ nātha tvayā vibhramakāraka \|*  
*tṛṇekaśca kuṭhāro vai hanyatāṃ śīghrameva hi \|\| 42 \|\|*

Nārada said:—

42\. O lord, forgive, O cause of fierce agitation. Why shall a dagger be thrust to cut a blade of grass, Let the demon be killed soon.

## \|\| 8.21.43 \|\|

*iti saṃprārthitaśśaṃbhuḥ sarvānrakṣogaṇānprabhuḥ \|*  
*huṃkāreṇaiva cāstreṇa bhasmasātkṛtavāṃstadā \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Thus requested, lord Śiva reduced the Rākṣasa to ashes with only a Huṃ sound for hīs weapon.

## \|\| 8.21.44 \|\|

*sarve te rākṣasā dagdhāḥ śaṃkareṇa kṣaṇaṃ mune \|*  
*babhūvustatra sarveṣāṃ devānāṃ paśyatādbhutam \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Even as all the gods were watching, O sage, the Rākṣasas were burnt down by Śiva in a trice.

## \|\| 8.21.45 \|\|

*dāvānalagato vahniryathā ca vanamādahet \|*  
*tathā śivena kruddhena rākṣasānāṃ balaṃ kṣaṇāt \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Just as the conflagration consumes the forest so did the fury of Śiva destroy the army of the Rākṣasas in a trice.

## \|\| 8.21.46 \|\|

*bhīmasyaiva ca kiṃ bhasma na jñātaṃ kenacittadā \|*  
*parivārayuto dagdho nāma na śrūyate kvacit \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. None could see even the ashes of Bhīma. He was burnt down along with his followers. There was no trace of him to be seen.

## \|\| 8.21.47 \|\|

*tataśśivasya kṛpayā śāṃtiṃ prāptā munīśvarāḥ \|*  
*devāssarve ca śakrādyāssvāsthyaṃ prāpākhilaṃ jagat \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. The flame of the fury of Śiva spread from forest to forest. The ashes of the Rākṣasas spread everywhere in the forest.

## \|\| 8.21.48 \|\|

*krodhajvālā maheśasya nissasāra vanādvanam \|*  
*rākṣasānāṃ ca tadbhasma sarvaṃ vyāptaṃ vane'khilam \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. The fiery flame of the anger of the great lord Śiva came out and spread from forest to forest. The ashes of the Rākṣasas spread throughout the forest.

## \|\| 8.21.49 \|\|

*tataścauṣadhayo jātā nānākāryakarāstathā \|*  
*rūpāntaraṃ tato nṝṇāṃ bhavedveṣāṃtaraṃ tathā \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. From the ashes different herbs cropped up effectively in various ways. Men could assume different forms and features through the ashes.

## \|\| 8.21.50 \|\|

*bhūtapretapiśācādi dūrataśca tato vrajet \|*  
*tanna kāryaṃ ca yaccaiva tato na bhavati dvijāḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Evil spirits, ghosts are removed far by the ashes O brahmins, there is no task which cannot be carried out through it.

## \|\| 8.21.51 \|\|

*tataḥ prārthitaśśambhurmunibhiśca viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*sthātavyaṃ svāminā hyatra lokānāṃ sukhahetave \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Then Śiva was implored by the gods and sages “O lord, you should stay here itself for the happiness of the people.

## \|\| 8.21.52 \|\|

*ayaṃ vai kutsito deśa ayodhyālokaduḥkhadaḥ \|*  
*bhavaṃtaṃ ca tadā dṛṣṭvā kalyāṇaṃ saṃbhaviṣyati \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. This despicable land causing distress to the people of Ayodhyā will yield welfare to the people at your very sight.

## \|\| 8.21.53 \|\|

*bhīmaśaṃkaranāmā tvaṃ bhavitā sarvasādhakaḥ \|*  
*etalliṃgaṃ sadā pūjyaṃ sarvāpadvinivārakam \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. You will be known as Bhīmaśaṅkara—a phallic image which shall be worshipped always. It will avert mishaps and be conducive to the attainment of all desires.

## \|\| 8.21.54 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityevaṃ prārthitaśśambhurlokānāṃ hitakārakaḥ \|*  
*tatraivāsthitavānprītyā svatantro bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

54\. Thus requested, Śiva the benefactor of the worlds, the lord favourably disposed towards his devotees remained there itself.

## Chapter 22 - The greatness of Viśveśvara, the arrival of Rudra at Kāśī

## \|\| 8.22.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ataḥparaṃ pravakṣyāmi śrūyatāmṛṣisattamāḥ \|*  
*viśveśvarasya māhātmyaṃ mahāpātakanāśanam \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. O excellent sages, hereafter I shall narrate the greatness of Viśveśvara, destructive of great sins. May it be heard.

## \|\| 8.22.2-3 \|\|

*yadidaṃ dṛśyate kiṃcijjagatyāṃ vastumātrakam \|*  
*cidānanda svarūpaṃ ca nirvikāraṃ sanātanam \|\| 2 \|\|*

*tasyaiva kaivalyaraterdvitīyecchā tatobhavat \|*  
*sa eva saguṇo jātaśśiva ityabhidhīyate \|\| 3 \|\|*

2-3. That which is seen as the substantive, as an entity in the form of knowledge and bliss, eternal and free from aberration, desired for a second. That second, of the tree of salvation, was possessed of attributes. It is named Śiva.

## \|\| 8.22.4 \|\|

*sa eva hi dvidhā jātaḥ puṃstrīrūpaprabhedataḥ \|*  
*yaḥ pumānsa śivaḥ khyātaḥ strīśaktissā hi kathyate \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. That split itself into two in the male and the female forms. The male is famous as Śiva and the female is known as Śakti.

## \|\| 8.22.5-6 \|\|

*cidānandesvarūpābhyāṃ puruṣāvapi nirmitau \|\| 5 \|\|*

*adṛṣṭābhyāṃ tadā tābhyāṃ svabhāvānmunisattamāḥ \|*  
*tāvadṛṣṭvā tadā tau ca svamātṛpitarau dvijāḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

5-6. O great sages, the unseen Cit and Ānanda created Prakṛti and Puruṣa. O brahmins, on seeing their parents, Prakṛti and Puruṣa were plunged in doubt.

## \|\| 8.22.7 \|\|

*mahāsaṃśayamāpannau prakṛtiḥ puruṣaśca tau \|*  
*tadā vāṇī samutpannā nirguṇātparamātmanaḥ \|*  
*tapaścaiva prakartavyaṃ tatassṛṣṭiranuttamā \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Then a voice arose from the great attributeless Ātman—“Penance shall be pursued by you for the generation of excellent creation.”

## \|\| 8.22.8 \|\|

*prakṛtipuruṣābūcatuḥ \|*  
*tapasastu sthalaṃnāsti kutrāvābhyāṃ prabho'dhunā \|*  
*sthitvā tapaḥ prakartavyaṃ tava śāsanataśśiva \|\| 8 \|\|*

Prakṛti and Puruṣa said:—

8\. O lord Śiva, there is no place for penance. Where shall we sit and perform this penance at your behest?

## \|\| 8.22.9-10 \|\|

*tataśca tejasassāraṃ paṃcakrośātmakaṃ śubham \|*  
*sarvopakaraṇairyuktaṃ suṃdaraṃ nagaraṃ tathā \|\| 9 \|\|*

*nirmāya preṣitaṃ tatsvaṃ nirguṇena śivena ca \|*  
*aṃtarikṣe sthitaṃ tacca puruṣasya samīpataḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

9-10. Then a beautiful city endowed with the requisite articles, the auspicious essence of brilliance extending to five Krośas was created and established by Śiva devoid of attributes, in the firmament near the Puruṣa.

## \|\| 8.22.11 \|\|

*tadadhiṣṭhāya hariṇā sṛṣṭikāmanayā tataḥ \|*  
*bahukālaṃ tapastaptaṃ taddhyānamavalaṃbya ca \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Occupying it with a desire for creation and meditating, Visṇu performed penance for a long time.

## \|\| 8.22.12 \|\|

*śrameṇa jaladhāraśca vividhāścābhavaṃstadā \|*  
*tābhirvyāptaṃ ca tacchūnyaṃ nānyatkiṃcidadṛśyata \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Due to his exertion various currents of water began to flow. The void was pervaded by the water-currents. Nothing else was seen.

## \|\| 8.22.13 \|\|

*tataśca viṣṇunā dṛṣṭaṃ kimetaddṛśyate'dbhutam \|*  
*ityāścaryaṃ tadā dṛṣṭvā śirasaḥ kampanaṃ kṛtam \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. On seeing it and thinking “What is this wonderful thing in sight”, Viṣṇu shook his head.

## \|\| 8.22.14 \|\|

*tataśca patitaḥ karṇānmaṇiśca purataḥ prabho \|*  
*tadbabhūva mahattīrthaṃ nāmato maṇikarṇikā \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Then a jewel fell in front from the ear of the lord. It became the great holy centre named Maṇikarṇikā.

## \|\| 8.22.15 \|\|

*jalaughe plāvyamānā sā paṃcakrośī yadābhavata \|*  
*nirguṇena śivenāśu triśūlena dhṛtā tadā \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. When the holy centre extending to five Krośas began to float on the waters, it was supported on Trident by Śiva devoid of attributes.

## \|\| 8.22.16 \|\|

*viṣṇustatraiva suṣvāpa prakṛtyā svastriyā saha \|*  
*tannābhikamalājjāto brahmā śaṃkaraśāsanāt \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Viṣṇu slept there itself along with his consort Prakṛti. At the behest of Śiva, Brahmā was born of his umbilical lotus.

## \|\| 8.22.17 \|\|

*śivājñāṃ sa samāsādya sṛṣṭicakre'dbhutā tadā \|*  
*caturddaśamitā lokā brahmāṃḍe yatra nirmitāḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. At the behest of Śiva, he evolved a wonderful creation. The fourteen worlds were created in the Cosmic Egg.

## \|\| 8.22.18 \|\|

*yojanānāṃ ca paṃcāśatkoṭisaṃkhyāpramāṇataḥ \|*  
*brahmāṃḍasyaiva vistāro munibhiḥ parikīrtitaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. The extent of the Cosmic Egg is fifty crores of Yojanas as glorified by the sages.

## \|\| 8.22.19 \|\|

*brahmāṃḍe karmaṇā baddhā prāṇino māṃ kathaṃ punaḥ \|*  
*prāpsyaṃtīti vicintyaitatpaṃcakrośī vimocitā \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. “How will the beings in the Cosmic Egg bound by action attain me otherwise?”, the holy centre of Pañcakrośī was released by him after thinking like this.

## \|\| 8.22.20 \|\|

*iyaṃ ca śubhadā loke karma nāśakarī matā \|*  
*mokṣaprakāśikā kāśī jñānadā mama supriyā \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. “This Kāśī is the bestower of auspiciousness in the world. It is destructive of action. It illuminates salvation. It is the bestower of knowledge. It is my great favourite.

## \|\| 8.22.21 \|\|

*avimuktaṃ svayaṃ liṃgaṃ sthāpitaṃ paramātmanā \|*  
*na kadācittvayā tyājyamidaṃ kṣetraṃ mamāṃśaka \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. The phallic image Avimukta is installed by the great Śiva himself. O you born of my parts, this holy centre should never be abandoned by you.”

## \|\| 8.22.22 \|\|

*ityuktvā ca triśūlātsvādavatāryya harassvayam \|*  
*mocayāmāsa bhuvane martyaloke hi kāśikām \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. After saying this, Śiva himself brought Kāśikā from his Trident and released it in the mortal world.

## \|\| 8.22.23 \|\|

*brahmaṇaśca dine sā hi na vinaśyati niścitam \|*  
*tadā śivastriśūlena dadhāti munayaśca tām \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. At the close of the day of Brahmā (when all other objects perish) it certainly does not perish. Then, O sages, Śiva upholds it by means of his Trident.

## \|\| 8.22.24 \|\|

*punaśca brahmaṇā sṛṣṭau kṛtāyāṃ sthāpyate dvijāḥ \|*  
*karmaṇā karṣaṇāccaiva kāśīti paripaṭhyate \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. O brahmins, when the creation is resumed again by Brahmā it is replaced. It is called Kāśī because it pulls out or destroys our actions.

## \|\| 8.22.25 \|\|

*avimukteśvaraṃ liṃgaṃ kāśyāṃ tiṣṭhati sarvadā \|*  
*muktidātṛ ca lokānāṃ mahāpātakināmapi \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. The phallic image Avimukteśvara stays in Kāśī always, yielding salvation to the people including even the great sinners.

## \|\| 8.22.26 \|\|

*anyatra prāpyate muktissārūpyādirmunīśvarāḥ \|*  
*atraiva prāpyate jīvaiḥ sāyujyā muktiruttamā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. O great sages, the Sārūpya and other types of salvation are attained elsewhere. The excellent type of salvation Sāyujya is attained here alone.

## \|\| 8.22.27 \|\|

*yeṣāṃ kvāpi gatirnāsti teṣāṃ vārāṇasī purī \|*  
*paṃcakrośī mahāpuṇyā hatyākoṭivināśanī \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Of those who do not attain salvation elsewhere, the city of Vārāṇasī is the resort. The meritorious holy centre Pañcakrośī is destructive of sins of crores of slaughter.

## \|\| 8.22.28 \|\|

*amarā maraṇaṃ sarve vāṃchatīha pare ca ke \|*  
*bhuktimuktipradā caiṣā sarvadā śaṃkarapriyā \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. The immortal gods too desire death here. What then of others? Thus holy centre yields worldly pleasures and salvation always. It is a favourite resort of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.22.29 \|\|

*brahmā ca ślāghate cāmūṃ viṣṇussiddhāśca yoginaḥ \|*  
*munayaśca tathaivānye trilokasthā janāḥ sadā \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Brahmā praises this city. So also do the Siddhas, Yogins, sages and other people in the three worlds and Viṣṇu too.

## \|\| 8.22.30 \|\|

*kāśyāśca mahimānaṃ vai vaktuṃ varṣaśatairapi \|*  
*śaknomyahaṃ na sarvaṃ hi yathāśakti bruve tataḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. I cannot describe adequately and entirely the greatness of Kāśī even in hundreds of years. I shall explain to the extent I can.

## \|\| 8.22.31 \|\|

*kailāsasya patiryo vai hyaṃtassattvo bahistamāḥ \|*  
*kālāgnirnāmataḥ khyāto nirguṇo guṇavānbhavaḥ \|*  
*praṇipātairanekaiśca vacanaṃ cedamabravīt \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. The lord of Kailāsa, who is Sāttvaic within and Tāmasaic without, who is famous in the name of Kālāgni and who is the supreme Being with and without attributes spoke after making many obeisances.

## \|\| 8.22.32 \|\|

*\|\| rudra uvāca \|*  
*viśveśvara maheśāna tvadīyo'smi na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*kṛpāṃ kuru mahādeva mayi tvaṃ sāmba ātmaje \|\| 32 \|\|*

Rudra said:—

32\. O lord of the universe, O great lord, I belong to you undoubtedly. O great god, be merciful to me, your son, O lord, accompanied by mother Pārvatī.

## \|\| 8.22.33 \|\|

*sthātavyaṃ ca sadātraiva lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā \|*  
*tārayasva jagannātha prārthayāmi jagatpate \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. O lord of the universe, you shall stay here for the benefit of the worlds. I request you, O ruler of the universe to save the universe.

## \|\| 8.22.34 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*avimukte'pi dāntātmā taṃ saṃprārthya punaḥ punaḥ \|*  
*netrāśrūṇi pramucyaiva prītaḥ provāca śaṃkaram \|\| 34 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

34\. Avimukta, of suppressed and controlled soul, shed tears from his eyes and imploring him again and again spoke to Śiva.

## \|\| 8.22.35 \|\|

*avimukta uvāca \|*  
*devadeva mahādeva kālāmayasubheṣaja \|*  
*tvaṃ trilokapatissatyaṃ sevyo brahmācyutādibhiḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

Avimukta said:—

35\. O great lord, O lord of gods, O good panacea for all the evils of time, you are in truth the lord of the three worlds. You are worthy of being served by Brahmā, Viṣṇu and others,

## \|\| 8.22.36 \|\|

*kāśyāṃ puryāṃ tvayā deva rājadhānī pragṛhyatām \|*  
*mayā dhyānatayā stheyamaciṃtya sukhahetave \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. O lord, may you establish your capital in the city of Kāśī. For the sake of inconceivable pleasure I shall slay in meditation.

## \|\| 8.22.37 \|\|

*muktidātā bhavāneva kāmadaśca na cāparaḥ \|*  
*tasmāttvamupakārāya tiṣṭhomāsahitassadā \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. You alone are the bestower of liberation and worldly desires. For rendering help to the people you stay here always along with Umā.

## \|\| 8.22.38 \|\|

*jīvānbhavābdherakhilāṃstāraya tvaṃ sadāśiva \|*  
*bhaktakāryyaṃ kuru hara prārthayāmi punaḥpunaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. O Sadāśiva, save the living beings from the ocean of worldly existence. O Śiva, carry out the task of your devotees. I request you again and again.

## \|\| 8.22.39 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityevaṃ prārthitastena viśvanāthena śaṃkaraḥ \|*  
*lokānāmupakārārthaṃ tasthau tatrāpi sarvarāṭ \|\| 39 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

39\. Thus requested by Viśvanātha, Śiva the sovereign ruler of all, stayed there for rendering help to the people.

## \|\| 8.22.40 \|\|

*yaddinaṃ hi samārabhya haraḥ kāśyāmupāgataḥ \|*  
*tadārabhya ca sā kāśī sarvaśreṣṭhatarābhavat \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Kāśī became the most excellent centre for all since Śiva came to Kāśī.

## Chapter 23 - The greatness of the Jyotirliṅga Kāśī-Viśveśvara

## \|\| 8.23.1 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*evaṃ vārāṇasī puṇyā yadi sūta mahāpurī \|*  
*tatprabhāvaṃ vadāsmākamavimuktasya ca prabho \|\| 1 \|\|*

The sages said:—

1\. O Sūta, if the great city of Vārāṇasī is so meritorious please narrate its greatness to us as well as that of lord Avimukta.

## \|\| 8.23.2 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*vakṣye saṃkṣepatassamyagvārāṇasyāssuśobhanam \|*  
*viśveśvarasya māhātmyaṃ śrūyatāṃ ca munīśvarāḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

2\. O great sages, I shall explain neatly and succinctly the splendid greatness of Vārāṇasī and Viśveśvara. May it be listened to.

## \|\| 8.23.3 \|\|

*kadācitpārvatī devī śaṅkaraṃ parayā mudā \|*  
*lokakāmanayāpṛcchanmāhātmyamavimuktayoḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Once goddess Pārvatī asked Śiva with great joy about the greatness of the two Avimuktas—Kāśī and Viśveśvara, out of desire for the welfare of the worlds.

## \|\| 8.23.4 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*asya kṣetrasya māhātmyaṃ vaktumarhasya śeṣataḥ \|*  
*mamopari kṛpāṃ kṛtvā lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā \|\| 4 \|\|*

Pārvatī said:—

4\. It behoves you to explain the greatness of this shrine entirely I taking pity on me and desiring the benefit of the worlds.

## \|\| 8.23.5 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*devyāstadvacanaṃ śrutvā devadevo jagatprabhuḥ \|*  
*pratyuvāca bhavānīṃ tāṃ jīvānāṃ priyahetave \|\| 5 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

5\. On hearing the words of the goddess, the lord of the gods, the lord of the universe, replied to her for the pleasure of all living beings.

## \|\| 8.23.6 \|\|

*parameśvara uvāca \|*  
*sādhu pṛṣṭaṃ tvayā bhadre lokānāṃ sukhadaṃ śubham \|*  
*kathayāmi yathārthaṃ vai mahā tmyamavimuktayoḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

Lord Śiva said:—

6\. What, gentle lady, has been asked by you is splendid. It is auspicious and pleasing to the people. I shall explain as a matter of fact the greatness of the two Avimuktas.

## \|\| 8.23.7 \|\|

*idaṃ guhyatamaṃ kṣetraṃ sadā vārāṇasī mama \|*  
*sarveṣāmeva jaṃtūnāṃ heturmokṣasya sarvathā \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Vārāṇasī is my mysterious shrine. It is the cause of salvation for the people in every respect.

## \|\| 8.23.8 \|\|

*asminsiddhāssadā kṣetre madīyaṃ vratamāśritāḥ \|*  
*nānāliṃgadharā nityaṃ mama lokābhikāṃkṣiṇaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. In this holy centre, the Siddhas have always taken to my holy rites. They hold different phallic images. They yearn to attain my world.

## \|\| 8.23.9 \|\|

*abhyasyaṃti mahāyogaṃ jitātmāno jitendriyāḥ \|*  
*paraṃ pāśupataṃ śrautaṃ bhuktimuktiphalapradam \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Those who have conquered themselves and controlled their sense-organs pursue the great Pāśupata Yoga which is explained in the Vedas, yielding worldly pleasures and salvation.

## \|\| 8.23.10 \|\|

*rocate me sadā vāso vārāṇasyāṃ maheśvari \|*  
*hetunā yena sarvāṇi vihāya śṛṇu taddhruvam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. O great goddess, the residence in Vārāṇasī is always appealing to me. Forsaking everything listen to the cause of its greatness.

## \|\| 8.23.11 \|\|

*yo me bhaktaśca vijñānī tāvubhau muktibhāginau \|*  
*tīrthāpekṣā ca na tayorvihitā vihite samau \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. The two deserve liberation—he who is my devotee and he who has perfect knowledge. Their is not the dependence on holy centres. They are equanimous in regard to what is prescribed and what is forbidden.

## \|\| 8.23.12 \|\|

*jīvanmuktau tu tau jñeyau yatrakutrāpi vai mṛtau \|*  
*prāpnuto mokṣamāśveva mayoktaṃ niścitaṃ vacaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. They must be known as liberated souls, no matter where they die. They are sure to attain salvation. This decisive word has been uttered by me.

## \|\| 8.23.13 \|\|

*atra tīrthe viśeṣostyavimuktākhye parottame \|*  
*śrūyatāṃ tattvayā devi paraśakte sucittayā \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Here in this excellent holy centre Avimukta, there is one special thing to be noted. O goddess, O great Śakti, please listen attentively.

## \|\| 8.23.14 \|\|

*sarve varṇā āśramāśca bālayauvanavārddhakāḥ \|*  
*asyāṃ puryāṃ mṛtāścettsyurmuktā eva na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. People of all castes, of all stages of life, whether children, youths or the aged, if they die in this city, are undoubtedly liberated.

## \|\| 8.23.15-16 \|\|

*aśuciśca śucirvāpi kanyā pariṇatā tathā \|*  
*vidhavā vātha vā vaṃdhyā rajodoṣayutāpi vā \|\| 15 \|\|*

*prasūtā saṃskṛtā kāpi yādṛśī tādṛśī dvijāḥ \|*  
*atra kṣetre mṛtā cetsyānmokṣabhāṅ nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

15-16. O brahmins, whether pure or impure, whether virgin or married, whether widow or barren, whether suffering from menstrual disorders or defects or whether recently delivered of a child, of whatever nature she may be, if she dies at this holy centre she attains salvation. There is no doubt in this.

## \|\| 8.23.17 \|\|

*svedajaścāṃḍajo vāpi dyudbhijjo'tha jarāyujaḥ \|*  
*mṛto mokṣamavāpnoti yathātra na tathā kvacit \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. The sweat-born, (lice, bugs etc.) the egg-born (birds), the germinating ones (plants and trees) and the embryo-born (mammals) these living beings do not attain salvation elsewhere as they do at this place.

## \|\| 8.23.18 \|\|

*jñānāpekṣā na cātraiva bhattayapekṣā na vai punaḥ \|*  
*karmāpekṣā na devyatra dānāpekṣā na caiva hi \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. O goddess, there is no dependence on perfect knowledge here, nor that on devotion, nor that on holy rites, nor that on charitable gifts.

## \|\| 8.23.19 \|\|

*saṃskṛtyapekṣā naivātra dhyānāpekṣā na karhicit \|*  
*nāmāpekṣārcanāpekṣā sujātīnāṃ tathātra na \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. There is no dependence on culture, nor that on meditation af anytime, nor that on repetition of names, nor that on adoration, nor that even on noble nativity.

## \|\| 8.23.20 \|\|

*mama kṣetre mokṣade hi yo vā vasati mānavaḥ \|*  
*yathā tathā mṛtaḥ syāccenmokṣamāpnoti niścitam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Whoever may be the man staying in my holy centre, whatever may be the manner of his death, if he dies here, he certainly attains salvation.

## \|\| 8.23.21 \|\|

*etanmama puraṃ divyaṃ guhyādguhyataraṃ priye \|*  
*brahmādayo'pi jānaṃti māhātmyaṃ nāsya pārvati \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. O beloved, this divine city of mine is most mysterious one. O Pārvatī, even Brahmā and others do not know its greatness.

## \|\| 8.23.22 \|\|

*mahatkṣetramidaṃ tasmādavimuktamiti smṛtam \|*  
*sarvebhyo naimiṣādibhyaḥ paraṃ mokṣapradaṃ mṛte \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Hence this holy centre is known as Avimukta. It is greater than Naimiṣa and all other holy centres in yielding salvation to the deceased.

## \|\| 8.23.23 \|\|

*dharmasyopaniṣatsatyaṃ mokṣasyopaniṣatsamam \|*  
*kṣetratīrthopaniṣadamavimuktaṃ vidurbudhāḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Truth is the esoteric doctrine of Dharmas. Equanimity is the esoteric doctrine of salvation. Learned men know Avimukta as the esoteric doctrine of all shrines and holy centres.

## \|\| 8.23.24 \|\|

*kāmaṃ bhuṃjansvapankrīḍankurvanhi vividhāḥ kriyāḥ \|*  
*avimukte tyajanprāṇāñjaṃturmokṣāya kalpate \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Any being, enjoying pleasures at his will, sleeping, sporting or performing different activities, attains salvation if he casts off his life at Avimukta.

## \|\| 8.23.25 \|\|

*kṛtvā pāpasahasrāṇi piśācatvaṃ varaṃ nṛṇām \|*  
*na ca kratusahasratvaṃ svarge kāśīṃ purīṃ vinā \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. It is far better for men to become ghost after committing thousands of sins and stay at Kāśī than to attain heaven after performing a thousand sacrifices but without going to the city of Kāśī.

## \|\| 8.23.26 \|\|

*tasmātsarvaprayatnena sevyate kāśikā purī \|*  
*avyaktaliṃgaṃ munibhirdhyāyate ca sadāśivaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Hence the people resort to Kāśī with every strenuous effort. The sages meditate on Sadāśiva in the form of his phallic image.

## \|\| 8.23.27 \|\|

*yadyatphalaṃ samuddiśya tapantyatra naraḥ priye \|*  
*tebhyaścāhaṃ praya cchāmi samyaktattatphalaṃ dhuvam \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. O beloved, I distribute the different fruits among different men who perform penance with those ends in view.

## \|\| 8.23.28 \|\|

*sāyujyamātmanaḥ paścādīpsitaṃ sthānameva ca \|*  
*na kutaścitkarmabaṃdhastyajatāmatra vai tanum \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. No entanglement of actions binds those who cast off their bodies here. Afterwards they realise complete identity with the godhead as their goal.

## \|\| 8.23.29 \|\|

*brahmā devarṣibhissārddhaṃ viṣṇurvāpi divākaraḥ \|*  
*upāsate mahātmānassarve māmiha cāpare \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Brahma along with the gods and the sages, Viṣṇu, the sun, and all other noble souls perform my worship.

## \|\| 8.23.30 \|\|

*viṣayāsaktacitto'pi tyakta dharmarucirnaraḥ \|*  
*iha kṣetre mṛto yo vai saṃsāraṃ na punarviśet \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. He who dies in this holy centre does not get rebirth even if he happens to be a person whose mind is drawn towards sensual pleasures or who has eschewed interest in virtue.

## \|\| 8.23.31 \|\|

*kiṃ punarnirmamā dhīrāsattvasthā daṃbhavarjitāḥ \|*  
*kṛtinaśca nirāraṃbhāssarve te mayi bhāvitāḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. What then, if they are free from attachment, devoid of arrogance, possessed of Sāttvaic nature, blessed ones devoted to me and not initiating evil activities.

## \|\| 8.23.32 \|\|

*janmāṃtarasahasreṣu janma yogī samāpnuyāt \|*  
*tadihaiva paraṃ mokṣaṃ maraṇādadhigacchati \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Among thousands of rebirths, a Yogin is born here. Then, dying here, he attains the highest salvation.

## \|\| 8.23.33 \|\|

*atra liṃgānyanekāni bhaktaissaṃsthāpitāni hi \|*  
*sarvakāmapradānīha mokṣadāni ca pārvati \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. O Pārvatī, there are many phallic forms installed by my devotees. They yield all desires and bestow salvation too.

## \|\| 8.23.34 \|\|

*paṃcakrośaṃ caturdikṣu kṣetrametatprakīrtitam \|*  
*samaṃtācca tathā jaṃtormṛtikāle'mṛtapradam \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. This holy centre is glorified as extending to five Krośas in every one of the four directions all round. At the time of death it yields immortal beatitude.

## \|\| 8.23.35 \|\|

*apāpaśca mṛto yo vai sadyo mokṣaṃ samaśnute \|*  
*sapāpaśca mṛtau yassyātkāyavyūhānsamaśnute \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. If a sinless man dies, he attains salvation immediately. If a sinner dies here he assumes multitudes of births.

## \|\| 8.23.36-37 \|\|

*yātanāṃ sonubhūyaiva paścānmokṣamavāpnuyāt \|*  
*pātakaṃ yo'vimuktākhye kṣetre'sminkurute dhruvam \|\| 36 \|\|*

*bhairavīṃ yātanāṃ prāpya varṣāṇāmayute punaḥ \|*  
*tato mokṣamavāpnoti bhuktvā pāpaṃ ca sundari \|\| 37 \|\|*

36-37. He attains salvation only after experiencing tortures. He who commits sins in the holy centre of Avimukta, experiences tortures at the hands of Bhairava for ten thousand years and then attains salvation, O beautiful lady.

## \|\| 8.23.38 \|\|

*iti te ca samākhyātā pāpācāre ca yā gatiḥ \|*  
*evaṃ jñātvā narassamyaksevayedavimuktakam \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Thus the course of events in regard to one who commits sins has been narrated to you. A man should realise this and worship Avimukta properly.

## \|\| 8.23.39 \|\|

*kṛtakarmakṣayo nāsti kalpakoṭiśatairapi \|*  
*avaśyameva bhoktavyaṃ kṛtaṃ karma śubhāśubham \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. An action once performed is never wasted even in hundreds of crores of Kalpas. Good or bad, one must of necessity enjoy the fruit of one’s action,

## \|\| 8.23.40 \|\|

*kevalaṃ cāśubhaṃ karma narakāya bhavediha \|*  
*śubhaṃ svargāya jāyeta dvābhyāṃ mānuṣyamīritam \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Exclusively inauspicious action is contributory to hellish distress. Exclusively auspicious action is conducive to the attainment of heaven. A mixture of the two is said to be resulting in human birth.

## \|\| 8.23.41 \|\|

*janma samyagasamyak ca nyūnādhikye bhavediha \|*  
*ubhayośca kṣayo muktirbhavetsatyaṃ hi pārvati \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. The birth will be good or evil in accordance with the predominance or deficiency of the one or the other. The annihilation of good and evil is liberation. This is the truth, O Pārvatī.

## \|\| 8.23.42 \|\|

*karma ca trividhaṃ proktaṃ karmakāṇḍe maheśvari \|*  
*saṃcitaṃ kriyamāṇaṃ ca prārabdhaṃ ceti baṃdhakṛt \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. O great goddess, the action that causes bondage is said to be threefold as mentioned in the Karmakāṇḍa section of the Vedas. It is (1) hoarded (Sañcita) (2) the current (Kriyamāṇa) and (3) that which has started yielding results (Prārabdha).

## \|\| 8.23.43-44 \|\|

*pūrvajanmasamudbhūtaṃ saṃcitaṃ samudāhṛtam \|*  
*bhujyate ca śarīreṇa prārabdhaṃ parikīrtitam \|\| 43 \|\|*

*janmanā yacca kriyate karma sāṃpratam \|*  
*śubhāśubhaṃ ca deveśi kriyamāṇaṃ vidurbudhāḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

43-44. The actions of the previous births are classified as hoarded. The action the fruit of which is being experienced in the present birth is Prārabdha. O goddess, whether good or bad the action performed in this birth is known as Kriyamāṇa.

## \|\| 8.23.45 \|\|

*prārabdhakarmaṇo bhogātkṣayaścaiva cānyathā \|*  
*upāyena dvayornāśaḥ karmaṇoḥ pūjanādinā \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. The annihilation of the Prārabdha Karman is only through enjoyment and not otherwise. The destruction of the other two types of action is possible through worship alone.

## \|\| 8.23.46 \|\|

*sarveṣāṃ karmaṇāṃ nāśo nāsti kāśīṃ purīṃ vinā \|*  
*sarvaṃ ca sulabhaṃ tīrthaṃ durllabhā kāśikā purī \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Except in Kāśī in no other place can all the actions be wiped off. The other holy centres are easily accessible but the city of Kāśī is difficult of access.

## \|\| 8.23.47 \|\|

*pūrvajanmakṛtaṃ cedvai kāśīdarśanamādarāt \|*  
*tadā kāśīṃ ca saṃprāpya labhenmṛtyuṃ na cānyathā \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. If Kāśī had been visited with devotion in the previous birth, the person in the present birth reaches Kāśī and dies there and not otherwise.

## \|\| 8.23.48 \|\|

*kāśīṃ prāpya naro yastu gaṃgāyāṃ snānamācaret \|*  
*tadā ca kriyamāṇasya saṃcitasyāpi saṃkṣayaḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. If after reaching Kāśī a man takes his ceremonial bath in the Gaṅges, the two types of actions viz. the current and the hoarded are destroyed.

## \|\| 8.23.49 \|\|

*prārabdhaṃ na vinā bhogo naśya tīti suniścitam \|*  
*mṛtiśca tasya saṃjātā tadā tasya kṣayo bhavet \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. It is certain that the Prārabdha is not destroyed except through enjoyment. When that is enjoyed it is also destroyed.

## \|\| 8.23.50 \|\|

*pūrvaṃ caiva kṛtā kāśī paścātpāpaṃ samācaret \|*  
*tadbījena balavatā nīyate kāśikā punaḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

50 If a man visits Kāśī first and commits sin afterwards, the seed of that action takes him again to Kāśī.

## \|\| 8.23.51 \|\|

*tadā sarvāṇi pāpāni bhasmasācca bhavaṃti hi \|*  
*tasmātkāśīṃ narassevetkarmanirmūlanīṃ dhruvam \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Then his sins are reduced to ashes. Hence a man shall resort to Kāśī. That certainly debars Karmans to fructify.

## \|\| 8.23.52 \|\|

*eko'pi brāhmaṇo yena kāśyāṃ saṃvāsitaḥ priye \|*  
*kāśīvāsamavāpyaiva tato muktiṃ sa viṃdati \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. O beloved, if even a single brahmin is afforded shelter and residence at Kāśī, the man who affords shelter and residence, gets the benefit of residence in Kāśī himself and thence he attains salvation.

## \|\| 8.23.53 \|\|

*kāśyāṃ yo vai mṛtaścaiva tasya janma punarnahi \|*  
*samuddiśya prayāge ca mṛtasya kāmanāphale \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. If a person dies in Kāśī, he is freed from rebirth. If a man dies at Prayāga, his desires for worldly enjoyment and salvation are fulfilled.

## \|\| 8.23.54 \|\|

*saṃyogaśca tayoścetsyātkāśījanyaphalaṃ vṛthā \|*  
*yadi na syāttayoryogastīrtharājaphalaṃ vṛthā \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. If both the desire for worldly enjoyment and salvation are fulfilled at Prayāga, the fruit of salvation derived from residence in Kāśī is likely to be in vain. If only the fruit of salvation is the goal, the fruits of Prayāga are likely to be in vain.

## \|\| 8.23.55 \|\|

*tasmānmacchāsanādviṣṇussṛṣṭiṃ sākṣāddhi nūtanām \|*  
*vidhāya manasoddiṣṭāṃ tatsiddhiṃ yacchati dhruvam \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Hence at my behest, Viṣṇu creates something afresh and accords him the fruit as mentally conceived by him.

## \|\| 8.23.56 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityādi bahumāhātmyaṃ kāśyāṃ vai munisattamāḥ \|*  
*tathā viśveśvarasyāpi bhuktimuktipradaṃ satām \|\| 56 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

56\. O excellent sages, such things constitute the greatness of Kāśī and Viśveśvara yielding worldly pleasures and salvation to the good.

## \|\| 8.23.57 \|\|

*ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi māhātmyaṃ tryaṃbakasya ca \|*  
*yacchrutvā sarvapāpebhyo mucyate mānavaḥ kṣaṇāt \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Hereafter I shall explain the greatness of Tryambaka on hearing which a man gets rid of his sins altogether.

## Chapter 24 - The greatness of Gautama

## \|\| 8.24.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*śrūyatāmṛṣayaḥ śreṣṭhāḥ kathāṃ pāpapraṇāśinīm \|*  
*kathayāmi yathā vyāsātsadgurośca śrutā mayā \|\| 1 \|\|*

Summary: The greatness of Gautama in the context of the greatness of Tryambakeśvara.

Sūta said:—

1\. O excellent sages, listen. I shall tell you the story that dispels sins in the manner I have heard it from Vyāsa, the good preceptor.

## \|\| 8.24.2 \|\|

*purā ṛṣivaraścāsīdgautamo nāma viśrutaḥ \|*  
*ahalyā nāma tasyāsītpatnī paramadhārmikī \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Formerly there was an excellent sage named Gautama. His wife Ahalyā was extremely virtuous.

## \|\| 8.24.3 \|\|

*dakṣiṇasyāṃ diśi hi yo girirbrahmeti saṃjñakaḥ \|*  
*tatra tena tapastaptaṃ varṣāṇāma yutaṃ tathā \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. A penance extending to ten thousand years was performed by him on the Brahmagiri mountain in the southern quarter.

## \|\| 8.24.4 \|\|

*kadācicca hyanāvṛṣṭirabhavattatra suvratāḥ \|*  
*varṣāṇāṃ ca śataṃ raudrī lokā duḥkhamupāgatāḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. O great ones of good rites, once a terrible drought lasting for a hundred years occurred there. All the people were put to great distress.

## \|\| 8.24.5 \|\|

*ārdraṃ ca pallavaṃ na sma dṛśyate pṛthivītale \|*  
*kuto jalaṃ vidṛśyeta jīvānāṃ prāṇadhārakam \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Not a single juicy tender sprout was seen on the face of the earth. How could there be a water, the sustainer of living beings.

## \|\| 8.24.6 \|\|

*tadā te munayaścaiva manuṣyāḥ paśavastathā \|*  
*pakṣiṇaśca mṛgāstatra gatāścaiva diśo daśa \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Then the sages and other men, the beasts, birds and the deer of the forest went to the different quarters.

## \|\| 8.24.7 \|\|

*tāṃ dṛṣṭvā carṣayo viprāḥ prāṇāyāmaparāyaṇāḥ \|*  
*dhyānena ca tadā kecitkālaṃ ninyussudāruṇam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. On seeing the drought, the sages and the brahmins spent the horrible time in meditation and performing Prāṇāyāmas.

## \|\| 8.24.8 \|\|

*gautamo'pi svayaṃ tatra varuṇārthe tapaśśubham \|*  
*cakāra caiva ṣaṇmāsaṃ prāṇāyāmaparāyaṇaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Gautama himself performed an suspicions penance of Varuṇa for six months engrossed in Prāṇāyāma.

## \|\| 8.24.9 \|\|

*tataśca varuṇastasmai varaṃ dātuṃ samāgatāḥ \|*  
*prasanno'smi varaṃ brūhi dadāmi ca vaco'bravīt \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Then Varuṇa came there to grant him the boon. He said—“I am delighted. Mention the boon you wish to have. I shall grant it.”

## \|\| 8.24.10 \|\|

*tataśca gautamastaṃ vai vṛṣṭiṃ ca prārthayattadā \|*  
*tatassa varuṇastaṃ vai pratyuvāca muniṃ dvijāḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. O brahmins, then Gautama requested him for rain. Then Varuṇa replied to the sage.

## \|\| 8.24.11 \|\|

*varuṇa uvāca \|*  
*devājñāṃ ca samullaṃghya kathaṃ kuryāmahaṃ ca tām \|*  
*anyatprārthaya sujño'si yadahaṃ karavāṇi te \|\| 11 \|\|*

Varuṇa said:—

11\. How can I countermand the behest of the gods? Please ask for something else. You are a wise man. I shall grant it.

## \|\| 8.24.12 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityetadvacanaṃ tasya varuṇasya mahātmanaḥ \|*  
*paropakārī tacchutvā gotamo vākyamabravīta \|\| 12 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

12\. On hearing these words of Varuṇa the great Ātman, Gautama, eager to help others spoke thus.

## \|\| 8.24.13 \|\|

*gautama uvāca \|*  
*yadi prasanno deveśa yadi deyo varo mama \|*  
*yadahaṃ prārthayāmyadya kartavyaṃ hi tvayā tathā \|\| 13 \|\|*

Gautama said:—

13\. O lord of gods, if you are delighted, if a boon is to be granted to me, you shall grant it according to my desire.

## \|\| 8.24.14 \|\|

*yatastvaṃ jalarāśīśastasmāddeyaṃ jalaṃ mama \|*  
*akṣayaṃ sarvadeveśa divyaṃ nityaphalapradam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Since you are the lord of waters, you shall give me water. O lord of gods, you shall give me divine, everlasting water yielding permanent results.

## \|\| 8.24.15 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*iti saṃprārthitastena varuṇo gautamena vai \|*  
*uvāca vacanaṃ tasmai gartaśca kriyatāṃ tvayā \|\| 15 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

15\. Requested thus by Gautama, Varuṇa said—“Please dig a ditch here”.

## \|\| 8.24.16 \|\|

*ityukte ca kṛtastena gartto hastapramāṇataḥ \|*  
*jalena pūritastena divyena varuṇena saḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. When this was said, a ditch of a hand’s depth was dug by him. It was filled with divine water by Varuṇa.

## \|\| 8.24.17 \|\|

*athovāca muniṃ devo varuṇo hi jalādhipaḥ \|*  
*gautamaṃ muniśārdūlaṃ paropakṛtiśālinam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Then lord Varuṇa the king of waters spoke to Gautama, the excellent sage, eager to help others.

## \|\| 8.24.18 \|\|

*varuṇa uvāca \|*  
*akṣayyaṃ ca jalaṃ te'stu tīrthabhūtaṃ mahāmune \|*  
*tava nāmnā ca vikhyātaṃ kṣitāvetadbhaviṣyati \|\| 18 \|\|*

Varuṇa said:—

18\. O great sage, let there be a perennial supply of water in this ditch that has become sacred. This will become famous on the earth by your name.

## \|\| 8.24.19 \|\|

*atra dattaṃ hutaṃ taptaṃ surāṇāṃ yajanaṃ kṛtam \|*  
*pitṝṇāṃ ca kṛtaṃ śrāddhaṃ sarvamevākṣayaṃ bhavet \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Charitable gifts made here, rites performed here, penance pursued here, the sacrifices done here for the gods and the Śrāddha offered to the manes, everything will be imperishable.

## \|\| 8.24.20 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktāṃtarddadhe devasstutastena maharṣiṇā \|*  
*gautamo'pi sukhaṃ prāpa kṛtvānyopakṛtiṃ muniḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

20\. After saying this, the lord eulogised by that sage vanished. After rendering help to others, the sage Gautama too was happy.

## \|\| 8.24.21 \|\|

*maddatto hyāśrayaḥ puṃsāṃ mahattvāyopajāyate \|*  
*mahāṃtastatsvarūpaṃ ca paśyaṃti netare'śubhāḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Seeking the support of great men is conducive to greatness. Only great men see its real nature, not others, the inauspicious ones.

## \|\| 8.24.22 \|\|

*yādṛṅnaraṃ ca seveta tādṛśaṃ phalamaśnute \|*  
*mahatassevayocca tvaṃ kṣudrasya kṣudratāṃ tathā \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Man attains fruits in accordance with the nature of the man one serves. Loftiness is achieved by serving the great and meanness by serving the mean.

## \|\| 8.24.23 \|\|

*siṃhasya maṃdire sevā muktāphalakarī matā \|*  
*śṛgālamaṃdire sevā tvasthilābhakarī smṛtā \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Service in the abode of a lion is sure to yield pearls. Service in the hutment of a jackal is profitable only in yielding bones.

## \|\| 8.24.24 \|\|

*uttamānāṃ svabhāvoyaṃ paraduḥkhāsahiṣṇutā \|*  
*svayaṃ dukhaṃ ca saṃprāptaṃ manyatenyasya vāryate \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. This is the nature of the excellent people that they cannot bear others’ misery. They consider others’ misery their own and try to eradicate it.

## \|\| 8.24.25 \|\|

*vṛkṣāśca hāṭakaṃ caiva caṃdanaṃ cekṣukastathā \|*  
*ete bhuvi parārthe ca dakṣā evaṃ na kecana \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Trees, gold, sandal paste and the sugarcane exist for the enjoyment of the people. Men similarly competent are very rare in the world.

## \|\| 8.24.26 \|\|

*dayāluramadasparśa upakārī jitendriyaḥ \|*  
*etaiśca puṇyastambhaistu caturbhirdhāryyate mahī \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. A kind man, a man unpuffed by arrogance a person habitually helping others and a person with perfect control over his sense-organs, these are the four holy pillars that support the earth.

## \|\| 8.24.27 \|\|

*tataśca gautamastatra jalaṃ prāpya sudurlabham \|*  
*nityanaimittikaṃ karma cakāra vidhivattadā \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Having secured rare water thus, Gautama performed his routine and ceremonial rites duly.

## \|\| 8.24.28 \|\|

*tato vrīhīnyavāṃścaiva nīvārānapyanekadhā \|*  
*vāpayāmāsa tatraiva havanārthaṃ munīśvaraḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Thereafter he sowed different food-grains and barleys there itself for the performance of his sacrifice.

## \|\| 8.24.29 \|\|

*dhānyāni vividhānīha vṛkṣāśca vividhāstathā \|*  
*puṣpāṇi ca phalānyeva hyāsaṃstatrāyanekaśaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. Different kinds of food-grains, trees, flowers and fruits were produced in plenty there.

## \|\| 8.24.30 \|\|

*tacchutvā ṛṣayaścānye tatrāyā tāssahasraśaḥ \|*  
*paśavaḥ pakṣiṇaścānye jīvāśca bahavo'gaman \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. On hearing of it, the sages came there in thousands. Many beasts, birds and other living beings flocked there.

## \|\| 8.24.31 \|\|

*tadvanaṃ sundaraṃ hyāsītpṛthivyāṃ maṃḍale param \|*  
*tadakṣayakarāyogādanāvṛṣṭirna duḥkhadā \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. The forest became very beautiful, the most excellent on the surface of the world. In view of this imperishable contact the drought was not distressing.

## \|\| 8.24.32 \|\|

*ṛṣayo'pi vane tatra śubhakarmaparāyaṇāḥ \|*  
*vāsaṃ cakruraneke ca śiṣyabhāryyāsutānvitāḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. The sages in the forest were engaged in auspicious rites. They stayed there along with their disciples, children and womenfolk.

## \|\| 8.24.33 \|\|

*dhānyā ni vāpayāmāsuḥ kālakramaṇahetave \|*  
*ānaṃdastadvane hyāsītprabhāvādgautamasya ca \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. They sowed food-grains for their livelihood. There was great joy and bliss in that forest, thanks to the power of Gautama.

## Chapter 25 - The planned arrangement of Gautama

## \|\| 8.25.1 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*kadācidgautamenaiva jalārthaṃ preṣitā nijāḥ \|*  
*śiṣyāstatra gatā bhaktyā kamaṃḍalukarā dvijāḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. O brahmins, once Gautama sent his disciples there for water. Carrying out his orders they reached the spot with the water-pots in their hands.

## \|\| 8.25.2 \|\|

*śiṣyāñjalasamīpe tu gatāndṛṣṭvā nyaṣedhayan \|*  
*jalārthamagatāṃstatra carṣipatnyopyanekaśaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Many of the womenfolk who had assembled there for water saw the disciples of Gautama who had come for water. They prevented them from taking water.

## \|\| 8.25.3 \|\|

*ṛṣipatnyo vayaṃ pūrvaṃ grahīṣyāmo vidūrataḥ \|*  
*paścāccaiva jalaṃ grāhyamityevaṃ paryabhartsayan \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. “We are the wives of the sages. We shall take water first. You stand far off. You can take water afterwards.” Thus they rebuked them.

## \|\| 8.25.4-5 \|\|

*parāvṛtya tadā taiśca ṛṣipatnyai niveditam \|*  
*sā cāpi tānsamādāya samāśvāsya ca taiḥ svayam \|\| 4 \|\|*

*jalaṃ nītvā dadau tasmai gautamāya tapasvinī \|*  
*nityaṃ nirvāhayāmāsa jalena ṛṣisattamaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

4-5. The disciples returned and informed the wife of the sage Gautama. The wife of the sage took them with her, pacified those women, herself took water and gave it to Gautama. The excellent sage performed the routine rites with that water.

## \|\| 8.25.6 \|\|

*tāścaivamṛṣipatnyastu kruddhāstāṃ paryabhartsayan \|*  
*parāvṛtya gatāssarvāstūṭajānkuṭilāśayāḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. The other women, the wives of the sages, became infuriated and rebuked her. They returned to their huts with evil intentions lurking in their minds.

## \|\| 8.25.7 \|\|

*svāmyagre viparītaṃ ca tadvṛttaṃ nikhilaṃ tataḥ \|*  
*duṣṭāśayābhiḥ strībhiśca tābhirvai viniveditam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. The entire incident was distorted and intimated to their husbands by those evil-minded women.

## \|\| 8.25.8 \|\|

*atha tāsāṃ vacaḥ śrutvā bhāvikarmavaśāttadā \|*  
*gautamāya ca saṃkuddhāścāsaṃste paramarṣayaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. On hearing their words, the great sages became furious with Gautama as it was destined to be.

## \|\| 8.25.9 \|\|

*vighnārthaṃ gautamasyaiva nānāpūjopahārakaiḥ \|*  
*gaṇeśaṃ pūjayāmāsussaṃkuddhāste kubuddhayaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. The infuriated sages of crooked intellect worshipped Gaṇeśa with various articles of worship in order to put obstacle in the activity of Gautama.

## \|\| 8.25.10 \|\|

*āvirbabhūva ca tadā prasanno hi gaṇeśvaraḥ \|*  
*uvāca vacanaṃ tatra bhaktādhīnaḥ phalapradaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. The delighted Gaṇeśvara who is subservient to the devotees and who is the bestower of benefits appeared before them and said.

## \|\| 8.25.11 \|\|

*gaṇeśa uvāca \|*  
*prasanno'smi varaṃ brūta yūyaṃ kiṃ karavāṇyaham \|*  
*tadīyaṃ tadvacaḥ śrutvā ṛṣayaste'buvaṃstadā \|\| 11 \|\|*

Gaṇeśa said:—

11\. “I am pleased with you. Mention the boon you wish to choose. What shall I do?” On hearing his words the sages said then.

## \|\| 8.25.12 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*tvayā yadi varo deyo gautamassvāśramādbahiḥ \|*  
*niṣkāsyaṃ no ṛṣibhiḥ paribhartsya tathā kuru \|\| 12 \|\|*

The sages said:—

12\. If the boon is to be given by you, let Gautama be turned out of his hermitage after being rebuked by the sages. Please do so.

## \|\| 8.25.13 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*sa evaṃ prārthitastaistu vihasya vacanaṃ punaḥ \|*  
*provācebhamukhaḥ prītyā bodhayaṃstānsatāṃ gatiḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

13\. On being requested thus, the elephant-faced deity, the goal of the good, laughed and said gently enlightening them.

## \|\| 8.25.14 \|\|

*gaṇeśa uvāca \|*  
*śrūyatāmṛṣayassarve yuktaṃ na kriyate'dhunā \|*  
*aparādhaṃ vinā tasmai krudhyatāṃ hānireva ca \|\| 14 \|\|*

Gaṇeśa said:—

14\. May this be heard, O sages. This is not proper. Those who become furious without any offence are sure to incur loss.

## \|\| 8.25.15 \|\|

*upaskṛtaṃ purā yaistu tebhyo duḥkhaṃ hitaṃ na hi \|*  
*yadā ca dīyate duḥkhaṃ tadā nāśo bhavediha \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. According misery to the people who had rendered help before is not beneficial. If it is carried out, ruin will surely follow.

## \|\| 8.25.16 \|\|

*īdṛśaṃ ca tapaḥ kṛtvā sādhyate phalamuttamam \|*  
*śubhaṃ phalaṃ svayaṃ hitvā sādhyate nāhitaṃ punaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Excellent results are sought after performing, penance. No harm is sought after voluntarily eschewing auspicious results.

## \|\| 8.25.17 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityevaṃ vacanaṃ śrutvā tasya te munisattamāḥ \|*  
*buddhimohaṃ tadā prāptā idameva vaco'bruvan \|\| 17 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

17\. Even after hearing these words, the excellent sages, still deluded in their intellect, spoke these words.

## \|\| 8.25.18 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*kartavyaṃ hi tvayā svāminnidameva na cānyathā \|*  
*ityuktastu tadā devo gaṇeśo vākyamabravīt \|\| 18 \|\|*

The sages said:—

18\. “O lord, this must be done by you. Not otherwise.” Gaṇeśa who was thus implored spoke.

## \|\| 8.25.19 \|\|

*gaṇeśa uvāca \|*  
*asādhussādhutāṃ caiva sādhuścāsādhutāṃ tathā \|*  
*kadācidapi nāpnoti brahmoktamiti niścitam \|\| 19 \|\|*

Gaṇeśa said:—

19\. This has been decisively said by Brahmā—“A wicked man never attains saintly nature nor docs a saintly man assume perversion at any time.”

## \|\| 8.25.20 \|\|

*yadā ca bhavatāṃ duḥkhaṃ jātaṃ cānaśanātpurā \|*  
*tadā sukhaṃ pradattaṃ vai gautamena maharṣiṇā \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Formerly when you were distressed by starvation, the sage Gautama afforded you happiness.

## \|\| 8.25.21 \|\|

*idānīṃ vai bhavadbhiśca tasmai duḥkhaṃ pradīyate \|*  
*netadyuktatamaṃ loke sarvathā suvicāryatām \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Now you intend to inflict misery on him. This is not proper. Please think over this in all aspects.

## \|\| 8.25.22 \|\|

*strībalānmohitā yūyaṃ na me vākyaṃ kariṣyatha \|*  
*etaddhitatamaṃ tasya bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Deluded by your women-folk you never cared to act by my advice. There is no doubt that my advice will be beneficial to him at the end.

## \|\| 8.25.23 \|\|

*punaścāyamṛṣiśreṣṭho dāsyate vassukhaṃ dhruvam \|*  
*tāraṇaṃ na ca yuktaṃ syādvaramanyaṃ vṛṇīta vai \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Certainly the excellent sage will render happiness unto you. Deception is not proper. Please choose another boon.

## \|\| 8.25.24 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityevaṃ vacanaṃ tena gaṇeśena mahātmanā \|*  
*yadyapyuktamṛṣibhyaśca tadapyete na menire \|\| 24 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

24\. Although this advice was given by Gaṇeśa, the great Ātman, still the sage did not pay heed.

## \|\| 8.25.25 \|\|

*bhaktādhīnatayā sotha śivaputrobravīttadā \|*  
*udāsīnena manasā tānṛṣīnduṣṭaśemuṣīn \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Then Gaṇeśa, who is subservient to hīs devotees, spoke indifferently to those sages of evil intent.

## \|\| 8.25.26 \|\|

*gaṇeśa uvāca \|*  
*bhavadbhiḥ prārthyate yacca kariṣye'haṃ tathā khalu \|*  
*paścādbhāvi bhavedeva ityuktvāṃtardadhe punaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

Gaṇeśa said:—

26\. I shall do what is being requested by you all. What is destined to happen will surely happen. After saying this he vanished.

## \|\| 8.25.27 \|\|

*gautamassa na jānāti munīnāṃ vai durāśayam \|*  
*ānandamanasā nityaṃ patnyā karma cakāra tat \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Gautama did not know the evil intention of the sages. He went on performing his routine along with his wife with delighted mind.

## \|\| 8.25.28 \|\|

*tadantare ca yajjātaṃ caritaṃ varayogataḥ \|*  
*tadduṣṭarṣiprabhāvāttu śrūyatāṃ tanmunīśvarāḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. O great sages, hear what happened in the meantime as a result of the influence of those wicked sages by the granting of the boon.

## \|\| 8.25.29 \|\|

*gautamasya ca kedāre tatrāsanvrīhayo yavāḥ \|*  
*gaṇeśastatra gaurbhūtvā jagāma kila durbalā \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. There were food-grains and barleys in Gautama’s field. Gaṇeśa assumed the form of a feeble cow and went there.

## \|\| 8.25.30 \|\|

*kaṃpamānā ca sā gatvā tatra tadvarayogataḥ \|*  
*vrīhīnsaṃbhakṣayāmāsa yavāṃśca munisattamāḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. O excellent sages, staggering and faltering, the cow, as a result of the boon began to eat the food-grains and barleys.

## \|\| 8.25.31 \|\|

*etasminnantare daivādgautamastatra cāgataḥ \|*  
*sa dayālustṛṇastaṃmbairvārayāmāsa tāṃ tadā \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. In the meantime Gautama happened to come there casually. Compassionate by nature, he warded off the cow with a bunch of grass blades.

## \|\| 8.25.32 \|\|

*tṛṇastaṃbena sā spṛṣṭā papāta pṛthivītale \|*  
*mṛtā ca tatkṣaṇādeva tadṛṣeḥ paśyatastadā \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. No sooner was it touched by the blades of grass than the cow fell down dead even as the sage was watching it.

## \|\| 8.25.33 \|\|

*ṛṣayaśchannarūpāste ṛṣipatnyastathāśubhāḥ \|*  
*ūcustatra tadā sarve kiṃ kṛtaṃ gautamena ca \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. The sages and their wicked wives who were hiding there began to shout, “O see, what is done by Gautama.”

## \|\| 8.25.34 \|\|

*gautamo'pi tathāhalyāmāhūyāsītsuvismitaḥ \|*  
*uvāca duḥkhato viprā dūyamānena cetasā \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. O brahmins, the bewildered Gautama called Ahalyā and spoke with a dejected, distressed mind.

## \|\| 8.25.35 \|\|

*gautama uvāca \|*  
*kiṃ jātaṃ ca kathaṃ devi kupitaḥ parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*kiṃ kartavyaṃ kva gantavyaṃ hatyā ca samupasthitā \|\| 35 \|\|*

Gautama said:—

35\. O gentle lady, what has happened? How did it happen? Certainly lord Śiva is angry. What should be dene? Where shall we go? The sin of slaughter has befallen me.

## \|\| 8.25.36 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|\|etasminnantare vipro gautamaṃ paryabhartsayan \|*  
*viprapatnyastathā'halyāṃ durvacobhirvyathāṃ daduḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

36\. In the meantime the brahmins reproached Gautama. The wives of the brahmins tormented Ahalyā with threats.

## \|\| 8.25.37 \|\|

*durbuddhayaśca tacchiṣyāssutāsteṣāṃ tathaiva ca \|*  
*gautama paribhartsyaiva pratyūcurdhigvaco muhuḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. The evil-minded disciples and sons of the brahmins reviled at Gautama repeating the words, “Fie on you.”

## \|\| 8.25.38 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*mukhaṃ na darśanīyaṃ te gamyatāṃ gamyatāmiti \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā goghnamukhaṃ sadyassacailaṃ snānamācaret \|\| 38 \|\|*

The sages said:—

38\. Your face is not fit to be seen. Go hence. If a person sees the face of a cow-killer, he shall take bath in full dress.

## \|\| 8.25.39 \|\|

*yāvadāśramamadhye tvaṃ tāvadeva havirbhujaḥ \|*  
*pitaraśca na gṛhṇaṃti hyasmaddattaṃ hi kiñcana \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. As long as you stay in the midst of the hermitage, the sacrificial fires and the manes will not accept our gifts.

## \|\| 8.25.40 \|\|

*tasmādgacchānyatastvaṃ ca parivārasamanvitaḥ \|*  
*vilambaṃ kuru naiva tvaṃ dhenuhanpāpakāraka \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Hence, O sinner, O slayer of a cow, go somewhere else along with your followers. Do not delay.

## \|\| 8.25.41 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā te ca taṃ sarve pāṣāṇaissamatāḍayan \|*  
*vyathāṃ daduratīvāsmai tvahalyāṃ ca duruktibhiḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

41\. Saying this, they pelted hīm with stones. They inflicted pain on him and Ahalyā with insulting words.

## \|\| 8.25.42 \|\|

*tāḍito bhartsito duṣṭairgautamo giramabravīt \|*  
*ito gacchāmi munayo hyanyatra nivasāmyaham \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Beaten and reproachad by the wicked persons Gautama spoke these words—“O sages, I am going away from here. I shall stay somewhere else.

## \|\| 8.25.43 \|\|

*ityuktvā gautamastasmātsthānācca nirgatastadā \|*  
*gatvā krośaṃ tadā cakre hyāśramaṃ tadanujñayā \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. After saying this, Gautama went out of that place. Going a krośa he made his hermitage there with their permission.

## \|\| 8.25.44 \|\|

*yāvaccaivābhiśāpo vai tāvatkāryyaṃ na kiṃcana \|*  
*na karmaṇyadhikāro'sti daive pitrye'tha vaidike \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. As long as there is this curse, no rite shall be performed. You are not authorised in any of the rites of god, manes or the Vedic rituals.

## \|\| 8.25.45 \|\|

*māsārdhaṃ ca tato nītvā munīnsaṃprārthayattadā \|*  
*gautamo munivaryyassa tena duḥkhena dukhitaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. The excellent sage Gautama excessively distressed by this regrettable incident spent half of month thus and then requested.

## \|\| 8.25.46 \|\|

*gautama uvāca \|*  
*anukaṃpyo bhavadbhiśca kathyatāṃ kriyate mayā \|*  
*yathā madīyaṃ pāpaṃ ca gacchatviti nivedyatām \|\| 46 \|\|*

Gautama said:—

46\. I am to be pitied by you. Please tell me how my sin can be wiped off. I shall do as you bid me.

## \|\| 8.25.47 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktāste tadā viprā nocuścaiva parasparam \|*  
*atyaṃtaṃ sevayā pṛṣṭā militā hyekatassthitāḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

47\. Thus requested the brahmins did not say anything. When they were implored humbly they met separately and remained aloof.

## \|\| 8.25.48 \|\|

*gautamo dūrataḥ sthitvā natvā tānṛṣisattamān \|*  
*papraccha vinayāviṣṭaḥ kiṃ kāryaṃ hi mayādhunā \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Gautama stood far off. Bowing down to the sages he humbly asked them what he should do.

## \|\| 8.25.49 \|\|

*ityukte muninā tena gautamena mahātmanā \|*  
*militāssakalāste vai munayo vākyamabruvan \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. When this was said by Gautama of noble soul, the sages spoke these words.

## \|\| 8.25.50 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*niṣkṛtiṃ hi vinā śuddhirjāyate na kadācana \|*  
*tasmāttvaṃ dehaśuddhyarthaṃ prāyaścittaṃ samācara \|\| 50 \|\|*

The sages said:—

50\. “Without expiation purity cannot be attained. Hence, for the sake of purification you shall perform the rites of atonement.

## \|\| 8.25.51 \|\|

*trivāraṃ pṛthivīṃ sarvāṃ krama pāpaṃ prakāśayan \|*  
*punarāgatya cātraiva cara māsavrataṃ tathā \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Go round the earth three times announcing your sin. After returning to this place perform the holy Vrata for a month.

## \|\| 8.25.52 \|\|

*śatamekottaraṃ caiva brahmaṇo'sya girestathā \|*  
*prakramaṇaṃ vidhāyaivaṃ śuddhiste ca bhaviṣyati \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. If you go round this mountain Brahmagiri hundred and one times you can attain purity.

## \|\| 8.25.53 \|\|

*athavā tvaṃ samānīya gaṃgāsnānaṃ samācara \|*  
*pārthivānāṃ tathā koṭiṃ kṛtvā devaṃ niṣevaya \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. Or you fetch water from Gaṅgā and perform the ceremonial ablution. Propitiate lord Śiva making a crore of his phallic images.

## \|\| 8.25.54 \|\|

*gaṃgāyāṃ ca tataḥ snātvā punaścaiva bhaviṣyati \|*  
*purā daśa tathā caikaṃ girestvaṃ kramaṇaṃ kuru \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. At the outset, you circumambulate this mountain eleven times. Taking bath in Gaṅgā you will become pure thereafter.

## \|\| 8.25.55 \|\|

*śata kuṃbhaistathā snātvā pārthivaṃ niṣkṛtirbhavet \|*  
*iti tairṣibhiḥ proktastathetyomiti tadvacaḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Performing the ablution of the earthen phallic image of Śiva with a hundred potful of water, the expiatory rite will be completed.” When he was thus urged by the sages he said in the affirmative.

## \|\| 8.25.56 \|\|

*pārthivānāṃ tathā pūjāṃ gireḥ prakramaṇaṃ tathā \|*  
*kariṣyāmi muniśreṣṭhā ājñayā śrīmatāmiha \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. O excellent sages, at the behest of your honours I shall worship the earthen phallic image of Śiva and also circumambulate the mountain.

## \|\| 8.25.57 \|\|

*ityuktvā sarṣivaryaśca kṛtvā prakramaṇaṃ gireḥ \|*  
*pūjayāmāsa nirmāya pārthivānmunisattamaḥ \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. After saying this and circumambulating the mountain, the excellent sage made earthen phallic images of Śiva and worshipped them.

## \|\| 8.25.58 \|\|

*ahalyā ca tatassādhvī tacca sarvaṃ cakāra sā \|*  
*śiṣyāśca pratiśiṣyāśca cakrussevāṃ tayostadā \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. The chaste lady Ahalyā too acted accordingly. Their disciples and the disciples of their disciples rendered service to them.

## Chapter 26 - The greatness of Tryambakeśvara

## \|\| 8.26.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*evaṃ kṛte tu ṛṣiṇā sastrīkena dvijāśśivaḥ \|*  
*āvirbabhūva sa śivaḥ prasannassagaṇastadā \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. O brahmins, when all this was performed thus by the sage along with his wife, the delighted Śiva accompanied by his Gaṇas appeared before him.

## \|\| 8.26.2 \|\|

*atha prasannassa śivo varaṃ brūhi mahāmune \|*  
*prasanno'haṃ subhaktyā ta ityuvāca kṛpānidhiḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Then the gladdened Śiva, the storehouse of mercy said—“O great sage, mention the boon you wish to choose. I am delighted with your devotion.”

## \|\| 8.26.3 \|\|

*tadā tatsuṃdaraṃ rūpaṃ dṛṣṭvā śaṃbhormahātmanaḥ \|*  
*praṇamya śaṃkaraṃ bhaktyā stutiṃ cakre mudānvitaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. On seeing, the beautiful form of Śiva, the supreme soul, the sage bowed to Śiva with devotion and eulogised him joyously.

## \|\| 8.26.4 \|\|

*stutvā bahu praṇamyeśaṃ baddhāñjalipuṭaḥ sthitaḥ \|*  
*niṣpāpaṃ kuru māṃ devābravīditi sa gautamaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. After eulogising and bowing to him many times, Gautama stood there with palms joined in reverence and said—“O lord, make me sinless.”

## \|\| 8.26.5 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityākarṇya vacastasya gautamasya mahātmanaḥ \|*  
*suprasannataro bhūtvā śivo vākyamupādade \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. On hearing these words of the noble soul Gautama, Śiva became much delighted and spoke.

## \|\| 8.26.6 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*dhanyo'si kṛtakṛtyo'si niṣpāpo'si sadā mune \|*  
*etairduṣṭaiḥ kila tvaṃ ca cchalito'si khilātmabhiḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

6\. O sage, you are blessed. You shall be contented. You are already sinless. You have been deceived by these wicked knaves.

## \|\| 8.26.7 \|\|

*tvadīyadarśanāllokā niṣpāpāśca bhavaṃti hi \|*  
*kiṃ punastvaṃ sapāpo'si madbhaktiniratassadā \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Indeed, on seeing you, people become sinless. How can you be a sinner? You are always engaged in devotion to me.

## \|\| 8.26.8 \|\|

*upadravastvayi mune yaiḥ kṛtastu durātmabhiḥ \|*  
*te pāpāśca durācārā hatyāvaṃtasta eva hi \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. O sage, indeed, all the wicked persons, by whom you have been harassed, are sinners, evil-minded and murder-stained.

## \|\| 8.26.9 \|\|

*eteṣāṃ darśanādanye pāpiṣṭhāḥ saṃbhavaṃtu ca \|*  
*kṛtaghnāśca tathā jātā naiteṣāṃ niṣkṛtiḥ kvacit \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Others too are likely to be sinners on seeing these. They have become already ungrateful. There is no way of atonement for them.

## \|\| 8.26.10 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā śaṃkarastasmai teṣāṃ duścaritaṃ tadā \|*  
*bahūvāca prabhurviprāḥ satkado'satsu daṃḍadaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

10\. After saying this, O brahmins, lord Śiva, the bestower of happiness to the good and chastiser of the wicked told him their evil actions in detail.

## \|\| 8.26.11 \|\|

*śarvoktamiti sa śrutvā suvismitamanā ṛṣiḥ \|*  
*supraṇamya śivaṃ bhaktyā sāṃjaliḥ punarabravīt \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. On hearing the words of Śiva, the sage was greatly surprised. After bowing to him with devotion) he spoke again to him with palms joined in reverence.

## \|\| 8.26.12 \|\|

*gautama uvāca \|*  
*ṛṣibhistairmaheśāna hyupakāraḥ kṛto mahān \|*  
*yadyevaṃ na kṛtaṃ taistu darśanaṃ te kuto bhavet \|\| 12 \|\|*

Gautama said:—

12\. O lord Śiva, a great help has been rendered by these sages. If they had not done like this, how could I have had your vision?

## \|\| 8.26.13 \|\|

*dhanyāste ṛṣayo yaistu mahyaṃ śubhataraṃ kṛtam \|*  
*taddurācaraṇādeva mama svārtho mahānabhūt \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Blessed indeed are the sages who have pursued this auspicious activity towards me. It is by their so-called evil action that my great self-interest is made possible;

## \|\| 8.26.14 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityevaṃ tadvacaśśrutvā suprasanno maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*gautamaṃ pratyuvācāśu kṛpādṛṣṭyā vilokya ca \|\| 14 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

14\. On hearing his words, lord Śiva became all the more delighted. Looking at Gautama mercifully he replied,

## \|\| 8.26.15 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*ṛṣi dhanyosi vipreṃdra ṛṣe śreṣṭhataro'si vai \|*  
*jñātvā māṃ suprasannaṃ hi vṛṇu tvaṃ varamuttamam \|\| 15 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

15\. O leading brahmin, you are a blessed sage. You are the most excellent among sages. Realising that I am highly delighted, you choose the excellent boon.

## \|\| 8.26.16-17 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*gautamo'pi vicāryaiva loke viśrutamityuta \|*  
*anyathā na bhavedeva tasmāduktaṃ samācaret \|\| 16 \|\|*

*niścityaivaṃ muniśreṣṭho gautamaśśivabhaktimān \|*  
*sāṃjalirnataśīrṣo hi śaṃkaraṃ vākyamabravīt \|\| 17 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

16-17. Gautama thought—“It is thus that the scandal was spread in the world. Otherwise it would not have been so. Hence I must do what is proper”. After thinking like this, the excellent sage Gautama, devoted to Śiva, spoke to Śiva with palms joined in reverence and head bent down.

## \|\| 8.26.18 \|\|

*gautama uvāca \|*  
*satyaṃ nātha bravīṣi tvaṃ tathāpi paṃcabhiḥ kṛtam \|*  
*nānyathā bhavatītyatra yajjātaṃ jāyatāṃ tu tat \|\| 18 \|\|*

Gautama said:—

18\. O lord, what you say is true. Still what is done by the five leading persons cannot be otherwise. So, let what has happened happen.

## \|\| 8.26.19 \|\|

*yadi prasanno deveśa gaṃgā ca dīyatāṃ mama \|*  
*kuru lokopakāraṃ hi namaste'stu namo'stu te \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. O lord of gods, if you are delighted, may Gaṅgā be given to me. Render help to the worlds. Obeisance be to you.

## \|\| 8.26.20 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā vacanaṃ tasya dhṛtvā vai pādapaṃkajam \|*  
*namaścakāra deveśaṃ gautamo lokakāmyayā \|\| 20 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

20\. After saying these words and grasping his lotus-like feet, Gautama made obeisance to the lord of gods with a desire for the welfare of the worlds.

## \|\| 8.26.21-22 \|\|

*tatastu śaṃkaro devaḥ pṛthivyāśca divaśca saḥ \|*  
*sāraṃ caiva samuddhṛtya rakṣitaṃ pūrvameva tat \|\| 21 \|\|*

*vivāhe brahmaṇā dattamavaśiṣṭaṃ ca kiṃcana \|*  
*tattasmai dattavāñcchaṃbhurmunaye bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

21-22. The essence of the earth and heaven had been extracted of yore, a part of this had been given to the sage by Brahmā at his marriage. What had been held over, lord Śiva favourably disposed towards his devotees, now gave to the sage.

## \|\| 8.26.23 \|\|

*gaṃgājalaṃ tadā tatra strīrūpamabhavatparam \|*  
*tasyāścaiva ṛṣiśreṣṭhaḥ stutiṃ kṛtvā natiṃ vyadhāt \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Then the waters of Gaṅgā assumed the form of a lady and stood there. The excellent sage eulogised her and made obeisance.

## \|\| 8.26.24 \|\|

*gautama uvāca \|*  
*dhanyāsi kṛtakṛtyāsi pāvitaṃ bhuvanaṃ tvayā \|*  
*māṃ ca pāvaya gaṃge tvaṃ patataṃ niraye dhruvam \|\| 24 \|\|*

Gautama said:—

24\. O Gaṅgā, you are blessed. You are contented. The whole world has been sanctified by you. Sanctify me too. I am likely to fall into hell.

## \|\| 8.26.25 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*śaṃbhuścāpi tadovāca sarveṣāṃ hitakṛcchṛṇu \|*  
*gaṃge gautamamenaṃ tvaṃ pāvayasva madājñayā \|\| 25 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

25\. Śiva too then spoke—“O Gaṅgā, listen. You are beneficial to all. At my behest, sanctify Gautama also.”

## \|\| 8.26.26 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*iti śrutvā vacastasya śaṃbhośca gautamasya ca \|*  
*uvācaiva śivaṃ gaṃgā śivabhaktirhi pāvanī \|\| 26 \|\|*
