26\. On hearing these words of Śiva and Gautama, Gaṅgā, the holy Śakti of Śiva, spoke to Śiva.

## \|\| 8.26.27 \|\|

*gaṃgovāca \|*  
*ṛṣiṃ tu pāvayitvāhaṃ parivārayutaṃ prabho \|*  
*gamiṣyāmi nijasthānaṃ vacassatyaṃ bravīmi ha \|\| 27 \|\|*

Gaṅgā said:—

27\. O lord, after sanctifying the sage along with his followers, I shall return to my abode. I am telling you the truth.

## \|\| 8.26.28 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityukto gaṃgayā tatra maheśo bhaktavatsalaḥ \|*  
*lokopakaraṇārthāya punargagāṃ vaco'bravīt \|\| 28 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

28\. When this was mentioned by Gaṅgā, lord Śiva, favourably disposed towards his devotees, spoke again to Gaṅgā keeping in mind the welfare of the worlds.

## \|\| 8.26.29 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*tvayā sthātavyamatraiva vrajedyāvatkaliryugaḥ \|*  
*vaivasvato manurdevi hyaṣṭāviṃśattamo bhavet \|\| 29 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

29\. O goddess, till the advent of the Kali Yuga, when the son of Vivasvat shall be the twenty-eighth Manu, you shall stay here alone.

## \|\| 8.26.30 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*iti śrutvā vacastasya svāminaśśaṃkarasya tat \|*  
*pratyuvāca punargaṃgā pāvanī sā saridvarā \|\| 30 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

30\. On hearing these words of lord Śiva, Gaṅgā the sanctifying leading river replied.

## \|\| 8.26.31 \|\|

*gaṃgovāca \|*  
*māhātmyamadhikaṃ cetsyānmama svāminmaheśvara \|*  
*sarvebhyaśca tadā sthāsye dharāyāṃ tripurāntakaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

Gaṅgā said:—

31\. O lord Śiva, O slayer of the Tripuras, if my greatness were to surpass that of all, I shall stay on the earth.

## \|\| 8.26.32 \|\|

*kiṃ cānyacca śṛṇu svāminvapuṣā sundareṇa ha \|*  
*tiṣṭha tvaṃ matsamīpe vai sagaṇasāṃbikaḥ prabho \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. O lord, please listen to something more. O lord, please abide with me with this beautiful body of yours along with your consort and the Gaṇas.

## \|\| 8.26.33 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*evaṃ tasyā vacaḥ śrutvā śaṃkaro bhaktavatsalaḥ \|*  
*lokopakaraṇārthāya punargaṃgāṃ vacobravīt \|\| 33 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

33\. On hearing her words, Śiva favourably disposed towards his devotees, spoke again to Gaṅgā for rendering help to the worlds.

## \|\| 8.26.34 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*dhanyāsi śrūyatāṃ gaṃge hyahaṃ bhinnastvayā na hi \|*  
*tathāpi sthīyate hyatra sthīyatāṃ ca tvayāpi hi \|\| 34 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

34\. O Gaṅgā you are blessed. Listen. I am not distinct from you. Still I stay here. You too must stay.

## \|\| 8.26.35 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityevaṃ vacanaṃ śrutvā svāminaḥ parameśituḥ \|*  
*prasannamānasā bhūtvā gaṃgā ca pratyapūjayat \|\| 35 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

35\. On hearing these words of lord Śiva, Gaṅgā was delighted in the mind and she accepted his advice.

## \|\| 8.26.36-37 \|\|

*etasminnaṃtare devā ṛṣayaśca purātanāḥ \|*  
*sutārthānyapyanekāni kṣetrāṇi vividhāni ca \|\| 36 \|\|*

*āgatya gautamaṃ sarve gaṃgāṃ ca giriśaṃ tathā \|*  
*jayajayeti bhāṣaṃtaḥ pūjayāmāsurādarāt \|\| 37 \|\|*

36-37. In the meantime, the gods and sages, several holy centres and holy rivers came there. Proclaiming “Be victorious, be victorious”, they respectfully worshipped Gautama, Gaṅgā and Pārvatī.

## \|\| 8.26.38 \|\|

*tataste nirjarā sarve teṣāṃ cakruḥ stutiṃ mudā \|*  
*karān baddhvā nataskaṃdhā haribrahmādayastadā \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Thereafter Viṣṇu, Brahmā and other gods eulogised them joyously joining their palms and drooping down their shoulders.

## \|\| 8.26.39 \|\|

*gaṃgā prasannā tebhyaśca giriśaścocatustadā \|*  
*varaṃ brūta suraśreṣṭhā dadvo vaḥ priyakāmyayā \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Gaṅgā and Pārvatī were delighted and they said “O excellent gods, mention the boon you desire to choose. We shall grant the same with a desire to do what is pleasing to you.”

## \|\| 8.26.40 \|\|

*devā ūcuḥ \|*  
*yadi prasanno deveśa prasannā tvaṃ saridvare \|*  
*sthātavyamatra kṛpayā naḥ priyārthaṃ tathā nṛṇām \|\| 40 \|\|*

The gods said:—

40\. “O lord of gods, O leading, river, if you are delighted, you shall stay here taking pity on us and for the benefit of men.”

## \|\| 8.26.41 \|\|

*\|\| gaṃgovāca \|*  
*yūyaṃ sarvapriyārthaṃ ca tiṣṭhathātra na kiṃ punaḥ \|*  
*gautamaṃ kṣālayitvāhaṃ gamiṣyāmi yathāgatam \|\| 41 \|\|*

Gaṅgā said:—

41\. Why don't you stay here for the pleasure and welfare of all? After washing off the sin of Gautama I shall go back the way I have come.

## \|\| 8.26.42 \|\|

*bhavatsu me viśeṣotra jñeyaścaiva kathaṃ surāḥ \|*  
*tatpramāṇaṃ kṛtaṃ cetsyāttadā tiṣṭhāmyasaṃśayam \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. O gods, how may my speciality be known apart from yours? If you can prove the same, I shall undoubtedly stay here

## \|\| 8.26.43 \|\|

*\|\| sarve ūcuḥ \|*  
*siṃharāśau yadā syādvai gurussarvasuhṛttamaḥ \|*  
*tadā vayaṃ ca sarve tvāgamiṣyāmo na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

The gods said:—

43\. When Jupiter, the most friendly to all, comes to the zodiac of Leo all of us will come. There is no doubt about it.

## \|\| 8.26.44-45 \|\|

*ekādaśa ca varṣāṇi lokānāṃ pātakaṃ tviha \|*  
*kṣālitaṃ yadbhavedevaṃ malināssmaḥ saridvare \|\| 44 \|\|*

*tasyaiva kṣālanāya tvāyāsyāmassarvathā priye \|*  
*tvatsakāśaṃ mahādevi procyate satyamādarāt \|\| 45 \|\|*

44-45. O leading river, we will become unclean ourselves by cleaning off the sins of the people for eleven years. O beloved great goddess, we will come to you for washing that off in every respect. With respect we reveal this truth to you.

## \|\| 8.26.46 \|\|

*anugrahāya lokānāmasmākaṃ priyakāmyayā \|*  
*sthātavyaṃ śaṃkareṇāpi tvayā caiva saridvare \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. To bless the worlds, to do what is pleasing to us Śiva shall stay here and you too, O leading river.

## \|\| 8.26.47-48 \|\|

*yāvatsiṃhe guruścaiva sthāsyāmastāvadeva hi \|*  
*tvayi snānaṃ trikālaṃ ca śaṃkarasya ca darśanam \|\| 47 \|\|*

*kṛtvā svapāpaṃ nikhilaṃ vimokṣyāmo na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*svadeśāṃśca gamiṣyāmo bhavacchāsanato vayam \|\| 48 \|\|*

47-48. We shall stay here as long as Jupiter is in Leo. We shall take our bath thrice a day and visit Śiva. Then we shall get rid of our sins and with your permission return to our native lands.

## \|\| 8.26.49 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityevaṃ prārthitastaistu gautamena maharṣiṇā \|*  
*sthito'sau śaṃkaraḥ prītyā sthitā sā ca saridvarā \|\| 49 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

49\. Thus requested by them as well as by the sage Gautama, Śiva stayed there with pleasure. The excellent river Gaṅgā too stayed.

## \|\| 8.26.50 \|\|

*sā gaṃgā gautamī nāmnā liṃgaṃ tryaṃbakamīritam \|*  
*khyātā khyātaṃ babhūvātha mahāpātakanāśanam \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Gaṅgā there became famous as Gautamī. The phallic image became famous by the name Tryambaka. Both are destructive of great sins.

## \|\| 8.26.51 \|\|

*taddinaṃ hi samārabhya siṃhasthe ca bṛhaspatau \|*  
*āyāṃti sarvatīrthāni kṣetrāṇi devatāni ca \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. From that time onwards whenever Jupiter enters Leo, the holy rivers, sacred centres and the gods come there.

## \|\| 8.26.52 \|\|

*sarāṃsi puṣkarādīni gaṃgādyāssaritastathā \|*  
*vāsudevādayo devāḥ saṃti vai gotamītaṭe \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. The holy lakes Puṣkara, and others, the sacred river Gaṅgā and others, Vāsudeva and other gods stay on the banks of the Gautamī river.

## \|\| 8.26.53 \|\|

*yāvattatra sthitānīha tāvatteṣāṃ phalaṃ na hi \|*  
*svapradeśe samāyātāstarhyeteṣāṃ phalaṃ bhavet \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. While they are here they lose sacredness in their original places. They will have it back when they return there.

## \|\| 8.26.54 \|\|

*jyotirliṃgamidaṃ proktaṃ tryaṃbakaṃ nāma viśrutam \|*  
*sthitaṃ taṭe hi gautamyā mahāpātakanāśanam \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. This is a Jyotirliṅga famous as Tryambaka. It is stationed on the banks of the Gautamī destructive of great sins.

## \|\| 8.26.55 \|\|

*yaḥ paśyedbhaktito jyotirliṃgaṃ tryaṃbakanāmakam \|*  
*pūjayetpraṇametstutvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. He who sees the Jyotirliṅga Tryambaka with devotion and worships with obeisance after eulogising it is freed from all sins.

## \|\| 8.26.56 \|\|

*jyotirliṃgaṃ tryaṃbakaṃ hi pūjitaṃ gautamena ha \|*  
*sarvakāmapradaṃ cātra paratra paramuktidam \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. This very Jyotirliṅga Tryambaka was worshipped by Gautama. Being worshipped it yields all desires here and bestows liberation hereafter.

## \|\| 8.26.57 \|\|

*iti vaśca samākhyātaṃ yatpṛṣṭo'haṃ munīśvarāḥ \|*  
*kimanyadicchatha śrotuṃ tad brūyāṃ vo na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. O great sages, thus what I have been asked has been narrated to you all. What else do you wish to hear? I shall explain it to you. No doubt need be entertained in this respect.

## Chapter 27 - The greatness of the Jyotirliṅga Tryambakeśvara

## \|\| 8.27.1 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*gaṃgā ca jalarūpeṇa kuto jātā vada prabho \|*  
*tanmāhātmyaṃ viśeṣeṇa kuto jāta vada prabho \|\| 1 \|\|*

The sages said:—

1\. O holy lord, whence did Gaṅgā originate in the form of water? How could it attain its greatness? Please narrate.

## \|\| 8.27.2 \|\|

*yairviprairgautamāyeva duḥkhaṃ dattaṃ durātmabhiḥ \|*  
*teṣāṃ kiṃca tato jātamucyatāṃ vyāsa sadguro \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. What happened to those wicked brahmins who tormented Gautama? O sage with Vyāsa as your good preceptor, please narrate.

## \|\| 8.27.3 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*evaṃ saṃprārthitā gaṃgā gautamena tadā svayam \|*  
*brahmaṇaśca girerviprā drutaṃ tasmādavātarat \|\| 3 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

3\. O brahmins, Gaṅgā who was thus requested by Gautama immediately descended from the mountain Brahma-giri.

## \|\| 8.27.4 \|\|

*auduṃbarasya śākhāyāstatpravāho vinissṛtaḥ \|*  
*tatra snānaṃ mudā cakre gautamo viśruto muniḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Its current flowed from the branches of an Udumbara tree. Gautama, the famous sage, took his bath joyously.

## \|\| 8.27.5 \|\|

*gautamasya ca ye śiṣyā anye caiva maharṣayaḥ \|*  
*samāgatāśca te tatra snānaṃ cakrurmudānvitāḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Gautama’s disciples and other sages too came there and joyously took their bath.

## \|\| 8.27.6 \|\|

*gaṃgādvāraṃ ca tannāma prasiddhamabhavattadā \|*  
*sarvapāpaharaṃ ramyaṃ darśanānmunisattamaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. O excellent sages, the place became well known as Gaṅgādvāra. It is beautiful and its sight dispels all sins.

## \|\| 8.27.7 \|\|

*gautamasparddhinaste ca ṛṣayastatra cāgatāḥ \|*  
*snānārthaṃ tāṃśca sā dṛṣṭvā hyaṃtardhānaṃ gatā drutam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. The sages who were malicious towards Gautama came there to take their bath; but on seeing them Gaṅgā vanished immediately.

## \|\| 8.27.8 \|\|

*māmeti gautamastatra vyājahāra vaco drutam \|*  
*muhurmuhuḥ stuvan gaṃgāṃ sāṃjalirnatamastakaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Then Gautama hastened to that place and eulogised Gaṅgā again and again with palms joined in reverence and head bent down. He then spoke.

## \|\| 8.27.9 \|\|

*gautama uvāca \|*  
*ime ca śrīmadāṃdhāśca sādhavo vāpyasādhavaḥ \|*  
*etatpuṇyaprabhāveṇa darśanaṃ dīyatāṃ tvayā \|\| 9 \|\|*

Gautama said:—

9\. Whether these are blind in their arrogance due to their affluence, whether they are good or bad, you shall grant them your vision by the virtue of this holy region.

## \|\| 8.27.10 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*tato vāṇī samutpannā gaṃgāyā vyomamaṃḍalāt \|*  
*tacchṛṇudhvamṛṣiśreṣṭhā gaṃgāvacanamuttamam \|\| 10 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

10\. O excellent sages, then a voice arose from the firmament. Please listen to the excellent words that Gaṅgā spoke.

## \|\| 8.27.11 \|\|

*ete duṣṭatamāścaiva kṛtaghnāḥ svāmidrohiṇaḥ \|*  
*jālmāḥ pākhaṃḍinaścaiva draṣṭuṃ varjyāśca sarvadā \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. “These are the most wicked sages, most ungrateful and traitors to their lord. They are knaves and blasphemous heretics. They are unworthy of sight.”

## \|\| 8.27.12 \|\|

*gautama uvāca \|*  
*mātaśca śrūyatāmetanmahatā gira eva ca \|*  
*tasmāttvayā ca karttavyaṃ satyaṃ ca bhagavadvacaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

Gautama said:—

12\. “Mother please hear the words of the great lord which you shall make truthful.

## \|\| 8.27.13 \|\|

*apakāriṣu yo loka upakāraṃ karoti vai \|*  
*tena pūto bhavāmyatra bhagavadvacanaṃ tvidam \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. The lord has said, “I become sanctified by the person who in this world helps those who harm him."

## \|\| 8.27.14 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*iti śrutvā munervākyaṃ gautamasya mahātmanaḥ \|*  
*punarvāṇī samutpannā gaṃgāyā vyomamaṃḍalāt \|\| 14 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

14\. On hearing the words of the sage Gautama the great Ātman, the voice rose again from the firmament above Gaṅgā.

## \|\| 8.27.15 \|\|

*kathyate hi tvayā satyaṃ gautamarṣe śivaṃ vacaḥ \|*  
*tathāpi saṃgrahārtha ca prāyaścitaṃ caraṃtu vai \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. “O sage Gautama, what is said by you is true. These are auspicious words. Still let them perform expiatory rites in brief.

## \|\| 8.27.16 \|\|

*śatamekottaraṃ cātra kāryyaṃ prakramaṇaṃ gireḥ \|*  
*bhavacchāsanatastvetaistvadadhīnairviśeṣataḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. At your behest and particularly under your guidance, they shall circumambulate this mountain.

## \|\| 8.27.17 \|\|

*tataścaivādhikāraśca jāyate duṣṭakāriṇām \|*  
*maddarśane viśeṣeṇa satyamuktaṃ mayā mune \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Then these wicked persons will be authorised in having a vision of mine. O sage, this truth I mention to you.

## \|\| 8.27.18 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*iti śrutvā vacastasyāścakrurvai te tathā'khilāḥ \|*  
*saṃprārthya gautamaṃ dīnāḥ kṣaṃtavyo no'parādhakaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. On hearing these words, the sages did so duly after dejectedly requesting Gautama, “Our offence shall be excused.”

## \|\| 8.27.19 \|\|

*evaṃ kṛte tadā tena gautamena tadājñayā \|*  
*kuśāvartaṃ nāma cakre gaṅgādvārādadhogatam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. When they did so, a ditch named Kuiāvarta just beneath Gaṅgādvāra was made by Gautama with her permission.

## \|\| 8.27.20 \|\|

*tataḥ prādurabhūttatra sā tasya prītaye punaḥ \|*  
*kuśāvartaṃ ca vikhyātaṃ tīrthamāsīttaduttamam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. The sacred river flowed thence for the pleasure of the sage. Kuśāvarta became a famous and excellent place of pilgrimage.

## \|\| 8.27.21 \|\|

*tatra snāto naro yastu mokṣāya parikalpate \|*  
*tyaktvā sarvānaghānsadyo vijñānaṃ prāpya durlabham \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. A man who takes bath there becomes worthy of attaining salvation after immediately eschewing the sins and securing perfect knowledge which is difficult of access.

## \|\| 8.27.22 \|\|

*gautamo ṛṣayaścānye militāśca parasparam \|*  
*lajjitāste tadā ye ca kṛtaghnā hyabhavanpurā \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Gautama and the other sages mixed with one another. Those who had been ungrateful before were ashamed of their wickedness.

## \|\| 8.27.23 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*asmābhiranyathā sūta śrutaṃ tadvarṇayāmahe \|*  
*gautamastāndvijān kruddhaśśaśāpeti prabudhyatām \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. O Sūta, this narrative has been heard by us in another manner. We shall mention it. According to that the infuriated Gautama cursed the brahmins. May this be known to you.

## \|\| 8.27.24 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*dvijāstadapi satyaṃ vai kalpabhedasamāśrayāt \|*  
*varṇayāmi viśeṣeṇa tāṃ kathāmapi suvratā \|\| 24 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

24\. O brahmins, that is also true, for that happened in a different Kalpa. O great ones of good rites, I shall describe the particular points of that story also.

## \|\| 8.27.25 \|\|

*gautamopi ṛṣīndṛṣṭvā tadā durbhikṣapīḍitān \|*  
*tapaścakāra sumahadvaruṇasya mahātmanaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. On seeing the sages distressed by the famine, Gautama performed a great penance with god Varuṇa as the object of his worship.

## \|\| 8.27.26 \|\|

*akṣayyaṃ kalpayāmāsa jalaṃ varuṇadāṃ yayā \|*  
*tato vrīhīnyavāṃścaiva vāpayāmāsa bhūriśaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Through Vanina’s Māyā he caused a perennial supply of water. Then he made them sow food-grains and barleys in plenty.

## \|\| 8.27.27 \|\|

*evaṃ paropakārī sa gautamo munisattamāḥ \|*  
*āhāraṃ kalpayāmāsa tebhyaḥ svatapaso balāt \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Thus the excellent sage Gautama eager to help others afforded them sufficient food, thanks to the power of his penance.

## \|\| 8.27.28 \|\|

*kadācittatstriyo duṣṭā jalārthamapamānitāḥ \|*  
*ūcu patibhyastāḥ kruddhā gautamerṣyākaraṃ vacaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Once the wicked wives of those sages feeling insulted in an incident over taking water became furious and complained to their husbands slandering Gautama.

## \|\| 8.27.29 \|\|

*tataste bhinnamatayo gāṃ kṛtvā kṛtrimāṃ dvijāḥ \|*  
*taddhānyabhakṣaṇāsaktāṃ cakrustāṃ kuṭilāśayāḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. With their minds thus prejudiced the evil-minded brahmins created an artificial cow grazing in his grain-field.

## \|\| 8.27.30 \|\|

*svadhānyabhakṣaṇāsaktāṃ gāṃ dṛṣṭvā gautamastadā \|*  
*tṛṇena tāḍayāmāsa śanaistāṃ saṃnivārayan \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. On seeing a cow eating his food-grains, Gautama struck it mildly with a blade of grass trying to ward it off.

## \|\| 8.27.31 \|\|

*tṛṇasaṃsparśamātreṇa sā bhūmau patitā ca gauḥ \|*  
*mṛtā hyabhūtkṣaṇaṃ viprā bhāvikarmavaśāttadā \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. The cow fell down immediately, being touched by the blade of grass. O brahmins, it died in an instant due to the forces of unaviodable destiny.

## \|\| 8.27.32 \|\|

*gaurhatā gautameneti tadā te kuṭilāśayāḥ \|*  
*ekatrībhūya tatratyaiḥ sakalā ṛṣayo'vadan \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Then the wicked sages held a gathering and said “O a cow has been killed by Gautama”.

## \|\| 8.27.33 \|\|

*tatassa gautamo bhīto gaurhateti babhūva ha \|*  
*cakāra vismayaṃ nāryahalyāśiṣyaiśśivānugaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Then Gautama was frightened thinking that a cow had been killed. Along with his wife Ahalyā, his disciples, the devotees of Śiva, he began to wonder.

## \|\| 8.27.34 \|\|

*tatassa gautamo jñātvā tāṃ gāṃ krodhasamākulaḥ \|*  
*śaśāpa tānṛṣīn sarvān gautamo munisattamaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Then the excellent sage Gautama realised the truth and was angry. He cursed the sages.

## \|\| 8.27.35 \|\|

*gautama uvāca \|*  
*yūyaṃ sarve durātmāno duḥkhadā me viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*śivabhaktasya satataṃ syurvedavimukhāssadā \|\| 35 \|\|*

Gautama said:—

35\. You are evil-minded desiring to put me in distress as I am a devotee of Śiva. Hence you shall be averse to the Vedas always.

## \|\| 8.27.36 \|\|

*adyaprabhṛti vedokte satkarmaṇi viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*mā bhūyādbhavatāṃ śraddhā śaivamārge vimuktide \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Henceforth your faith in the rites of the Vedas, particularly with reference to the path of Śiva yielding salvation shall cease to exist.

## \|\| 8.27.37 \|\|

*adyaprabhṛti durmārge tatra śraddhā bhavettu vaḥ \|*  
*mokṣamārgavihīne hi sadā śrutibahirmukhe \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Henceforth your attention, strayed from the path of salvation, will be diverted to evil paths extraneous to the tenets of the Vedas.

## \|\| 8.27.38 \|\|

*adyaprabhṛti bhālāni mṛlliptāni bhavantu vaḥ \|*  
*srasadhvaṃ narake yūyaṃ bhālamṛllepanāddvijāḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. From today let your foreheads be smeared with clay. Fall, ye all, deep down into the hell, O brahmins with foreheads smeared with clay.

## \|\| 8.27.39 \|\|

*bhavaṃto mā bhaviṣyaṃtu śivaika paradaivatāḥ \|*  
*anyadevasamatvena jānaṃtu śivamadvayam \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. You will not have Śiva as your sole deity. You will be considering the matchless Śiva on a par with other gods.

## \|\| 8.27.40 \|\|

*mā bhūyādbhavatāṃ prītiśśivapūjādikarmaṇi \|*  
*śivaniṣṭheṣu bhakteṣu śivaparvasu sarvadā \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. You will never have any interest in Śiva’s worship and other rites, in devotees staunchly faithful to Śiva and in the festivals of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.27.41 \|\|

*adya dattā mayā śāpā yāvaṃto duḥkhadāyakāḥ \|*  
*tāvaṃtassaṃtu bhavatāṃ saṃtatāvapi sarvadā \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Let all these curses causing misery, descend on your progeny as well.

## \|\| 8.27.42 \|\|

*aśaivāssaṃtu bhavatāṃ putrapautrādayo dvijāḥ \|*  
*putraissahaiva tiṣṭhaṃtu bhavaṃto narake dhruvam \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. All your descendants—sons, grandsons and others, O brahmins, shall be non-Śaivitcs. You shall stay certainly in hell along with your sons.

## \|\| 8.27.43 \|\|

*tato bhavaṃtu caṇḍālā duḥkhadāridryapīḍitāḥ \|*  
*śaṭhā nindākarāssarve taptamudrāṃkitāssadā \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Afterwards you shall be born as Cāṇḍālas harassed by miseries and poverty. You shall be rogues, censuring others and marked with red-hot seals.

## \|\| 8.27.44 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*iti śaptvā munīn sarvān gautamassvāśramaṃ yayau \|*  
*śivabhaktiṃ cakārāti sa babhūva supāvanaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

44\. After cursing the sages, Gautama returned to his hermitage. He performed the devotional rites of Śiva and became very holy,

## \|\| 8.27.45 \|\|

*tatastaiḥ khinnahṛdayā ṛṣayastekhilā dvijāḥ \|*  
*kāṃcyāṃ cakrurnivāsaṃ hi śaivadharmabahiṣkṛtāḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Then the brahmins and sages distressed by the curses of Gautama put up their residence in Kāñcī. They were excluded from the rites of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.27.46 \|\|

*tatputrāścābhavansarve śaivadharmabahiṣkṛtāḥ \|*  
*agre tadvadbhaviṣyaṃti kalau bahujanāḥ khalāḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Their sons too remained excluded from the sacred rites of Śiva. In future, in the Kali age too, they will be born as rogues and illiterate ordinary people.

## \|\| 8.27.47 \|\|

*iti proktamaśeṣeṇa tadvṛttaṃ munisattamāḥ \|*  
*pūrvavṛttamapi prājñāḥ śrutaṃ sarvaistu cādarāt \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. O excellent sages, thus I have narrated their stories. O intelligent ones, their previous story too has been heard by all.

## \|\| 8.27.48 \|\|

*iti vaśca samākhyāto gautamyāśca samudbhavaḥ \|*  
*māhātmyamuttamaṃ caiva sarvapāpaharaṃ param \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Thus the origin of Gautamī river has been narrated to you. So also its glory destructive of all sins.

## \|\| 8.27.49 \|\|

*tryaṃbakasya ca māhātmyaṃ jyotirliṃgasya kīrtitam \|*  
*yacchrutvā sarvapāpebhyo mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. The glory of the Jyotirliṅga Tryambaka is also narrated to you. On hearing this, one is released from all sins. There is no doubt about this.

## \|\| 8.27.50 \|\|

*ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi vaidyanātheśvarasya hi \|*  
*jyotirliṃgasya māhātmyaṃ śrūyatāṃ pāpahārakam \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. Hereafter I shall narrate the glory of the Jyotirliṅga Vaidyanātheśvara which dispels all sins. May it be heard.

## Chapter 28 - The glory of the Jyotirliṅga Vaidyanātheśvara

## \|\| 8.28.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*rāvaṇaḥ rākṣasaśreṣṭho mānī mānaparāyaṇaḥ \|*  
*ārarādha haraṃ bhaktyā kailāse parvatottame \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. Rāvaṇa the arrogant and prestige-minded leader of Rākṣasas propitiated Śiva with devotion on the excellent mountain Kailāsa.

## \|\| 8.28.2 \|\|

*ārādhitaḥ kiyatkālaṃ na prasanno haro yadā \|*  
*tadā cānyattapaścakre prāsādārthe śivasya saḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Though the worship continued for a long time, Śiva was not delighted. Then Rāvaṇa performed another penance to delight Śiva.

## \|\| 8.28.3-4 \|\|

*nataścāyaṃ himavatassiddhisthānasya vai gireḥ \|*  
*paulastyo rāvaṇaśśrīmāndakṣiṇe vṛkṣakhaṃḍake \|\| 3 \|\|*

*bhūmau gartaṃ vara kṛtvā tatrāgniṃ sthāpya sa dvijāḥ \|*  
*tatsannidhau śivaṃ sthāpya havanaṃ sa cakāra ha \|\| 4 \|\|*

3-4. The glorious Rāvaṇa, scion of the family of Pulastya dug a deep ditch among the groves of trees on the southern side of Himavat, the mountain that is usually the spot of Siddhi. O brahmins, he then kindled fire within it. Near it he installed the idol of Śiva and performed a sacrifice.

## \|\| 8.28.5 \|\|

*grīṣme paṃcāgnimadhyastho varṣāsu sthaṃḍileśayaḥ \|*  
*śīte jalāṃtarastho hi tridhā cakre tapaśca saḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. He performed three types of penance. During the summer he stationed himself in the midst of five fires, during rainy days he lay on the bare ground and in the winter he stood within water and performed his penance.

## \|\| 8.28.6 \|\|

*cakāraivaṃ bahutapo na prasannastadāpi hi \|*  
*paramātmā maheśāno durārādhyo durātmabhiḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Thus he performed penance a great deal. Even then Śiva the supreme soul, difficult to be propitiated by the wicked was not delighted.

## \|\| 8.28.7 \|\|

*tataśśirāṃsi chittvā ca pūjanaṃ śaṃkarasya vai \|*  
*prārabdhaṃ daityapatinā rāvaṇena mahātmanā \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Then the ambitious Rāvaṇa, lord of Daityas, began to worship Śiva by cutting off his heads.

## \|\| 8.28.8-9 \|\|

*ekaikaṃ ca śiraśchinnaṃ vidhinā śivapūjane \|*  
*evaṃ satkramatastena cchinnāni nava vai yadā \|\| 8 \|\|*

*ekasminnavaśiṣṭe tu prasannaśśaṃkarastadā \|*  
*āvirbabhūva tatraiva saṃtuṣṭo bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

8-9. In the due performance of the worship he cut off his heads one by one. Thus when he had cut off his nine heads and one head remained, the delighted Śiva favourably disposed towards his devotees, appeared in front of him.

## \|\| 8.28.10 \|\|

*śirāṃsi pūrvavatkṛtvā nīrujāni tathā prabhuḥ \|*  
*manorathaṃ dadau tasmādatulaṃ balamuttamam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. The lord reinstated the severed heads without causing pain and conferred on him his desire as well as unequalled excellent strength.

## \|\| 8.28.11 \|\|

*prasādaṃ tasya saṃprāpya rāvaṇassa ca rākṣasaḥ \|*  
*pratyuvāca śivaṃ śambhuṃ nataskaṃdhaḥ kṛtāṃjaliḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Securing his favour, the Rākṣasa Rāvaṇa replied to Śiva, with palms joined in reverence and shoulders drooping down.

## \|\| 8.28.12 \|\|

*rāvaṇa uvāca \|*  
*prasanno bhava deveśa laṃkāṃ ca tvāṃ nayāmyaham \|*  
*saphalaṃ kuru me kāmaṃ tvāmahaṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

Rāvaṇa said:—

12\. O lord of gods, be pleased. I am taking your image to Laṅkā. Please make my desire fruitful. I seek refuge in you.

## \|\| 8.28.13 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktaśca tadā tena śaṃbhurvai rāvaṇena saḥ \|*  
*pratyuvāca vicetaskaḥ saṃkaṭaṃ paramaṃ gataḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

13\. Thus addressed by Rāvaṇa, Śiva was in a dilemma and a bit disinclined. He replied.

## \|\| 8.28.14 \|\|

*\|\| śiva uvāca \|*  
*śrūyatāṃ rākṣasaśreṣṭha vaco me sāravattayā \|*  
*nīyatāṃ svagṛhe me hi sadbhaktyā liṃgamuttamam \|\| 14 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

14\. O excellent Rākṣasa, hear. My words are significant. Let my excellent phallic image be taken to your abode.

## \|\| 8.28.15 \|\|

*bhūmau liṃgaṃ yadā tvaṃ ca sthāpayiṣyasi tatra vai \|*  
*sthāsyatyatra na saṃdeho yathecchasi tathā kuru \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Wherever this phallic image is placed on the ground il will become stationary. There is no doubt about it. Do as you please.

## \|\| 8.28.16 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktaśśaṃbhunā tena rāvaṇo rākṣaseśvaraḥ \|*  
*tatheti tatsamādāya jagāma bhavanaṃ nijam \|\| 16 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

16\. Thus warned by Śiva, Rāvaṇa, the king of Rākṣasas, took it saying “So be it” and went to his abode.

## \|\| 8.28.17 \|\|

*āsīnmūtrotsargakāmo mārge hi śivamāyayā \|*  
*tatstaṃbhituṃ na śaktobhūtpaulastyo rāvaṇaḥ prabhuḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Thanks to Śiva’s Māyā, he became desirous of passing urine while yet on his way. Lord Rāvaṇa of the family of Pulastya could not stop this urge.

## \|\| 8.28.18 \|\|

*dṛṣṭvaikaṃ tatra vai gopaṃ prārthya liṃgaṃ dadau ca tat \|*  
*muhūrtake hyatikrāṃte gopobhūdvikalastadā \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. He saw a cowherd there and requested him to hold the image. About an hour elapsed, when Rāvaṇa did not return, the cowherd became nervous.

## \|\| 8.28.19-21 \|\|

*bhūmau saṃsthāpayāmāsa tadbhāreṇātipīḍitaḥ \|*  
*tatraiva tatsthitaṃ liṃgaṃ vajasārasamudbhavam \|*  
*sarvakāmapradaṃ caiva darśanātpāpahārakam \|\| 19 \|\|*

*vaidyanātheśvaraṃ nāmnā talliṃgamabhavanmune \|*  
*prasiddhaṃ triṣu lokeṣu bhuktimuktipradaṃ satām \|\| 20 \|\|*

*jyotirliṃgamidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ darśanātpūjanādapi \|*  
*sarvapāpaharaṃ divyaṃ bhuktivarddhanamuttamam \|\| 21 \|\|*

19-21. He was distressed by its heavy weight. He placed it on the ground. The phallic image made of the essence of diamond remained fixed there. O sage, that phallic image is known as Vaidyanātheśvara. It yields all desires and dispels sins at its very sight. It is famous in the three worlds. It grants worldly pleasures here and salvation hereafter.

This Jyotirliṅga is the most excellent of all. By its sight or worship it destroys sins. It is divine and conducive to salvation.

## \|\| 8.28.22 \|\|

*tasmiṃliṃge sthite tatra sarvalokahitāya vai \|*  
*rāvaṇaḥ svagṛhaṃ gatvā varaṃ prāpya mahottamam \|*  
*priyāyai sarvamācakhyau sukhenāti mahāsuraḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. It stood there for the benefit of the worlds. Rāvaṇa returned to his abode after securing the blissful boon.

## \|\| 8.28.23 \|\|

*tacchrutvā sakalā devāśśakrādyā munayastathā \|*  
*parasparaṃ samāmantrya śivāsaktadhiyo'malāḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. On hearing of it Indra and other gods and the pious sages with minds fixed in Śiva consulted one another.

## \|\| 8.28.24 \|\|

*tasminkāle surāssarve haribrahmādayo mune \|*  
*ājagmustatra suprītyā pūjāṃ cakrurviśeṣataḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. O sage, Viṣṇu, Brahmā and other gods arrived at the spot and devotedly performed the worship of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.28.25 \|\|

*pratyakṣaṃ taṃ tadā dṛṣṭvā pratiṣṭhāpya ca te surāḥ \|*  
*vaidyanātheti saṃprocya natvā nutvā divaṃ yayuḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. The gods saw the deity personally and after consecrating the image they called Vaidyanātha. They then bowed to the image and eulogised it. Then they returned to heaven.

## \|\| 8.28.26 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*tasmiṃlliṃge sthite tatra rāvaṇe ca gṛhaṃ gate \|*  
*kiṃ ki caritramabhūttāta tatastadvada vistarāt \|\| 26 \|\|*

The sages said:—

26\. O dear, when the image was installed and Rāvaṇa had returned to his abode, what happened? Please narrate it in detail.

## \|\| 8.28.27 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*rāvaṇopi gṛhaṃ gatvā varaṃ prāpya mahottamam \|*  
*priyāyai sarvamācakhyau mumodāti mahāsuraḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

27\. After securing the great and excellent boon, the great Asura Rāvaṇa returned to his abode and mentioned everything to his beloved wife. He rejoiced much.

## \|\| 8.28.28 \|\|

*tacchrutvā sakalaṃ devāśśakrādyā munayastathā \|*  
*parasparaṃ sumūcuste samudvignā munīśvarāḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. O great sages, on hearing everything Indra and other gods and sages too became extremely dejected. They spoke to one another.

## \|\| 8.28.29 \|\|

*devādaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*rāvaṇoyaṃ durātmā hi devadrohī khalaḥ kudhīḥ \|*  
*śivādvaraṃ ca saṃprāpya duḥkhaṃ dāsyati no'pi saḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

The gods and sages said:—

29\. “Rāvaṇa is a wicked knave. He is evil-minded and hater of the gods. After securing the boon from Śiva, he will make us more miserable.

## \|\| 8.28.30 \|\|

*kiṃ kurmaḥ kva ca gacchāmaḥ kiṃ bhaviṣyati vā punaḥ \|*  
*duṣṭaśca dakṣatāṃ prāptaḥ kiṃkiṃ no sādhayiṣyati \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. What shall we do? Where shall we go? What will happen now? What evil deeds this wicked fellow will not do after becoming more efficient?”

## \|\| 8.28.31 \|\|

*iti duḥkhaṃ samāpannāśśakrādyā munayassurāḥ \|*  
*nāradaṃ ca samāhūya papracchurvikalāstadā \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. Thus distressed, Indra and other gods, and the sages too invited Nārada and asked him dejectedly.

## \|\| 8.28.32 \|\|

*devā ūcuḥ \|*  
*sarvaṃ kāryyaṃ samarthosi kartuṃ tvaṃ munisattama \|*  
*upāyaṃ kuru devarṣe devānāṃ duḥkhanāśane \|\| 32 \|\|*

The gods said:—

32\. O excellent sage, you can do everything. O celestial sage, find out some means to dispel the grief of the gods.

## \|\| 8.28.33 \|\|

*rāvaṇoyaṃ mahāduṣṭaḥ kiṃki naiva kariṣyati \|*  
*kva yāsyāmo vayaṃ cātra duṣṭenāpīḍitā vayam \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. What evil deeds the extremely wicked Rāvaṇa will not do? Where shall we go, being harassed by the wicked fellow?

## \|\| 8.28.34 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*duḥkhaṃ tyajata bho devā yuktiṃ kṛtvā ca yāmyaham \|*  
*devakāryaṃ kariṣyāmi kṛpayā śaṃkarasya vai \|\| 34 \|\|*

Nārada said:—

34\. O gods, give up your sorrow. I shall plan out and go. By the mercy of Śiva, I shall carry out the task of the gods.

## \|\| 8.28.35 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā sa tu devarṣiragamadrāvaṇālayam \|*  
*satkāraṃ samanuprāpya prītyovācākhilaṃ ca tat \|\| 35 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

35\. Thus saying, the celestial sage went to Rāvaṇa’s abode. After receiving the formal welcome he spoke with great pleasure.

## \|\| 8.28.36 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*rākṣasottama dhanyastvaṃ śaivavaryyastapomanāḥ \|*  
*tvāṃ dṛṣṭvā ca mano medya prasannamati rāvaṇa \|\| 36 \|\|*

Nārada said:—

36\. “O excellent Rākṣasa, you are a blessed sage, a great devotee of Śiva. At your sight today my mind is much delighted.

## \|\| 8.28.37 \|\|

*svavṛttaṃ brūhyaśeṣeṇa śivārādhanasaṃbhavam \|*  
*iti pṛṣṭastadā tena rāvaṇo vākyamabravīt \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Please narrate the details how you propitiate Śiva. Thus asked by him Rāvaṇa replied.

## \|\| 8.28.38 \|\|

*rāvaṇa uvāca \|*  
*gatvā mayā tu kailāse taporthaṃ ca mahāmune \|*  
*tatraiva bahukālaṃ vai tapastaptaṃ sudāruṇam \|\| 38 \|\|*

Rāvaṇa said:—

38\. O great sage, after going to Kailāsa for penance, I performed a severe penance for a long time.

## \|\| 8.28.39 \|\|

*yadā na śaṃkarastuṣṭastataśca parivartitam \|*  
*āgatya vṛkṣakhaṃḍe vai punastaptaṃ mayā mune \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. O sage, when Śiva was not pleased, I returned from there to the grove and resumed penance.

## \|\| 8.28.40 \|\|

*grīṣme paṃcāgnimadhye tu varṣāsu sthaṃḍileśayaḥ \|*  
*śīte jalāṃtarastho hi kṛtaṃ caiva tridhā tapaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Standing in the midst of five fires during the summer, lying on the bare ground during rains and staying inside water during the winter I performed three-fold penance.

## \|\| 8.28.41 \|\|

*evaṃ mayā kṛtaṃ tatra tapotyugraṃ munīśvara \|*  
*tathāpi śaṃkaro mahyaṃ na prasanno'bhavanmanāk \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. O great sage, thus I performed a severe penance there. Still Śiva was not even slightly pleased with me.

## \|\| 8.28.42-43 \|\|

*tadā mayā tu kruddhena bhūmau gartaṃ vidhāya ca \|*  
*tatrāgniṃ samādhāya pārthivaṃ ca prakalpya ca \|\| 42 \|\|*

*gaṃdhaiśca caṃdanaiścaiva dhūpaiśca vividhaistadā \|*  
*naivedyaiḥ pūjitaśśambhurārārtikavidhānataḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

42-43. I then became angry. I dug a ditch in the ground and kindled fire. I made earthen idols. By waving lights in the course of worship I worshipped Śiva with scents, sandal paste, incense and food-offerings.

## \|\| 8.28.44 \|\|

*praṇipātaiḥ stavaiḥ puṇyaistoṣitaśśaṃkaro mayā \|*  
*gītairnṛtyaiśca vādyaiśca mukhāṃgulisamarpaṇaiḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Śiva was propitiated by me by obeisances, eulogies, songs, dances, musical instruments and mystic gestures of faces and fingers.

## \|\| 8.28.45 \|\|

*etaiśca vividhaiścānyairupāyairbhūribhirmune \|*  
*śāstroktena vidhānena pūjito bhagavān haraḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. By such different modes and various other means lord Śiva was worshipped by me according to the rules laid down in the Śāstras.

## \|\| 8.28.46 \|\|

*na tuṣṭaḥ sanmukho jāto yadā ca bhagavānharaḥ \|*  
*tadāhaṃ duḥkhitobhūvaṃ tapaso'prāpya satphalam \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. When lord Śiva was not pleased and did not appear in my presence I was distressed as I could not obtain the fruits of penance.

## \|\| 8.28.47 \|\|

*dhik śarīraṃ balaṃ caiva dhik tapaḥ karaṇaṃ mama \|*  
*ityuktvā tu mayā tatra sthāpitegnau hutaṃ bahu \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. “Fie upon my body. Fie upon my strength. Fie upon my penance”. Saying this I performed several sacrifices in the fire that had been kept there.

## \|\| 8.28.48-49 \|\|

*punaśceti vicāryaiva tvakṣāmyagnau nijāṃ tanum \|*  
*saṃchinnāni śirāṃsyeva tasmin prajvalite śucau \|\| 48 \|\|*

*succhitvaikaikaśastāni kṛtvā śuddhāni sarvaśaḥ \|*  
*śaṃkarāyārpitānyeva navasaṃkhyāni vai mayā \|\| 49 \|\|*

48-49. Then I thought—“I will cast off my body into the fire”. Thereafter I cut off my heads one by one, purified them and offered them to the fire. Thus nine heads were offered by me.

## \|\| 8.28.50 \|\|

*yāvacca daśamaṃ chettuṃ prārabdhamṛṣisattama \|*  
*tāvadāvirabhūttatra jyotīrūpo harassvayam \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. O excellent sage, when I was about to cut off my tenth head, Śiva appeared before me as a mass of splendour.

## \|\| 8.28.51 \|\|

*māmeti vyāharat prītyā drutaṃ vai bhaktavatsalaḥ \|*  
*prasannaśca varaṃ brūhi dadāmi manasepsitam \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. The lord favourably disposed towards his devotees immediately spoke to me, “I am delighted. Mention the boon you wish to have. I shall grant you what you desire in your mind,”

## \|\| 8.28.52 \|\|

*ityukte ca tadā tena mayā dṛṣṭo maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*prāṇatassaṃstutaścaiva karau baddhvā subhaktitaḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. When lord Śiva said this I eulogised and bowed to him with palms joined in great devotion.

## \|\| 8.28.53 \|\|

*tadā vṛtaṃ mayaitacca dehi me hyatulaṃ balam \|*  
*yadi prasanno deveśa durllabhaṃ kiṃ bhavenmama \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. Then I requested the lord, “O lord of gods, if you are pleased nothing will be inaccessible to me? Please give me unequalled strength.”

## \|\| 8.28.54 \|\|

*śivena parituṣṭena sarvaṃ dattaṃ kṛpālunā \|*  
*mahyaṃ manobhilaṣitaṃ girā procya tathāstviti \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. All that I had desired in my mind was granted by the merciful and satisfied śiva, saying the words “So be it”

## \|\| 8.28.55 \|\|

*amoghayā sudṛṣṭyā vai vaidyavadyojitāni me \|*  
*śirāṃsi saṃdhayitvā tu dṛṣṭāni paramātmanā \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Reinstating my heads like a physician they were glanced at by Śiva, the supreme soul with unfailing benign look.

## \|\| 8.28.56 \|\|

*evaṃkṛte tadā tatra śarīraṃ pūrvavanmama \|*  
*jātaṃ tasya prasādācca sarvaṃ prāptaṃ phalaṃ mayā \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. When this was done, my body regained its former state. Thanks to his grace, all benefits were derived by me.

## \|\| 8.28.57 \|\|

*tadā ca prārthito me saṃsthitosau vṛṣabhadhvajaḥ \|*  
*vaidyanātheśvaro nāmnā prasiddhobhūjjagattraye \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Then, requested by me, Śiva stayed there permanently in the name of Vaidyanātheśvara. He has now become famous in the three worlds.

## \|\| 8.28.58 \|\|

*darśanātpūjanājjyotirliṃgarūpo maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*bhuktimuktiprado loke sarveṣāṃ hitakārakaḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. Lord Śiva in the form of Jyotirliṅga confers worldly pleasures and salvation by his sight and worship. He is the benefactor of all in the world.

## \|\| 8.28.59 \|\|

*jyotirliṃgamahaṃ tadvai pūjayitvā viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*praṇipatyāgataścātra vijetuṃ bhuvanatrayam \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. I particularly worshipped that Jyotirliṅga and bowed to it. I have returned home and intend to conquer the three worlds.

## \|\| 8.28.60 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*tadīyaṃ tadvacaḥ śrutvā devarṣirjātasaṃbhramaḥ \|*  
*vihasya ca manasyeva rāvaṇaṃ nārado'bravīt \|\| 60 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

60\. The celestial sage was excited on hearing his words. Laughing within himself, Nārada spoke to Rāvaṇa.

## \|\| 8.28.61 \|\|

*nārada uvāca \|*  
*śrūyatāṃ rākṣasaśreṣṭha kathayāmi hitaṃ tava \|*  
*tvayā tadeva karttavyaṃ maduktaṃ nānyathā kvacit \|\| 61 \|\|*

Nārada said:—

61\. May this be heard, O excellent Rākṣasa, I shall tell you what is beneficial to you. You shall do as I say and not otherwise at any cost.

## \|\| 8.28.62 \|\|

*tvayoktaṃ yacchivenaiva hitaṃ dattaṃ mamādhunā \|*  
*tatsarvaṃ ca tvayā satyaṃ na mantavyaṃ kadācana \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. What you said just now that everything was granted by Śiva should never be taken for granted.

## \|\| 8.28.63 \|\|

*ayaṃ vai vikṛtiṃ prāptaḥ kiṃ kiṃ naiva bravīti ca \|*  
*satyaṃ naiva bhavettadvai kathaṃ jñeyaṃ priyosti me \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. What will he not say in his state of mental aberration? It will never come out to be true. How can it be proved? You are my favourite.

## \|\| 8.28.64 \|\|

*iti gatvā punaḥ kāryyaṃ kuru tvaṃ hyahitāya vai \|*  
*kailāsoddharaṇe yatnaḥ kartavyaśca tvayā punaḥ \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. Hence you shall go again and do this for your own benefit. You shall endeavour to lift up Kailāsa.

## \|\| 8.28.65 \|\|

*yadi caivoddhṛtaścāyaṃ kailāso hi bhaviṣyati \|*  
*tadaiva saphalaṃ sarvaṃ bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. Everything will come out to be fruitful only when Kailāsa is lifted up. It is doubtlessly so.

## \|\| 8.28.66 \|\|

*pūrvavatsthāpayitvā tvaṃ punarāgaccha vai sukham \|*  
*niścayaṃ paramaṃ gatvā yathecchasi tathā kuru \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. You replace it as before and return happily. Decide ultimately and do as you wish.

## \|\| 8.28.67 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktassa hitaṃ mene rāvaṇo vidhimohita \|*  
*satyaṃ matvā munervākyaṃ kailāsamagamattadā \|\| 67 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

67\. Rāvaṇa, deluded by fate, considered it beneficial when thus advised. Believing in the advice of the sage he went to Kailāsa.

## \|\| 8.28.68 \|\|

*gatvā tatra samuddhāraṃ cakre tasya giressa ca \|*  
*tatrasthaṃ caiva tatsarvaṃ viparyastaṃ parasparam \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. Reaching there he lifted up the mountain. Everything on the mountain became topsyturvy and mixed up with one another.

## \|\| 8.28.69 \|\|

*girīśopi tadā dṛṣṭvā kiṃ jātamiti sobravīt \|*  
*girijā ca tadā śaṃbhuṃ pratyuvāca vihasya tam \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. On seeing it, Śiva said “What has happened?” Śivā laughed and replied.

## \|\| 8.28.70 \|\|

*girijovāca \|*  
*sacchiśyasya phalaṃ jātaṃ samyagjātaṃ tu śiṣyataḥ \|*  
*śāntātmane suvīrāya dattaṃ yadatulaṃ balam \|\| 70 \|\|*

Śivā said:—

70\. You have got the return of favouring your disciple, indeed. Something good has happened through this disciple, now that an unequalled strength has been bestowed on a quiet soul who is a great hero.

## \|\| 8.28.71 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*girijāyāśca sākūtaṃ vacaḥ śrutvā maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*kṛtaghnaṃ rāvaṇaṃ matvā śaśāpa baladarpitam \|\| 71 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

71\. On hearing the words of Śivā with a veiled hint, lord Śiva considered Rāvaṇa ungrateful. He cursed him for being arrogant of his strength.

## \|\| 8.28.72 \|\|

*mahādeva uvāca \|*  
*re re rāvaṇa durbhakta mā garvaṃ vaha durmate \|*  
*śīghraṃ ca tava hastānāṃ darpaghnaśca bhavediha \|\| 72 \|\|*

Lord Śiva said:—

72\. O Rāvaṇa, O base devotee, O wicked-minded, do not be arrogant. A destroyer of the arrogance of your mighty hands will come soon.

## \|\| 8.28.73 \|\|

*sūta uvāva \|*  
*iti tatra ca yajjātaṃ nāradaḥ śrutavāṃstadā \|*  
*rāvaṇopi prasannātmā'gātsvadhāma yathāgatam \|\| 73 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

73\. What thus happened there Nārada too heard. Rāvaṇa delighted in his mind returned to his abode.

## \|\| 8.28.74 \|\|

*niścayaṃ paramaṃ kṛtvā balī balavimohitaḥ \|*  
*jagadvaśaṃ hi kṛtavānrāvaṇaḥ paradarpahā \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. Coming to a firm decision, the mighty Rāvaṇa, deluded of his own strength, destroyed the arrogance of his enemies and brought the whole universe under his control.

## \|\| 8.28.75 \|\|

*śivājñayā ca prāptena divyāstreṇa mahaujasā \|*  
*rāvaṇasya prati bhaṭo nālaṃ kaścidabhūttadā \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. Due to the divine weapons and great strength secured at the behest of Śiva, there was none to counterchallenge Rāvaṇa in fight.

## \|\| 8.28.76 \|\|

*ityetacca samākhyātaṃ vaidyanātheśvarasya ca \|*  
*māhātmyaṃ śṛṇvatāṃ pāpaṃ nṛṇāṃ bhavati bhasmasāt \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. By listening to this glory of lord Vaidyanātha the sinners get their sins reduced to ashes.

## Chapter 29 - The havoc of the Rākṣasas of Dārukāvana

## \|\| 8.29.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi nāgeśākhyaṃ parātmanaḥ \|*  
*jyotīrūpaṃ yathā jātaṃ paramaṃ liṃgamuttamam \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. Hereafter I shall explain how the extremely excellent Jyotirliṅga Nāgeśa of the supreme soul came into being.

## \|\| 8.29.2 \|\|

*dārukā rākṣasī kācitpārvatī varadarpitā \|*  
*dārukaśca patistasyā babhūva balavattaraḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. There was a certain ogress Dārukā who became arrogant by the boon granted by Pārvatī. Her husband Dāruka was also very powerful.

## \|\| 8.29.3 \|\|

*bahubhī rākṣasaistatra cakāra kadanaṃ satām \|*  
*yajñadhvaṃsaṃ ca lokānāṃ dharmadhvaṃsaṃ tadākarot \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Along with many Rākṣasas he tormented the good. He destroyed their sacrifices and spoiled their rites.

## \|\| 8.29.4 \|\|

*paścime sāgare tasya vanaṃ sarvasamṛddhimat \|*  
*yojanānāṃ ṣoḍaśabhirvistṛtaṃ sarvato diśam \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. His fertile and flourishing forest was on the shores of the Western ocean. It extended to sixteen Yojanas in every direction.

## \|\| 8.29.5 \|\|

*dārukā svavilāsārthaṃ yatra gacchati tadvanam \|*  
*bhūmyā ca tarubhistatra sarvopakaraṇairyutam \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Wherever Dārukā went in that forest for her sportive pleasure, the land in that spot acquired all requisites and became full of trees.

## \|\| 8.29.6 \|\|

*dārukāyai dadau devī tadvanasyāvalokanam \|*  
*prayāti tadvanaṃ sā hi patyā saha yadṛcchayā \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. The goddess had entrusted Dārukā with the supervision of that forest. She casually visited that forest along with her consort.

## \|\| 8.29.7 \|\|

*tatra sthitvā tadā sopi sarveṣāṃ ca bhayaṃ dadau \|*  
*dāruko rākṣasaḥ patnyā tayā dārukayā saha \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Staying there, the Rākṣasa Dāruka and his wife Dārukā terrorised the people.

## \|\| 8.29.8 \|\|

*te sarve pīḍitā lokā aurvasya śaraṇaṃ yayuḥ \|*  
*natvā prītyā viśeṣeṇa tamūcurnatamastakāḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. The harassed people sought refuge in Aurva. They paid homage to him with reverence and with their head bent down with pleasure they requested him thus.

## \|\| 8.29.9 \|\|

*lokā ūcuḥ \|*  
*maharṣe śaraṇaṃ dehi no cedduṣṭaiśca māritāḥ \|*  
*sarvaṃ kartuṃ samarthosi tejasā dīptimānasi \|\| 9 \|\|*

The people said:—

9\. O great sage, grant us shelter, if not, we will be killed by the wicked Dāruka. You are competent to do everything. You are brilliant with your splendour.

## \|\| 8.29.10 \|\|

*pṛthvyāṃ na vartate kaścittvāṃ vinā śaraṇaṃ ca naḥ \|*  
*yāmo yasya samīpe tu sthitvā sukhamavāpnumaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. There is none in the world except you in whom we can seek refuge and staying near whom we can be happy.

## \|\| 8.29.11 \|\|

*tvāṃ dṛṣṭvā rāśrasāssarve palā yaṃte vidūrataḥ \|*  
*tvayi śaivaṃ sadā tejo vibhāti jvalano yathā \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. On seeing you, the Rākṣasas run off. The splendour of Śiva always shines in you like fire.

## \|\| 8.29.12 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityevaṃ prārthito lokairaurvo hi munisattamaḥ \|*  
*śocamānaḥ śaraṇyaśca rakṣāyai hi vaco'bravīt \|\| 12 \|\|*

Sūla said:—

12\. Thus requested by the people the excellent sage Aurva, worthy of being resorted to, bewailed their lot and spoke these words for their protection.

## \|\| 8.29.13 \|\|

*aurva uvāca \|*  
*pṛthivyāṃ yadi rakṣāṃsi hiṃsyurvai prāṇinastadā \|*  
*svayaṃ prāṇairviyujyeyū rākṣasā balavattarāḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

Aurva said:—

13\. If the Rākṣasas torture living beings on the earth they will die themselves although they are very powerful.

## \|\| 8.29.14 \|\|

*yadā yajñā na hanyeraṃstadā prāṇairviyojitāḥ \|*  
*bhavaṃtu rākṣasāssarve satyametanmayocyate \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Whenever the people are killed, let the Rākṣasas too die. This is the truth I say.

## \|\| 8.29.15 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā vacanaṃ tebhyassamāśvāsya prajāḥ punaḥ \|*  
*tapaścakāra vividhamaurvo lokasukhāvahaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

15\. After promising to the subjects and consoling them, Aurva continued different types of penance, conferring happiness on the people.

## \|\| 8.29.16 \|\|

*devāstadā te vijñāya śāpasya kāraṇaṃ hi tat \|*  
*yuddhāya ca samudyogaṃ cakrurdevāribhissaha \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Realising the cause for the curse, the gods made attempts to fight with the demons.

## \|\| 8.29.17 \|\|

*sarvaiścaiva prayatnaiśca nānāyudhadharāssurāḥ \|*  
*sarve śakrādayastatra yuddhārthaṃ samupāgatāḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. With multifarious efforts and holding all types of weapons Indra and the other gods arrived there for the battle.

## \|\| 8.29.18 \|\|

*tāndṛṣṭvā rākṣasāstatra vicāre tatparāḥ punaḥ \|*  
*babhūvuste'khilā duṣṭā mitho ye yatra saṃsthitāḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. On seeing them the Rākṣasas began to ponder. They consulted with one another.

## \|\| 8.29.19 \|\|

*rākṣasā ūcuḥ \|*  
*kiṃ kartavyaṃ kva gaṃtavyaṃ saṃkaṭaṃ samupāgatāḥ \|*  
*yuddhyate mriyate caiva yuddhyate na vihanyate \|\| 19 \|\|*

Rākṣasas said:—

19\. “What shall be done? Where shall we go? A difficult situation has come up. We are fighting and dying. They are fighting but they are not killed.

## \|\| 8.29.20 \|\|

*tathaiva sthīyate cedvai bhakṣyate kiṃ parasparam \|*  
*duḥkhaṃ hi sarvathā jātaṃ ka enaṃ vinivārayet \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. If this situation continues what shall happen to us? A great misery has befallen us. Who can ward this off?”

## \|\| 8.29.21 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*vicāryeti ca te tatra dārukādyāśca rākṣasāḥ \|*  
*upāyaṃ na vijānanto duḥkhaṃ prāptāssadā hi vai \|\| 21 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

21\. Thinking thus and unable to find out a remedy Dāruka and his companions felt miserable indeed.

## \|\| 8.29.22 \|\|

*dārukā rākṣasī cāpi jñātvā duḥkhaṃ samāgatam \|*  
*bhavānyāśca varaṃ tañca kathayāmāsa sā tadā \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. On knowing that a great calamity had arrived, the the demoness Dārukā spoke of the boon granted by Pārvatī.

## \|\| 8.29.23 \|\|

*dārukovāca \|*  
*mayā hyārādhitā pūrvaṃ bhavapatnī varaṃ dadau \|*  
*vanaṃ gaccha nijaiḥ sārdhaṃ yatra gaṃtuṃ tvamicchasi \|\| 23 \|\|*

Dārukā said:—

23\. Propitiated by me formerly, the wife of Śiva had granted me a boon. You can go to the forest along with your people if you desire to go.

## \|\| 8.29.24 \|\|

*tadvaraśca mayā prāptaḥ kathaṃ duḥkhaṃ viṣahyate \|*  
*jalaṃ vanaṃ ca nītvā vai sukhaṃ stheyaṃ tu rākṣasaiḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. That boon has been secured by me. How is this misery to be borne? Taking your people to the island you can stay with the Rākṣasas happily.

## \|\| 8.29.25 \|\|

*bhūta uvāca \|*  
*tasyāstadvacanaṃ śrutvā rākṣasyā harṣamāgatāḥ \|*  
*ucussarve mithaste hi rākṣasā nirbhayāstadā \|\| 25 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

25\. On hearing the words of the Rākṣasī, the Rākṣasas were delighted. Getting rid of their fear they spoke to one another.

## \|\| 8.29.26 \|\|

*dhanyeyaṃ kṛtakṛtyeyaṃ rājñyā vai jīvitāssvayam \|*  
*natvā tasyai ca tatsarvaṃ kathayāmāsurādarāt \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. “She is blessed. She is fortunate. We have been saved by her.” After bowing to her they said respectfully.

## \|\| 8.29.27 \|\|

*yadi gaṃtuṃ bhavecchaktirgamyatāṃ kiṃ vicāryate \|*  
*tatra gatvā jale devi sukhaṃ sthāsyāma nityaśaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. “If we can go let us go. Why ponder over it? O gentle lady, we shall go and stay there permanently in in the waters.”

## \|\| 8.29.28 \|\|

*etasminnaṃtare lokā devaissārddhaṃ samāgatāḥ \|*  
*yuddhāya vividhairduḥkhaiḥ pīḍitā rākṣasaiḥ purā \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. In the meantime the people who had been tortured by the Rākṣasas formerly came there for fighting, accompanied by the gods.

## \|\| 8.29.29-31 \|\|

*pīḍitāśca tadā tasyā bhavānyā balamāśritāḥ \|*  
*samagraṃ nagaraṃ nītvā jalasthalasamanvitam \|\| 29 \|\|*

*jayajayeti devyāstu stutimuccārya rākṣasī \|*  
*tata uḍḍīyanaṃ kṛtvā sapakṣo girirāḍyathā \|\| 30 \|\|*

*samudrasya ca madhye sā saṃsthitā nirbhayā tadā \|*  
*sakalaiḥ parivāraiśca mumudeti śivānugā \|\| 31 \|\|*

29-31. In their dilemma the Rākṣasas resorted to the boon granted by Pārvatī. They took their belongings to the city in the ocean. The demoness shouted cries of victory to the goddess and flew like the winged king of mountains. She then stayed fearlessly in the middle; of the ocean. The follower of Śiva accompanied by her attendants rejoiced much.

## \|\| 8.29.32 \|\|

*tatra siṃdhau ca te sthitvā nagare ca vilāsinaḥ \|*  
*rākṣasāśca sukhaṃ prāpu्rnirbhayāśca vijahrire \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Staying in the city inside the ocean, the sportive Rākṣasas became happy and fearless.

## \|\| 8.29.33 \|\|

*rākṣasāśca pṛthivyāṃ vai nājagmuśca kadācana \|*  
*muneśśāpabhayādeva babhramuste cale tadā \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. For fear of the sage’s curse, Rākṣasas did not come on the earth. They moved about in the waters.

## \|\| 8.29.34 \|\|

*nauṣu sthitāñjanānnītvā nagare tatra tāṃstadā \|*  
*cikṣipurbandhanāgāre kāṃścijjaghnustadā hi te \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. They abducted travellers in the boats and imprisoned them in the city. They killed some of them.

## \|\| 8.29.35 \|\|

*yathāyathā punaḥ pīḍāṃ cakruste rākṣasāstadā \|*  
*tatrasthitā bhavānyāśca varadānācca nirbhayāḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Staying there fearlessly, thanks to the boon granted by Pārvatī, the Rākṣasas worked havoc as before.

## \|\| 8.29.36 \|\|

*yathāpūrvaṃ sthale loke bhayaṃ cāsīnnirantaram \|*  
*tathā bhayaṃ jale teṣāmāsīnnityaṃ munīśvarāḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. O great sages, the people had the terror in the waters now incessantly in the manner they had fear on land before.

## \|\| 8.29.37 \|\|

*kadācidrākṣasī sā ca nissṛtā nagarājjale \|*  
*ruddhvā mārgaṃ sthitā lokapīḍārthaṃ dharaṇau ca hi \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Sometimes the Rākṣasī came out of the city in the water and blocked the path to the land in order to harass the people.

## \|\| 8.29.38 \|\|

*etasminnaṃtare tatra nāvo bahutarāḥ śubhāḥ \|*  
*āgatā bahudhā tatra sarvato lokasaṃvṛtāḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. In the meantime several beautiful boats arrived there all full of people.

## \|\| 8.29.39 \|\|

*tā nāvaśca tadā dṛṣṭvā harṣaṃ saṃprāpya rākṣasāḥ \|*  
*drutaṃ gatvā hi tatrasthānvegātsaṃdadhrire khalāḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. On seeing those boats the wicked Rākṣasas were delighted much. Rushing at the people on board they bound them forcefully.

## \|\| 8.29.40 \|\|

*ājagmurnagaraṃ te ca tānādāya mahābalāḥ \|*  
*cikṣipurbandhanāgāre baddhvā hi nigaḍairdṛḍhaiḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. The naughty Rākṣasas came to the city taking those people with them. They fettered them and put them in prisons.

## \|\| 8.29.41 \|\|

*baddhāste nigaḍairlokā saṃsthitā baṃdhanālaye \|*  
*atīva duḥkhamājagmurbhartsitāste muhurmuhuḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. The people thus bound in fetters and imprisoned became very unhappy. They were rebuked again and again.

## \|\| 8.29.42 \|\|

*teṣāṃ madhye ca yo'dhīśassa vaiśyassupriyābhidhaḥ \|*  
*śivapriyaśśubhācāraśśaivaścāsītsadātanaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Their leader was a certain Vaiśya named Supriya. His conduct was auspicious and pleasing to Śiva. He was? permanent devotee of Siva.

## \|\| 8.29.43 \|\|

*vinā ca śivapūjāṃ vai na tiṣṭhati kadācana \|*  
*sarvathā śivadharmā hi bhasmarudrākṣabhūṣaṇaḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. He never remained without the worship of Śiva. In every respect he followed the sacred rites of Śiva. Bhasma and Rudrākṣa were his ornaments.

## \|\| 8.29.44 \|\|

*yadi pūjā na jātā cenna bhunakti tadā tu saḥ \|*  
*atastatrāpi vaiśyo'sau cakāra śivapūjanam \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. If on any day he could not worship Śiva he did not take his food. The Vaiśya performed the worship of Śiva there in the prison also.

## \|\| 8.29.45 \|\|

*kārāgṛhagatassopi bahūṃścāśikṣayattadā \|*  
*śivamaṃtraṃ ca pūjāṃ ca pārthivīmṛṣisattamāḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. O excellent sages, he taught the mantra of Śiva and the mode of worshipping the earthen phallic image to several of his fellow-prisoners.

## \|\| 8.29.46 \|\|

*te sarve ca tadā tatra śivapūjāṃ svakāmadām \|*  
*cakrire vidhivattatra yathādṛṣṭaṃ yathāśrutam \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. They performed there the worship of Śiva duly in the manner they saw and heard. It yielded them their desires.

## \|\| 8.29.47 \|\|

*kecittatra sthitā dhyāne baddhvāsanamanuttamam \|*  
*mānasīṃ śivapūjāṃ ca keciccakrurmudānvitāḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Some of them stayed in meditation taking up excellent postures. Some of them joyously performed the mental worship of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.29.48 \|\|

*tadadhīśena tatraiva pratyakṣaṃ śivapūjanam \|*  
*kṛtaṃ ca pārthivasyaiva vidhānena munīśvarāḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. O great sages, the direct worship of Śiva was performed by the leader then in accordance with the rites of worshipping the earthen phallic image.

## \|\| 8.29.49 \|\|

*anye ca ye na jānanti vidhānaṃ smaraṇaṃ param \|*  
*namaśśivāya maṃtreṇa dhyāyaṃtaśśaṃkaraṃ sthitāḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. Those who did not know the great rite remained meditating on Śiva with the five-syllabled mantra “Namaḥ Śivāya”.

## \|\| 8.29.50 \|\|

*supriyo nāma yaścāsīdvaiśyavaryaśśivapriyaḥ \|*  
*dhyāyaṃśca manasā tatra cakāra śivapūjanam \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. The leading Vaiśya Supriya, a favourite of Śiva, performed the worship of Śiva by mental meditation

## \|\| 8.29.51 \|\|

*yathoktarūpī śaṃbhuśca pratyakṣaṃ sarvamādade \|*  
*sopi svayaṃ na jānāti gṛhyate na śivena vai \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Śiva in the form mentioned before took up everything directly. The Vaiśya was aware that his offerings were taken by Śiva.

## \|\| 8.29.52 \|\|

*evaṃ ca kriyamāṇasya vaiśyasya śivapūjanam \|*  
*vyatīyustatra ṣaṇmāsā nirvighnena munīśvarāḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. O great sages, even as the Vaiśya continued to perform the worship of Śiva, six months passed by without hindrance.

## \|\| 8.29.53 \|\|

*ataḥ paraṃ ca yajjātaṃ caritaṃ śaśimaulinaḥ \|*  
*tacchṛṇudhvamṛṣiśreṣṭhāḥ sāvadhānena cetasā \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. The event that happened thereafter, the story of the moon-crested lord, O great sages, please listen to with attention.

## Chapter 30 - The greatness of the Jyotirliṅga Nāgeśvara

## \|\| 8.30.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*kadācitsevakastasya rākṣasasya durātmanaḥ \|*  
*tadagre suṃdaraṃ rūpaṃ śaṃkarasya dadarśa ha \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:

1\. Once an attendant of the wicked Rākṣasa saw the beautiful form of Śiva in front of him.

## \|\| 8.30.2 \|\|

*tasmai niveditaṃ rājñe rākṣasānāṃ yathārthakam \|*  
*sarvaṃ taccaritaṃ tena sakautukamathādbhutam \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. That wonderful incident was truthfully intimated to the king of Rākṣasas.

## \|\| 8.30.3 \|\|

*rājāpi tatra cāgatya rākṣasānāṃ sa dārukaḥ \|*  
*vihvalassabalaśśīghraṃ paryapṛcchacca taṃ śivam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. The king of the Rākṣasas, Dāruka, though very powerful was excited much. He hastened to the Vaiśya and asked him.

## \|\| 8.30.4 \|\|

*dāruka uvāca \|*  
*kiṃ dhyāyasi hi vaiśya tvaṃ satyaṃ vada mamāgrataḥ \|*  
*evaṃ sati na mṛtyuste mama vākyaṃ ca nānyathā \|\| 4 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

4\. O Vaiśya, what is it that you meditate upon? Tell me the truth. Then you will not die. My words cannot be otherwise.

## \|\| 8.30.5 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*tenoktaṃ ca na jānāmi tacchrutvā kupitasya vai \|*  
*rākṣasānprerayāmāsa hanyatāṃ rākṣasā ayam \|\| 5 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

5\. He replied “I do not know.” On hearing it the Rākṣasa was angry. He incited the Rākṣasas—“O Rākṣasas, let him be killed.”

## \|\| 8.30.6 \|\|

*taduktāste tadā haṃtuṃ nānāyudhadharā gatāḥ \|*  
*drutaṃ taṃ vaiśyaśārdūlaṃ śaṃkarāsaktacetasam \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Urged by him, they seized various weapons and rushed at the leading Vaiśya whose mind was attached to Śiva, with desire to kill him.

## \|\| 8.30.7 \|\|

*tānāgatāṃstadā dṛṣṭvā bhayavitrastalocanaḥ \|*  
*śivaṃ sasmāra suprītyā tannāmāni jagau muhuḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. On seeing them come, the Vaiśya remembered Śiva with devotion and repeated his names though his eyes indicated that he was extremely frightened.

## \|\| 8.30.8 \|\|

*vaiśyapatiruvāca \|*  
*pāhi śaṃkara deveśa pāhi śaṃbho śiveti ca \|*  
*duṣṭādasmāttrilokeśa khalahanbhaktavatsala \|\| 8 \|\|*

The leading Vaiśya said:—

8\. O Śiva, save me. O lord of gods, save me. O Śiva, save me now from this wicked Rākṣasa. O lord of the three worlds, O slayer of the wicked, O one favourably disposed towards his devotees.

## \|\| 8.30.9 \|\|

*sarvasvaṃ ca bhavānadya mama deva tvameva hi \|*  
*tvadadhīnastvadīyo'haṃ tvatprāṇassarvadā prabho \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. O lord, you alone are my entire possession. I belong to you. I am subservient lo you. O lord, my very life is always in you.

## \|\| 8.30.10 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*iti saṃprārthitaśśaṃbhurvivarānnirgatastadā \|*  
*bhavanenottamenātha caturdvārayutena ca \|\| 10 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

10\. Thus implored, Śiva appeared out of the crevice with an excellent temple with four doors.

## \|\| 8.30.11 \|\|

*madhyajyotissvarūpaṃ ca śivarūpaṃ tadadbhutam \|*  
*parivārasamāyuktaṃ dṛṣṭvā cāpūjayatsa vai \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Seeing in its midst the wonderfully brilliant form of Śiva along with his attendants he worshipped him.

## \|\| 8.30.12-13 \|\|

*pūjitaśca tadā śaṃbhuḥ prasanno hyabhavatsvayam \|*  
*astraṃ pāśupataṃ nāma dattvā rākṣasapuṃgavān \|\| 12 \|\|*

*jaghāna sopakaraṇāṃstānsarvānsagaṇāndrutam \|*  
*arakṣacca svabhaktaṃ vai duṣṭahā sa hi śaṃkaraḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

12-13. On being worshipped, Śiva became delighted. He gave him the Pāśupata missile. He immediately slew all the Rākṣasas with all their attendants and adjuncts. He saved thus his devotee. Indeed Śiva is the slayer of the wicked.

## \|\| 8.30.14 \|\|

*sarvāṃstāṃśca tadā hatvā varaṃ prādādvanasya ca \|*  
*atyadbhutakaraśśaṃbhussvalīlāttasuvigrahaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. After killing all of them, Śiva the creator of his Oracles and who assumes bodies in the course of sports granted a boon to the forest.

## \|\| 8.30.15 \|\|

*asminvane sadā varṇadharmā vai saṃbhavaṃtu ca \|*  
*brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ hi tathaiva ca \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. May the rites and activities of the four castes, Brahmins, Kṣatriyas, Vaiśyas and Śūdras flourish in this forest always.

## \|\| 8.30.16 \|\|

*bhavatvatra muniśreṣṭhāstāmasā na kadācana \|*  
*śivadharmapravaktāraśśivadharmapravartakāḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Let there never be people of the Tāmasaic qualities. Let there be excellent sages, the propounders and the followers of the Śaiva cult.

## \|\| 8.30.17 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*etasminsamaye sā vai rākṣasī dārukāhvayā \|*  
*devyāḥ stutiṃ cakārāsau pārvatyā dīnamānasā \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. In the meantime the Rākṣasī Dārukā dejected in her mind, eulogised Pārvatī.

## \|\| 8.30.18 \|\|

*prasannā ca tadā devī kiṃ karomītyuvāca hi \|*  
*sāpyuvāca punastatra vaṃśo me rakṣyatāṃ tvayā \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. The goddess being pleased said “What shall I do?” She replied—“Let my race be protected by you”.

## \|\| 8.30.19 \|\|

*rakṣayiṣyāmi te vaṃśaṃ satyaṃ ca kathyate mayā \|*  
*ityuktvā ca śivenaiva vigrahaṃ sā cakāra ha \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. “I shall protect your race. I say this truth to you.” Saying this the Goddess quarrelled with Śiva.

## \|\| 8.30.20 \|\|

*śivopi kupitāṃ devīṃ dṛṣṭvā varavaśaḥ prabhuḥ \|*  
*pratyuvāceti suprītyā yathecchasi tathā kuru \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. On seeing the goddess furious, the lord, bound by the boon granted by him, replied with pleasure. “Do as you please”.

## \|\| 8.30.21 \|\|

*sūt uvāca \|*  
*iti śrutvā vacastasya svapateśśaṃkarasya vai \|*  
*suprasannā vihasyāśu pārvatī vākyamabravīt \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. On hearing the words of her husband Śiva, the delighted Pārvatī laughed and spoke these words.

## \|\| 8.30.22 \|\|

*pārvatyuvāca \|*  
*bhavadīyaṃ vacastathyaṃ yugāṃte saṃbhaviṣyati \|*  
*tāvacca tāmasī sṛṣṭirbhavatviti mataṃ mama \|\| 22 \|\|*

Pārvatī said:—

22\. “Your words will be true at the end of this Yuga. Till then, I think, there can be the Tāmasaic creations as well.

## \|\| 8.30.23 \|\|

*anyathā pralayassyādvai satyaṃ me vyāhṛtaṃ śiva \|*  
*pramāṇīkriyatāṃ nātha tvadīyāsmi tvadāśrayā \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Otherwise the dissolution will take place even now. O Śiva, let the words uttered by me be proved true. O lord, I belong to you. I depend on you.

## \|\| 8.30.24 \|\|

*iyaṃ ca dārukā devī rākṣasī śaktikā mama \|*  
*baliṣṭhā rākṣasīnāṃ ca rakṣorājyaṃ praśāstu ca \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. This Rākṣasī Dārukā is my own Śakti. She is the most powerful among the Rākṣasīs. Let her rule over the kingdom of the Rākṣasas.

## \|\| 8.30.25 \|\|

*imā rākṣasapatnyastu prasaviṣyaṃti putrakān \|*  
*te sarve militāścaiva vane vāsāya me matāḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. These wives of the Rākṣasas will give birth to sons. I have permitted all of them to live jointly in the forest.

## \|\| 8.30.26 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityevaṃ vacanaṃ śrutvā pārvatyāssvastriyāḥ prabhuḥ \|*  
*prasannamānaso bhūtvā śaṃkaro vākyamabravīt \|\| 26 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

26\. On hearing these words of Pārvatī, lord Śiva was delighted in his mind and spoke these words.

Śiva said;—

## \|\| 8.30.27 \|\|

*śaṅkara uvāca \|*  
*iti bravīṣi tvaṃ vai cecchṛṇu madvacanaṃ priye \|*  
*sthāsyāmyasminvane prītyā bhaktānāṃ pālanāya ca \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. O beloved, if you say so, please listen to my words. I will stay in this forest with pleasure for protecting the devotees.

## \|\| 8.30.28 \|\|

*atra me varṇadharmastho darśanaṃ prītisaṃyutam \|*  
*kariṣyati ca yo vai sa cakravartī bhaviṣyati \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. He who maintains the discipline of rites and activities prescribed for different castes and has a sight of me with pleasure here will become an emperor.

## \|\| 8.30.29-30 \|\|

*anyathā kaliparyāye satyasyādau nṛpeśvara \|*  
*mahāsenasuto yo vai vīraseneti viśrutaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

*sa me bhaktyātivikrāṃto darśanaṃ me kariṣyati \|*  
*darśanaṃ me sa kṛtvaiva cakravartī bhaviṣyati \|\| 30 \|\|*

29-30. Or, at the end of the recurring cycle of Kali or in the beginning of Kṛta Age, the leading king equipped with a vast army, famous in the name of Vīrasena son of Mahāsena, will have a sight of me. He will be a very valorous devotee of mine. After viewing me he will become an emperor.

## \|\| 8.30.31 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityevaṃ daṃpatī tau ca kṛtvā hāsyaṃ parasparam \|*  
*sthitau tatra svayaṃ sākṣānmahattvakārakau dvijāḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

31\. Exchanging pleasantries thus, the couple, O brahmins, indulging in great sports remained there.

## \|\| 8.30.32 \|\|

*jyotirliṃgasvarūpo hi nāmnā nāgeśvaraśśivaḥ \|*  
*nāgeśvarī śivā devī babhūva ca satāṃ priyau \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. The Jyotirliṅga form of Śiva came to be known as Nāgeśvara. Goddess Śivā became Nāgeśvarī. Both of them are favourites of the devotees.

## \|\| 8.30.33 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*vīrasenaḥ kathaṃ tatra yāsyate dārukāvane \|*  
*kathamarciṣyati śivaṃ tvaṃ tadvada mahāmate \|\| 33 \|\|*

The sages said:—

33\. O intelligent one, how will Vīrasena go to Dārukāvana and worship Śiva? Please narrate it.

## \|\| 8.30.34 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*niṣadhe suṃdare deśe kṣatriyāṇāṃ kule ca saḥ \|*  
*mahāsenasuto vīrasenaścaiva śivapriyaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

34\. Vīrasena, son of Mahāsena, was a great favourite of Śiva. He was born of a Kṣatriya family in the beautiful land of Niṣadha.

## \|\| 8.30.35 \|\|

*pārthiveśārcanaṃ kṛtvā tapaḥ paramaduṣkaram \|*  
*cakāra vīraseno vai varṣāṇāṃ dvādaśāvadhiḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Worshipping the earthen image of the lord, Vīrasena performed a severe penance, lasting for twelve years.

## \|\| 8.30.36-39 \|\|

*tataḥ prasanno deveśaḥ pratyakṣaṃ prāha śaṃkaraḥ \|*  
*kāṣṭhasya matsyikāṃ kṛtvā trapudhātu vilepanām \|\| 36 \|\|*

*vidhāya yogamāyāṃ ca dāsyāmi vīrasenaka \|*  
*tāṃ gṛhītvā praviśyaitaṃ nṛbhissaha vrajādhunā \|\| 37 \|\|*

*tatastvaṃ tatra gatvā ca vivare ca kṛte mayā \|*  
*praviśya ca tadā pūjāṃ kṛtvā nāgeśvarasya ca \|\| 38 \|\|*

*tataḥ pāśupataṃ prāpya hatvā ca rākṣasīmukhān \|*  
*mayi dṛṣṭe tadā kiṃcinnyūnaṃ te na bhaviṣyati \|\| 39 \|\|*

36-39. Then Śiva, the delighted lord of the gods, became visible to him and said:—Make a fish-like canoe of wood. Coat it with tin and other metals. O Vīrasena, I shall create Yogamāyā and give it to you. Enter the canoe accompanied by Yogamāyā and your men. Proceed to the island in the Western ocean. Enter the crevice made by me. Perform the worship of Nāgeśvara. Thereafter secure the missile Pāśupata and slay the Rākṣasī and others. When I appear before you; you will not remain deficient in anything.

## \|\| 8.30.40 \|\|

*pārvatyāśca balaṃ caiva saṃpūrṇaṃ vai bhaviṣyati \|*  
*anye ca mleccharūpā ye bhaviṣyaṃti vane śubhāḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Pārvatī’s strength will grow to be complete. The Mlecchas of the forest will become pious and auspicious.

## \|\| 8.30.41 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā śaṃkarastatra vīrasenaṃ hi duḥkhaha \|*  
*kṛtvā kṛpāṃ ca mahatīṃ tatraivāṃtarddadhe prabhuḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. After saying this to Vīrasena and bestowing his mercy on everyone lord Śiva, the destroyer of miseries, vanished there itself.

## \|\| 8.30.42 \|\|

*iti dattavarasso'pi śivena paramātmanā \|*  
*śaktassa vai tadā kartuṃ saṃbabhūva na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Having received the boon form Śiva the supreme soul, Vīrasena became competent to do everything, of course.

## \|\| 8.30.43 \|\|

*evaṃ nāgeśvaro deva utpanno jyotiṣāṃ patiḥ \|*  
*liṃgarūpastrilokasya sarvakāmapradassadā \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. Thus is the manifestation of Nāgeśvara, the lord in the form of a Jyotirliṅga conferring the attainment of desires to the three worlds.

## \|\| 8.30.44 \|\|

*etadyaśśṛṇuyānnityaṃ nāgeśodbhavamādarāt \|*  
*sarvānkāmāniyāddhīmānmahāpātakanāśanān \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. He who listens to the origin of Nāgeśa respectfully attains all desires. He becomes intelligent. And his sins are destroyed.

## Chapter 31 - The greatness of Rāmeśvara

## \|\| 8.31.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi liṃgaṃ rāmeśvarābhidham \|*  
*utpannaṃ ca yathā pūrvamṛṣayaśśṛṇutādarāt \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. Hereafter I shall explain how the phallic image Rāmeśvara originated formerly. O sages, listen to it respectfully.

## \|\| 8.31.2 \|\|

*purā viṣṇuḥ pṛthivyāṃ cāvatatāra satāṃ priyaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Formerly, Viṣṇu the favourite of the good, took his incarnation as Rāma on the earth.

## \|\| 8.31.3 \|\|

*tatra sītā hṛtā viprā rāvaṇenorumāyinā \|*  
*prāpitā svagṛhaṃ sā hi laṃkāyāṃ janakātmajā \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. O brahmins, Rāma’s wife Sītā, the daughter of Janaka, was kidnapped by Rāvaṇa the wielder of great Māyā and taken to his abode in Laṅkā.

## \|\| 8.31.4 \|\|

*anveṣaṇaparastasyāḥ kiṣkindhākhyāṃ purīmagāt \|*  
*sugrīvahitakṛdbhūtvā vālinaṃ saṃjaghāna ha \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Searching for her, Rāma went to the city called Kiṣkindhā. Having alliance with Sugrīva for his benefit he killed Bāli.

## \|\| 8.31.5 \|\|

*tatra sthitvā kiyatkālaṃ tadanveṣaṇatatparaḥ \|*  
*sugrīvādyairlakṣmaṇena vicāraṃ kṛtavānsa vai \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. After staying there for sometime, he held consultations with Lakṣmaṇa, Sugrīva and others eager to find her out.

## \|\| 8.31.6 \|\|

*kapīnsaṃpreṣayāmāsa caturdikṣu nṛpātmajaḥ \|*  
*hanumatpramukhānrāmastadanveṣaṇahetave \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. The prince Rāma sent the monkeys, the chief of whom was Hanūmat, to various directions in order to find her out.

## \|\| 8.31.7 \|\|

*atha jñātvā gatāṃ laṃkāṃ sītāṃ kapivarānanāt \|*  
*sītācūḍāmaṇiṃ prāpya mumude so'ti rāghavaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. On getting the crest-jewel of Sītā and on knowing from the excellent monkey that she was in Laṅkā, Rāma rejoiced much.

## \|\| 8.31.8-9 \|\|

*sakapīśastadā rāmo lakṣmaṇena yuto dvijāḥ \|*  
*sugrīvapramukhaiḥ puṇyairvānarairbalavattaraiḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

*padmairaṣṭādaśākhyaiśca yayau tīraṃ payonidheḥ \|*  
*dakṣiṇe sāgare yo vai dṛśyate lavaṇākaraḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

8-9. O brahmins, accompanied by Hanumān the chief of monkeys, Lakṣmaṇa, Sugrīva and other monkey chiefs of great might and a vast army of monkeys eighteen thousand billions in number Rāma reached the shores of the Southern salt ocean.

## \|\| 8.31.10 \|\|

*tatrāgatya svayaṃ rāmo velāyāṃ saṃsthito hi saḥ \|*  
*vānaraissevyamānastu lakṣmaṇena śivapriyaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Reaching there, Rāma the favourite of Śiva stationed himself on the shore, being served by the monkeys and his brother Lakṣmaṇa.

## \|\| 8.31.11-13 \|\|

*hā jānaki kuto yātā kadā ceyaṃ miliṣyati \|*  
*agādhassāgaraścaivātāryā senā ca vānarī \|\| 11 \|\|*

*rākṣaso giridharttā ca mahābalaparākramaḥ \|*  
*laṃkākhyo durgamo durga iṃdrajittanayosya vai \|\| 12 \|\|*

*ityevaṃ sa vicāryaiva taṭe sthitvā salakṣmaṇaḥ \|*  
*āśvāsito vanaukobhiraṃgadādipurassaraiḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

11-13, “Alas where has Jānakī gone? When will she meet me. The ocean is very deep. The army of the monkeys is to be taken across. The Rākṣasa of great strength and valour, the uplifter of the mountain Kailāsa has resorted to his stronghold Laṅkā. His son is Indrajit, the conqueror of Indra. Thus thought Rāma in the company of Lakṣmaṇa standing on the shore itself. He was appeased by Aṅgada and other monkeys.

## \|\| 8.31.14 \|\|

*etasminnaṃtare tatra rāghavaśśaivasattamaḥ \|*  
*uvāca bhrātaraṃ prītyā jalārthī lakṣmaṇābhidham \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Then Rāma the excellent devotee of Śiva, desirous of water, lovingly spoke to his brother Lakṣmaṇa.

## \|\| 8.31.15 \|\|

*rāma uvāca \|*  
*bhrātarlakṣmaṇa vīreśāhaṃ jalārthī pipāsitaḥ \|*  
*tadānaya drutaṃ pātho vānaraiḥ kaiścideva hi \|\| 15 \|\|*

Rāma said:—

15\. O brother Lakṣmaṇa, lord of heroes, I am thirsty. I desire water. You get some water quickly through some monkey.

## \|\| 8.31.16 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*tacchrutvā vānarāstatra hyadhāvaṃta diśo daśa \|*  
*nītvā jalaṃ ca te procuḥ praṇipatya puraḥ sthitāḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

16\. On hearing it the monkeys ran in all directions. They brought water, bowed to Rāma and standing before him said.

## \|\| 8.31.17 \|\|

*vānarā ūcuḥ \|*  
*jalaṃ ca gṛhyatāṃ svāminnānītaṃ tattvadājñayā \|*  
*mahottamaṃ ca susvādu śītalaṃ prāṇatarpaṇam \|\| 17 \|\|*

The monkeys said:—

17\. O lord, at your behest we have brought excellent water, sweet, cool and refreshing. Please take it.

## \|\| 8.31.18 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*suprasannataro bhūtvā kṛpādṛṣṭyā vilokya tān \|*  
*tacchrutvā rāmacandro'sau svayaṃ jagrāha tajjalam \|\| 18 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

18\. On hearing it Rāma was much delighted. With a benign glance at them he accepted the water offered.

## \|\| 8.31.19 \|\|

*sa śaivastajjalaṃ nītvā pātumārabdhavānyadā \|*  
*tadā ca smaraṇaṃ jātamitthamasya śivecchayā \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. When Rāma, the devotee of Śiva, took the water and was about to drink it, it occurred to him at the will of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.31.20 \|\|

*na kṛtaṃ darśanaṃ śaṃbhorgṛhyate ca jalaṃ katham \|*  
*svasvāminaḥ pareśasya sarvānaṃdapradasya vai \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. “I have not yet had the sight of Śiva, the great lord, the bestower of all bliss. How can the water be taken in?”

## \|\| 8.31.21 \|\|

*ityuktvā ca jalaṃ pītaṃ tadā raghuvareṇa ca \|*  
*paścācca pārthivīṃ pūjāṃ cakāra raghunaṃdanaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. After saying this, he drank water and then performed the worship of Śiva’s earthen image.

## \|\| 8.31.22 \|\|

*āvāhanādikāṃścaiva hyupacārānprakalpya vai \|*  
*vidhivatṣoḍaśa prītyā devamānarca śaṅkaram \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. He worshipped Śiva performing all the sixteen ancillary services like invocation etc. with devotion according to the rules obtaining.

## \|\| 8.31.23 \|\|

*praṇipātaisstavairdivyaiśśivaṃ saṃtoṣya yatnataḥ \|*  
*prārthayāmāsa sadbhaktyā sa rāmaśśaṃkaraṃ mudā \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. After propitiating him with obeisances, divine eulogies etc. strenuously, Rāma joyously prayed to Śiva with great devotion.

## \|\| 8.31.24 \|\|

*rāma uvāca \|*  
*svāmiñchaṃbho mahādeva sarvadā bhaktavatsala \|*  
*pāhi māṃ śaraṇāpannaṃ tvadbhaktaṃ dīnamānasam \|\| 24 \|\|*

Rāma said:—

24\. O lord Śiva, O great lord, always favourably disposed towards your devotees, save me your devotee, dejected in the mind and seeking refuge in you.

## \|\| 8.31.25 \|\|

*etajjalamagādhaṃ ca vāridherbhavatāraṇa \|*  
*rāvaṇākhyo mahāvīro rākṣaso balavattaraḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. O Bhavatāraṇa (one who takes devotees across the ocean of worldly existence), this ocean is very deep. The Rākṣasa Rāvaṇa is heroic and mighty.

## \|\| 8.31.26 \|\|

*vānarāṇāṃ balaṃ hyetaccaṃcalaṃ yuddhasādhanam \|*  
*mamakāryaṃ kathaṃ siddhaṃ bhaviṣyati priyāptaye \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. The army of monkeys is a very fickle instrument of war. How can my task be achieved resulting in pleasing acquisition.

## \|\| 8.31.27 \|\|

*tasmindeva tvayā kāryaṃ sāhāyyaṃ mama suvrata \|*  
*sāhāyyaṃ te vinā nātha mama kāryyaṃ hi durlabham \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. In that, O lord of good rites, help must be rendered to me. O lord, without your help my purpose is difficult to be achieved.

## \|\| 8.31.28 \|\|

*tvadīyo rāvaṇo'pīha durjjayassarvathākhilaiḥ \|*  
*tvaddattavaradṛptaśca mahāvīrastrilokajit \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Rāvaṇa is your devotee. He is invincible in every respect to everyone. He is haughty due to the boons granted by you. He is a great hero who has conquered the three worlds.

## \|\| 8.31.29 \|\|

*apyahaṃ tava dāso'smi tvadadhīnaśca sarvathā \|*  
*vicāryeti tvayā kāryaḥ pakṣapātassadāśiva \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. O Sadāśiva, I too am your slave, in every respect subservient to you. Considering this, you shall be partial to me.

## \|\| 8.31.30-31 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityevaṃ sa ca saṃprārthya namaskṛtya punaḥpunaḥ \|*  
*tadā jayajayetyuccairuddhoṣaiśśaṃkareti ca \|\| 30 \|\|*

*iti stutvā śivaṃ tatra maṃtradhyānaparāyaṇaḥ \|*  
*punaḥ pūjāṃ tataḥ kṛtvā svāmyagre sa nanarta ha \|\| 31 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

30-31. After praying and bowing to the lord again and again, he repeated loudly, “O Śiva, be victorious, be victorious” and eulogised Śiva. Repeating the mantras and meditating, he performed the worship again and danced before the lord.

## \|\| 8.31.32 \|\|

*premī viklinnahṛdayo gallanādaṃ yadākarot \|*  
*tadā ca śaṃkaro devassuprasanno babhūva ha \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. With the heart drenched with devotional love he produced the throat-sound. Lord Śiva was much delighted.

## \|\| 8.31.33 \|\|

*sāṃgassaparivāraśca jyotīrūpo maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*yathoktarūpamamalaṃ kṛtvāvirabhavaddrutam \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Assuming the pure form as mentioned and accompanied by all his attendants, lord Śiva of brilliant form appeared before him immediately.

## \|\| 8.31.34 \|\|

*tatassaṃtuṣṭahṛdayo rāmabhaktyā maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*śivamastu varaṃ brūhi rāmeti sa tadābravīt \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Then, satisfied in his heart with Rāma’s devotion lord Śiva said—“O Rāma, may there be welfare to you. Mention the boon you wish to have.”

## \|\| 8.31.35 \|\|

*tadrūpaṃ ca tadā dṛṣṭvā sarve pūtāstatassvayam \|*  
*kṛtavānrāghavaḥ pūjāṃ śivadharmaparāyaṇaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. On seeing that form, they became sanctified. Rāma himself, interested in Saiva cult, performed the worship.

## \|\| 8.31.36 \|\|

*stutiṃ ca vividhāṃ kṛtvā praṇipatya śivaṃ mudā \|*  
*jayaṃ ca prārthayāmāsa rāvaṇājau tadātmanaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Eulogising in diverse ways and bowing to Śiva joyously he requested for his victory in the imminent fight with Rāvaṇa.

## \|\| 8.31.37 \|\|

*tataḥ prasannahṛdayo rāmabhaktyā maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*jayostu te mahārāja prītyā sa punarabravīt \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Delighted in his heart at Rāma’s devotion, lord Śiva said lovingly—“O great king, may you be victorious”.

## \|\| 8.31.38 \|\|

*śivadattaṃ jayaṃ prāpya hyanujñāṃ samavāpya ca \|*  
*punaśca prārthayāmāsa sāṃjalirnatamastakaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Securing the blessing of victory granted by Śiva and receiving the permission of the lord he prayed again with palms joined in reverence and head bent down.

## \|\| 8.31.39 \|\|

*\|\| rāma uvāca \|*  
*tvayā stheyamiha svāmiṃllokānāṃ pāvanāya ca \|*  
*pareṣāmupakārārthaṃ yadi tuṣṭo'si śaṃkara \|\| 39 \|\|*

Rāma said:—

39\. O Śiva, if you are pleased, you shall stay here, O lord, to sanctify the worlds and tç render help to the people.

## \|\| 8.31.40 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktastu śivastatra liṃgarūpo'bhavattadā \|*  
*rāmeśvaraśca nāmnā vai prasiddho jagatītale \|\| 40 \|\|*

Sūla said:—

40\. Thus implored Śiva took up the phallic image named Rāmeśvara. It is now famous on the surface of the world.

## \|\| 8.31.41 \|\|

*rāmastu tatprabhāvādvai sindhumuttīrya cāṃjasā \|*  
*rāvaṇādīnnihatyāśu rākṣasānprāpa tāṃ priyām \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. It was by Śiva’s favour that Rāma crossed the ocean soon, killed Rāvaṇa and other Rākṣasas and regained his beloved.

## \|\| 8.31.42 \|\|

*rāmeśvarasya mahimādbhuto'bhūdbhuvi cātulaḥ \|*  
*bhuktimuktipradaścaiva sarvadā bhaktakāmadaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. The greatness of Rāmeśvara became wonderful and unequalled in the world. It yields worldly pleasures and salvation and bestows all desires on the devotees always.

## \|\| 8.31.43 \|\|

*divyagaṃgājalenaiva snāpayiṣyati yaśśivam \|*  
*rāmeśvaraṃ ca sadbhaktyā sa jīvanmukta eva hi \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. He who performs the ablution of Śiva with the waters of the divine Gaṅgā and worships Rāmeśvara with great devotion becomes a living liberated soul indeed.

## \|\| 8.31.44 \|\|

*iha bhuktvākhilānbhogāndevānāṃ durlabhānapi \|*
