*aṃte prāpya paraṃ jñānaṃ kaivalyaṃ prāpnuyāddhruvam \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. After enjoying all pleasures here, rare even to the gods and deriving pçrfect knowledge in the end he will certainly attain salvation.

## \|\| 8.31.45 \|\|

*iti vaśca samākhyātaṃ jyotirligaṃ śivasya tu \|*  
*rāmeśvarābhidhaṃ divyaṃ śṛṇvatāṃ pāpahārakam \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Thus has been narrated to you the story of Rāmeśvara, the Jyotirliṅga of Śiva. It is divine. It destroys the sins of the devotees who listen to it.

## Chapter 32 - The narrative of Sudehā and Sudharmā

## \|\| 8.32.1 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*ataḥ paraṃ ca ghuśmeśaṃ jyotirliṃgamudāhṛtam \|*  
*tasyaiva ca sumāhātmyaṃ śrūyatāmṛṣisattamaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. O excellent sages, the next Jyotirliṅga to be mentioned is Ghuśmeśa. May its greatness be listened to.

## \|\| 8.32.2 \|\|

*dakṣiṇasyāṃ diśi śreṣṭho girirdeveti saṃjñakaḥ \|*  
*mahāśobhānvito nityaṃ rājate'dbhuta darśanaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. There is the excellent mountain in the Southern region named Deva. It shines with great splendour and is very wonderful to view.

## \|\| 8.32.3 \|\|

*tasyaiva nikaṭe kaścidbhāradvājakulodbhavaḥ \|*  
*sudharmā nāma vipraśca nyavasadbrahmavittamaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. A certain brahmin Sudharmā who was the most excellent among the knowers of Brahman and who was born of Bhāradvāja Family lived near it.

## \|\| 8.32.4 \|\|

*tasya priyā sudehā ca śivadharmaparāyaṇaḥ \|*  
*patisevāparā nityaṃ gṛhakarmavicakṣaṇā \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. His beloved wife Sudehā was interested in Śaiva cult and sacred rites. She was skilful in all household activities and she devoutly served her husband.

## \|\| 8.32.5 \|\|

*sudharmā ca dvijaśreṣṭho devatātithipūjakaḥ \|*  
*vedamārgaparo nityamagni sevāparāyaṇaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. The excellent brahmin Sudharmā worshipped gods and guests. He was devotedly attached to the Vedic path and scrupulously maintained the sacrificial fires.

## \|\| 8.32.6 \|\|

*trikālasaṃdhyayā yuktassūryyarūpasamadyutiḥ \|*  
*śiṣyāṇāṃ pāṭhakaścaiva vedaśāstravicakṣaṇaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. He had the brilliance of the Sun. He performed Sandhyā prayers thrice a day. He taught disciples and was an expert in the Vedas and Śāstras both.

## \|\| 8.32.7 \|\|

*dhanavāṃśca paro dātā saujanyaguṇabhājanaḥ \|*  
*śivakarmarato nityaṃ śaivaśśaivajanapriyaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. He was affluent and had also the qualities of gentlemanliness. He was a liberal donor, a devotee of Śiva, a favourite of his followers and he engaged himself in the sacred rites of Śiva-cult.

## \|\| 8.32.8 \|\|

*āyurbahu vyatīyāya tasya dharmaṃ prakurvataḥ \|*  
*putraśca nābhavattasya ṛtuḥ syādaphalaḥ striyāḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. A long period in his life thus passed when he continued his religious rites but no son was born to him. His wife’s menstrual periods were fruitless. She did not conceive.

## \|\| 8.32.9 \|\|

*tena duḥkhaṃ kṛtaṃ naiva vastujñānapareṇa hi \|*  
*ātmanastārakaścātmā hyātmanaḥ pāvanaśca saḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. He did not grieve over this because he realised the reality of different things. He knew that the soul is the uplifter of the soul and the soul is the sanctifier of the soul.

## \|\| 8.32.10 \|\|

*ityevaṃ mānasaṃ dhṛtvā duḥkhaṃ na kṛtavānstadā \|*  
*sudehā ca tadā duḥkhaṃ cakāra putrasambhavam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Thinking thus he did not feel aggrieved even mentally. But Sudehā was distressed for not having a son.

## \|\| 8.32.11 \|\|

*nityaṃ ca svāminaṃ sā vai prārthayadyatnasādhane \|*  
*putrotpādanahetośca sarvavidyāviśāradam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Everyday she implored her learned husband to strive for the acquisition of a son.

## \|\| 8.32.12-15 \|\|

*so'pi striyaṃ tadā bhartsya kiṃ putraśca kariṣyati \|*  
*kā mātā kaḥ pitā putraḥ ko baṃdhuśca priyaśca kaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

*sarvaṃ svārthaparaṃ devi trilokyāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*jānīhi tvaṃ viśeṣeṇa buddhyā śokaṃ na vai kuru \|\| 13 \|\|*

*tasmāddevi tvayā duḥkhaṃ tyajanīyaṃ suniścitam \|*  
*nityaṃ mahyaṃ tvayā naiva kathanīyaṃ śubhavrate \|\| 14 \|\|*

*evaṃ tāṃ sannivāryyaiva bhagavaddharmatatparaḥ \|*  
*āsītparamasaṃtuṣṭo dvandvaduḥkhaṃ samatyajat \|\| 15 \|\|*

12-15. He used to reproach her saying, “O gentle lady, there is no son, no mother, no father, no lover. Every one is selfishly inclined in the world, there is no doubt about this. Know this particularly with a keen intellect. Do not be aggrieved. O gentle lady, misery shall be eschewed by you, certainly. O one of auspicious rites, you shall not pester me like this everyday.” After enlightening her, he became interested in the worship of the lord. He was perfectly contented. He did not feel the misery or joy of mutually contrary interests.

## \|\| 8.32.16 \|\|

*kadācicca sudehā vai gehe ca sahavāsinaḥ \|*  
*jagāma priyagoṣṭhyarthaṃ vivādastatra saṃgataḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Once Sudehā went to the neighbour’s house for a friendly chat but a quarrel arose there.

## \|\| 8.32.17 \|\|

*tatpatnī strīsvabhāvācca bhartsitā sā tayā tadā \|*  
*uktā ceti duruktyā vai sudehā viprakāminī \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. She was reproached by the neighbour’s wife as natural to women. Sudehā was aggrieved with her harsh words.

## \|\| 8.32.18 \|\|

*dvijapatnyuvāca \|\| aputriṇi kathaṃ garvaṃ kuruṣe putriṇī hyaham \|*  
*maddhanaṃ bhokṣyate putro dhanaṃ te kaśca bhokṣyate \|\| 18 \|\|*

The neighbourly woman said:—

18\. O sonless woman, why are you arrogant? I have i son who will inherit my wealth. But there is none to inherit your property.

## \|\| 8.32.19 \|\|

*nūnaṃ hariṣyate rājā tvaddhanaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*dhigdhiktvāṃ te dhanaṃ dhikca dhikte mānaṃ hi vandhyake \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Undoubtedly the king will confiscate your estate. O barren woman, fie upon you, upon your wealth, upon your honour and prestige.

## \|\| 8.32.20 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*bhartsitā tābhiriti sā gṛhamāgatya duḥkhitā \|*  
*svāmine kathayāmāsa taduktaṃ sarvamādarāt \|\| 20 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

20\. Thus reproached by her she returned home depressed. She narrated to her husband all what her neighbour had said to her.

## \|\| 8.32.21 \|\|

*brāhmaṇo'pi tadā duḥkhaṃ na cakāra subuddhimān \|*  
*kathitaṃ kathyatāmeva yadbhāvi tadbhavetpriye \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. The intelligent brahmin was not grieved by hearing it. He said “Let her say what she wishes, O beloved, what is destined to happen will happen.”

## \|\| 8.32.22 \|\|

*ityevaṃ ca tadā tena hyāśvastāpi punaḥ punaḥ \|*  
*na tadā sātyajadduḥkhaṃ hyāgrahaṃ kṛtavatyasau \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Thus pacified and consoled again and again by her husband she did not stop worrying. She again pleaded.

## \|\| 8.32.23 \|\|

*sudehovāca \|*  
*yathā tathā tvayā putrassamutpādyaḥ priyo'si me \|*  
*tyakṣāmi hyanyathāhaṃ ca dehaṃ dehabhṛtāṃ vara \|\| 23 \|\|*

Sudehā said:—

23\. You are my husband. Somehow you shall beget a son. O excellent among men, otherwise I shall cast off this body.

## \|\| 8.32.24 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*evamuktaṃ tayā śrutvā sudharmā brāhmaṇottamaḥ \|*  
*śivaṃ sasmāra manasā tadāgrahanipīḍitaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

24\. On hearing what she said, Sudharmā the excellent brahmin, harassed by her importunities, mentally remembered Śiva.

## \|\| 8.32.25 \|\|

*agneragre'kṣipatpuṣpadvayaṃ vipro hyataṃdritaḥ \|*  
*manasā dakṣiṇaṃ puṣpaṃ tanmene putrakāmadam \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. The alert brahmin offered two flowers to the sacrificial fire. He thought in his mind that the right side flower would yield a son.

## \|\| 8.32.26 \|\|

*evaṃ kṛtvā paṇaṃ patnīmuvāca brāhmaṇassa ca \|*  
*anayorgrāhyamekaṃ te puṣpaṃ putra phalāptaye \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Making this as a condition the brahmin told his wife. “You shall take one of these two flowers to decide about the acquisition of a son”

## \|\| 8.32.27-28 \|\|

*tayā ca manasā dhṛtvā putraścaiva bhavenmama \|*  
*tadā ca svāminā yacca dhṛtaṃ puṣpaṃ sametu mām \|\| 27 \|\|*

*ityuktvā ca tayā tatra namaskṛtya śivaṃ tadā \|*  
*natvā cāgniṃ punaḥ prārthya gṛhītaṃ puṣpamekakam \|\| 28 \|\|*

27-28. She thought “I will surely have a son. Let the insignia flower come to me.” Then she bowed to Śiva and the sacrificial fire. After praying again she picked up one of the flowers.

## \|\| 8.32.29 \|\|

*svāminā ciṃtitaṃ yacca tadgṛhītaṃ tayā na hi \|*  
*sudehayā vimohena śivecchāsaṃbhavena vai \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. The insignia flower was missed by her due to delusion caused by Śiva’s desire.

## \|\| 8.32.30 \|\|

*taddṛṣṭvā puruṣaścaiva niśvāsaṃ paryamocayat \|*  
*smṛtvā śivapadāṃbhojamuvāca nijakāminīm \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. On seeing it the husband sighed deeply. Remembering the lotus-like feet of Śiva he spoke to his beloved.

## \|\| 8.32.31 \|\|

*sudharmovāca \|*  
*nirmitaṃ ceśvareṇaiva kathaṃ caivānyathā bhavet \|*  
*āśāṃ tyaja priye tvaṃ ca paricaryyāṃ kuru prabhoḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

Sudharmā said:—

31\. O beloved, how can what is ordained by the lord be otherwise. Cast off your vain hopes. Render service to the lord.

## \|\| 8.32.32 \|\|

*ityuktvā tu svayaṃ vipra āśāṃ parivihāya ca \|*  
*dharmakāryaratasso'bhūcchaṃkaradhyānatatparaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. After saying so and giving up hope himself, the brahmin became engaged in sacred rites and meditation on Śiva.

## \|\| 8.32.33 \|\|

*sā sudehāgrahaṃ naiva mumocātmajakāmyayā \|*  
*pratyuvāca patiṃ premṇā sāṃjalirnatamastakā \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Yearning for a son Sudehā did not leave off her pleadings. With palms joined in reverence and bending her head she lovingly said to her husband.

## \|\| 8.32.34 \|\|

*sudehovāca \|*  
*mayi putro na cāstvanyā patnīṃ kuru madājñayā \|*  
*tasyāṃ nūnaṃ sutaścaiva bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

Sudehā said:—

34\. Let there be no son born of me. With my consent you shall take another wife. Undoubtedly she will bear you a son.

## \|\| 8.32.35 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*tadaiva prathito vai sa brahmaṇaśśaivasattamaḥ \|*  
*uvāca svapriyāṃ tāṃ ca sudehāṃ dharma tatparaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

35\. Requested thus by his beloved wife, that brahmin the most excellent of the devotees of Śiva and fond of sacred rites spoke to her.

## \|\| 8.32.36 \|\|

*sudharmovāca \|*  
*tvadīyaṃ ca madīyaṃ ca sarvaṃ duḥkhaṃ gataṃ dhruvam \|*  
*tasmāttvaṃ dharmavighnaṃ ca priyo mā kuru sāṃpratam \|\| 36 \|\|*

Sudharmā said:—

36\. Our miseries have gone off, O beloved, do not put obstacles in our holy rites now.

## \|\| 8.32.37 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityevaṃ vāritā sā ca svāmātuḥ putrikāṃ tadā \|*  
*gṛhamānīya bhartāraṃ vṛṇu tvenāmidaṃ jagau \|\| 37 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

37\. Although prevented thus, she brought her mother’s younger daughter to her house and said to her husband “Take her.”

## \|\| 8.32.38 \|\|

*sudharmovāca \|*  
*idānīṃ vadasi tvaṃ ca matpriyeyaṃ tataḥ punaḥ \|*  
*putrasūśca yadā syādvai tadā sparddhāṃ kariṣyasi \|\| 38 \|\|*

Sudharmā said:—

38\. Now you say that she must be my beloved wife. But should she give birth to a son you will begin to be jealous of her.

## \|\| 8.32.39 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā tena patinā sā sudehā ca tatpriyā \|*  
*punaḥ prāha karau baddhvā sudharmāṇaṃ patiṃ dvijāḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

39\. O brahmins, when thus warned by her husband, Sudehā joined her palms in reverence and spoke to him,

## \|\| 8.32.40 \|\|

*nāhaṃ sparddhāṃ bhaginyā vai kariṣye dvijasattama \|*  
*upayacchasva putrārthamimāmājñāpayāmi ca \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. “O excellent brahmin, I will not be jealous of my own sister. Please marry her and beget a son. I urge upon you.”

## \|\| 8.32.41 \|\|

*ityevaṃ prārthitasso'pi sudharmā priyayā tayā \|*  
*ghuśmāṃ tāṃ samupāyaṃsta vivāhavidhinā dvijaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Thus implored by her, Sudharmā married that lady Ghuśmā with due ceremonies.

## \|\| 8.32.42 \|\|

*tatastāṃ pariṇīyātha prārthayāmāsa tāṃ dvijaḥ \|*  
*tvadīyeyaṃ kaniṣṭhā hi sadā poṣyānaghe priye \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. After marrying her he requested Sudehā, “O my pious beloved, this is your younger sister. You must bring her up.”

## \|\| 8.32.43 \|\|

*uktaiva sa ca dharmātmā sudharmā śaivasattamaḥ \|*  
*yathāyogyaṃ cakārāśu dharmasaṃgrahamātmanaḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. After saying this, Sudharmā, the most excellent devotee of Śiva, pursued hīs sacred rites to the utmost with his mind leaning towards virtue.

## \|\| 8.32.44 \|\|

*sā cāpi mātṛputrīṃ tāṃ dāsīvatparyavarttata \|*  
*parityajya virodhaṃ hi pupoṣāharniśaṃ priyā \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Sudehā served her sister like a hired wench eschewing all opposition and lovingly nurtured her.

## \|\| 8.32.45 \|\|

*kaniṣṭhā caiva yā patnī svasranujñāmavāpya ca \|*  
*pārthivānsā cakārāśu śriyamekottaraṃ śatam \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. The junior wife with the consent of her sister, made hundred and one earthen idols every day.

## \|\| 8.32.46 \|\|

*vidhānapūrvakaṃ ghuṣmā sopacārasamanvitam \|*  
*kṛtvā tānprākṣipattatra taḍāge nikaṭasthite \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. According to the sacred rules Ghuśmā rendered devotional services to these images and threw them in the neighbouring lake.

## \|\| 8.32.47 \|\|

*evaṃ nityaṃ sā cakāra śivapūjāṃ svakāmadām \|*  
*visṛjya punarāvāhya tatsaparyyāvidhānataḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Thus she performed the wish-yielding worship of Śiva every day leaving them off and invoking them again in accordance with the rules of devotional service.

## \|\| 8.32.48 \|\|

*kurvantyā nityamevaṃ hi tasyāśśaṃkarapūjanam \|*  
*lakṣasaṃkhyābhavatpūrṇā sarvakāmaphalapradā \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Performing the worship of Śiva thus regularly she completed a hundred-thousand images and their worship which would yield the benefits of all desires.

## \|\| 8.32.49 \|\|

*kṛpayā śaṃkarasyaiva tasyāḥ putro vyajāyata \|*  
*sundarassubhagaścaiva kalyāṇaguṇabhājam \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. By the mercy of Śiva a son was born to her. He was beautiful, fortunate and endowed with all auspicious qualities.

## \|\| 8.32.50 \|\|

*taṃ dṛṣṭvā paramaprītaḥ sa vipro dharmavittamaḥ \|*  
*anāsaktassukhaṃ bheje jñānadharmaparāyaṇaḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. On seeing him the brahmin was much delighted. Being the most excellent of all who knew virtue and engrossed in the virtues of knowledge he enjoyed the pleasure with due detachment.

## \|\| 8.32.51 \|\|

*sudehā tāvadasyāstu sparddhāmugrāṃ cakāra sā \|*  
*prathamaṃ śītalaṃ tasyā hṛdayaṃ hyasivatpunaḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Then Sudehā grew fiercely jealous of her. Her heart which was cool and quiet before began to blaze like fire now.

## \|\| 8.32.52 \|\|

*tataḥ paraṃ ca yajjātaṃ kutsitaṃ karma duḥkhadam \|*  
*sāvadhānena manasā śrūyatāṃ tanmunīśvarā \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. O great sages, may the despicable event that followed, causing misery, be heard with attention.

## Chapter 33 - Origin and glory of the Jyolirliṅga Ghuśmeśvara

## \|\| 8.33.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*putraṃ dṛṣṭvā kaniṣṭhāyā jyeṣṭhā duḥkhamupāgatā \|*  
*virodhaṃ sā cakārāśu na sahaṃtī ca tatsukham \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. On seeing the son of the younger sister, the elder sister was much distressed. Unable to see her pleasure she became inimical.

## \|\| 8.33.2 \|\|

*sarve putraprasūtiṃ tāṃ praśaśaṃsurnirantaram \|*  
*tayā tatsahyate na sma śiśo rūpādikaṃ tathā \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Her people celebrated the birth of a son continually. But she could not bear the sight of the beautiful child.

## \|\| 8.33.3 \|\|

*supriyaṃ tanayaṃ taṃ ca pitrossadguṇabhājanam \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā'bhavattadā tasyā hṛdayaṃ taptamagnivat \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. On seeing the son, a great favourite of the parents and endowed with all good qualities, her heart began to blaze like fire.

## \|\| 8.33.4 \|\|

*etasminnaṃtare viprāḥ kanyāṃ dātuṃ samāgatāḥ \|*  
*vivāhaṃ tasya tatraiva cakāra vidhivacca saḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. In the meantime many brahmins came there offering their daughters in marriage. Sudharmā celebrated the marriage of his son duly.

## \|\| 8.33.5 \|\|

*sudharmā ghuśmayā sārddhamānandaṃ paramaṃ gataḥ \|*  
*sarve saṃbaṃdhinastasyāṃ ghuśmāyāṃ mānamādadhuḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Sudharmā was extremely delighted in the company of Ghuśmā. All the relatives honoured Ghuśmā much.

## \|\| 8.33.6 \|\|

*taṃ dṛṣṭvā sā sudehā hi manasi jvalitā tadā \|*  
*atyantaṃ duḥkhamāpannā hā hatāsmīti vādinī \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. On seeing that, Sudehā blazed furiously in her mind. Extremely distressed she began to lament, “O I am doomed.”

## \|\| 8.33.7 \|\|

*sudharmmā gṛhamāgatya vadhūṃ putraṃ vivāhitam \|*  
*utsāhaṃ darśayāmāsa priyābhyāṃ harṣayanniva \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. When the daughter-in-law came to the house and the son was duly married, Sudharmā evinced his enthusiasm to both the wives with an intention of delighting them equally.

## \|\| 8.33.8 \|\|

*abhavaddharṣitā ghuṣmā sudehā duḥkhamāgatā \|*  
*na sahaṃtī sukhaṃ tacca duḥkhaṃ kṛtvāpatadbhuvi \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. But Ghuśmā was delighted and Sudehā was distressed. Unable to bear her happiness, she fell on the ground.

## \|\| 8.33.9 \|\|

*ghuśmā'vadadvadhūputrau tvadīyau na madīyakau \|*  
*vadhūḥ putraśca tāṃ prītyā prasūṃ śvaśramamanyata \|\| 9 \|\|*

Ghuśmā said:—

9\. “These are your own son and daughter-in-law not mine.” But the daughter-in-law considered Sudehā her mother-in-law and the son considered her his mother.

## \|\| 8.33.10 \|\|

*bharttā priyāṃ tāṃ jyeṣṭhāṃ ca mene naiva kaniṣṭhikām \|*  
*tathāpi sā tadā jyeṣṭhā svāntarmalavatī hyabhūt \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. The husband too loved the elder more than he loved the younger. Still the elder wife retained dirt in her mind.

## \|\| 8.33.11 \|\|

*ekasmindivase jyeṣṭhā sā sudehā ca duḥkhinī \|*  
*hṛdaye saṃcicinteti duḥkhaśāṃtiḥ kathaṃ bhavet \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. One day the senior wife Sudehā, extremely distressed in her heart, thought how she could subdue her misery.

## \|\| 8.33.12 \|\|

*sudehovāca \|*  
*madīyo hṛdayāgniśca ghuśmānetrajalena vai \|*  
*bhaviṣyati dhruvaṃ śāṃto nānyathā duḥkhajena hi \|\| 12 \|\|*

Sudehā said:—

12\. Certainly the fire in my heart can be tranquilised only through the tears of Ghuśmā caused by her misery and not otherwise.

## \|\| 8.33.13 \|\|

*ato'haṃ mārayāmyadya tatputraṃ priyavādinam \|*  
*agre bhāvi bhavedevaṃ niścayaḥ paramo mama \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. So I shall kill her son today, the son that speaks pleasing words to her. What is destined to happen shall happen. This is my final decision.

## \|\| 8.33.14 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*kadaryyāṇāṃ vicāraśca kṛtyākṛtye bhavennahi \|*  
*kaṭhoraḥ prāyaśo viprāḥ sāpatno bhāva ātmahā \|\| 14 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

14\. O brahmins, the inimical nature among co-wives is extremely ruthless and suicidal. The wicked and the mean do not ponder what shall be done or not done.

## \|\| 8.33.15 \|\|

*ekasmindivase jyeṣṭhā suptaṃ putraṃ vadhūyutam \|*  
*cicchide niśi cāṃgeṣu gṛhītvā churikāṃ ca sā \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. One day during the night as the son was sleeping with his wife she took a big knife and cut off his limbs.

## \|\| 8.33.16-17 \|\|

*sarvāṃgaṃ khaṇḍayāmāsa rātrau ghuśmāsutasya sā \|*  
*nītvā sarasi tatraivākṣipaddṛptā mahābalā \|\| 16 \|\|*

*yatra kṣiptāni liṃgāni ghuśmayā nityameva hi \|*  
*tatra kṣiptvā samāyātā suṣvāpa sukhamāgatā \|\| 17 \|\|*

16-17. The senior wife cut off the limbs of Ghuśmā’s son at night. Strong that she was she took them to the lake where the earthen images of Śiva were usually hurled by Ghuśmā. After casting off the limbs into the lake she returned and slept.

## \|\| 8.33.18 \|\|

*prātaścaiva samutthāya ghuśmā nityaṃ tathākarot\|\| \|*  
*sudharmā ca svayaṃ śreṣṭho nityakarma samācarat \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. In the morning Ghuśmā got up and performed her daily household duties. Sudharmā too performed his daily sacred rites.

## \|\| 8.33.19 \|\|

*etasminnaṃtare sā ca jyeṣṭhā kāryaṃ gṛhasya vai \|*  
*cakārānandasaṃyuktā suśāṃtahṛdayānalā \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. In the meantime the senior wife too got up and performed her duties with great pleasure, now that the fire in her heart had been tranquilised.

## \|\| 8.33.20 \|\|

*prātaḥkāle samutthāya vadhūśśayyāṃ vilokya sā \|*  
*rudhirārdrāṃ dehakhaṃḍairyuktāṃ duḥkhamupāgatā \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Getting up in the morning, the daughter-in-law saw to her horror the bed wet with blood and a few scattered pieces of the body.

## \|\| 8.33.21 \|\|

*śvaśrūṃ nivedayāmāsa putraste ca kuto gataḥ \|*  
*śayyā ca rudhirārdrā vai dṛśyaṃte dehakhaṃḍakāḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. She told her mother-in-law, “where has your son gone? The bed is wet with blood. Pieces of the body are also seen.”

## \|\| 8.33.22 \|\|

*hā hatāsmi kṛtaṃ kena duṣṭaṃ karma śucivrate \|*  
*ityuccārya rurodātivividhaṃ tatpriyā ca sā \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. The daughter-in-law fond of her husband began to lament—“O I am doomed. O my mother of holy rites, who has perpetrated this wicked deed?”

## \|\| 8.33.23 \|\|

*jyeṣṭhā duḥkhaṃ tadāpannā hā hatāsmi kileti ca \|*  
*bahirduḥkhaṃ cakārāsau manasā harṣasaṃyutā \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. The senior wife, though delighted inwardly, showed great distress outwardly saying “O I am doomed!”

## \|\| 8.33.24 \|\|

*ghuśmā cāpi tadā tasyā vadhvā dukhaṃ niśamya sā \|*  
*na cacāla vratāttasmānnityapārthivapūjanāt \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Ghuśmā heard the pitiable lamentation of her daughter-in-law but did not leave off her sacred rite, the daily worship of Śiva’s earthen image.

## \|\| 8.33.25 \|\|

*manaścaivotsukaṃ naiva jātaṃ tasyā manāgapi \|*  
*bhartāpi ca tathaivāsīdyāvadvratavidhirbhavet \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Her mind did not exhibit any agitation. The husband too remained calm till the completion of the sacred rite.

## \|\| 8.33.26 \|\|

*madhyāhne pūjanāṃte ca dṛṣṭvā śayyāṃ bhayāvahām \|*  
*tathāpi na tadā kiñcitkṛtaṃ duḥkhaṃ hi ghuśmayā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. At noon when the worship was concluded she looked at the terrible bed. Still Ghuśmā did not show signs of grief.

## \|\| 8.33.27-29 \|\|

*yenaiva cārpitaścāyaṃ sa vai rakṣāṃ kariṣyati \|*  
*bhaktapriyassa vikhyātaḥ kālakālassatāṃ gatiḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

*yadi no rakṣitā śaṃbhurīśvaraḥ prabhurekalaḥ \|*  
*mālākāra ivāsau yānyuṅkte tānviyunakti ca \|\| 28 \|\|*

*adya me ciṃtayā kiṃ syāditi tattvaṃ vicārya sā \|*  
*na cakāra tadā duḥkhaṃ śive dhairyaṃ samāgatā \|\| 29 \|\|*

27-29. “He who has given this boy will protect him too. The annihilator of Kāla, the goal of the good, is famous as a favourite of his devotee. Our protector, the sole lord, Śiva is like a gardener. He separates those whom he has united. What can I gain by worrying over it?” Realising this truth, being emboldened in dedicating herself to Śiva she did not grieve much.

## \|\| 8.33.30 \|\|

*pārthivāṃśca gṛhītvā sā pūrvavatsvasthamānasā \|*  
*śaṃbhornāmānyuccaraṃtī jagāma sarasastaṭe \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Taking the day’s earthly phallic images she went towards the bank of the lake repeating the names of Śiva, maintaining the normalcy of her mind as afore.

## \|\| 8.33.31 \|\|

*kṣiptvā ca pārthivāṃstatra parāvarttata sā yadā \|*  
*tadā putrastaḍāgastho dṛśyate sma taṭe tayā \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. When she cast off the earthen phallic images and returned she saw her son standing on the bank of the lake.

## \|\| 8.33.32 \|\|

*putra uvāca \|*  
*mātarehi miliṣyāmi mṛto'haṃ jīvito'dhunā \|*  
*tava puṇyaprabhāvāddhi kṛpayā śaṃkarasya vai \|\| 32 \|\|*

The son said:—

32\. I shall meet my mother. After dying I have been resuscitated, thanks to the power of her virtue and the mercy of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.33.33 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*jīvitaṃ taṃ sutaṃ dṛṣṭvā ghuṣmā sā tatprasūrdvijāḥ \|*  
*prahṛṣṭvā nābhavattatra duḥkhitā na yathā purā \|\| 33 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

33\. O brahmins, on seeing her son restored to life the mother was not delighted as she was not distressed before.

## \|\| 8.33.34 \|\|

*etasminsamaye tatra svāvirāsīcchivo drutam \|*  
*jyotirūpo maheśaśca saṃtuṣṭaḥ pratyuvāca ha \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. At that time, lord Śiva, appeared before her immediately in the form of a mass of splendour. He said delightedly.

## \|\| 8.33.35 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*prasanno'smi varaṃ brūhi duṣṭayā mārito hyayam \|*  
*enāṃ ca mārayiṣyāmi triśūlena varānane \|\| 35 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

35\. O exccllent-faced lady, I am delighted. Mention the boon you wish to have This boy had been killed by that wicked lady. I shall kill her with my trident.

## \|\| 8.33.36 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*purā tadā varaṃ vavre supraṇamya śivaṃ natā \|*  
*rakṣaṇīyā tvayā nātha sudeheyaṃ svasā mama \|\| 36 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

36\. Bowing down to Śiva she requested for the boon “O lord, Sudehā, my sister, must be protected by you.”

## \|\| 8.33.37 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*apakāraḥ kṛtastasyāmupakāraḥ kathaṃ tvayā \|*  
*kriyate hananīyā ca sudehā duṣṭakāriṇī \|\| 37 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

37\. Harm has been done by her. Why should you render help to her? Sudchā the perpetrator of wicked deeds must be killed.

## \|\| 8.33.38 \|\|

*ghuśmovāca \|*  
*tava darśanamātreṇa pātakaṃ naiva tiṣṭhati \|*  
*idānīṃ tvāṃ ca vai dṛṣṭvā tatpāpaṃ bhasmatāṃ vrajet \|\| 38 \|\|*

Ghuśmā said:—

38\. No sin stays, the moment you are seen. Now, on seeing you her sin will be reduced to ashes.

## \|\| 8.33.39 \|\|

*apakāreṣu yaścaiva hyupakāraṃ karoti ca \|*  
*tasya darśanamātreṇa pāpaṃ dūrataraṃ vrajet \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. ‘Sin is far removed at the mere sight of the person who renders help to those who harm him.

## \|\| 8.33.40 \|\|

*iti śrutaṃ mayā deva bhagavadvākyamadbhutam \|*  
*tasmāccaivaṃ kṛtaṃ yena kriyatāṃ ca sadāśiva \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. These wonderful words of the lord have been heard by me O lord. Hence, O Sadāśiva let it not be done otherwise.

## \|\| 8.33.41 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktastu tayā tatra prasanno'tyabhavatpunaḥ \|*  
*maheśvaraḥ kṛpāsiṃdhuḥ samūce bhaktavatsalaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

41\. Thus requested by her, lord Śiva the ocean of mercy, favourably disposed towards his devotees was delighted and spoke thus.

## \|\| 8.33.42 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*anyadvaraṃ brūhi ghuśme dadāmi ca hitaṃ tava \|*  
*tvadbhaktyā suprasanno'smi nirvikārasvabhāvataḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

42\. O Ghuśmā, request for another boon. I shall grant you what is beneficial to you. I am delighted at your devotion and your nature of being free from aberrations.

## \|\| 8.33.43 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*sovāca tadvacaśśrutvā yadi deyo varastvayā \|*  
*lokānāṃ caiva rakṣārthamatra stheyaṃ madākhyayā \|\| 43 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

43\. She said after hearing his words “If a boon is to be given by you, you shall stay here in my name for protecting the worlds.”

## \|\| 8.33.44 \|\|

*tadovāca śivastatra suprasanno maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*sthāsye'tra tava nāmnāhaṃ ghuśmeśākhyassukhapradaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Then the delighted lord Śiva said—“I shall stay here in your name. I shall be named Ghuśmeśa and I shall confer happiness.

## \|\| 8.33.45 \|\|

*ghuśmeśākhyaṃ suprasiddhaṃ liṃgaṃ me jāyatāṃ śubham \|*  
*idaṃ sarastu liṃgānāmālayaṃ jāyatāṃ sadā \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. May my phallic image named Ghuśmeśa be auspicious and famous. Let this lake be the storehouse of phallic images always.

## \|\| 8.33.46 \|\|

*tasmācchivālayaṃ nāma prasiddhaṃ bhuvanatraye \|*  
*sarvakāmapradaṃ hyetaddarśanātsyātsadā saraḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Hence it will be famous in the three worlds in the name of “Śivālaya.” This lake shall always yield desires by its mere sight.

## \|\| 8.33.47 \|\|

*tava vaṃśe śataṃ caikaṃ puruṣāvadhi suvrate \|*  
*īdṛśāḥ putrakāḥ śreṣṭhā bhaviṣyaṃti na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. O lady of good rites, upto a hundred and one generations in your family sons of great excellence like this will be born undoubtedly.

## \|\| 8.33.48 \|\|

*sustrīkāssudhanāścaiva svāyuṣyāśca vicakṣaṇāḥ \|*  
*vidyāvaṃto hyudārāśca bhuktimuktiphalāptaye \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. They will have good wives, plenty of wealth, longevity and skill in everything. They will be learned and liberal. They will attain worldly pleasures and salvation.

## \|\| 8.33.49 \|\|

*śatamekottaraṃ caiva bhaviṣyaṃti guṇādhikāḥ \|*  
*īdṛśo vaṃśavistāro bhaviṣyati suśobhanaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. They will be hundred and one endowed with many qualities. Your family will expand like this splendidly.

## \|\| 8.33.50 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā ca śivastatra liṃgarūpo'bhavattadā \|*  
*ghuśmeśo nāma vikhyātaḥ saraścaiva śivālayam \|\| 50 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

50\. After saying this Śiva assumed the form of a phallic image named Ghuśmeśa. He became famous. The lake was called Śivālaya.

## \|\| 8.33.51 \|\|

*sudharmā sa ca ghuśmā ca sudehā ca samāgatāḥ \|*  
*pradakṣiṇaṃ śivasyāśu śatamekottaraṃ dadhuḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Sudharmā, Ghuśmā and Sudehā came there and circumambulated Śiva hundred and one times.

## \|\| 8.33.52 \|\|

*pūjāṃ kṛtvā maheśasya militvā ca parasparam \|*  
*hitvā cāṃtarmalaṃ tatra lebhire paramaṃ sukham \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. After performing the worship of lord Śiva, fondly meeting one another and eschewing the dirt of the mind they attained great happiness.

## \|\| 8.33.53 \|\|

*putraṃ dṛṣṭvā sudehā sā jīvitaṃ lajjitābhavat \|*  
*tau kṣamāpyācaradviprā nijapāpāpahaṃ vratam \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. On seeing the son regained to life, Sudehā was ashamed. O brahmins, she performed the expiatory rite for her sins and requested for forgiveness.

## \|\| 8.33.54 \|\|

*ghuśmeśākhyamidaṃ liṃgamitthaṃ jātaṃ munīśvarāḥ \|*  
*taṃ dṛṣṭvā pūjayitvā hi sukhaṃ saṃvarddhate sadā \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. O great sages, the phallic image named Ghuśmeśa originated thus. Its very sight and worship renders happiness always.

## \|\| 8.33.55 \|\|

*iti vaśca samākhyātā jyotirliṃgāvalī mayā \|*  
*dvādaśapramitā sarvakāmadā bhukti muktidā \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. Thus I have narrated to you the twelve Jyotirliṅgas which yield all desires, worldly pleasures and salvation.

## \|\| 8.33.56 \|\|

*etajjyotirliṃgakathāṃ yaḥ paṭhecchṛṇuyādapi \|*  
*mucyate sarvapāpebhyo bhuktiṃ muktiṃ ca viṃdati \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. He who reads or listens to this narrative of Jyotirliṅgas gets rid of sins and attains worldly pleasures and salvation.

## Chapter 34 - Acquisition of Sudarśana by Viṣṇu

## \|\| 8.34.1 \|\|

*vyāsa uvāca \|*  
*iti śrutvā vacastasya sūtasya ca munīśvarāḥ \|*  
*samūcustaṃ supraśasya lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā \|\| 1 \|\|*

Vyāsa said:—

1\. O great sages, on hearing the words of Sūta the sages praised him and spoke desiring the welfare of the worlds.

## \|\| 8.34.2 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*sūta sarvaṃ vijānāsi tataḥ pṛcchāmahe vayam \|*  
*harīśvarasya liṃgasya mahimānaṃ vada prabho \|\| 2 \|\|*

The sages said:—

2\. O Sūta, you know everything. Hence we ask you. O holy lord, please narrate the greatness of the phallic image Harīśvara.

## \|\| 8.34.3 \|\|

*cakraṃ sudarśanaṃ prāptaṃ viṣṇuneti śrutaṃ purā \|*  
*tadārādhanatastāta tatkathā ca viśeṣataḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. O dear, this was heard by us formerly that by propitiating Harīśvara, Viṣṇu acquired his discus Sudarśana. Please narrate that story particularly.

## \|\| 8.34.4 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*śrūyatāṃ ca ṛṣiśreṣṭhā harīśvarakathā śubhā \|*  
*yatassudarśanaṃ labdhaṃ viṣṇunā śaṃkarātpurā \|\| 4 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

4\. O great sages, may the auspicious story of Harīśvara be heard, how Sudarśana was acquired by Viṣṇu formerly from Śiva.

## \|\| 8.34.5 \|\|

*kasmiṃścitsamaye daityāḥ saṃjātā balavattarāḥ \|*  
*lokāṃste pīḍayāmāsurdharmalopaṃ ca cakrire \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. On a certain occasion the Daityas became very powerful. They harassed the worlds and violated sacred rites.

## \|\| 8.34.6 \|\|

*te devāḥ pīḍitā daityairmahābalaparākramaiḥ \|*  
*svaṃ dukhaṃ kathayāmāsurviṣṇuṃ nirjararakṣakam \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Tormented by the Daityas of great strength and exploit, the gods narrated their misery to Viṣṇu, their saviour.

## \|\| 8.34.7 \|\|

*devā ūcuḥ \|*  
*kṛpāṃ kuru prabho tvaṃ ca daityaissaṃpīḍitā bhṛśam \|*  
*kutra yāmaśca kiṃ kurmaśśaraṇyaṃ tvāṃ samāśritāḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

The gods said:—

7\. O lord, be merciful. We are very much afflicted by the Daityas. Where shall we go? What shall we do? We have resorted to you, for you are worthy of seeking refuge by us.

## \|\| 8.34.8 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityevaṃ vacanaṃ śrutvā devānāṃ duḥkhitātmanām \|*  
*smṛtvā śivapadāṃbhojaṃ viṣṇurvacanamabravīta \|\| 8 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

8\. On hearing these words of the gods distressed in mind, Viṣṇu remembered the lotus-like feet of Śiva and said.

## \|\| 8.34.9 \|\|

*viṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*kariṣyāmi ca vaḥ kāryyamārādhya giriśaṃ surāḥ \|*  
*baliṣṭhāśśatravo hyete vijetavyāḥ prayatnataḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

Viṣṇu said:—

9\. O gods, I shall carry out your task after propitiating Śiva. These powerful enemies shall be conquered with great effort.

## \|\| 8.34.10 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktāste surāssarve viṣṇunā prabhaviṣṇunā \|*  
*matvā daityānhatānduṣṭānyayurdhāma svakaṃsvakam \|\| 10 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

10\. Thus consoled by lord Viṣṇu, the gods thought that the Daityas would be surely killed. They then went to their respective abodes.

## \|\| 8.34.11 \|\|

*viṣṇurapyamarāṇāṃ tu jayārthamabhajacchivam \|*  
*sarvāmarāṇāmadhipaṃ sarvasākṣiṇamavyayam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. For the victory of the gods, Viṣṇu worshipped śiva, the lord of all the gods, the imperishable and the cosmic witness of all.

## \|\| 8.34.12 \|\|

*gatvā kailāsanikaṭe tapastepe harissvayam \|*  
*kṛtvā kuṃḍaṃ ca saṃsthāpya jātavedasamagrataḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. He went to Kailāsa and dug a pit there wherein he kindled fire and performed penance.

## \|\| 8.34.13 \|\|

*pārthivena vidhānena maṃtrairnānāvidhairapi \|*  
*stotraiścaivāpyanekaiśca giriśaṃ cābhajanmudā \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. He worshipped Śiva joyously with various mantras and hymns prescribed for the worship of earthen image.

## \|\| 8.34.14 \|\|

*kamalaissaraso jātairmānasākhyānmunīśvarāḥ \|*  
*baddhvā caivāsanaṃ tatra na cacāla harissvayam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. O great sages, he worshipped with the lotus-flowers growing in the Mānasa lake. He sat firmly in the Yogic posture and did not move at all.

## \|\| 8.34.15 \|\|

*prasādāvadhi caivātra stheyaṃ vai sarvathā mayā \|*  
*ityevaṃ niścayaṃ kṛtvā samānarca śivaṃ hariḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Thinking decisively—“By all means I shall stay here till the lord is pleased” Viṣṇu worshipped Śiva.

## \|\| 8.34.16 \|\|

*yadā naiva harastuṣṭo babhūva haraye dvijāḥ \|*  
*tadā sa bhagavānviṣṇurvicāre tatparo'bhavat \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. O brahmins, when Śiva was not pleased with Viṣṇu, the latter began to think anxiously.

## \|\| 8.34.17 \|\|

*vicāryaivaṃ svamanasi sevanaṃ bahudhā kṛtam \|*  
*tathāpi na harastuṣṭo babhūvotikaraḥ prabhuḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. After thinking thus he rendered him various services. Still, lord Śiva, indulging in divine sports was not satisfied.

## \|\| 8.34.18 \|\|

*tatastu vismito viṣṇurbhaktyā paramayānvitaḥ \|*  
*sahasrairnāmabhiḥ prītyā tuṣṭāva parameśvaram \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Surprised much at this, Viṣṇu, endowed with great devotion, eulogised lord Śiva by reciting his thousand names with pleasure.

## \|\| 8.34.19 \|\|

*pratyekaṃ kamalaṃ tasmai nāmamaṃtramudīrya ca \|*  
*pūjayāmāsa vai śaṃbhuṃ śaraṇāgatavatsalam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Repeating every name, he worshipped Śiva, favourably disposed towards those who seek refuge in him? with a lotus flower.

## \|\| 8.34.20 \|\|

*parīkṣārthaṃ viṣṇubhaktestadā vai śaṃkareṇa ha \|*  
*kamalānāṃ sahasrāttu hṛtamekaṃ ca nīrajam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. In order to test the piety of Viṣṇu, one of the thousand lotus-flowers was taken away by Śiva.

## \|\| 8.34.21 \|\|

*na jñātaṃ viṣṇunā tacca māyākāraṇamadbhutam \|*  
*nyūnaṃ taccāpi sañjñāya tadanveṣaṇatatparaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. That wonderful Māyā of Śiva was not noticed by Viṣṇu. But when he came to know of the shortage he started to search.

## \|\| 8.34.22 \|\|

*babhrāma sakalāṃ pṛthvīṃ tatprītyai sudṛḍhavrataḥ \|*  
*tadaprāpya viśuddhātmā netramekamudāharat \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. In order to find it out he roamed over the world, steadily maintaining his sacred rite. But not finding the same, the pious Viṣṇu offered one of his eyes (instead of the lotus).

## \|\| 8.34.23 \|\|

*taṃ dṛṣṭvā sa prasanno'bhūcchaṃkarassarvaduḥkhahā \|*  
*āvirbabhūva tatraiva jagāda vacanaṃ harim \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. On seeing it Śiva who destroys all miseries was delighted and appeared before him. He spoke these words to Viṣṇu.

## \|\| 8.34.24 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*prasanno'smi hare tubhyaṃ varaṃ brūhi yathepsitam \|*  
*mano'bhilaṣitaṃ dadmi nādeyaṃ vidyate tava \|\| 24 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

24\. O Viṣṇu, I am delighted with you. Mention the boon you wish to have. I shall grant you what you desire. There is nothing which cannot be given to you.

## \|\| 8.34.25 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*tacchrutvā śaṃbhuvacanaṃ keśavaḥ prītamānasaḥ \|*  
*mahāharṣasamāpanno hyabravītsāṃjaliśśivam \|\| 25 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

25\. On hearing the words of Śiva, Viṣṇu was pleased in his mind. Extremely delighted he spoke to Śiva with palms joined in reverence.

## \|\| 8.34.26 \|\|

*viṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*vācyaṃ kiṃ me tvadagre vai hyantaryāmī tvamāsthitaḥ \|*  
*tathāpi kathyate nātha tava śāsanagauravāt \|\| 26 \|\|*

Viṣṇu said:—

26\. What is there to be mentioned to you? You are the immanent soul. Still, O Lord, I mention the same with due deference to your behest.

## \|\| 8.34.27 \|\|

*daityaiśca pīḍitaṃ viśvaṃ sukhaṃ no nassadā śiva \|*  
*daityānhaṃtuṃ mama svāminsvāyudhaṃ na pravarttate \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. O Sadāśiva, the entire universe is afflicted by the Daityas. We have no pleasure at all. Our weapons are in effectual in killing the Daityas.

## \|\| 8.34.28 \|\|

*kiṃ karomi kva gacchāmi nānyo me rakṣakaḥ paraḥ \|*  
*ato'haṃ parameśāna śaraṇaṃ tvāṃ samāgataḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. What shall I do? Where shall I go? I have no greater saviour than you. O lord, I seek refuge in you.

## \|\| 8.34.29 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvā ca namaskṛtya śivāya paramātmane \|*  
*sthitaścaivāgrataśśaṃbhoḥ svayaṃ ca purupīḍitaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

29\. After saying this he stood in affliction and bowed to Śiva, the supreme soul.

## \|\| 8.34.30 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*iti śrutvā vaco viṣṇordevadevo maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*dadau tasmai svakaṃ cakraṃ tejorāśiṃ sudarśanam \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. On hearing these words of Viṣṇu, Śiva, the lord of gods, gave him his discus Sudarśana shining with the mass of splendour.

## \|\| 8.34.31 \|\|

*tatprāpya bhagavānviṣṇurdaityāṃstānbalavattarān \|*  
*jaghāna tena cakreṇa drutaṃ sarvānvinā śramam \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. On receiving it Viṣṇu slew the powerful demons immediately with the discus without any strain.

## \|\| 8.34.32 \|\|

*jagatsvāsthyaṃ paraṃ lebhe babhūvussukhinassurāḥ \|*  
*suprītaḥ svāyudhaṃ prāpya harirāsīnmahāsukhī \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Thereupon the universe derived normalcy, the gods became happy. After receiving the Sudarśana missile Viṣṇu was greatly delighted.

## \|\| 8.34.33 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*kiṃ tannāmasahasraṃ vai kathaya tvaṃ hi śāṃkaram \|*  
*yena tuṣṭo dadau cakraṃ haraye sa maheśvaraḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

The sages said:—

33\. What is that hymn of Śiva that consists of his thousand names? What is that hymn whereby Śíva was so much delighted that he gave Viṣṇu the discus.

## \|\| 8.34.34 \|\|

*tanmāhātmyammama brūhi śivasaṃvādapūrvakam \|*  
*kṛpālutvaṃ ca śaṃbhorhi viṣṇūpari yathātatham \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Please expound the glory of this hymn to us and narrate the dialogue between Śiva and Viṣṇu. The merciful nature of Śiva towards Viṣṇu may also be narrated to us.

## \|\| 8.34.35 \|\|

*vyāsa uvāca \|*  
*iti teṣāṃ vacaśśrutvā munīnāṃ bhāvitātmanām \|*  
*smṛtvā śivapadāṃbhojaṃ sūto vacanamabravīt \|\| 35 \|\|*

Vyāsa said:—

35\. On hearing the words of the pious sages, Sūta spoke after remembering the lotus-like feet of Śiva.

## Chapter 35 - Śiva-sahasranāma: the thousand names of Śiva

## \|\| 8.35.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*śrūyatāmṛṣayaḥ śreṣṭhāḥ kathayāmi yathā śrutam \|*  
*viṣṇunā prārthito yena saṃtuṣṭaḥ parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*tadāhaṃ kathayāmyadya puṇyaṃ nāma sahasrakam \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. May this be heard, O great sages. I shall now recount the hymn of thousand names (see notes) of Śiva, whereby Śiva was satisfied.

## \|\| 8.35.2-131 \|\|

*śrīviṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*śivo haro mṛḍo rudraḥ puṣkaraḥ puṣpalocanaḥ \|*  
*arthigamyaḥ sadācāraḥ śarvaḥ śaṃbhurmaheśvaraḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

*caṃdrāpīḍaścaṃdramaulirviśvaṃ viśvaṃbhareśvaraḥ \|*  
*vedāṃtasārasaṃdohaḥ kapālī nīlalohitaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

*dhyānādhāro'paricchedyo gaurībharttā gaṇeśvaraḥ \|*  
*aṣṭamūrtirviśvamūrtistrivargasvargasādhanaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

*jñānagamyo dṛḍhaprajño devadevastrilocanaḥ \|*  
*vāmadevo mahādevaḥ paṭuḥ parivṛḍho dṛḍhaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

*viśvarūpo virūpākṣo vāgīśaḥ śucisattamaḥ \|*  
*sarvapramāṇasaṃvādī vṛṣāṅko vṛṣavāhanaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

*īśaḥ pinākī khaṭvāṃgī citraveṣaściraṃtanaḥ \|*  
*tamoharo mahāyogī goptā brahmā ca dhūrjaṭiḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

*kālakālaḥ kṛttivāsāḥ subhagaḥ praṇavātmakaḥ \|*  
*unnadhraḥ puruṣo juṣyo durvāsāḥ puraśāsanaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

*divyāyudhaḥ skaṃdaguruḥ parameṣṭhīḥ parātparaḥ \|*  
*anādimadhyanidhano girīśo girijādhavaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

*kuberabaṃdhuḥ śrīkaṃṭho lokavarṇottamo mṛduḥ \|*  
*samādhivedyaḥ kodaṃḍī nīlakaṃṭhaḥ parasvadhīḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

*viśālākṣo mṛgavyādhaḥ sureśaḥ sūryatāpanaḥ \|*  
*dharmadhāma kṣamākṣetraṃ bhagavānbhaganetrabhit \|\| 11 \|\|*

*ugraḥ paśupatistārkṣyaḥ priyabhaktaḥ paraṃtapaḥ \|*  
*dātā dayākaro dakṣaḥ karmaṃdīḥ kāmaśāsanaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

*śmaśānanilayaḥ sūkṣmaḥ śmaśānastho maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*lokakarttā mṛgapatirmahākarttā mahauṣadhiḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

*uttaro gopatirgoptā jñānagamyaḥ purātanaḥ \|*  
*nītiḥ sunītiḥ śuddhātmā somaḥ somarataḥ sukhī \|\| 14 \|\|*

*somapo'mṛtapaḥ saumyo mahātejā mahādyutiḥ \|*  
*tejomayo'mṛtamayo'nnamayaśca sudhāpatiḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

*ajātaśatrurālokaḥ saṃbhāvyo havyavāhanaḥ \|*  
*lokakaro vedakaraḥ sūtrakāraḥ sanātanaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

*maharṣikapilācāryo viśvadīptistrilocanaḥ \|*  
*pinākapāṇirbhūdevaḥ svastidaḥ svastikṛtsudhīḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

*dhātṛdhāmā dhāmakaraḥ sarvagaḥ sarvagocaraḥ \|*  
*brahmasṛgviśvasṛksargaḥ karṇikāraḥ priyaḥ kaviḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

*śākho viśākho gośākhaḥ śivo bhiṣaganuttamaḥ \|*  
*gaṃgāplavodako bhavyaḥ puṣkalaḥ sthapatiḥ sthiraḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

*vijitātmā vidheyātmā bhūtavāhanasārathiḥ \|*  
*sagaṇo gaṇakāyaśca sukīrticchinnasaṃśayaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

*kāmadevaḥ kāmapālo bhasmoddhūlitavigrahaḥ \|*  
*bhasmapriyo bhasmaśāyī kāmī kāṃtaḥ kṛtāgamaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

*samāvarto'nivṛttātmā dharmapuṃjaḥ sadāśivaḥ \|*  
*akalmaṣaśca puṇyātmā caturbāhurdurāsadaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

*durlabho durgamo durgaḥ sarvāyudhaviśāradaḥ \|*  
*adhyātmayoganilayaḥ sutaṃtustaṃtuvardhanaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

*śubhāṃgo lokasāraṃgo jagadīśo janārdanaḥ \|*  
*bhasmaśuddhikaro merurojasvī śuddhavigrahaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

*asādhyaḥ sādhusādhyaśca bhṛtyamarkaṭarūpadhṛk \|*  
*hiraṇyaretāḥ paurāṇo ripujīvaharo balī \|\| 25 \|\|*

*mahāhrado mahāgartassiddhovṛṃdāravaṃditaḥ \|*  
*vyāghracarmāṃbaro vyālī mahābhūto mahānidhiḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

*amṛto'mṛvapuḥ śrīmānpāñcajanyaḥ prabhaṃjanaḥ \|*  
*paṃcaviṃśatitattvasthaḥ pārijātaḥ parātparaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

*sulabhassuvrataśśūro vāṅmayaikanidhirnidhiḥ \|*  
*varṇāśramagururvarṇī śatrujicchatrutāpanaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

*āśramaḥ kṣapaṇaḥ kṣāmo jñānavānacaleśvaraḥ \|*  
*pramāṇabhūto durjñeyaḥ suparṇo vāyuvāhanaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

*dhanurdharo dhanurvedo guṇaḥ śaśiguṇākaraḥ \|*  
*satyassatyaparo'dīno dharmo godharmaśāsanaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

*anaṃtadṛṣṭirānaṃdo daṃḍo damayitā damaḥ \|*  
*abhicāryyo mahāmāyo viśvakarma viśāradaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

*vītarāgo vinītātmā tapasvībhūtabhāvanaḥ \|*  
*unmattaveṣaḥ pracchanno jitakāmo'jitapriyaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

*kalyāṇaprakṛtiḥ kalpaḥ sarvalokaprajāpatiḥ \|*  
*tarasvī tārako dhīmānpradhānaḥ prabhuravyayaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

*lokapāloṃ'tarhitātmā kalpādiḥ kamalekṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*vedaśāstrārthatattvajño'niyamo niyatāśrayaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

*caṃdraḥ sūryaḥ śaniḥ keturvarāṃgo vidrumacchaviḥ \|*  
*bhaktivaśyaḥ paraṃ brahma mṛgabāṇāparṇo'naghaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

*adriradryālayaḥ kāṃtaḥ paramātmā jagadguruḥ \|*  
*sarvakarmālayastuṣṭo maṃgalyo maṃgalāvṛtaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

*mahātapā dīrghatapāḥ sthaviṣṭha sthaviro dhruvaḥ \|*  
*ahaḥ saṃvatsaro vyāptiḥ pramāṇaṃ paramaṃ tapaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

*saṃvatsarakaro maṃtraḥ pratyayaḥ sarvatāpanaḥ \|*  
*ajaḥ sarveśvarassiddho mahātejā mahābalaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

*yogī yogyo mahāretā siddhiḥ sarvādiragrahaḥ \|*  
*vasurvasumanāḥ satyaḥ sarvapāpaharo haraḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

*sukīrti śobhanassragvī vedāṃgo vedavinmuniḥ \|*  
*bhrājiṣṇurbhojanaṃ bhoktā lokanātho durādharaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

*amṛtaśśāśvataśśāṃto bāṇahastaḥ pratāpavān \|*  
*kamaṃḍaludharo dhanvī hyavāṅmanasagocaraḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

*atīṃdriyo mahāmāyassarvavāsaścatuṣpathaḥ \|*  
*kālayogī mahānādo mahotsāho mahābalaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

*mahābuddhirmahāvīryo bhūtacārī puraṃ daraḥ \|*  
*niśācaraḥ pretacārī mahāśaktirmahādyutiḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

*anirdeśyavapuḥ śrīmānsarvācāryamanogatiḥ \|*  
*bahuśrutirmahāmāyo niyatātmā dhruvo'dhruvaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

*tejastejo dyutidharo janakaḥ sarvaśāsakaḥ \|*  
*nṛtyapriyo nṛtyanityaḥ prakāśātmā prakāśakaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

*spaṣṭākṣaro budho maṃtraḥ samānaḥ sārasaṃplavaḥ \|*  
*yugādikṛdyugāvarto gaṃbhīro vṛṣavāhanaḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

*iṣṭo viśiṣṭaḥ śiṣṭeṣṭaḥ sulabhaḥ sāraśo dhanaḥ \|*  
*tīrtharūpastīrthanāmā tīrthādṛśyastu tīrthadaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

*apāṃnidhiradhiṣṭhānaṃ vijayo jayakālavit \|*  
*pratiṣṭhitaḥ pramāṇajño hiraṇyakavaco hariḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

*vimocanassuragaṇo vidyeśo biṃdusaṃśrayaḥ \|*  
*vātarūpo'malonmāyī vikartā gahano guhaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

*karaṇaṃ kāraṇaṃ kartā sarvabaṃdhavimocanaḥ \|*  
*vyavasāyo vyavasthānaḥ sthānado jagadādijaḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

*gurudo lalito'bhedo bhāvātmātmani saṃsthitaḥ \|*  
*vīreśvaro vīrabhadro vīrāsanavidhirguruḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

*vīracūḍāmaṇirvettā cidānaṃdo nadīdharaḥ \|*  
*ājñādhārastriśūlī ca śipiviṣṭaḥ śivālayaḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

*bālakhilyo mahāvīrastigmāṃśurbadhiraḥ khagaḥ \|*  
*abhirāmaḥ suśaraṇaḥ subrahmaṇyaḥ sudhāpatiḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

*maghavānkauśiko gomānvirāmaḥ sarvasādhanaḥ \|*  
*lalāṭākṣo viśvadehaḥ sāraḥ saṃsāracakrabhṛt \|\| 54 \|\|*

*amoghadaṃḍī madhyastho hiraṇyo brahmavarcasaḥ \|*  
*paramārthaḥ paromāyī śaṃbaro vyāghralocanaḥ \|\| 55 \|\|*

*rucirbahurucirvedyo vācaspatirahaspatiḥ \|*  
*ravirvirocanaḥ skaṃdaḥ śāstā vaivasvato yamaḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

*yuktirunnatakīrtiśca sānurāgaḥ puraṃjayaḥ \|*  
*kailāsādhipatiḥ kāṃtaḥ savitā ravilocanaḥ \|\| 57 \|\|*

*viśvottamo vītabhayo viśvabharttā'nivāritaḥ \|*  
*nityo niyatakalyāṇaḥ puṇyaśravaṇakīrttanaḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

*dūraśravo viśvasaho dhyeyo duḥsvapnanāśanaḥ \|*  
*uttāraṇo duṣkṛtihā vijñeyo duḥsaho'bhavaḥ \|\| 59 \|\|*

*anādirbhūrbhuvo lakṣmīḥ kirīṭī tridaśādhipaḥ \|*  
*viśvagoptā viśvakarttā suvīro rucirāṃgadaḥ \|\| 60 \|\|*

*janano janajanmādiḥ prītimānnītimāndhruvaḥ \|*  
*vaśiṣṭhaḥ kaśyapo bhānurbhīmo bhīmaparākramaḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

*praṇavaḥ satpathācāro mahākośo mahādhanaḥ \|*  
*janmādhipo mahā devaḥ sakalāgamapāragaḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

*tattvaṃ tattvavidekātmā vibhurviṣṇurvibhūṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*ṛṣirbrāhmaṇa aiśvaryajanmamṛtyujarātigaḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

*paṃcayajñasamutpattirviśveśo vimalodayaḥ \|*  
*ātmayoniranādyaṃto vatsalo bhaktalokadhṛk \|\| 64 \|\|*

*gāyatrīvallabhaḥ prāṃśurviśvāvāsaḥ prabhākaraḥ \|*  
*śiśurgirirataḥ samrāṭ suṣeṇaḥ suraśatruhā \|\| 65 \|\|*

*anemiriṣṭanemiśca mukundo vigatajvaraḥ \|*  
*svayaṃjyotirmahājyotistanujyotiracaṃcalaḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

*piṃgalaḥ kapilaśmaśrurbhālanetrastrayītanuḥ \|*  
*jñānaskaṃdo mahānītirviśvotpattirupaplavaḥ \|\| 67 \|\|*

*bhago vivasvānādityo gatapāro bṛhaspatiḥ \|*  
*kalyāṇaguṇanāmā ca pāpahā puṇyadarśanaḥ \|\| 68 \|\|*

*udārakīrtirudyogī sadyogī sadasattrapaḥ \|*  
*nakṣatramālī nākeśaḥ svādhiṣṭhānaḥ ṣaḍāśrayaḥ \|\| 69 \|\|*

*pavitraḥ pāpahārī ca maṇipūro nabhogatiḥ \|*  
*hṛtpuṃḍarīkamāsīnaḥ śakraḥ śāṃto vṛṣākapiḥ \|\| 70 \|\|*

*uṣṇo gṛhapatiḥ kṛṣṇaḥ samartho'narthanāśanaḥ \|*  
*adharmaśatrurajñeyaḥ puruhūtaḥ puruśrutaḥ \|\| 71 \|\|*

*brahmagarbho bṛhadgarbho dharmadhenurdhanāgamaḥ \|*  
*jagaddhitaiṣī sugataḥ kumāraḥ kuśalāgamaḥ \|\| 72 \|\|*

*hiraṇyavarṇo jyotiṣmānnānābhūtarato dhvaniḥ \|*  
*ārogyo namanādhyakṣo viśvāmitro dhaneśvaraḥ \|\| 73 \|\|*

*brahmajyotirvasudhāmā mahājyotiranuttamaḥ \|*  
*mātāmaho mātariśvā nabhasvānnāgahāradhṛk \|\| 74 \|\|*

*pulastyaḥ pulaho'gastyo jātūkarṇyaḥ parāśaraḥ \|*  
*nirāvaraṇanirvāro vairaṃcyo viṣṭaraśravāḥ \|\| 75 \|\|*

*ātmabhūraniruddho'trirjñānamūrtirmahāyaśāḥ \|*  
*lokavīrāgraṇīrvīraścaṇḍaḥ satyaparākramaḥ \|\| 76 \|\|*

*vyālakalpo mahākalpaḥ kalpavṛkṣaḥ kalādharaḥ \|*  
*alaṃkariṣṇuracalo rociṣṇurvikramonnataḥ \|\| 77 \|\|*

*āyuḥ śabdapatirvāgmī plavanaśśikhisārathiḥ \|*  
*asaṃsṛṣṭo'tithiśśatruḥ pramāthī pādapāsanaḥ \|\| 78 \|\|*

*vasuśravā kavyavāhaḥ pratapto viśvabhojanaḥ \|*  
*japyo jarādiśamano lohitaśca tanūnapāt \|\| 79 \|\|*

*pṛṣadaśvo nabhoyoniḥ supratīkastamisrahā \|*  
*nidāghastapano meghabhakṣaḥ parapuraṃjayaḥ \|\| 80 \|\|*

*sukhānilassuniṣpannassurabhiśśiśirātmakaḥ \|*  
*vasaṃto mādhavo grīṣmo nabhasyo bījavāhanaḥ \|\| 81 \|\|*

*aṃgirā gururātreyo vimalo viśvapāvanaḥ \|*  
*pāvanaḥ purajicchakrastraividyo navavāraṇa \|\| 82 \|\|*

*manobuddhirahaṃkāraḥ kṣetrajñaḥ kṣetrapālakaḥ \|*  
*jamadagnirbalanidhirvigālo viśvagālavaḥ \|\| 83 \|\|*

*aghoro'nuttaro yajñaḥ śreyo niḥśreyasapradaḥ \|*  
*śailo gaganakuṃdābho dānavārirariṃdamaḥ \|\| 84 \|\|*

*cāmuṇḍo janakaścārurniśśalyo lokaśalyadhṛk \|*  
*caturvedaścaturbhāvaścaturaścatura priyaḥ \|\| 85 \|\|*

*āmnāyo'tha samāmnāyastīrthadevaśivālayaḥ \|*  
*bahurūpo mahārūpassarvarūpaścarācaraḥ \|\| 86 \|\|*

*nyāyanirmāyako neyo nyāyagamyo niraṃjanaḥ \|*  
*sahasramūrddhā deveṃdrassarvaśastraprabhaṃjanaḥ \|\| 87 \|\|*

*muṃḍī virūpo vikṛto daṃḍī nādī guṇottamaḥ \|*  
*piṃgalākṣo hi bahvayo nīlagrīvo nirāmayaḥ \|\| 88 \|\|*

*sahasrabāhussarveśaśśaraṇyassarvalokadhṛk \|*  
*padmāsanaḥ paraṃ jyotiḥ pāramparyyaphalapradaḥ \|\| 89 \|\|*

*padmagarbho mahāgarbho viśvagarbho vicakṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*parāvarajño varado vareṇyaśca mahāsvanaḥ \|\| 90 \|\|*

*devāsuragururdevo devāsuranamaskṛtaḥ \|*  
*devāsuramahā mitro devāsuramaheśvaraḥ \|\| 91 \|\|*

*devāsureśvaro divyo devāsuramahāśrayāḥ \|*  
*devadevo'nayo'ciṃtyo devatātmātmasaṃbhavaḥ \|\| 92 \|\|*

*sadyo mahāsuravyādho devasiṃho divākaraḥ \|*  
*vibudhāmacaraḥ śreṣṭhaḥ sarvadevottamottama \|\| 93 \|\|*

*śivajñānarataḥ śrīmāñśikhī śrīparvatapriyaḥ \|*  
*vajrahastassiddhakhaṅgo narasiṃhanipātanaḥ \|\| 94 \|\|*

*brahmacārī lokacārī dharmacārī dhanādhipaḥ \|*  
*nandī naṃdīśvaro'naṃto nagnavratadharaśśuciḥ \|\| 95 \|\|*

*liṃgādhyakṣaḥ surādhyakṣo yugādhyakṣo yugāpahaḥ \|*  
*svadhāmā svagataḥ svargī svaraḥ svaramayaḥ svanaḥ \|\| 96 \|\|*

*bāṇādhyakṣo bījakartā karmakṛddharmasaṃbhavaḥ \|*  
*daṃbho lobho'tha vai śaṃbhussarva bhūtamaheśvaraḥ \|\| 97 \|\|*

*śmaśānanilayastryakṣassa turapratimākṛtiḥ \|*  
*lokottarasphuṭolokaḥ tryaṃbako nāgabhūṣaṇaḥ \|\| 98 \|\|*

*aṃdhakāri makhadveṣī viṣṇukaṃdharapātanaḥ \|*  
*hīnadoṣo'kṣayaguṇo dakṣāriḥ pūṣadaṃtabhit \|\| 99 \|\|*

*pūrṇaḥ pūrayitā puṇyaḥ sukumāraḥ sulocanaḥ \|*  
*sanmārgamapriyo dhūrttaḥ puṇyakīrtiranāmayaḥ \|\| 100 \|\|*

*manojavastīrthakaro jaṭilo niyameśvaraḥ \|*  
*jīvitāṃtakaro nityo vasuretā vasupradaḥ \|\| 101 \|\|*

*sadgatiḥ siddhidaḥ siddhiḥ sajjātiḥ khalakaṃṭakaḥ \|*  
*kalādharo mahākālabhūtaḥ satyaparāyaṇaḥ \|\| 102 \|\|*

*lokalāvaṇyakartā ca lokottarasukhālayaḥ \|*  
*caṃdrasaṃjīvanaśśāstā lokagrāho mahādhipaḥ \|\| 103 \|\|*

*lokabaṃdhurlokanāthaḥ kṛtajñaḥ kṛtibhūṣitaḥ \|*  
*anapāyo'kṣaraḥ kāṃtaḥ sarvaśastrabhṛtāṃ varaḥ \|\| 104 \|\|*

*tejomayo śrutidharo lokamānī ghṛṇārṇavaḥ \|*  
*śucismitaḥ prasannātmā hyajeyo duratikramaḥ \|\| 105 \|\|*

*jyotirmayo jagannātho nirākāro jaleśvaraḥ \|*  
*tumbavīṇo mahākāyo viśokaśśokanāśanaḥ \|\| 106 \|\|*

*trilokapastrilokeśaḥ sarvaśuddhiradhokṣajaḥ \|*  
*avyaktalakṣaṇo devo vyakto'vyakto viśāṃpatiḥ \|\| 107 \|\|*

*paraḥ śivo vasurnāsāsāro mānadharo yamaḥ \|*  
*brahmā viṣṇuḥ prajāpālo haṃso haṃsagatirvayaḥ \|\| 108 \|\|*

*vedhā vidhātā dhātā ca sraṣṭā harttā caturmukhaḥ \|*  
*kailāsaśikharāvāsī sarvāvāsī sadāgati \|\| 109 \|\|*

*hiraṇyagarbho druhiṇo bhūtapālo'tha bhūpatiḥ \|*  
*sadyogī yogavidyogīvarado brāhmaṇapriya \|\| 110 \|\|*

*devapriyo devanātho devako devaciṃtakaḥ \|*  
*viṣamākṣo virūpākṣo vṛṣado vṛṣavardhanaḥ \|\| 111 \|\|*

*nirmamo nirahaṃkāro nirmoho nirupadravaḥ \|*  
*darpahā darpado dṛptaḥ sarvārthaparivarttakaḥ \|\| 112 \|\|*

*sahasrārcirbhūtibhūṣaḥ snigdhākṛtiradakṣiṇaḥ \|*  
*bhūtabhavyabhavannātho vibhavo bhūtināśanaḥ \|\| 113 \|\|*

*artho'nartho mahākośa parakāyaikapaṃḍita \|*  
*niṣkaṃṭakaḥ kṛtānaṃdo nirvyājo vyājamardanaḥ \|\| 114 \|\|*

*sattvavānsāttvikaḥ satyaḥ kṛtasnehaḥ kṛtāgamaḥ \|*  
*akaṃpito guṇagrāhī naikātmānaikakarmakṛt \|\| 115 \|\|*

*suprītaḥ sukhadaḥ sūkṣmaḥ sukaro dakṣiṇānilaḥ \|*  
*naṃdiskaṃdo dharo dhuryaḥ prakaṭaḥ prītivardhanaḥ \|\| 116 \|\|*

*aparājitaḥ sarvasaho goviṃdaḥ sattvavāhanaḥ \|*  
*adhṛtaḥ svadhṛtaḥ siddhaḥ pūtamūrtiryaśodhanaḥ \|\| 117 \|\|*

*vārāhaśṛṃgadhṛk śṛṃgī balavānekanāyakaḥ \|*  
*śrutiprakāśaḥ śrutimāne kabaṃdhuranekadhṛk \|\| 118 \|\|*

*śrīvatsalaḥ śivāraṃbhaḥ śāṃtabhadraḥ samo yaśaḥ \|*  
*bhūyaśo bhūṣaṇo bhūtirbhūtikṛdbhūtabhāvanaḥ \|\| 119 \|\|*

*akaṃpo bhaktikāyastu kālahāniḥ kalāvibhuḥ \|*  
*satyavratī mahātyāgī nityaśāṃtiparāyaṇaḥ \|\| 120 \|\|*

*parārthavṛttirvarado viraktastu viśāradaḥ \|*  
*śubhadaḥ śubhakartā ca śubhanāmā śubhaḥ svayam \|\| 121 \|\|*

*anarthito guṇagrāhī hyakartā kanakaprabhaḥ \|*  
*svabhāvabhadro madhyastha śatrughno vighnanāśanaḥ \|\| 122 \|\|*

*śikhaṃḍī kavacī śūlī jaṭī muṃḍī ca kuṃḍalī \|*  
*amṛtyuḥ sarvadṛk siṃhastejorāśirmahāmaṇiḥ \|\| 123 \|\|*

*asaṃkhyeyo'prameyātmā vīryavān vīryakovidaḥ \|*  
*vedyaśca vai viyogātmā saptāvaramunīśvaraḥ \|\| 124 \|\|*

*anuttamo durādharṣo madhuraḥ priyadarśanaḥ \|*  
*sureśa smaraṇaḥ sarvaḥ śabdaḥ pratapatāṃ varaḥ \|\| 125 \|\|*

*kālapakṣaḥ kālakālaḥ sukṛtī kṛtavāsukiḥ \|*  
*maheṣvāso mahībhartā niṣkalaṃko viśṛṃkhala \|\| 126 \|\|*

*dyumaṇistaraṇirdhanyaḥ siddhidaḥ siddhisādhanaḥ \|*  
*viśvatassaṃvṛtastu vyūḍhorasko mahābhujaḥ \|\| 127 \|\|*

*sarvayonirnirātaṃko naranārāyaṇapriyaḥ \|*  
*nirlepo yatisaṃgātmā nirvyaṃgo vyaṃganāśanaḥ \|\| 128 \|\|*

*stavyaḥ stavapriyaḥ stotā vyāsamūrtirniraṃkulaḥ \|*  
*niravadyamayopāyo vidyārāśiśca satkṛtaḥ \|\| 129 \|\|*

*praśāṃtabuddhirakṣuṇṇaḥ saṃgraho nityasuṃdaraḥ \|*  
*vaiyāghradhuryo dhātrīśaḥ saṃkalpaḥ śarvarīpatiḥ \|\| 130 \|\|*

*paramārthagururdattaḥ sūrirāśritavatsalaḥ \|*  
*somo rasajño rasadaḥ sarvasattvāvalaṃbanaḥ \|\| 131 \|\|*

Viṣṇu said:—

## \|\| 8.35.132 \|\|

*evaṃ nāmnāṃ sahasreṇa tuṣṭāva hi haraṃ hariḥ \|*  
*prārthayāmāsa śambhuṃ vai pūjayāmāsa paṃkajaḥ \|\| 132 \|\|*

132\. Thus Viṣṇu eulogised Śiva with the thousand names. Śiva was prayed to and worshipped with lotuses.

## \|\| 8.35.133 \|\|

*tataḥ sa kautukī śambhuścakāra caritaṃ dvijāḥ \|*  
*mahādbhutaṃ sukhakaraṃ tadeva śṛṇutādarāt \|\| 133 \|\|*

133\. O brahmins, then the enthusiastic Śiva performed something wonderful and pleasing. Listen to that attentively.

Notes on the Śiva-sahasranāma:

In fact the text records more than one thousand names. Some are identical in form: śivālayaḥ śivālayȧḥ; trilocanaḥ-trilocanaḥ; kṛttivāsāḥ-kṛttibhūṣitaḥ; śubhaḥ-śubhaḥ; śubhadaḥ-śubhakartā; muṇḍī-muṇḍī; others are repetitions in sense: trilocanaḥ-tryakṣaḥ; virūpākṣaḥ-viṣamākṣaḥ; nīlagnīvaḥ-nīlakaṇṭhaḥ; bhālanetraḥ-lalāṭākṣaḥ; śmaśānanilayaḥ-śmaśānasthaḥ; others are the names of gods and sages: “caturbhujaḥ, caturmukhaḥ, dhātā, vidhātā,vedhāḥ” (of Brahma), “govindaḥ, viṣṇuḥ, janārdanaḥ, jagadīśaḥ” (of Viṣṇu), “sureśaḥ, śaktraḥ, purandaraḥ” (of Indra), “datta” (of Dattātreya), “druhaṇa, durvāsasa, kapila” (of the sages). On the other hand, sorne of the well known, significant names such as “ahirbudhnya, ajaikapād, raivata” are missing.

## Chapter 36 - Efficacy of the thousand names of Śiva

## \|\| 8.36.1 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*śrutvā viṣṇukṛtaṃ divyaṃ paranāmavibhūṣitam \|*  
*sahasranāmasvastotraṃ prasanno'bhūnmaheśvaraḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. On hearing the eulogy made by Viṣṇu consisting of the hymn of his thousand divine names lord Śiva became delighted.

## \|\| 8.36.2 \|\|

*parīkṣārthaṃ harerīśaḥ kamaleṣu maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*gopayāmāsa kamalaṃ tadaikaṃ bhuvaneśvaraḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. In order to test Viṣṇu, Śiva, the lord of the worlds concealed one of the lotus-flowers.

## \|\| 8.36.3 \|\|

*paṃkajeṣu tadā teṣu sahasreṣu babhūva ca \|*  
*nyūnamekaṃ tadā viṣṇurvihvalaśśivapūjane \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. There was a shortage of one lotus-flower from among the thousand lotuses. Hence Viṣṇu was distressed in the course of his worship of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.36.4 \|\|

*hṛdā vicāritaṃ tena kuto vai kamalaṃ gatam \|*  
*yātaṃ yātu sukhenaiva mannetraṃ kamalaṃ na kim \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. He thought within himself “Where could the lotus-flower have gone? If it has gone, let it go for good. Is not my own eye a lotus?

## \|\| 8.36.5 \|\|

*jñātveti netramuddhṛtya sarvasattvāvalambanāt \|*  
*pūjayāmāsa bhāvena stavayāmāsa tena ca \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. After thinking thus, he plucked out his eye and repeated the last name Sarvasattvāvalambana. Full of emotion he worshipped and eulogised Śiva.

## \|\| 8.36.6 \|\|

*tataḥ stutamatho dṛṣṭvā tathābhūtaṃ haro harim \|*  
*mā meti vyāharanneva prādurāsījjagadguruḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. On seeing Viṣṇu in that attitude, Viṣṇu the preceptor of the universe appeared before him and forbade him do that.

## \|\| 8.36.7 \|\|

*tasmādavatatārāśu maṇḍalātpārthivasya ca \|*  
*pratiṣṭhitasya hariṇā svaliṃgasya maheśvaraḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Lord Śiva descended from the pedestal of that earthern phallic image installed by Viṣṇu.

## \|\| 8.36.8 \|\|

*yathoktarūpiṇaṃ śambhuṃ tejorāśisamutthitam \|*  
*namaskṛtya puraḥ sthitvā sa tuṣṭāva viśeṣataḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Bowing to Śiva, rising up in the form of a mass of splendour as mentioned before, Viṣṇu stood in front of him and eulogised him particularly.

## \|\| 8.36.9 \|\|

*tadā prāha mahādevaḥ prasannaḥ prahasanniva \|*  
*samprekṣya kṛpayā viṣṇuṃ kṛtāṃjalipuṭaṃ sthitam \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Looking mercifully at Viṣṇu, who stood before him with palms joined in reverence, the delighted lord Śiva laughingly said then.

## \|\| 8.36.10 \|\|

*śaṅkara uvāca \|*  
*jñātaṃ mayedaṃ sakalaṃ tava cittepsitaṃ hare \|*  
*devakāryaṃ viśeṣeṇa devakāryyaratātmanaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

10\. O Viṣṇu, all the desires in your mind, particularly the task of the gods have been understood by me. Indeed you are engaged in the task of the gods.

## \|\| 8.36.11 \|\|

*devakāryyasya siddhyarthaṃ daityanāśāya cāśramam \|*  
*sudarśanākhyaṃ cakraṃ ca dadāmi tava śobhanam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. For the achievement of the task of the gods and for the destruction of the Daityas without strain, I shall give the auspicious discus Sudarśana to you.

## \|\| 8.36.12 \|\|

*yadrūpaṃ bhavatā dṛṣṭaṃ sarvalokasukhāvaham \|*  
*hitāya tava deveśa dhṛtaṃ bhāvaya taddhruvam \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. O lord of the gods, for your benefit ponder over this form, pleasing to all the worlds, which has seen by you (now).

## \|\| 8.36.13 \|\|

*raṇājire smṛtaṃ tadvai devānāṃ duḥkhanāśanam \|*  
*idaṃ cakramidaṃ rūpamidaṃ nāmasahasrakam \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. If this discus, this form and this hymn of thousand names is remembered in the battlefield it will quell the misery of the gods.

## \|\| 8.36.14 \|\|

*ye śṛṇvanti sadā bhaktyā siddhi syādanapāyinī \|*  
*kāmānāṃ sakalānāṃ ca prasādānmama suvrata \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. O one of good rites, thanks to my blessings, all those who listen to this will achieve their desires without serious dangers.

## \|\| 8.36.15 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*evamuktvā dadau cakraṃ sūryāyutasamaprabham \|*  
*sudarśanaṃ svapādotthaṃ sarvaśatruvināśanam \|\| 15 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

15\. After saying this, the lord gave the discus of the brilliance of ten thousand suns, born of his own feet and destructive of all enemies, to Viṣṇu.

## \|\| 8.36.16 \|\|

*viṣṇuścāpi susaṃskṛtya jagrāhodaṅmukhastadā \|*  
*namaskṛtya mahādevaṃ viṣṇurvacanamabravīt \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Viṣṇu purified himself and accepted it facing the north. After making obeisance to lord Śiva, Viṣṇu spoke these words to him.

## \|\| 8.36.17 \|\|

*viṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu deva mayā dhyeyaṃ paṭhanīyaṃ ca kiṃ prabho \|*  
*duḥkhānāṃ nāśanārthaṃ hi vada tvaṃ lokaśaṃkara \|\| 17 \|\|*

Viṣṇu said:—

17\. O lord, O benefactor of the worlds, listen. Please tell me what I shall meditate upon and what I shall recite for the destruction of the distress.

## \|\| 8.36.18 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*iti pṛṣṭastadā tena santuṣṭastu śivo'bravīt \|*  
*prasannamānaso bhūtvā viṣṇuṃ devasahāyakam \|\| 18 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

18\. On being thus asked by him, Śiva becoming satisfied and delighted in his mind spoke to Viṣṇu who helped the gods.

## \|\| 8.36.19 \|\|

*\|\| śiva uvāca \|*  
*rūpaṃ dhyeyaṃ hare me hi sarvānarthapraśāntaye \|*  
*anekaduḥkhanāśārthaṃ paṭha nāmasahasrakam \|\| 19 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

19\. O Viṣṇu, it is my form that is to be meditated upon for subduing all mishaps. Recite my thousand names for destroying the distress that troubles you.

## \|\| 8.36.20 \|\|

*dhāryyaṃ cakraṃ sadā me hi savārbhīṣṭasya siddhaye \|*  
*tvayā viṣṇo prayatnena sarvacakravaraṃ tvidam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. For the attainment of your desires you shall hold this discus always. O Viṣṇu, this is the best of all discuses.

## \|\| 8.36.21 \|\|

*anye ca ye paṭhiṣyanti pāṭhayiṣyanti nityaśaḥ \|*  
*teṣāṃ duḥkhaṃ na svapne'pi jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. There is no doubt that those who recite and teach this hymn will have no misery at all even in dreams.

## \|\| 8.36.22 \|\|

*rājñā ca saṃkaṭe prāpte śatāvṛttiṃ caredyadā \|*  
*sāṅgaḥ ca vidhisaṃyuktaṃ kalyāṇaṃ labhate naraḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. When kings are in distress this worship with thousand names shall be repeated hundred times in accordance with the rules laid down and after due performance of the ancillary services people will have welfare.

## \|\| 8.36.23 \|\|

*roganāśakaraṃ hyetadvidyāvittadamuttamam \|*  
*sarvakāmapradaṃ puṇyaṃ śivabhaktipradaṃ sadā \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. This is destructive of distress. It yields learning and wealth. It is excellent and meritorious and bestows all desires and devotion to Śiva always.

## \|\| 8.36.24 \|\|

*yaduddiśya phalaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ paṭhiṣyanti narāstviha \|*  
*sapsyante nātra saṃdehaḥ phalaṃ tatsatyamuttamam \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. People undoubtedly derive excellent benefits which they will have in view in reciting this.

## \|\| 8.36.25 \|\|

*yaśca prātassamutthāya pūjāṃ kṛtvā madīyikām \|*  
*paṭhate matsamakṣaṃ vai nityaṃ siddhirna dūrataḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Achievement is not far off if a person rising in the morning, performs my worship and recites this in my presence everyday.

## \|\| 8.36.26 \|\|

*aihikīṃ siddhimāpnoti nikhilāṃ sarvakāmikām \|*  
*ante sāyujyamuktiṃ vai prāpnotyatra na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. There is no doubt in this that he will attain all the worldly pleasures and desires and in the end Sāyujya type of salvation.

## \|\| 8.36.27 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*evamuktvā tadā viṣṇuṃ śaṃkaraḥ prītamānasaḥ \|*  
*upaspṛśya karābhyāṃ tamuvāca giriśaḥ punaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

27\. Delighted in his mind, Śiva spoke thus to Viṣṇu and touched him with his hands. Śiva then told him again.

## \|\| 8.36.28 \|\|

*\|\| śiva uvāca \|*  
*varado'smi suraśreṣṭha varānvṛṇu yathepsitān \|*  
*bhaktyā vaśīkṛto nūnaṃ stavenānena suvrataḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

28\. O most excellent of gods, I am the granter of the boons. Choose the boons you desire to have. O one of good rites, I am attracted by your devotion and by this hymn as well.

## \|\| 8.36.29 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityukto devadevena devadevaṃ praṇamya tam \|*  
*suprasannataro viṣṇussāṃjalirvākyamabravīt \|\| 29 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

29\. Urged thus the delighted Viṣṇu bowed to the lord of gods and spoke with palms joined in reverence.

## \|\| 8.36.30 \|\|

*viṣṇuruvāca \|*  
*yathedānīṃ kṛpānātha kriyate cānyataḥ parā \|*  
*kāryyā caiva viśeṣeṇa kṛpālutvāttvayā prabho \|\| 30 \|\|*

Viṣṇu said:—

30\. O lord, you shall be equally merciful to others as well just as you are merciful to me now.

## \|\| 8.36.31 \|\|

*tvayi bhaktirmahādeva prasīda varamuttamam \|*  
*nānyamicchāmi bhaktānāmārttayo naiva yatprabho \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. O great lord, devotion to you is the excellent boon I wish to have. Be pleased. I do not wish for anything else, because, O lord, devotees are never distressed.

## \|\| 8.36.32 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*tacchrutvā vacanaṃ tasya dayā vānsutarāṃ bhavaḥ \|*  
*pasparśa ca tadaṃgaṃ vai prāha śītāṃśuśekharaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

32\. On hearing these words, the extremely sympathetic lord touched him. Then the moon-crested deity spoke.

## \|\| 8.36.33 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*mayi bhaktissadā te tu hare syādanapāyinī \|*  
*sadā vandyaśca pūjyaśca loke bhava surairapi \|\| 33 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

33\. O Viṣṇu, your devotion to me will always remain firm. Be worthy of the reverence and worship of all gods in the world.

## \|\| 8.36.34 \|\|

*viṣvaṃbhareti te nāma sarvapāpaharaṃ param \|*  
*bhaviṣyati na saṃdeho matprasādātsurottama \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. O most excellent god, there is no doubt in this that, thanks to my favour, your name—Viśvambhara (bearer of the universe) will be destructive of all sins.

## \|\| 8.36.35 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityuktvāṃtardadhe rudrassarvadeveśvaraḥ prabhuḥ \|*  
*paśyatastasya viṣṇostu tatraiva ca munīśvarāḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

35\. O great sages, after saying this, lord Śiva, the lord of the gods, vanished there itself even as Viṣṇu was watching.

## \|\| 8.36.36 \|\|

*janārdano'pi bhagavānvacanācchaṅkarasya ca \|*  
*prāpya cakraṃ śubhaṃ tadvai jaharṣāti svacetasi \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. After securing the auspicious discus and hearing the words of Śiva, lord Viṣṇu was extremely delighted in his mind.

## \|\| 8.36.37 \|\|

*kṛtvā dhyānaṃ ca tacchambhoḥ stotrametannirantaram \|*  
*papāṭhādhyāpayāmāsa bhaktebhyastadupādiśat \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Meditating on Śiva, he repeated the hymn incessantly and instructed the devotees too in this.

## \|\| 8.36.38 \|\|

*iti pṛṣṭaṃ mayākhyātaṃ śṛṇvatāmpāpahārakam \|*  
*ataḥparaṃ ca kiṃ śreṣṭhāḥ praṣṭumicchatha vai punaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. O excellent ones, thus I have narrated the story in accordance with your enquiry. It destroys the sins of those who listen to it. What then do you wish to ask?

## Chapter 37 - Devotion to lord Śiva

## \|\| 8.37.1 \|\|

*\|\| ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*sūtasūta mahābhāga jñānavānasi suvrata \|*  
*punareva śivasya vai caritaṃ brūhi vistarāt \|\| 1 \|\|*

The sages said:—

1\. O Sūta, O fortunate Sūta, you are endowed with knowledge, O one of good rites. Please narrate the story of Śiva again in detail.

## \|\| 8.37.2 \|\|

*purātanāśca rājāna ṛṣayo devatāstathā \|*  
*ārādhanañca tasyaiva cakurdevavarasya hi \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. The ancient kings, sages and gods propitiated that excellent deity Śiva alone.

## \|\| 8.37.3 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*sādhu pṛṣṭamṛṣiśreṣṭhāḥ śrūyatāṃ kathayāmi vaḥ \|\| \|*  
*caritraṃ śāṃkaraṃ ramyaṃ śṛṇvatāṃ bhuktimuktidam \|\| 3 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

3\. O great sages, you have put the question properly. I shall narrate the charming story of Śiva. It is conducive to the attainment of worldly pleasures and salvation by those who listen to it. May it be heard.

## \|\| 8.37.4 \|\|

*etadeva purā pṛṣṭo nāradena pitāmahaḥ \|*  
*pratyuvāca prasannātmā nāradaṃ munisattamam \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Brahmā was asked by Nārada this alone. Delighted in his mind, he replied to Nārada, the excellent sage.

## \|\| 8.37.5 \|\|

*brahmovāca \|*  
*śṛṇu nārada suprītyā śāṃkaraṃ caritaṃ varam \|*  
*pravakṣyāmi bhavatsnehānmahāpātakanāśanam \|\| 5 \|\|*

Brahmā said:—

5\. O Nārada, listen. I shall narrate the excellent story of Śiva with pleasure out of affection to you. It destroys great sins

## \|\| 8.37.6 \|\|

*ramayā sahito viṣṇuśśivapūjāṃ cakāra ha \|*  
*kṛpayā parameśasya sarvānkāmānavāpa hi \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Viṣṇu, accompanied by Lakṣmī, performed the worship of Śiva. Thanks to the mercy of lord Śiva, he attained all desires.

## \|\| 8.37.7 \|\|

*ahaṃ pitāmahaścāpi śivapūjanakārakaḥ \|*  
*tasyaiva kṛpayā tāta viśvasṛṣṭikarassadā \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. I, the grandfather of the worlds, am a worshipper of Śiva. Thanks to his mercy that I create the worlds always.

## \|\| 8.37.8 \|\|

*śivapūjākarā nityaṃ matputrāḥ paramarṣayaḥ \|*  
*anye ca ṛṣayo ye te śivapūjanakārakāḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. My sons, the great sages, always worship Śiva. Many other sages also do so.

## \|\| 8.37.9 \|\|

*nārada tvaṃ viśeṣeṇa śivapūjanakārakaḥ \|*  
*saptarṣayo vasiṣṭhādyāḥ śivapūjanakārakāḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. O Nārada, particularly you are a worshipper of Śiva. The seven sages, Vasiṣṭha and others too are the worshippers of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.37.10 \|\|

*aruṃdhatī madāsādhvī lopāmudrā tathaiva ca \|*  
*ahalyā gautamastrī ca śivapūjanakārikāḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. The great chaste lady Arundhatī, Lopāmudrā, and Ahalyā the wife of Gautama, do also worship Śiva.

## \|\| 8.37.11-12 \|\|

*durvāsāḥ kauśikaśśaktirdadhīco gautamastathā \|*  
*kaṇādo bhārgavo jīvo vaiśaṃpāyana eva ca \|\| 11 \|\|*

*ete ca munayassarve śivapūjākarā matāḥ \|*  
*tathā parāśaro vyāsaśśivapūjāratassadā \|\| 12 \|\|*

11-12. These sages, viz.—Durvāsas, Kauśika, Śakti, Dadhīci, Gautama, Kaṇāda, Jīva, Bhārgava, Vaiśampāyana, Parāśara and Vyāsa are the worshippers of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.37.13 \|\|

*upamanyurmahābhaktaśśivasya paramātmanaḥ \|*  
*yājñavalkyo mahāśaivo jaiminirgarga eva ca \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Upamanyu is a great devotee of Śiva, the great soul. Yājñavalkya, Jaimini and Garga are great followers of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.37.14 \|\|

*śukaśca śaunakādyāśca śaṅkarasya prapūjakāḥ \|*  
*anye'pi bahavassanti munayo munisattamāḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Śukra, Śaunaka and others are the worshippers of Śiva. There are many other sages, excellent sages, who worship Śiva.

## \|\| 8.37.15 \|\|

*aditirdevamātā ca nityaṃ prītyā cakāra ha \|*  
*pārthivīṃ śaivapūjāṃ vai savadhūḥ prematatparā \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Aditi, the mother of the gods, performed the worship of Śiva in earthen phallic idols every day. She is a lady engaged in Śiva’s devotion.

## \|\| 8.37.16 \|\|

*śakrādayo lokapālā vasavaśca surāstathā \|*  
*mahārājikadevāśca sādhyāśca śivapūjakāḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Indra and other guardians of the quarters,\[2\] Vasus, Mahārājika gods, Sādhyas are the worshippers of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.37.17 \|\|

*gandharvā kinnarādyāścopasurāśśivapūjakāḥ \|*  
*tathā'surā mahātmānaśśivapūjākarā matāḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. The subordinate gods, Gandharvas, Kinnaras and others are worshippers of Śiva. Similarly the Asuras of noble minds are the worshippers of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.37.18 \|\|

*hiraṇyakaśipurdetyassānujatsasuto mune \|*  
*śivapūjākaro nityaṃ virocanabalī tathā \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. O sage, the Daitya Hiraṇyakaśipu, his sons and younger brother, as well as Virocana and Bali worship Śiva every day.

## \|\| 8.37.19 \|\|

*mahāśaiva smṛto bāṇo hiraṇyākṣasutāstathā \|*  
*vṛṣaparvā danustāta dānavāḥ śivapūjakāḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Bāṇa is a great follower of Śiva. So also the sons of Hiraṇyākṣa. O dear one, Vṛṣaparvan and other Dānavas are the worshippers of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.37.20 \|\|

*śeṣaśca vāsukiścaiva takṣakaśca tathā pare \|*  
*śivabhaktā mahānāgā garuḍādyāśca pakṣiṇaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20 Śeṣa, Vāsuki, Takṣaka and many other great serpents are devotees of Śiva. Garuḍa and other birds too are so.

## \|\| 8.37.21 \|\|

*sūryacandrāvubhau devau pṛthvyāṃ vaṃśapravarttakau \|*  
*śivasevāratau nityaṃ savaṃśyau tau munīśvara \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. O great sage, the two gods, the Sun and the Moon who started two lines of kings on the earth are devoted to the service of Śiva always along with their descendants.

## \|\| 8.37.22 \|\|

*manavaśca tathā cakrussvāyaṃbhuvapurassarāḥ \|*  
*śivapūjāṃ viśeṣeṇa śivaveṣadharā mune \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. O sage, the self-born Brahmā and other Manus performed the worship of Śiva and they were in the guise of Śiva too.

## \|\| 8.37.23 \|\|

*priyavrataśca tatputrāstathā cottānapātsutaḥ \|*  
*tadvaṃśāścaiva rājānaśśivapūjanakārakāḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Priyavrata, his son Uttānapāda, and his son and successors, were kings worshipping Śiva.

## \|\| 8.37.24 \|\|

*dhruvaśca ṛṣabhaścaiva bharato nava yoginaḥ \|*  
*tadbhrātaraḥ pare cāpi śivapūjanakārakāḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Dhruva, Ṛṣabha, Bharata, his brothers the nine Yogins and others too were worshippers of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.37.25 \|\|

*vaivasvatasutāstārkṣya ikṣvākupramukhā nṛpāḥ \|*  
*śivapūjāratātmānaḥ sarvadā sukhabhoginaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Vaivasvata’s sons, Tārkṣya, Ikṣvāku and other kings, were always devoted to the worship of Śiva and enjoyed happiness.

## \|\| 8.37.26 \|\|

*kakutsthaścāpi māṃdhātā sagaraśśaivasattamaḥ \|*  
*mucukundo hariścandraḥ kalmāṣāṃghristathaiva ca \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Kakutstha, Māndhātā, Sagara, Mucukunda, Hariścandra and Kalmāṣāṅghri were all excellent devotees of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.37.27 \|\|

*bhagīrathādayo bhūpā bahavo nṛpasattamāḥ \|*  
*śivapūjākarā jñeyāḥ śivaveṣavidhāyinaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Bhagīratha and other kings, many excellent kings are known as worshippers of Śiva assuming the guise of Śiva,

## \|\| 8.37.28 \|\|

*khaṭvāṃgaśca mahārājo devasāhāyyakārakaḥ \|*  
*vidhitaḥ pārthivīmmūrtiṃ śivasyāpūjayatsadā \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. The great king Khaṭvāṅga, who helped the gods, worshipped the earthen idol of Śiva always.

## \|\| 8.37.29 \|\|

*tatputro hi dilīpaśca śivapūjanakṛtsadā \|*  
*raghustattanayaḥ śaivaḥ suprītyāḥ śivapūjakaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. His son Dilīpa was a perpetual worshipper of Śiva. Raghu, his son, was a great devotee of Śiva. He worshipped Śiva with pleasure.

## \|\| 8.37.30 \|\|

*ajaśśivārcakastasya tanayo dharmayuddhakṛt \|*  
*jāto daśaratho bhūyo mahārājo viśeṣataḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Aja, his son, who waged a virtuous war was a worshipper of Śiva. Daśaratha who was born of him became a great king.

## \|\| 8.37.31 \|\|

*putrārthe pārthivī mūrtti śaivī daśaratho hi saḥ \|*  
*samānarca viśeṣeṇa vasi ṣṭhasyājñayā muneḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. At the behest of the sage Vasiṣṭha, Daśaratha particularly worshipped the earthen idol of Śiva for obtaining sons.

## \|\| 8.37.32 \|\|

*putreṣṭiṃ ca cakārāsau pārthivo bhavabhaktimān \|*  
*ṛṣyaśṛṅgamunerājñāṃ saṃprāpya nṛpasattamaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. As directed by Ṛṣyaśṛṅga this excellent king, devoted to Śiva performed the sacrifice called Putreṣṭi.

## \|\| 8.37.33 \|\|

*kausalyā tatpriyā mūrtti pārthivīṃ śāṃkarīṃ mudā \|*  
*ṛṣyaśṛṃgasamādiṣṭā samānarca sutāptaye \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Urged by Ṛṣyaśṛṅga, Kausalyā, the beloved queen of Daśaratha joyously performed the worship of the earthen idol of Śiva for the acquisition of a son.

## \|\| 8.37.34 \|\|

*sumitrā ca śivaṃ prītyā kaikeyī nṛpavallabhā \|*  
*pūjayāmāsa satputraprāptaye munisattama \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. O excellent sage, Sumitrā, and Kaikeyi the beloved queens, performed the worship of Śiva with pleasure for the acquisition of good sons.

## \|\| 8.37.35 \|\|

*śivaprasādatastā vai putrānprāpuśśubhaṃkarān \|*  
*mahāpratāpino vīrānsanmārganiratānmune \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. O sage, they obtained auspicious sons of great valour and prowess who followed the path of the good. It was due to the favour of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.37.36 \|\|

*tataḥ śivājñayā tasmāttāsu rājñassvayaṃ hariḥ \|*  
*caturbhiścaiva rūpaiścāvirbabhūva nṛpātmajaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Then at the behest of Śiva, Viṣṇu himself was born of the ladies in four different forms.

## \|\| 8.37.37 \|\|

*kausalyāyāḥ suto rāma sumitrāyāśca lakṣmaṇa \|*  
*śatrughnaścaiva kaikeyyā bharataśceti suvratāḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Kausalyā’s son was Rāma, Sumitrā’s Lakṣmaṇa and Śatrughna and Kaikeyī’s Bharata. All of them performed Śiva’s rites.

## \|\| 8.37.38 \|\|

*rāmassasahajo nityaṃ pārthivaṃ samapūjayat \|*  
*bhasma rudrākṣadhārī ca virajāgamamāsthitaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Rāma performed the worship of the earthen idol every day in the company of his brothers. He wore Bhasma and Rudrākṣa and followed the Śiva-cult (virajāgama).

## \|\| 8.37.39 \|\|

*tadvaṃśe ye samutpannā rājānaḥ sānugā mune \|*  
*te sarve pārthivaṃ liṃgaṃ śivasya samapūjayan \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. O sage, the kings born of his family worshipped the earthen idol of Śiva along with their followers.

## \|\| 8.37.40 \|\|

*sudyumnaśca mahārājaśśaivo munisuto mune \|*  
*śivaśāpātpriyāhetorabhūnnārī sasevakaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. O sage, the great king Sudyumna, the son of a sage, became a woman along with his attendants as a result of Śiva’s curse for the sake of his beloved.

## \|\| 8.37.41 \|\|

*pārthiveśasamarcātaḥ punasso'bhūtpumānvaraḥ \|*  
*māsaṃ strī puruṣo māsamevaṃ strītvaṃ nyavarttata \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Thanks to the worship of the earthen image of Śiva, he became an excellent man again. By remaining a man for a month and a woman for a month, his womanhood disappeared.

## \|\| 8.37.42 \|\|

*tato rājyaṃ parityajya śivadharmaparāyaṇaḥ \|*  
*śivaveṣadharo bhaktyā durlabhaṃ mokṣamāptavān \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Then he forsook the kingdom and engaged himself in the Śiva-cult assuming the guise of Śiva. By virtue of devotion he attained salvation inaccessible to others.

## \|\| 8.37.43-44 \|\|

*purūravāśca tatputro mahārājastu pūjaka \|*  
*śivasya devadevasya tatsutaḥ śivapūjakaḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

*bharatastu mahāpūjāṃ śivasyaiva sadākarot \|*  
*nahuṣaśca mahā śaivaḥ śivapūjārato hyabhūt \|\| 44 \|\|*

43-44. His son Purūravas, a great king, was a worshipper of lord Śiva. His son Bharata performed the great worship of Śiva always. Nahuṣa, a great devotee was interested in the worship of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.37.45 \|\|

*yayātiḥ śivapūjātaḥ sarvānkāmānavāptavān \|*  
*ajījanatsutānpaṃca śivadharmaparāyaṇān \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Yayāti realised his desires due to Śiva’s worship. He begot five sons all interested in Śiva cult.

## \|\| 8.37.46 \|\|

*tatsutā yadumukhyāśca paṃcāpi śivapūjakāḥ \|*  
*śivapūjāprabhāveṇa sarvānkāmāṃśca lebhire \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Yadu and others, the five sons of Yayāti were the worshippers of Śiva. Thanks to the power of the worship of Śiva, they realised all their desires.

## \|\| 8.37.47 \|\|

*anye'pi ye mahābhāgāḥ samānarcuśśivaṃ hi te \|*  
*tadvaṃśyā anyavaṃśyāśca bhuktimuktipradaṃ mune \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Other fortunate persons of his race and of other races, O sage, worshipped Śiva and obtained worldly pleasures and salvation.

## \|\| 8.37.48 \|\|

*kṛṣṇena ca kṛtaṃ nityaṃ badarīparvatottame \|*  
*pūjanaṃ tu śivasyaiva saptamāsāvadhi svayam \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Kṛṣṇa himself performed the worship of Śiva on the excellent mountain Badari for seven months.

## \|\| 8.37.49 \|\|

*prasannādbhagavāṃstasmādvarāndivyānane kaśaḥ \|*  
*samprāpya ca jagatsarvaṃ vaśe'nayata śaṅkarāt \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. After securing several divine boons from Śiva who was delighted, lord Kṛṣṇa brought the entire universe under his control.

## \|\| 8.37.50 \|\|

*pradyumnaḥ tatsutastāta śivapūjākarassadā \|*  
*anye ca kārṣṇipravarāssāmbādyāśśivapūjakāḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. O dear, his son Pradyumna was a worshipper of Śiva always. Sāmba and other excellent descendants of Kṛṣṇa were also great worshippers of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.37.51 \|\|

*jarāsaṃdho mahāśaivastadvaṃśyāśca nṛpāstathā \|*  
*nimiśśaivaśca janakastatputrāśśivapūjakāḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Jarāsandha was a great devotee of Śiva. His successors were also so. Nimi and his son Janaka and his sons too were worshippers of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.37.52 \|\|

*nalena ca kṛtā pūjā vīrasenasutena vai \|*  
*pūrvajanmani yo bhillo vane pānthasurakṣakaḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. Worship of Śiva was performed by Nala the son of Vīrasena. In his previous birth he was a forester who protected the travellers there.

## \|\| 8.37.53 \|\|

*yatiśca rakṣitastena purā harasamīpataḥ \|*  
*svayaṃvyāghrādibhī rātrau bhakṣitaśca mṛto vṛṣāt \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. In the presence of Śiva a sage (who was Śiva himself) was saved by him. He was himself devoured by tigers and other beasts at night.

## \|\| 8.37.54 \|\|

*tena puṇyaprabhāveṇa sa bhillo hi nalo'bhavat \|*  
*cakravartī mahārājo damayantī priyo'bhavat \|\| 54 \|\|*
