54\. Thanks to that merit, the forester became Nala. He became a great emperor and the husband of Damayantī.

## \|\| 8.37.55 \|\|

*iti te kathitaṃ tāta yatpṛṣṭaṃ bhavatānagha \|*  
*śāṅkaraṃ caritaṃ divyaṃ kimanyatpraṣṭumicchasi \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. O dear, thus I have narrated the divine story of Śiva as asked by you. What else do you wish to hear?

## Chapter 38 - The greatness of Śivarātri

## \|\| 8.38.1 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*dhanyo'si kṛtakṛtyo'si jīvitaṃ saphalaṃ tava \|*  
*yacchrāvayasi nastāta maheśvarakathāṃ śubhām \|\| 1 \|\|*

The sages said:—

1\. O dear, you are blessed and contented. Your life is fruitful since you narrate to us the auspicious narrative of lord śiva.

## \|\| 8.38.2 \|\|

*bahubhiścarṣibhi ssūta śrutaṃ yadyapi vastu sat \|*  
*sandeho na mato'smākaṃ tadetatkathayāmi te \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. O Sūta, although this matter has been heard from many sages, our doubt is not set at rest. Hence we ask you.

## \|\| 8.38.3 \|\|

*kena vratena santuṣṭaḥ śivo yacchati satsukham \|*  
*kuśalaśśivakṛtye tvaṃ tasmātpṛcchāmahe vayam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. By what sacred rite is Śiva satisfied. By what sacred rite does he impart happiness to the good. You are expert in the knowledge of Śiva’s legends and so we ask you.

## \|\| 8.38.4 \|\|

*bhuktirmuktiśca labhyeta bhaktairyena vratena vai \|*  
*tadvada tvaṃ viśeṣeṇa vyāsaśiṣya namo'stu te \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. O disciple of Vyāsa, obeisance be to you. Please explain clearly the rite by which the devotee can secure both worldly pleasures and salvation.

## \|\| 8.38.5 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*samyakpṛṣṭamṛṣiśreṣṭhā bhavadbhiḥ karuṇātmabhiḥ \|*  
*smṛtvā śivapadāṃbhojaṃ kathayāmi yathāśrutam \|\| 5 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

5\. O great sages, the question is well put by you. There is sympathy brimming in your hearts. After remembering the lotus-like feet of Śiva I shall mention it in the manner I have heard.

## \|\| 8.38.6 \|\|

*yathā bhavadbhiḥ pṛccheta tathā pṛṣṭaṃ hi vedhasā \|*  
*hariṇā śivayā caiva tathā vai śaṃkaraṃ prati \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. The same question had been put by Brahmā, Viṣṇu and Sivā before Śiva in the manner in which you are asking me now.

## \|\| 8.38.7 \|\|

*kasmiṃścitsamaye taistu pṛṣṭaṃ ca paramātmane \|*  
*kena vratena santuṣṭo bhuktiṃ muktiṃ ca yacchasi \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. On a certain occasion they asked Śiva, the great soul. “What rite satisfies you to bestow worldly pleasures and salvation?”

## \|\| 8.38.8 \|\|

*iti pṛṣṭastadā taistu hariṇā tena vai tadā \|*  
*tadahaṃ kathayāmyadya śṛṇvatāṃ pāpahārakam \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. When he was asked thus, Siva replied thus. I shall mention it now. It quells the sins of those who listen to it.

## \|\| 8.38.9 \|\|

*\|\| śiva uvāca \|*  
*bhūri vratāni me santi bhuktimuktipradāni ca \|*  
*mukhyāni tatra jñeyāni daśasaṃkhyāni tāni vai \|\| 9 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

9\. There arc many rites yielding worldly pleasures and salvation. The most important among them are ten in number.

## \|\| 8.38.10 \|\|

*daśa śaivavratānyāhurjābālaśrutipāragāḥ \|*  
*tāni vratāni yatnena kāryāṇyeva dvijaissadā \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Those who have mastered the text “Jābālaśruti” have mentioned ten sacred rites of Śiva. These rites shall be performed by the brahmins always strenuously with great devotion.

## \|\| 8.38.11 \|\|

*pratyaṣṭamyāṃ prayatnena kartavyaṃ naktabhojanam \|*  
*kālāṣṭamyāṃ viśeṣeṇa hare tyājyaṃ hi bhojanam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. On Aṣṭamī days (the eighth day in the lunar fortnight) meals shall be taken only at night. O Viṣṇu, on Kālāṣṭamī day food shall be eschewed altogether.

## \|\| 8.38.12 \|\|

*ekādaśyāṃ sitāyāṃ tu tyājyaṃ viṣṇo hi bhojanam \|*  
*asitāyāṃ tu bhoktavyaṃ naktamabhyarcya māṃ hare \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. O Viṣṇu, on the eleventh day in the bright lunar fortnight, food during the day is forbidden. O Viṣṇu, on the eleventh day in the dark half food shall be taken at night after my worship.

## \|\| 8.38.13 \|\|

*trayodaśyāṃ sitāyāṃ tu kartavyaṃ niśi bhojanam \|*  
*asitāyāṃ tu bhūtāyāṃ tatra kāryaṃ śivavrataiḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. On the thirteenth day in the bright half, food shall be taken at night, In the dark half, it is forbidden to those who follow Śiva’s rites.

## \|\| 8.38.14 \|\|

*niśi yatnena kartavyaṃ bhojanaṃ somavāsare \|*  
*ubhayoḥ pakṣayorviṣṇo sarvasmiñchiva tatparaiḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. O Viṣṇu, in both the halves on every Monday, food shall be taken only during the night by the followers of Śiva-cult.

## \|\| 8.38.15 \|\|

*vrateṣveteṣu sarveṣu śaivā bhojyāḥ prayatnataḥ \|*  
*yathāśakti dvijaśreṣṭhā vratasaṃpūrtihetave \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. In all these rites, excellent brahmin devotees of Śiva shall be fed according to one’s ability for the completion of the sacred rites.

## \|\| 8.38.16 \|\|

*vratānyetāni niyamātkartavyāni dvijanmabhiḥ \|*  
*vratānyetāni tu tyaktvā jāyante taskarā dvijāḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The sacred rites shall always be performed regularly by the brahmins. By abandoning these Vratas, brahmins become robbers.

## \|\| 8.38.17-18 \|\|

*muktimārgapravīṇaiśca kartavyaṃ niyamāditi \|*  
*muktestu prāpakaṃ caiva catuṣṭayamudāhṛtam \|\| 17 \|\|*

*śivārcanaṃ rudrajapaṃ upavāsaśśivālaye \|*  
*vārāṇasyāṃ ca maraṇaṃ muktireṣā sanātanī \|\| 18 \|\|*

17-18. The rites shall be performed regularly by the persons who are adepts in the path of salvation. The four vratas that bring about salvation are the worship of Śiva, the repetition of Rudra Mantra by way of Japa, observance of fast in Śiva’s temple and death in Vārāṇasī. That salvation is eternal.

## \|\| 8.38.19 \|\|

*aṣṭamī somavāre ca kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśī \|*  
*śivatuṣṭikaraṃ caitannātra kāryā vicāraṇā \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Aṣṭamī that falls on Monday, and Caturdaśī in the dark half are sure to bring about the propitiation of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.38.20 \|\|

*caturṣvapi baliṣṭhaṃ hi śivarātrivrataṃ hare \|*  
*tasmāttadeva kartavyaṃ bhuktimuktiphalepsubhiḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. O Viṣṇu, among the four the most powerful is the rite of Śivarātri. Hence that alone shall be performed by those who desire the fruits of worldly pleasures and salvation.

## \|\| 8.38.21 \|\|

*etasmācca matādanyannāsti nṛṇāṃ hitāvaham \|*  
*etadvratantu sarveṣāṃ dharmasādhanamuttamam \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. There is no other rite more beneficial to men. This rite is the most excellent means of virtue for all.

## \|\| 8.38.22 \|\|

*niṣkāmānāṃ sakāmānāṃ sarveṣāṃ ca nṛṇāntathā \|*  
*varṇānāmāśramāṇāṃ ca strībālānāṃ tathā hare \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. To those without desire, to those with specific desires, to all men of all castes and stages of life, even to women and to children, this rite is very beneficial.

## \|\| 8.38.23 \|\|

*dāsānāṃ dāsikānāṃ ca devādīnāṃ tathaiva ca \|*  
*śarīriṇāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ hitametadvrataṃ varam \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. To men and women slaves, to gods and others and to all embodied souls this excellent rite is most beneficial.

## \|\| 8.38.24 \|\|

*māghasya hyasite pakṣe viśiṣṭā sātikīrtitā \|*  
*niśīthavyāpinī grāhyā hatyākoṭivināśinī \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. In the dark half of the month of Māgha, Śivarātri is specifically glorified. The rite of Śivarātri is performed when the Caturdaśī extends up-to mid-night. The observance of the rite on that Tithi quells the sin of even a crore of human slaughters.

## \|\| 8.38.25 \|\|

*taddine caiva yatkāryaṃ prātarārabhya keśava \|*  
*śrūyatāntanmano dattvā suprītyā kathayāmi te \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. O Viṣṇu, what shall be done on that day from morning onwards you hear with attention. I shall explain it to you with pleasure.

## \|\| 8.38.26 \|\|

*prātarutthāya medhāvī paramānandasaṃyutaḥ \|*  
*samācarennityakṛtyaṃ snānādikamatandritaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Rising in the morning, the intelligent man with great delight, shall perform his daily routine of bath with care.

## \|\| 8.38.27 \|\|

*śivālaye tato gatvā pūjayitvā yathāvidhi \|*  
*namaskṛtya śivaṃ paścātsaṃkalpaṃ samyagācaret \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. He shall then go to the temple of Śiva for worship. After making obeisance to Śiva, he shall observe the Ritualistic affirmation of his performance according to the prescribed course.

## \|\| 8.38.28 \|\|

*devadeva mahādeva nīlakaṇṭha namo'stu te \|*  
*kartumicchāmyahaṃ deva śivarātrivrataṃ tava \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. “O lord of gods, “O blue-necked, obeisance be to you. O lord, I wish to perform the Śivarātri rite sacred to you.

## \|\| 8.38.29 \|\|

*tava prabhāvāddeveśa nirvighnena bhavediti \|*  
*kāmādyāḥ śatravo māṃ vai pīḍāṃ kurvantu naiva hi \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. O lord of gods, thanks to your power, let it conclude peacefully. Let not passion and other enemies afflict me.”

## \|\| 8.38.30-32 \|\|

*evaṃ saṃkalpamāsthāya pūjādravyaṃ samāharet \|*  
*susthale caiva yalliṃgaṃ prasiddhaṃ cāgameṣu vai \|\| 30 \|\|*

*rātrau tatra svayaṃ gatvā saṃpādya vidhimuttamam \|*  
*śivasya dakṣiṇe bhāge paścime vā sthale śubhe \|\| 31 \|\|*

*nidhāya caiva taddravyaṃ pūjārthaṃ śivasannidhau \|*  
*punaḥ snāyāttadā tatra vidhipūrvaṃ narottamaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

30-32. After this affirmation the devotee shall collect the articles of worship. The phallic image shall be taken from a good place by the devotee himself at night by going there. The phallic image shall be that which is prescribed in the Āgamas. The materials of worship shall be placed on a good spot near Śiva either to the South or to the West. Then the devotee shall take bath again duly.

## \|\| 8.38.33 \|\|

*paridhāya śubhaṃ vastramantarvāsaśśubhantathā \|*  
*ācamya ca trivāraṃ hi pūjāraṃbhaṃ samācaret \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. The undercloth and the usual cloth shall be pure. The devotee shall perform the Ācamana thrice and begin worship.

## \|\| 8.38.34 \|\|

*yasya maṃtrasya yaddravyaṃ tena pūjāṃ samācaret \|*  
*amaṃtrakaṃ na kartavyaṃ pūjanaṃ tu harasya ca \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. The worship shall be performed in due concordance with the material and the mantras. Śiva’s worship shall always be performed in accompaniment with the mantras.

## \|\| 8.38.35 \|\|

*gītairvādyaistathā nṛtyairbhaktibhāvasamanvitaḥ \|*  
*pūjanaṃ prathame yāme kṛtvā maṃtraṃ japedbudhaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. The intelligent devotee shall repeat the mantras after performing worship in the first 3-hour period with music, songs, dances and devotional services.

## \|\| 8.38.36 \|\|

*pārthivaṃ ca tadā śreṣṭhaṃ vidadhyānmaṃtravānyadi \|*  
*kṛtanityakriyaḥ paścātpārthivaṃ ca samarcayet \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. If he knows the sacred verses, he shall make earthen images of excellent type. After performing the daily routine he shall worship the earthen image.

## \|\| 8.38.37 \|\|

*prathamaṃ pārthivaṃ kṛtvā paścātsthāpanamācaret \|*  
*stotrairnānāvidhairdevaṃ toṣayedvṛṣabhadhvajam \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. After making the earthen image, he shall install it thereafter. The devotee shall propitiate the bull-bannered lord, with several hymns.

## \|\| 8.38.38 \|\|

*māhātmyaṃ vratasaṃbhūtaṃ paṭhitavyaṃ sudhīmatā \|*  
*śrotavyaṃ bhaktavaryeṇa vratasampūrtikāmyayā \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. The glory of the Vrata shall be proclaimed by the intelligent devotee. It shall be heard by the devotee desiring the completion of the Vrata.

## \|\| 8.38.39-40 \|\|

*caturṣvapi ca yāmeṣu mūrtīnāṃ ca catuṣṭayam \|*  
*kṛtvāvāhanapūrvaṃ hi visargāvadhi vai kramāt \|\| 39 \|\|*

*kāryaṃ jāgaraṇaṃ prītyā mahotsava samanvitam \|*  
*prātaḥ snātvā punastatra sthāpayetpūjayecchivam \|\| 40 \|\|*

39-40. Thus four different idols shall be made for the four 3-hour periods. They shall be duly invoked and duly dismissed. All the intermediary rites shall also be performed. He shall keep awake with pleasure and jubilation. In the morning he shall take bath again. After installing the idol, he shall perform the worship.

## \|\| 8.38.41 \|\|

*tataḥ saṃprārthayecchaṃbhuṃ nataskandhaḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ \|*  
*kṛtasampūrṇa vratako natvā taṃ ca punaḥ punaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. After concluding the rites and bowing to Śiva again and again he shall pray to him with palms joined in devotion and shoulders drooping down.

## \|\| 8.38.42-43 \|\|

*niyamo yo mahādeva kṛtaścaiva tvadājñayā \|*  
*visṛjyate mayā svāminvrataṃ jātamanuttamam \|\| 42 \|\|*

*vratenānena deveśa yathāśaktikṛtena ca \|*  
*santuṣṭo bhava śarvādya kṛpāṃ kuru mamopari \|\| 43 \|\|*

42-43. “O great God, the rite I have started at your behest has been performed and concluded. It has become excellent. O lord, the idol is being ritualistically dismissed now. O lord of gods O Śiva, be satisfied with the Vrata that I have performed strenuously. Please be merciful to me.

## \|\| 8.38.44 \|\|

*puṣpāñjaliṃ śive dattvā dadyāddānaṃ yathāvidhi \|*  
*namaskṛtya śivāyaiva niyamaṃ taṃ visarjayet \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The devotee shall offer a handful of flowers to Śiva and make charitable gifts. After making obeisance to Śiva formally he shall conclude the rites.

## \|\| 8.38.45 \|\|

*yathāśakti dvijāñchaivānyatinaśca viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*bhojayitvā susantoṣya svayaṃ bhojanamācaret \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. After feeding brahmin devotees of Śiva and ascetics in particular, as far as possible and making them satiated, the devotee shall take food himself.

## \|\| 8.38.46 \|\|

*yāmeyāme yathā pūjā kāryā bhaktavarairhare \|*  
*śivarātrau viśeṣeṇa tāmahaṃ kathayāmi te \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. O Viṣṇu, I shall tell you how the worship is to be performed by the devotee, especially in every 3-hour period at the Śivarātri night

## \|\| 8.38.47 \|\|

*prathame caiva yāme ca sthāpitaṃ pārthivaṃ hare \|*  
*pūjayetparayā bhaktyā sūpacārairanekaśaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. O Viṣṇu, during the first 3-hour period the devotee shall worship with great devotion the earthen phallic image duly installed by way of good devotional services.

## \|\| 8.38.48 \|\|

*paṃcadravyaiśca prathamaṃ pūjanīyo harassadā \|*  
*tasya tasya ca mantreṇa pṛthagdravyaṃ samarpayet \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. Śiva shall always be worshipped with the five materials at the outset. The different articles of worship shall be separately offered with their respective mantras.

## \|\| 8.38.49 \|\|

*tacca dravyaṃ samarpyaiva jaladhārāṃ dadeta vai \|*  
*paścācca jaladhārābhirdravyāṇuttārayedbudhaḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. After offering the materials the steady water-pouring shall be made. The intelligent devotee shall consecrate the materials of worship with the steady pouring of water.

## \|\| 8.38.50 \|\|

*śatamaṣṭottaraṃ mantraṃ paṭhitvā jaladhārayā \|*  
*pūjayecca śivaṃ tatra nirguṇaṃ guṇarūpiṇam \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. He shall worship Śiva, both Nirguṇa and Saguṇa, by the steady pouring of water, reciting the 108 mantras.

## \|\| 8.38.51 \|\|

*gurudattena maṃtreṇa pūjayedvṛṣabhadhjam \|*  
*anyathā nāmamaṃtreṇa pūjayedvai sadāśivam \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. He shall worship the bull-bannered deity by reciting the mantra imparted by the preceptor. Or he shall worship Sadāśiva by means of Nāmamantras.

## \|\| 8.38.52 \|\|

*candanena vicitreṇa taṇḍulaiścāpyakhaṇḍitaiḥ \|*  
*kṛṣṇaiścaiva tilaiḥ pūjā kāryā śaṃbhoḥ parātmanaḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. The worship of Śiva the supreme soul shall be performed with sandal-paste, unbroken rice-grains and black gingelly seeds.

## \|\| 8.38.53 \|\|

*puṣpaiśca śatapatraiśca karavīraistathā punaḥ \|*  
*aṣṭabhirnāmamaṃtraiścārpayetpuṣpāṇi śaṃkare \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. Again Śiva shall be worshipped with lotus and and Karavīra flowers. The devotee shall offer the flowers with the eight Nāmamantras to Śiva.

## \|\| 8.38.54 \|\|

*bhavaḥ śarvastathā rudraḥ punaḥ paśupatistathā \|*  
*ugro mahāṃstathā bhīma īśāna iti tāni vai \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. They are Bhava, Śarva, Rudra, Paśupati, Ugra, Mahat, Bhīma and Īśāna.

## \|\| 8.38.55 \|\|

*śrīpūrvaiśca caturthyaṃtairnāmabhiḥ pūjayecchivam \|*  
*paścāddhūpaṃ ca dīpaṃ ca naivedyaṃ ca tataḥ param \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. When the names are used for worship they shall be prefixed with.“Om” and then the names shall be put in the Dative case. The incense and the lamps shall be used. The food-offering shall be made thereafter.

## \|\| 8.38.56 \|\|

*ādye yāme ca naivedyaṃ pakvānnaṃ kārayedbudhaḥ \|*  
*arghaṃ ca śrīphalaṃ dattvā tāmbūlaṃ ca nivedayet \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. In the first 3-hour period the intelligent devotee shall make cooked rice as food-offering. Half a cocoanut, and Tāmbūla shall be offered afterwards.

## \|\| 8.38.57 \|\|

*namaskāraṃ tato dhyānaṃ japaḥ prokto gurormanoḥ \|*  
*anyathā paṃcavarṇena toṣayettena śaṃkaram \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. Obeisance and meditation shall follow and the mantra taught by the preceptor shall be used for Japa. Or the devotee shall propitiate Śiva by means of the five-syllabled mantra.

## \|\| 8.38.58 \|\|

*dhenumudrāṃ pradarśyātha sujalaistarpaṇaṃ caret \|*  
*paṃcabrāhmaṇabhojaṃ ca kalpayedvai yathābalam \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. The devotee shall exhibit the mystic gesture of cow and offer Tarpaṇa with pure water. He shall then feed five Brahmins or more in accordance with his capacity.

## \|\| 8.38.59 \|\|

*mahotsavaśca kartavyo yāvadyāmo bhavediha \|*  
*tataḥ pūjāphalaṃ tasmai nivedya ca visarjayet \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. Then till the end of that 3-hour period festivities shall be observed. After dedicating the fruit of the worship to the deity himself, the ritualistic dismissal shall be performed.

## \|\| 8.38.60 \|\|

*punardvitīye yāme ca saṃkalpaṃ susamā caret \|*  
*athavaikadaiva saṃkalpya kuryātpūjāṃ tathāvidhām \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. Then in the second 3-hour period Saṃkalpa rites shall be performed duly as before. Or the Saṃkalpa rite shall be performed once for all the four 3-hour periods. Worship shall be performed as before,

## \|\| 8.38.61 \|\|

*dravyaiḥ pūrvaistathā pūjāṃ kṛtvā dhārāṃ samarpayet \|*  
*pūrvato dviguṇaṃ maṃtraṃ samuccāryārcayecchivam \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. After performing the worship with the materials as before, the Jaladhārā shall be offered. Then the devotee shall worship Śiva with gingelly seeds, barley grains and lotus-flowers.

## \|\| 8.38.62 \|\|

*pūrvaistilayavaiścātha kamalaiḥ pūjayecchivam \|*  
*bilvapatrairviśeṣeṇa pūjayetparameśvaram \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. The devotee shall worship lord Śiva particularly with the leaves of the Bilva tree.

## \|\| 8.38.63 \|\|

*arghyaṃ ca bījapūreṇa naivedyaṃ pāyasantathā \|*  
*maṃtrāvṛttistu dviguṇā pūrvato'pi janārdana \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. The Arghya shall be offered with citron fruit. The Naivedya shall consist of milk pudding. O Viṣṇu, the repetition of the mantra shall be twice as that of the previous one.

## \|\| 8.38.64 \|\|

*tataśca brāhmaṇānāṃ hi bhojyo saṃkalpamācaret \|*  
*anyatsarvaṃ tathā kuryādyāvacca dvitayāvadhi \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. Then the brahmins shall be fed sumptuously. Other rites shall be performd as before till the end of the second 3-hour period of time.

## \|\| 8.38.65 \|\|

*yāme prāpte tṛtīye ca pūrvavatpūjanaṃ caret \|*  
*yavasthāne ca godhūmāḥ puṣpāṇyarkabhavāni ca \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. In the third 3-hour period the worship shall be done as before. But instead of barley, wheat grains shall be used and the flowers of the sun-plant shall be offered.

## \|\| 8.38.66 \|\|

*dhūpaiśca vividhaistatra dīpairnānāvidhairapi \|*  
*naivedyāpūpakairviṣṇo śākairnānāvidhairapi \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. Incenses and lamps shall be of various types. O Viṣṇu, the Naivedya shall consist of fried pies and various vegetable dishes.

## \|\| 8.38.67 \|\|

*kṛtvaiva cātha karpūrairārārtika vidhiṃ careta \|*  
*arghyaṃ sadāḍimaṃ dadyāddviguṇaṃ japamācaret \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. The waving of light, as part of worship, shall be performed with camphor. The Arghya shall consist of the pomegranate. The Japa repetition shall be thrice that of the previous.

## \|\| 8.38.68 \|\|

*tataśca brahmabhojasya saṃkalpaṃ ca sadakṣiṇam \|*  
*utsavaṃ pūrvavatkuryā dyāvadyāmāvadhirbhavet \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. The feeding of brahmins shall follow along with the monetary gifts. Till the end of that period, festivities shall be celebrated as before.

## \|\| 8.38.69 \|\|

*yāme caturthe saṃprāte kuryāttasya visarjanam \|*  
*prayogādi punaḥ kṛtvā pūjāṃ vidhivadācaret \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. When the fourth 3-hour period arrives the ritualistic dismissal of the previous worship shall be performed. The worship shall be performed duly with due ritualistic gestures.

## \|\| 8.38.70 \|\|

*māṣaiḥ priyaṃgubhirmudgaissaptadhānyaistathāthavā \|*  
*śaṃkhīpuṣpairbilvapatraiḥ pūjayetparameśvaram \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. The devotee shall worship lord Śiva with black gram, green gram, Priyaṅgu grains or any of the seven cereals, the flowers of Śaṅkhī and the leaves of Bilva.

## \|\| 8.38.71 \|\|

*naivedyaṃ tatra dadyādvai madhurairvividhairapi \|*  
*athavā caiva māṣānnaistoṣayecca sadāśivam \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. The Naivedya shall be offered with different sweets or cooked black gram and rice in order to propitiate Sadā-Śiva.

## \|\| 8.38.72 \|\|

*arghaṃ dadyātkadalyāśca phalenaivāthavā hare \|*  
*vividhaiśca phalaiścaiva dadyādarghyaṃ śivāya ca \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. O Viṣṇu, Arghya shall be offered to Śiva with the plantain fruit or with different types of fruits.

## \|\| 8.38.73 \|\|

*pūrvato dviguṇaṃ kuryānmaṃtrajāpaṃ narottamaḥ \|*  
*saṃkalpaṃ brahmabhojasya yathāśakti caredbudhaḥ \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. The exellent man shall perform the mantra japa twice in number of that on the previous occasion. The intelligent devotee shall decide on the number of brahmins to be fed.

## \|\| 8.38.74 \|\|

*gītairvādyaistathā nṛtyairnayetkālaṃ ca bhaktitaḥ \|*  
*mahotsavairbhaktajanairyāvatsyādaruṇodayaḥ \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. Till day-break the devotee shall pass time in songs of prayer, musical instruments, devotional dances and other festivities along with his fellow devotees.

## \|\| 8.38.75 \|\|

*udaye ca tathā jāte punassnātvārcayecchivam \|*  
*nānāpūjopahāraiśca svābhiṣekamathācaret \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. When the sun rises he shall take his bath and then worship Śiva. The ablution of the deity shall be performed with devout services and adorations.

## \|\| 8.38.76 \|\|

*nānāvidhāni dānāni bhojyaṃ ca vividhantathā \|*  
*brāhmaṇānāṃ yatīnāṃ ca kartavyaṃ yāmasaṃkhyayā \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. Charitable gifts shall be offered, brahmins and ascetics shall be fed with different dishes, their number being in accordance with what was decided before.

## \|\| 8.38.77 \|\|

*śaṃkarāya namaskṛtya puṣpāñjalimathācaret \|*  
*prārthayetsustutiṃ kṛtvā mantrairetairvicakṣaṇaḥ \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. After making obeisance to Śiva, handful of flowers shall be offered. The skilful devotee shall then pray after eulogising the lord with the mantras:—

## \|\| 8.38.78 \|\|

*tāvakastvadgataprāṇastvaccitto'haṃ sadā mṛḍa \|*  
*kṛpānidhe iti jñātvā yathā yogyaṃ tathā kuru \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. “O Mṛḍa, the storehouse of mercy, knowing that I belong to you, my very life is embedded in you and my mind is dedicated to you, please do what is proper.

## \|\| 8.38.79 \|\|

*ajñānādyadi vā jñānājjapapūjādikaṃ mayā \|*  
*kṛpānidhitvājjñātvaiva bhūtanātha prasīda me \|\| 79 \|\|*

79\. O lord of living beings, be favourable to me viewing sympathetically at the Japa and worship conducted by me with the requisite knowledge or in ignorance.

## \|\| 8.38.80 \|\|

*anenaivopavāsena yajjātaṃ phalameva ca \|*  
*tenaiva prīyatāṃ devaḥ śaṃkaraḥ sukhadāyakaḥ \|\| 80 \|\|*

80\. May lord Śiva the bestower of happiness be pleased with the fruit arising out of this fast and other rites.

## \|\| 8.38.81 \|\|

*kule mama mahādeva bhajanaṃ te'stu sarvadā \|*  
*mābhūttasya kule janma yatra tvaṃ nahi devatā \|\| 81 \|\|*

81\. O lord Śiva, may your worship flourish in my family always. Let me not be born in the family where you are not a deity.”

## \|\| 8.38.82 \|\|

*puṣpāṃjaliṃ samarpyaivaṃ tilakāśiṣa eva ca \|*  
*gṛhṇīyādbrāhmaṇebhyaśca tataśśaṃbhuṃ visarjayet \|\| 82 \|\|*

82\. After offering Puṣpāñjali and accepting the Tilakas and blessings from the brahmins, the devotee shall ritualistically dismiss the deity.

## \|\| 8.38.83 \|\|

*evaṃ vrataṃ kṛtaṃ yena tasmāddūro haro na hi \|*  
*na śakyate phalaṃ vaktuṃ nādeyaṃ vidyate mama \|\| 83 \|\|*

83\. If the rite is performed thus, Śiva is not inaccessible to him. The fruit that he derives is inexplicable. There is nothing which I shall not give in return.

## \|\| 8.38.84 \|\|

*anāyāsatayā cedvai kṛtaṃ vratamidamparam \|*  
*tasya vai muktibījaṃ ca jātaṃ nātra vicāraṇā \|\| 84 \|\|*

84\. If this excellent rite is performed without attachment, salvation will come to him in the form of seed. There is no scope for any doubt in this matter.

## \|\| 8.38.85 \|\|

*pratimāsaṃ vrataṃ caiva kartavyaṃ bhaktito naraiḥ \|*  
*udyāpanavidhiṃ paścātkṛtvā sāṃgaphalaṃ labhet \|\| 85 \|\|*

85\. This Vrata shall be performed with great devotion every month. After performing the concluding rite, the devotee shall reap the fruit with subsidiary benefits.

## \|\| 8.38.86 \|\|

*vratasya karaṇānnūnaṃ śivo'haṃ sarvaduḥkhahā \|*  
*dadmi bhuktiṃ ca muktiṃ ca sarvaṃ vai vāñchitaṃ phalam \|\| 86 \|\|*

86\. Certainly for the performance of this Vrata, I, Śiva, the destroyer of all miseries grant all desired benefits, worldly pleasures and salvation.

## \|\| 8.38.87 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*iti śivavacanaṃ niśamya viṣṇurhitataramadbhutamājagāma dhāma \|*  
*tadanu vratamuttamaṃ janeṣu samacaradātmahiteṣu caitadeva \|\| 87 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

87\. After hearing these beneficial and wonderful words of Śiva, Viṣṇu returned to his abode. Thereafter this excellent rite became popular among the people desiring welfare of their soul

## \|\| 8.38.88 \|\|

*kadācinnāradāyātha śivarātrivratantvidam \|*  
*bhuktimuktipradaṃ divyaṃ kathayāmāsa keśavaḥ \|\| 88 \|\|*

88\. Once Viṣṇu narrated to Nārada this divine Śivarātrivrata which yields worldly pleasures and salvation.

## Chapter 39 - The conclusion of the rite of Śivarātri

## \|\| 8.39.1 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ucuḥ \|*  
*udyāpanavidhiṃ brūhi śivarātrivratasya ca \|*  
*yatkṛtvā śaṃkarassākṣātprasanno bhavati dhuvam \|\| 1 \|\|*

The sages said:—

1\. Please narrate the Udyāpana (formal concluding) of the rite of Śivarātri as a result of which Śiva himself becomes delighted.

## \|\| 8.39.2 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*śrūyatāmṛṣayo bhaktyā tadudyāpanamādarāt \|*  
*yasyānuṣṭhānataḥ pūrṇaṃ vrataṃ bhavati taddhruvam \|\| 2 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

2\. O sages, may the concluding rite be heard with devotion and respect. By performing it, the Vrata certainly becomes complete.

## \|\| 8.39.3 \|\|

*caturdaśābdaṃ kartavyaṃ śivarātrivrataṃ śubham \|*  
*ekabhaktaṃ trayodaśyāṃ caturdaśyāmupoṣaṇam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. The auspicious Śivarātrivrata shall be performed for fourteen years. On the Trayodaśī day only one meal shall be taken and a complete fast shall be observed on the Caturdaśī day.

## \|\| 8.39.4-5 \|\|

*śivarātridine prāpte nityaṃ saṃpādya vai vidhim \|*  
*śivālayaṃ tato gatvā pūjāṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi \|\| 4 \|\|*

*tataśca kārayeddivyaṃ maṇḍalaṃ tatra yatnataḥ \|*  
*gaurītilakanāmnā vai prasiddhaṃ bhuvanatraye \|\| 5 \|\|*

4-5. On the Śivarātri day after performing the routine, the devotee shall go to the temple of Śiva and perform worship. He shall make the mystical diagram famous in the three worlds by the name of Gaurītilaka.

## \|\| 8.39.6 \|\|

*tanmadhye lekhayeddivyaṃ liṃgato bhadramaṇḍalam \|*  
*athavā sarvatobhadraṃ maṇḍapāntaḥ prakalpayet \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. In the middle of the mystical diagram Liṅgatobhadra and Sarvatobhadra shall be delineated.

## \|\| 8.39.7 \|\|

*kuṃbhāstatra prakartavyāḥ prājāpatyavisaṃjñayā \|*  
*savastrāssaphalāstatra dakṣiṇāsahitāḥ śubhāḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. As in the Prājāpatya rite, auspicious water-pots shall be placed wrapped in cloth, with a cocoanut above and the Dakṣiṇā in.

## \|\| 8.39.8 \|\|

*maṇḍalasya ca pārśve vai sthāpanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ \|*  
*madhye caikaśca saṃsthāpyaḥ sauvarṇo vāparo ghaṭaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. The pots shall be carefully placed at the sides of the altar. In middle either a discus or a gold-pot shall be placed.

## \|\| 8.39.9-10 \|\|

*tatromāsahitāṃ śaṃbhumūrtinnirmāya hāṭakīm \|*  
*palena vā tadarddhena yathāśaktyāthavā vratī \|\| 9 \|\|*

*nidhāya vāmabhāge tu śivāmūrttimatandritaḥ \|*  
*madīyāṃ dakṣiṇe bhāge kṛtvā rātrau prapūjayet \|\| 10 \|\|*

9-10. The devotee shall make an idol of Śiva accompanied by Umā, of gold a pala in weight. According to his capacity the weight may be reduced to half of a pala. Śivā shall be on the left side and Śiva on the right. The devotee shall worship the idol at night.

## \|\| 8.39.11 \|\|

*ācāryaṃ varayettatra cartvigbhissahitaṃ śucim \|*  
*anujñātaśca tairbhaktyā śivapūjāṃ samācaret \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. He shall woo a pious preceptor accompanied by Ṛtviks. With their formal permission he shall begin the worship of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.39.12 \|\|

*rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kuryātpūjāṃ yāmodbhavāṃ caran \|*  
*rātrimākramayetsarvāṃ gītanṛtyādinā vratī \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. He shall keep awake during the night performing worship in the different 3-hour periods and singing songs of prayer or devoutly dancing.

## \|\| 8.39.13 \|\|

*evaṃ sampūjya vidhivatsaṃtoṣya prātareva ca \|*  
*punaḥ pūjāṃ tataḥ kṛtvā homaṃ kuryādyathāvidhi \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. After performing the worship thus in accordance with the injunctions he shall propitiate the lord. He shall worship again in the morning and perform Homa duly.

## \|\| 8.39.14 \|\|

*yathāśakti vidhānaṃ ca prājāpatyaṃ samācaret \|*  
*brāhmaṇānbhojayetprītyā dadyāddānāni bhaktitaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. He shall perform the Prājāpatya rites according to his ability. With pleasure he shall feed brahmins and with devotion he shall make charitable gifts.

## \|\| 8.39.15 \|\|

*ṛtvijaśca sapatnīkānvastrālaṅkārabhūṣaṇaiḥ \|*  
*alaṅkṛtya vidhānena dadyāddānaṃ pṛthakpṛthak \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. He shall give the Ṛtviks and their wives good clothes and ornaments for bedecking themselves and make gifts to them separately.

## \|\| 8.39.16 \|\|

*gāṃ savatsāṃ vidhānena yathopaskarasaṃyutām \|*  
*uktvācāryāya vai dadyācchivo me prīyatāmiti \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Saying “May Śiva be pleased”, he shall make the gift of a cow and a calf together with the essential requisites to the preceptor.

## \|\| 8.39.17 \|\|

*tattassakumbhāṃ tanmūrtiṃ savastrāṃ vṛṣabhe sthitām \|*  
*sarvālaṃkārasahitāmācāryāya nivedayet \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. He shall offer to him the waterpot and the Śiva idol with all ornaments, placing them on a bullock.

## \|\| 8.39.18 \|\|

*tataḥ saṃprārthayeddevaṃ maheśānaṃ mahāprabhum \|*  
*kṛtāṃjalirnataskandhassuprītyā gadgadākṣaraḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. He shall pray to lord Śiva the great lord with great pleasure, with palms joined in reverence, with shoulders drooping and words choked with emotion.

## \|\| 8.39.19 \|\|

*devadeva mahādeva śaraṇāgatavatsala \|*  
*vratenānena deveśa kṛpāṃ kuru mamopari \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. “O great lord, lord of the gods, favourably disposed to the devotees, be merciful to me, thanks to this Vrata.

## \|\| 8.39.20 \|\|

*mayā bhaktyanusāreṇa vratametatkṛtaṃ śivā \|*  
*nyūnaṃ sampūrṇatāṃ yātu prasādāttava śaṅkara \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. O Śiva, this Vrata is performed by me according to my ability. O lord, if it is deficient in any respect let it become perfect by your favour.

## \|\| 8.39.21 \|\|

*ajñānādyadi vā jñānājjapapūjādikaṃ mayā \|*  
*kṛtaṃ tadastu kṛpayā saphalaṃ tava śaṅkara \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Japa and worship and other things have been performed partly in ignorance and partly with knowledge by me. May these be fruitful, O Śiva, by your mercy.”

## \|\| 8.39.22 \|\|

*evaṃ puṣpāṃjaliṃ dattvā śivāya paramātmane \|*  
*namaskāraṃ tataḥ kuryātprārthanāṃ punareva ca \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. After saying this the flower-offering shall be made to Śiva the great soul. He shall make obeisance and pray again.

## \|\| 8.39.23 \|\|

*evaṃ vrataṃ kṛtaṃ yena nyūnaṃ tasya na vidyate \|*  
*manobhīṣṭāṃ tataḥ siddhiṃ labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. If the Vrata is performed thus it will be complete and no deficiency will remain. The devotee will attain Siddhi desired by him. There is no doubt in it.

## Chapter 40 - The glory of Śivarātri

## \|\| 8.40.1 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*sūta te vacanaṃ śrutvā parānandaṃ vayaṃ gatāḥ \|*  
*vistarātkathaya prītyā tadeva vratamuttamam \|\| 1 \|\|*

The sages said:—

1\. O Sūta, we are extremely delighted on hearing your words. Please narrate the excellent Vrata in detail.

## \|\| 8.40.2 \|\|

*kṛtaṃ purā tu keneha sūtaitadvratamuttamam \|*  
*kṛtvāpyajñānataścaiva prāptaṃ kiṃ phalamuttamam \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. O Sūta, by whom was this excellent Vrata performed formerly? Was an excellent benefit derived by any by performing it without full knowledge?

## \|\| 8.40.3 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*śrūyatāmṛṣayassarve kathayāmi purātanam \|*  
*itihāsaṃ niṣādasya sarvapāpapraṇāśanam \|\| 3 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

3\. May this be heard, O sages. I shall narrate the ancient story of the hunter which destroys all sins.

## \|\| 8.40.4 \|\|

*purā kaścidvane bhillo nāmnā hyāsīdgurudruhaḥ \|*  
*kuṭumbī balavānkrūraḥ krūrakarmaparāyaṇaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Formerly there was a hunter in a forest, Gurudruha by name. He had a large family. He was strong, ruthless and engaged in cruel activities.

## \|\| 8.40.5 \|\|

*nirantaraṃ vane gatvā mṛgānhanti sma nityaśaḥ \|*  
*cauryyaṃ ca vividhaṃ tatra karoti sma vane vasan \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Everyday he used to go to the forest incessantly and kill deer. There in the forest he committed thefts also in various ways.

## \|\| 8.40.6 \|\|

*bālyādārabhya teneha kṛtaṃ kiṃcicchubhaṃ nahi \|*  
*mahānkālo vyatīyāya vane tasya durātmanaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. No auspicious action was performed by him from childhood onwards. Thus the wicked fellow passed a long time in the forest.

## \|\| 8.40.7 \|\|

*kadācicchivarātriśca prāptāsīttatra śobhanā \|*  
*na durātmā sma jānāti mahadvananivāsakṛt \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Once it was a Śivarātri day. But the wicked hunter, staying in the great forest did not know that.

## \|\| 8.40.8 \|\|

*etasminsamaye bhillo mātrā pitrā striyā tathā \|*  
*prārthitaśca kṣudhā'viṣṭairbhakṣyaṃ dehi vanecara \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. On this occasion he was requested by his parents and wife who were oppressed by starvation, “O forest-roamer give us food.”

## \|\| 8.40.9 \|\|

*iti saṃprārthitaḥ so'pi dhanurādāya satvaram \|*  
*jagāma mṛgahiṃsārthaṃ babhrāma sakalaṃ vanam \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Thus requested he took up his bow and started immediately for hunting deer. He roamed here and there in the forest.

## \|\| 8.40.10 \|\|

*daivayogāttadā tena na prāptaṃ kiṃcideva hi \|*  
*astaprāptastadā sūryassa vai duḥkhamupāgataḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Due to adverse fate no game was secured by him. The sun too had set and he was greatly distressed.

## \|\| 8.40.11-12 \|\|

*kiṃ kartavyaṃ kva gaṃtavyaṃ na prāptaṃ me'dya kiṃcana \|*  
*bālāśca ye gṛhe teṣāṃ kiṃ pitrośca bhaviṣyati \|\| 11 \|\|*

*madīyaṃ vai kalatraṃ ca tasyāḥ kiṃcidbhaviṣyati \|*  
*kiṃcidgṛhītvā hi mayā gaṃtavyaṃ nānyathā bhavet \|\| 12 \|\|*

11-12. “What shall I do? Where shall I go? Nothing has been obtained by me. What will happen to my parents and my children at home? Then there is my wife too. What will happen to her? I must go home only with something in my hand. It is impossible to face them empty-handed.”

## \|\| 8.40.13 \|\|

*itthaṃ vicārya sa vyādho jalāśaya samīpagaḥ \|*  
*jalāvataraṇaṃ yatra tatra gatvā svayaṃ sthitaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Thinking thus, the hunter went near a pond. He stood near the path leading to water.

## \|\| 8.40.14 \|\|

*avaśyamatra kaścidvai jīvaścaivāgamiṣyati \|*  
*taṃ hatvā svagṛhaṃ prītyā yāsyāmi kṛtakāryakaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. “Some animal is sure to come here. I shall kill it and go home with pleasure. My purpose will be served fully.

## \|\| 8.40.15 \|\|

*iti matvā sa vai vṛkṣamekaṃ bilvetisaṃjñakam \|*  
*samāruhya sthitastatra jalamādāya bhillakaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Thinking thus the hunter climbed up a Bilva tree, taking some water with him. He sat on its branch.

## \|\| 8.40.16 \|\|

*kadā yāsyati kaścidvai kadā hanyāmahaṃ punaḥ \|*  
*iti buddhiṃ samāsthāya sthito'sau kṣuttṛṣānvitaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. With hunger and thirst oppressing him he waited and thought. “When will some animal come here? When shall I kill it?”

## \|\| 8.40.17 \|\|

*tadrātrau prathame yāme mṛgī tvekā samāgatā \|*  
*tṛṣārtā cakitā sā ca protphālaṃ kurvatī tadā \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. During the first part of the night a thirsty hind came there springing and jumping timidly.

## \|\| 8.40.18 \|\|

*tāṃ dṛṣṭvā ca tadā tena tadvadhārthamatho śaraḥ \|*  
*saṃhṛṣṭena drutaṃ viṣṇo dhanuṣi sve hi saṃdadhe \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. On seeing it he was much delighted. He fixed an arrow to his bow immediately in order to kill it.

## \|\| 8.40.19-20 \|\|

*ityevaṃ kurvatastasya jalaṃ bilvadalāni ca \|*  
*patitāni hyadhastatra śivaliṃgamabhūttataḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

*yāmasya prathamasyaiva pūjā jātā śivasya ca \|*  
*tanmahimnā hi tasyaiva pātakaṃ galitantadā \|\| 20 \|\|*

19-20. When he did this, some water (that he had with him) got spilt and a few leaves of the Bilva tree fell down. There was a phallic idol of Śiva beneath the tree. Hence this became his worship for the first part of the night. As a result of this hīs sin was dissolved.

## \|\| 8.40.21 \|\|

*tatratyaṃ caiva tacchabdaṃ śrutvā sā hariṇī bhiyā \|*  
*vyādhaṃ dṛṣṭvā vyākulā hi vacanaṃ cedamabravīt \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. On hearing the noise, the hind became frightened. It was much distressed on seeing the hunter. It spoke thus.

## \|\| 8.40.22 \|\|

*\|\|mṛgyuvāca \|*  
*kiṃ kartumicchasi vyādha satyaṃ vada mamāgrataḥ \|*  
*tacchutvā hariṇīvākyaṃ vyādho vacanamabravīt \|\| 22 \|\|*

The hind said:—

22\. “O hunter, what is it that you propose to do? Please speak truth before me.” On hearing the words of the hind, the hunter said.

## \|\| 8.40.23-24 \|\|

*\|\|vyādha uvāca \|*  
*kuṭumbaṃ kṣudhitaṃ me'dya hatvā tvāṃ tarpayāmyaham \|*  
*dāruṇaṃ tadvacaśśrutvā dṛṣṭvā taṃ durddharaṃ khalam \|\| 23 \|\|*

*kiṃ karomi kva gacchāmi hyupāyaṃ racayāmyaham \|*  
*itthaṃ vicāryaṃ sā tatra vacanaṃ cedamabravīt \|\| 24 \|\|*

The hunter said:—

23-24. “My family is starving today. I shall satisfy their hunger by killing you.” On hearing these terrible words and seeing that ruthless knave, the hind thought “What shall I do? Where shall I go? Well, I shall use a trick,” thinking thus the hind spoke.

## \|\| 8.40.25 \|\|

*\|\| mṛgyuvāca \|*  
*manmāṃsena sukhaṃ te syāddehasyānarthakāriṇaḥ \|*  
*adhikaṃ kiṃ mahatpuṇyaṃ dhanyāhaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

The Hind said:—

25\. There is no doubt that I am blessed. You will be happy with my flesh. What greater merit can I have through this body which is in every respect harmful?

## \|\| 8.40.26 \|\|

*upakārakarasyaiva yatpuṇyaṃ jāyate tviha \|*  
*tatpuṇyaṃ śakyate naiva vaktuṃ varṣaśatairapi \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. It is imposible to expatiate on the merit of a person who helps others even if we take a hundred years.

## \|\| 8.40.27 \|\|

*paraṃ tu śiśavo me'dya vartaṃte svāśrame'khilāḥ \|*  
*bhaginyai tānsamarpyaiva prāyāsye svāmine'tha vā \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. But all my infant fawns are in the hermitage. I shall intrust them to the care of my sister or husband and return.

## \|\| 8.40.28 \|\|

*na me mithyāvacastvaṃ hi vijānīhi vanecara \|*  
*āyāsyeha punaśceha samīpaṃ te na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. O forest-roamer, do not take my words to be lies. Undoubtedly I will come again to you.

## \|\| 8.40.29 \|\|

*sthitā satyena dharaṇī satyenaiva ca vāridhiḥ \|*  
*satyena jaladhārāśca satye sarvampratiṣṭhitam \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. The earth stays steady through truth. The ocean is steady through truth. The water flows steadily through truth. Everything is founded on truth.

## \|\| 8.40.30 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*ityukto'pi tayā vyādho na mene tadvaco yadā \|*  
*tadā suvismitā bhītā vacanaṃ sābravītpunaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

30\. When the hunter did not accept its proposal even after being implored thus, the bewildered and frightened hind spoke again:—

## \|\| 8.40.31-34 \|\|

*mṛgyuvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu vyādha pravakṣyāmi śapathaṃ hi karomyaham \|*  
*āgaccheyaṃ yathā te na samīpaṃ svagṛhādgatā \|\| 31 \|\|*

*brāhmaṇo vedavikretā sandhyāhīnastrikālakam \|*  
*striyassvasvāmino hyājñāṃ samullaṃghya kriyānvitāḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

*kṛtaghne caiva yatpāpaṃ tatpāpaṃ vimukhe hare \|*  
*drohiṇaścaiva yatpāpaṃ tatpāpaṃ dharmalaṃghane \|\| 33 \|\|*

*viśvāsaghātake tacca tathā vai chalakartari \|*  
*tena pāpena limpāmi yadyahaṃ nāgame punaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

The Hind said:—

31-34. O hunter, listen. I shall explain. I take this vow. After going home if I do not return to you let me be smeared with the sin that these people have viz—A brahmin who sells the Vedas, one who does not offer Sandhyā prayers, women who transgress the commands of their husbands and do various rites, an ungrateful person, a person who is averse to Śiva. a person who ill treats others, who violates Virtue, a person committing broach of trust, and a person who deceives others.

## \|\| 8.40.35 \|\|

*ityādyanekaśapathānmṛgī kṛtvā sthitā yadā \|*  
*tadā vyādhasya viśvasya gaccheti gṛhamabravīt \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. When the hind made these promises the hunter believed it and said “Go home”.

## \|\| 8.40.36 \|\|

*mṛgī hṛṣṭājalaṃ pītvā gatā svāśramamaṇḍalam \|*  
*tāvacca prathamo yāmastasya nidrāṃ vinā gataḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. The delighted hind drank water and went to its hermitage. By that time the first part of the night came to a close and the hunter did not have a wink of sleep.

## \|\| 8.40.37 \|\|

*tadīyā bhaginī yā vai mṛgī ca paribhāvitā \|*  
*tasyā mārgaṃ vicinvantī hyājagāma jalārthinī \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. The sister of the hind, not able to see it and so distressed came there in search of that hind. It too was thirsty.

## \|\| 8.40.38 \|\|

*tāṃ dṛṣṭvā ca svayaṃ bhillo'kārṣīdbāṇasya karṣaṇam \|*  
*pūrvavajjalapatrāṇi patitāni śivopari \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. On seeing the hind, the Bhilla drew the bow and was about to shoot the arrow. As before, water and Bilva leaves fell on Śiva.

## \|\| 8.40.39 \|\|

*yāmasya ca dvitīyasya tena śaṃbhormahātmanaḥ \|*  
*pūjā jātā prasaṃgena vyādhasya sukhadāyinī \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Incidentally it became the worship of the second part of the night. It was pleasing to the hunter.

## \|\| 8.40.40 \|\|

*mṛgī sā prāha taṃ dṛṣṭvā kiṃ karoṣi vanecara \|*  
*pūrvavatkathitaṃ tena tacchrutvāha mṛgī punaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. The hind asked “O forest-roamer what are you doing?” After seeing him, the hunter replied as before. On hearing it, the hind said again.

## \|\| 8.40.41-42 \|\|

*mṛgyuvāca \|*  
*dhanyāhaṃ śrūyatāṃ vyādha saphalaṃ dehadhāraṇam \|*  
*anityena śarīreṇa hyupakāro bhaviṣyati \|\| 41 \|\|*

*parantu mama bālāśca gṛhe tiṣṭhanti cārbhakāḥ \|*  
*bhartre tāṃśca samarpyaiva hyāgamiṣyāmyahaṃ punaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

41-42. “O hunter, listen. I am blessed. My life is fruitful. A service can be rendered through this evanescent body. But my fawns, young ones, are in the house. I shall entrust them to the care of my husband and come again.”

## \|\| 8.40.43 \|\|

*vyādha uvāca \|*  
*tvayā coktaṃ na manyehaṃ hanmi tvāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*tacchutvā hariṇī prāha śapathaṃ kurvatī hare \|\| 43 \|\|*

The hunter said:—

43\. “I do not agree to what you say. Undoubtedly I am going to kill you.” On hearing that the hind said on the oath of Śiva.

## \|\| 8.40.44-47 \|\|

*mṛgyuvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu vyādha pravakṣyāmi nāgaccheyaṃ punaryadi \|*  
*vācā vicalito yastu sukṛtaṃ tena hāritam \|\| 44 \|\|*

*pariṇītā striyaṃ hitvā gacchatyanyāṃ ca yaḥ pumān \|*  
*vedadharmaṃ samullaṃghya kalpitena ca yo vrajet \|\| 45 \|\|*

*viṣṇubhaktisamāyuktaḥ śivanindāṃ karoti yaḥ \|*  
*pitroḥ kṣayāhamāsādya śūnyaṃ caivākramediha \|\| 46 \|\|*

*kṛtvā ca pāratāpaṃ hi karoti vacanaṃ punaḥ \|*  
*tena pāpena liṃpāmi nāgaccheyaṃ punaryadi \|\| 47 \|\|*

The hind said:—

44-47. “O hunter, listen. I shall explain. If a person breaks his words he forfeits his merits. If I do not return let me be smeared with the sin that accrues to such people who forsake their legally wedded wife and cohabit with another, who transgress the Vedic virtue and follow an imaginary and fictitious cult; who profess to be devoted to Viṣṇu and censure Śiva, who perform the Kṣayāha rites of their parents on a Śūnyatithi day and who add insult to injury.”

## \|\| 8.40.48 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|\| \|*  
*ityuktaśca tayā vyādho gacchetyāha mṛgīṃ ca saḥ \|*  
*sā mṛgī ca jalaṃ pītvā hṛṣṭā'gacchatsvamāśramam \|\| 48 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

48\. Implored by the hind, the hunter said to the hind, “go.” It drank water and delightedly went away to its hermitage.

## \|\| 8.40.49-50 \|\|

*tāvaddvitīyo yāmo vai tasya nidrāṃ vinā gataḥ \|*  
*etasminsamaye tatra prāpte yāme tṛtīyake \|\| 49 \|\|*

*jñātvā vilaṃbaṃ cakitastadanveṣaṇatatparaḥ \|\| \|*  
*tadyāme mṛgamadrākṣījjalamārgagataṃ tataḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

49-50. By that time the second part of the night too came to a close without a wink of sleep to the hunter. When the third part arrived, the stag who was bewildered by the delay caused by the hind came there in search of it. The hunter saw it standing on the path leading to water.

## \|\| 8.40.51 \|\|

*puṣṭaṃ mṛgaṃ ca taṃ dṛṣṭvā hṛṣṭo vanacarassa vai \|*  
*śaraṃ dhanuṣi saṃdhāya hantuṃ taṃ hi pracakrame \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. On seeing the stout stag the hunter was delighted. He fixed the arrow to the bow and was about to kill it.

## \|\| 8.40.52 \|\|

*tadaivaṃ kurvatastasya bilvapatrāṇi kānicit \|*  
*tatprārabdhavaśādviṣṇo patitāni śivopari \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. O dear, while he attempted to do this, a few Bilva leaves fell on Śiva as a result of his Prārabdha.

## \|\| 8.40.53 \|\|

*tena tṛtīyayāmasya tadrātrau tasya bhāgyataḥ \|*  
*pūjā jātā śivasyaiva kṛpālutvaṃ pradarśitam \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. In view of this, the worship of the third part of that night became realised due to his good fortune. Śiva’s merciful nature was evident in this.

## \|\| 8.40.54 \|\|

*śrutvā tatra ca taṃ śabdaṃ kiṃ karoṣīti prāha saḥ \|*  
*kuṭumbārthamahaṃ hanmi tvāṃ vyādhaśceti sobravīt \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. On hearing the noise the stag asked, “what are you doing?” The hunter replied “I am going to kill you for the sake of my family.”

## \|\| 8.40.55 \|\|

*tacchrutvā vyādhavacanaṃ hariṇo hṛṣṭamānasaḥ \|*  
*drutameva ca taṃ vyādhaṃ vacanaṃ cedamabravīt \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. On hearing this, the stag was delighted in his mind. Immediately he spoke to the hunter.

## \|\| 8.40.56 \|\|

*hariṇa uvāca \|*  
*dhanyohaṃ puṣṭimānadya bhavattṛptirbhaviṣyati \|*  
*yasyāṃgaṃ nopakārāya tasya sarvaṃ vṛthā gatam \|\| 56 \|\|*

The stag said:—

56\. I am blessed that I am well-nourished and enough to satisfy your needs. Of a person everything is useless if his body is of no avail.

## \|\| 8.40.57 \|\|

*yo vai sāmarthyayuktaśca nopakāraṃ karoti vai \|*  
*tatsāmarthyaṃ bhavedvyarthaṃ paratra narakaṃ vrajet \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. If a person does not help others though he is capable of it, all his efficiency is vain. He will fall into hell after death.

## \|\| 8.40.58 \|\|

*parantu bālakān svāṃśca samarpya jananīṃ śiśūn \|*  
*āśvāsyāpyatha tān sarvānāgamiṣyāmyahaṃ punaḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. But I must entrust my young ones to the care of their mother. I shall console them and return again.”

## \|\| 8.40.59 \|\|

*ityuktastena sa vyādho vismitotīva cetasi \|*  
*manāk śuddhamanānaṣṭapāpapuṃjo vaco'bravīt \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. Thus requested the hunter was much surprised in his mind. With his heap of sins destroyed and with a purified mind he spoke these words.

## \|\| 8.40.60-61 \|\|

*vyādha uvāca \|*  
*ye ye samāgatāścātra tete sarve tvayā yathā \|*  
*kathayitvā gatā hyatra nāyāntyadyāpi vaṃcakāḥ \|\| 60 \|\|*

*tvaṃ cāpi saṃkaṭe prāpto vyalīkaṃ ca gamiṣyasi \|*  
*mama sañjīvanaṃ cādya bhaviṣyati kathaṃ mṛga \|\| 61 \|\|*

The hunter said:—

60-61. O stag, every animal that came here has gone after promising in the manner that you have done now They have not yet come. You too are in distress now and want to go under a false pretext. How, then, will I have my livelihood?

## \|\| 8.40.62 \|\|

*mṛga uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu vyādha pravakṣyāmi nānṛtaṃ vidyate mayi \|*  
*satyena sarvaṃ brahmāṇḍaṃ tiṣṭhatyeva carācaram \|\| 62 \|\|*

The stag said:—

62\. O hunter, listen, I shall explain. I do not utter lies. The entire universe including the mobile and immobile beings is steady, thanks to truth.

## \|\| 8.40.63 \|\|

*yasya vāṇī vyalīkā hi tatpuṇyaṃ galitaṃ kṣaṇāt \|*  
*tathāpi śṛṇu vai satyāṃ pratijñāṃ mama bhillaka \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. The merit of a liar melts away in a trice. Still O, hunter, listen to my truthful promise.

## \|\| 8.40.64-66 \|\|

*sandhyāyāṃ maithune ghasre śivarātryāṃ ca bhojane \|*  
*kūṭasākṣye nyāsahāre saṃdhyāhīne dvije tathā \|\| 64 \|\|*

*śivahīnaṃ mukhaṃ yasya nopakartā kṣamo'pi san \|*  
*parvaṇi śrīphalasyaiva troṭane'bhakṣyabhakṣaṇe \|\| 65 \|\|*

*asaṃpūjya śivaṃ bhasmarahitaścānnabhuk ca yaḥ \|*  
*eteṣāṃ pātakaṃ me syānnāgaccheyaṃ punaryadi \|\| 66 \|\|*

64-66. If I do not come again, let me have the sin of these activities viz:—in having sexual intercourse at dusk, in taking food on Śivarātri day, in perjury, in misappropriation of Trust funds, in neglecting Sandhyā prayers, in not uttering Śiva’s name with the mouth, in not helping despite the ability, in breaking cocoanut on Parvan days, in taking forbidden food, in taking food before worshipping Śiva or without applying Bhasma.

## \|\| 8.40.67 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*iti śrutvā vacastasya gaccha śīghraṃ samāvraja \|*  
*sa vyādhenaivamuktastu jalaṃ pītvā gato mṛgaḥ \|\| 67 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

67\. On hearing these words the hunter said “Go and return quickly.” Thus permitted by the hunter, the hunter drank water and went away.

## \|\| 8.40.68-69 \|\|

*te sarve militāstatra svāśrame kṛtasupraṇāḥ \|*  
*vṛttāṃtaṃ caiva taṃ sarvaṃ śrutvā samyak parasparam \|\| 68 \|\|*

*gantavyaṃ niścayeneti satyapāśena yaṃtritāḥ \|*  
*āśvāsya bālakāṃstatra gantumutkaṇṭhitāstadā \|\| 69 \|\|*

68-69. All these animals who had promised in good faith, met at the hermitage. Hearing the news of one another in its entirety they decided that they should go because they were bound by the promise. They consoled the fawns and became eager to go back.

## \|\| 8.40.70-71 \|\|

*mṛgī jyeṣṭhā ca yā tatra svāminaṃ vākyamabravīt \|*  
*tvāṃ vinā bālakā hyatra kathaṃ sthāsyaṃti vai mṛga \|\| 70 \|\|*

*prathamaṃ tu mayā tatra pratijñā ca kṛtā prabho \|*  
*tasmānmayā ca gantavyaṃ bhavadbhyāṃ sthīyatāmiha \|\| 71 \|\|*

70-71. The senior hind spoke to its mate “O stag, without you, how can the young fawns stay here? O lord, it was I who promised at the outset. So I shall go. Both of you stay here.”

## \|\| 8.40.72 \|\|

*iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā kaniṣṭhā vākyamabravīt \|*  
*ahaṃ tvatsevikā cādya gacchāmi sthīyatāṃ tvayā \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. On hearing those words the junior hind spoke “I am your servant. Hence I go. You shall stay here.”

## \|\| 8.40.73 \|\|

*tacchrutvā ca mṛgaḥ prāha gamyate tatra vai mayā \|*  
*bhavatyau tiṣṭhatāṃ cātra mātṛtaḥ śiśurakṣaṇam \|\| 73 \|\|*

73\. On hearing it, the stag said—“I am going there. Both of you stay here. Infants are guarded and looked after by their mothers.”

## \|\| 8.40.74 \|\|

*tatsvāmivacanaṃ śrutvā menāte tanna dharmataḥ \|*  
*procuḥ prītyā svabhartāraṃ vaidhavye jīvitaṃ ca dhik \|\| 74 \|\|*

74\. On hearing the words of their husband the two hinds did not consider it righteous. They lovingly told their husband, “Fie upon that life in widowhood.”

## \|\| 8.40.75 \|\|

*bālānāśvāsya tāṃstatra samarpya sahavāsinaḥ \|*  
*gatāste sarva evāśu yatrāste vyādhasattamaḥ \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. Then all of them consoled their fawns and entrusted them to the care of their neighbours. They went to the place where the hunter was waiting.

## \|\| 8.40.76 \|\|

*te bālā api sarve vai vilokyānusamāgatāḥ \|*  
*eteṣāṃ yā gatiḥ syādvai hyasmākaṃ sā bhavatviti \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. The fawns too seeing all these followed them thinking. “Let what befalls them happen to us as well.”

## \|\| 8.40.77 \|\|

*tān dṛṣṭvā harṣito vyādho bāṇaṃ dhanuṣi saṃdadhe \|*  
*punaśca jalapatrāṇi patitāni śivopari \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. On seeing them, the delighted hunter fitted the arrow to bis bow. Water and leaves of the Bilva tree fell on Śiva again.

## \|\| 8.40.78 \|\|

*tena jātā caturthasya pūjā yāmasya vai śubhā \|*  
*tasya pāpantadā sarvaṃ bhasmasādabhavat kṣaṇāt \|\| 78 \|\|*

78\. Thanks to that, the worship of the fourth part of the night too became auspiciously performed. Thereafter his sins were reduced to ashes in a trice.

## \|\| 8.40.79 \|\|

*mṛgī mṛgī mṛgaścocuśśīghraṃ vai vyādhasattama \|*  
*asmākaṃ sārthakaṃ dehaṃ kuru tvaṃ hi kṛpāṃ kuru \|\| 79 \|\|*

79\. The senior hind, the junior hind and the stag said—“O excellent hunter, make our body purposeful. Be merciful to us.”

## \|\| 8.40.80 \|\|

*\|\| śiva uvāca \|*  
*iti teṣāṃ vacaśśrutvā vyādho vismayamāgataḥ \|*  
*śivapūjāprabhāveṇa jñānaṃ durlabhamāptavān \|\| 80 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

80\. On hearing their words the hunter was surprised. Thanks to the power of the worship of Śiva, he acquired perfect knowledge inaccessible to others.

## \|\| 8.40.81 \|\|

*ete dhanyā mṛgāścaiva jñānahīnāssusaṃmatāḥ \|*  
*svīyenaiva śarīreṇa paropakaraṇe ratāḥ \|\| 81 \|\|*

81\. The deer are blessed. Though devoid of knowledge they are ready to help others by offering their own bodies.

## \|\| 8.40.82 \|\|

*mānuṣyaṃ janma saṃprāpya sādhitaṃ kiṃ mayādhunā \|*  
*parakāyaṃ ca saṃpīḍya śarīraṃ poṣitaṃ mayā \|\| 82 \|\|*

82\. What have I achieved despite being born as a human being? I have nourished my body by torturing others.

## \|\| 8.40.83 \|\|

*kuṭumbaṃ poṣitaṃ nityaṃ kṛtvā pāpānyanekaśaḥ \|*  
*evaṃ pāpāni hā kṛtvā kā gatirme bhaviṣyati \|\| 83 \|\|*

83\. I reared my family by committing many sins every day. Alas! what will be my fate after committing all these sins?

## \|\| 8.40.84 \|\|

*kāṃ vā gatiṃ gamiṣyāmi pātakaṃ janmataḥ kṛtam \|*  
*idānīṃ ciṃtayāmyevaṃ dhigdhik ca jīvanaṃ mama \|\| 84 \|\|*

84\. Sins have been committed by me ever since my birth. What goal shall I attain? “Fie, Fie upon my life!”

## \|\| 8.40.85 \|\|

*iti jñānaṃ samāpanno bāṇaṃ saṃvārayaṃstadā \|*  
*gamyatāṃ ca mṛgaśreṣṭhā dhanyāḥ stha iti cābravīt \|\| 85 \|\|*

85\. Acquiring perfect knowledge thus, he withdrew the arrow and explained—“O excellent deer! you are all blessed. You can safely go back.”

## \|\| 8.40.86 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*ityukte ca tadā tena prasannaśśaṃkarastadā \|*  
*pūjitaṃ ca svarūpaṃ hi darśayāmāsa saṃmatam \|\| 86 \|\|*

86\. When he said this, Śiva became delighted. He revealed his form that is worshipped and honoured by the good.

## \|\| 8.40.87 \|\|

*saṃspṛśya kṛpayā śaṃbhustaṃ vyādhaṃ prītito'bravīt \|*  
*varaṃ brūhi prasanno'smi vratenānena bhillaka \|\| 87 \|\|*

87\. Touching him mercifully Śiva spoke lovingly to the hunter, “O hunter, I am delighted at this Vrata. Ask for the boon you wish to have.”

## \|\| 8.40.88 \|\|

*vyādho'pi śivarūpaṃ ca dṛṣṭvā mukto'bhavatkṣaṇāt \|*  
*papāta śivapādāgre sarvaṃ prāptamiti buvan \|\| 88 \|\|*

88\. On seeing Śiva’s form, the hunter became liberated in a trice. He fell at Śiva’s feet saying, “Everything has been attained by me now.”

## \|\| 8.40.89 \|\|

*śivo'pi suprannātmā nāma datvā guheti ca \|*  
*vilokya taṃ kṛpādṛṣṭyā tasmai divyānvarānadāt \|\| 89 \|\|*

89\. Śiva, delighted in his mind, gave him the name Guha. Glancing at him mercifully he gave him a divine boon.

## \|\| 8.40.90 \|\|

*śiva uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu vyādhādya bhogāṃstvaṃ bhuṃkṣva divyānyathepsitān \|*  
*rājadhānīṃ samāśritya śṛṃgaverapure parām \|\| 90 \|\|*

Śiva said:—

90\. O hunter, listen. Have your capital at Śṛṅgaverapura and enjoy divine pleasures at your will.

## \|\| 8.40.91 \|\|

*anapāyā vaṃśavṛddhiśślāghanīyaḥ surairapi \|*  
*gṛhe rāmastava vyādha samāyāsyati niścitam \|\| 91 \|\|*

91\. Your race will flourish without any calamity. O hunter, certainly lord Rāma praised even by the gods will come to your house.

## \|\| 8.40.92 \|\|

*kariṣyati tvayā maitrī madbhaktasnehakārakaḥ \|*  
*matsevāsaktacetāstvaṃ muktiṃ yāsyasi durlabhām \|\| 92 \|\|*

92\. He will make alliance with you. Co-operating with my devotee, your mind indulged in rendering service to me, you will attain salvation rare to others.

## \|\| 8.40.93 \|\|

*etasminnaṃtare te tu kṛtvā śaṃkaradarśanam \|*  
*sarve praṇamya sanmuktiṃ mṛgayoneḥ prapedire \|\| 93 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

93\. In the meantime, having viewed Śiva, the deer bowed to him and attained liberation from their birth as deer.

## \|\| 8.40.94 \|\|

*vimānaṃ ca samāruhya divyadehā gatāstadā \|*  
*śivadarśanamātreṇa śāpānmuktā divaṃ gatāḥ \|\| 94 \|\|*

94\. They attained divine bodies, ascended the aerial chariot and went away. Released from curse at the very sight of Śiva they attained heaven.

## \|\| 8.40.95 \|\|

*vyādheśvaraḥ śivo jātaḥ parvate hyarbudācale \|*  
*darśanātpūjanātsadyo bhuktimuktipradāyakaḥ \|\| 95 \|\|*

95\. Śiva became Vyādheśvara on the Arbuda mountain. Viewing and worshipping this phallic idol bestows worldly pleasures and salvation.

## \|\| 8.40.96 \|\|

*vyādhopi taddinānnūnaṃ bhogānsa surasattama \|*  
*bhuktvā rāmakṛpāṃ prāpya śivasāyujyamāptavān \|\| 96 \|\|*

96\. From that day onwards the hunter attained godly pleasures and being favoured by the god attained Sāyujya salvation with Śiva.

## \|\| 8.40.97 \|\|

*ajñānātsa vratañcaitatkṛtvā sāyujyamāptavān \|*  
*kiṃ punarbhaktisaṃpannā yānti tanmayatāṃ śubhām \|\| 97 \|\|*

97\. Even after performing this rite in utter ignorance he attained Sāyujya; what about those who are endowed with devotion? They will surely attain complete identity with the lord.

## \|\| 8.40.98 \|\|

*vicāryya sarvaśāstrāṇi dharmāṃścaivāpyanekaśaḥ \|*  
*śivarātrivratamidaṃ sarvotkṛṣṭaṃ prakīrtitam \|\| 98 \|\|*

98\. After considering all Śāstras, and the sacred rites enjoined by these Śivarātrivrata, has been glorified as the most excellent.

## \|\| 8.40.99-100 \|\|

*vratāni vividhānyatra tīrthāni vividhāni ca \|*  
*dānāni ca vicitrāṇi makhāśca vividhāstathā \|\| 99 \|\|*

*tapāṃsi vividhānyeva japāścaivāpya nekaśaḥ \|*  
*naitena samatāṃ yānti śivarātrivratena ca \|\| 100 \|\|*

99-100. Different Vratas, various holy centres, gifts of variegated nature, diverse sacrifices, different austerities and many forms of Japas, are not equal to Śivarātrivrata.

## \|\| 8.40.101 \|\|

*tasmācchubhataraṃ caitatkartavyaṃ hitamīpsubhiḥ \|*  
*śivarātrivratandivyaṃ bhukti muktipradaṃ sadā \|\| 101 \|\|*

101\. Hence this auspicious Vrata shall be performed by those who wish for beneficence. Śīvarātri Vrata is divine and it always yields worldly pleasures and salvation.

## \|\| 8.40.102 \|\|

*etatsarvaṃ samākhyātaṃ śivarātrivrataṃ śubham \|*  
*vratarājeti vikhyātaṃ kimanyacchrotumicchasi \|\| 102 \|\|*

102\. Thus I have narrated the auspicious Śivatātri Vrata entirely. It is famous as the chief of sacred rites. What else do you wish to hear?

## Chapter 41 - Review of salvation

## \|\| 8.41.1 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*muktirnāma tvayā proktā tasyāṃ kiṃ nu bhavediha \|*  
*avasthā kīdṛśī tatra bhavediti vadasva naḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

The sages said.—

1\. What is liberation has been explained by you. What will happen in that state? What is the nature of that state? Please explain to us.

## \|\| 8.41.2 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|\| \|*  
*muktiścaturvidhā proktā śrūyatāṃ kathayāmi vaḥ \|*  
*saṃsārakleśasaṃhartrī paramānandadāyinī \|\| 2 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

2\. Liberation is of four types. May this be heard. I shall tell you. It quells the pain of worldly existence. It bestows the greatest bliss.

## \|\| 8.41.3 \|\|

*sārūpyā caiva sālokyā sānnidhyā ca tathā parā \|*  
*sāyujyā ca caturthī sā vratenānena yā bhavet \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. It is fourfold:—Sārūpya (identity in form), Sālokya (being within the view of the lord or in the same world), Sānnidhya (proximity with the lord), and Sāyujya (complete identity with him). The fourth arises out of this Vrata.

## \|\| 8.41.4 \|\|

*mukterdātā muniśreṣṭhāḥ kevalaṃ śiva ucyate \|*  
*brahmādyā na hi te jñeyā kevalaṃ ca trivargadāḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. O great sages, only Śiva can bestow liberation. Brahmā and others cannot do that. They shall be known as bestowers of virtue, wealth and love.

## \|\| 8.41.5 \|\|

*brahmādyāstriguṇādhīśāśśivastriguṇataḥ paraḥ \|*  
*nirvikārī parabrahma turyaḥ prakṛtitaḥ paraḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Brahmā and others are dependent on the attributes. Śiva is beyond the attributes. He is free from aberrations. He is the great Brahman, the fourth deity, greater than Prakṛti.

## \|\| 8.41.6 \|\|

*jñānarūpo'vyayaḥ sākṣī jñānagamyo'dvayassvayam \|*  
*kaivalyamuktidasso'tra trivargasya prado'pi hi \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. He is in the form of knowledge. He is imperishable and unchangeable. He is the cosmic witness, attainable through knowledge. He is without a second. He is the bestower of Trivarga (Dharma, Artha, Kāma) and the Kaivalya liberation.

## \|\| 8.41.7 \|\|

*kaivalyākhyā paṃcamī ca durlabhā sarvathā nṛṇām \|*  
*tallakṣaṇaṃ pravakṣyāmi śrūyatāmṛṣisattamāḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. A fifth type of salvation Kaivalya is inaccessible to men in every respect. O excellent sages, I shall explain its characteristics. May that be heard.

## \|\| 8.41.8-9 \|\|

*utpadyate yataḥ sarvaṃ yenaitatpālyate jagat \|*  
*yasmiṃśca līyate taddhi yena sarvamidaṃ tatam \|\| 8 \|\|*

*tadeva śivarūpaṃ hi paṭhyate ca munīśvarāḥ \|*  
*sakalaṃ niṣkalaṃ ceti dvividhaṃ vedavarṇitam \|\| 9 \|\|*

8-9. O great sages, Śiva’s form is twofold: Saguṇa and Nirguṇa as explained in the Vedas. It is that from which the entire universe originates, by which it is protected and in which it merges and by whom all this has been pervaded.

## \|\| 8.41.10 \|\|

*viṣṇunā tacca na jñātaṃ brahmaṇā na ca tattathā \|*  
*kumārādyaiśca na jñātaṃ na jñātaṃ nāradena vai \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. It is not known to Viṣṇu nor to Bhahmā. It is not known to Kumāra and others nor to Nārada.

## \|\| 8.41.11 \|\|

*śukena vyāsa putreṇa vyāsena ca munīśvaraiḥ \|*  
*tatpūrvaiścākhilairdevairvedaiḥ śāstraistathā na hi \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. It is not known to Śuka, son of Vyāsa nor to Vyāsa or other great sages. It is not known to the earlier beings, all the gods, all the Vedas and all the Śāstras.

## \|\| 8.41.12 \|\|

*satyaṃ jñānamanaṃtaṃ ca saccidānandasaṃjñitam \|*  
*nirguṇo nirupādhiścāvyayaḥ śuddho niraṃjanaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. It is truth, knowledge, the infinity. It is named Saccidānanda (existence, knowledge, bliss). It is Nirguṇa, Nirvyādhi (free from limitations and conditions). It is imperishable. It is pure and unsullied.

## \|\| 8.41.13 \|\|

*na rakto naiva pītaśca na śveto nīla eva ca \|*  
*na hrasvo na ca dīrghaśca na sthūlassūkṣma eva ca \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. It is neither red nor yellow, neither white nor blue, neither short nor long, neither gross nor subtle.

## \|\| 8.41.14 \|\|

*yato vāco nivartaṃte aprāpya manasā saha \|*  
*tadeva paramaṃ proktaṃ brahmaiva śivasaṃjñakam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. That great Brahman Śiva is said to be that from which all words return along with the mīnd, unable to attain it.

## \|\| 8.41.15 \|\|

*ākāśaṃ vyāpakaṃ yadvattathaiva vyāpakantvidam \|*  
*māyātītaṃ parātmānaṃ dvandvātītaṃ vimatsaram \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. This is all pervasive like the expansive firmament. It is the great soul beyond Māyā. It is beyond Dvandvas mutually clashing opposites and free from rivalry.

## \|\| 8.41.16 \|\|

*tatprāptiśca bhavedatra śivajñānodayāddhruvam \|*  
*bhajanādvā śivasyaiva sūkṣmamatyā satāṃ dvijāḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. O brahmins, good people attain it when knowledge of Śiva rises up or when they worship him with deep devotion.

## \|\| 8.41.17 \|\|

*jñānaṃ tu duṣkaraṃ loke bhajanaṃ sukaraṃ matam \|*  
*tasmācchivaṃ ca bhajata muktyarthamapi sattamāḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. The knowledge is difficult to attain but worship can easily be performed. Hence, O excellent sages, worship Śiva for the attainment of salvation too.

## \|\| 8.41.18 \|\|

*śivo hi bhajanādhīno jñānātmā mokṣadaḥ paraḥ \|*  
*bhaktyaiva bahavaḥ siddhā muktiṃ prāpuḥ parāṃ mudā \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Śiva is subservient to worship. He is knowledge-souled and the greatest bestower of salvation. Many Siddhas have joyously attained the great salvation through devotion alone.

## \|\| 8.41.19 \|\|

*jñānamātā śaṃbhubhaktirmuktibhuktipradā sadā \|*  
*sulabhā yatprasādāddhi satpremāṃkura lakṣaṇā \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Devotion to Śiva, characterised by sprouting pure love, is the mother of perfect knowledge. It is easy of access by means of his benevolence. It yields worldly pleasures and salvation too.

## \|\| 8.41.20-21 \|\|

*sā bhaktirvividhā jñeyā saguṇā nirguṇā dvijāḥ \|*  
*vaidhī svābhāvikī yāyā varā sāsā smṛtā parā \|\| 20 \|\|*

*naiṣṭhikyanaiṣṭhikībhedādvividhaiva hi kīrtitā \|*  
*ṣaḍvidhā naiṣṭhikī bhedāddvitīyaikavidhā smṛtā \|\| 21 \|\|*

20-21. Devotion is of various types. O brahmins, it is both Saguṇa and Nirguṇa. Whatever is legitimate andnatural is the most excellent and the greatest. It is again twofold: Naiṣṭhika (permanent and attended with ritualistic observances) and Anaiṣṭhika (non-Naiṣṭhika). The Naiṣṭhika is of six varieties and the other is only of one type.

## \|\| 8.41.22 \|\|

*vihitāvihitābhedāttāmanekāṃ vidurbudhāḥ \|*  
*tayorbahuvidhatvācca vistāro na hi varṇyate \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Learned men consider devotion to be of different sorts, such as ordained and non-ordained. Since they are of various types they are not explained in detail.

## \|\| 8.41.23 \|\|

*te navāṃge ubhe jñeye śravaṇādikabhedataḥ \|*  
*suduṣkare tatprasādaṃ vinā ca sukare tataḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Differentiated by the differences in those who observe Śravana (listening) etc. both these are of nine subdivisions each. Without his favour they are difficult of performance and with His favour they can easily be performed.

## \|\| 8.41.24 \|\|

*bhaktijñāne na bhinne hi śaṃbhunā varṇite dvijāḥ \|*  
*tasmādbhedo na kartavyastatkartussarvadā sukham \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. O brahmins, devotion and knowledge are essentially non-different from each other as explained by Śiva. Hence, they should not be differentiated. Happiness befalls him who observes them always.

## \|\| 8.41.25 \|\|

*vijñānaṃ na bhavatyeva dvijā bhaktivirodhinaḥ \|*  
*śaṃbhubhaktikarasyaiva bhavejjñānodayo drutam \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. O brahmins, a person who is against devotion will never attain perfect knowledge. Knowledge rises up immediately only in him who observes devotion to Śiva.

## \|\| 8.41.26 \|\|

*tasmādbhaktirmaheśasya sādhanīyā munīśvarāḥ \|*  
*tayaiva nikhilaṃ siddhaṃ bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Hence, O great sages, devotion to lord Śiva shall be practised. There is no doubt in this that everything is realisable solely through devotion.

## \|\| 8.41.27 \|\|

*iti pṛṣṭaṃ bhavadbhiryattadeva kathitaṃ mayā \|*  
*tacchutvā sarvapāpebhyo mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Thus, what has been asked by you has been explained. There is no doubt in this that on hearing this, one is rid of his sin completely.

## Chapter 42 - The difference between Saguṇa and Nirguṇa

## \|\| 8.42.1 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*śivaḥ ko vā hariḥ ko vā rudraḥ ko vā vidhiśca kaḥ \|*  
*eteṣu nirguṇaḥ ko vā hyetaṃ naśchindhi saṃśayam \|\| 1 \|\|*

The sages said:—

1\. Who is Śiva? Who is Viṣṇu? Who is Rudra? Who is Brahmā? Of these who is Nirguṇa? Please clear this doubt of ours.

## \|\| 8.42.2 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*yaccādau hi samutpannaṃ nirguṇātparamātmanaḥ \|*  
*tadeva śivasaṃjñaṃ hi vedavedāṃtino viduḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

2\. Masters of the Vedas and Vedānta know that which originates from the Nirguṇa great soul as the one termed Śiva.

## \|\| 8.42.3-4 \|\|

*tasmātprakṛtirutpannā puruṣeṇa samanvitā \|*  
*tābhyāntapaḥ kṛtaṃ tatra mūlasthe ca jale sudhīḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

*pañcakośīti vikhyātā kāśī sarvātivallabhā \|*  
*vyāptaṃ ca sakalaṃ hyetattajjalaṃ viśvato gatam \|\| 4 \|\|*

3-4. Prakṛti accompanied by Puruṣa originated from him. Penance was performed by them both. The famous city Kāśī known as Pañcakrośī, dear to all, originated in the water there at the base. There was water everywhere in the universe.

## \|\| 8.42.5 \|\|

*saṃbhāvya māyayā yuktastatra supto harissa vai \|*  
*nārāyaṇeti vikhyātaḥ prakṛtirnārāyaṇī matā \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Viṣṇu famous as Nārāyaṇa slept there enveloped by Māyā. The Prakṛti is known as Nārāyaṇī (belonging to Nārāyaṇa.)

## \|\| 8.42.6 \|\|

*tannābhikamale yo vai jātassa ca pitāmahaḥ \|*  
*tenaiva tapasā dṛṣṭassa vai viṣṇurudāhṛtaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. He who was born of his umbilical lotus was Pitāmaha. He who was seen by him through penance is said to be Viṣṇu.

## \|\| 8.42.7 \|\|

*ubhayorvādaśamane yadrūpaṃ sa darśitaṃ budhāḥ \|*  
*mahādeveti vikhyātaṃ nirguṇena śivena hi \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. The form that was revealed by Nirguṇa Śiva at the time of subduing the dispute between the two is, O learned ones, famous as Mahādeva.

## \|\| 8.42.8 \|\|

*tena proktamahaṃ śambhurbhaviṣyāmi kabhālataḥ \|*  
*rudro nāma sa vikhyāto lokānugrahakārakaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. This was said by him, “I will be Śiva with fire in my forehead.” He became famous by the name Rudra. He is the cause of blessing the worlds.

## \|\| 8.42.9 \|\|

*dhyānārthaṃ caiva sarveṣāmarūpo rūpavānabhūt \|*  
*sa eva ca śivassākṣādbhaktavātsalyakārakaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. The formless one became one with forms in order to facilitate meditation. He alone is Śiva himself, favourably disposed towards his devotees.

## \|\| 8.42.10 \|\|

*śive triguṇasambhinne rudre tu guṇadhāmani \|*  
*vastuto na hi bhedo'sti svarṇe tadbhūṣaṇe yathā \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. Essentially there is no difference between Śiva who is different from the three Guṇas and Rudra the abode of Guṇas, as in the case of gold and an ornament made thereof.

## \|\| 8.42.11 \|\|

*samānarūpakarmāṇau samabhaktagatipradau \|*  
*samānākhilasaṃsevyau nānālīlāvihāriṇau \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Their forms and activities are the same; they bestow good goal on the devotees equally; they are equally worthy of being served by all and they indulge in various divine sports.

## \|\| 8.42.12 \|\|

*sarvathā śivarūpo hi rudro raudraparākramaḥ \|*  
*utpanno bhaktakāryārthaṃ haribrahmasahāyakṛt \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Rudra of terrible exploits has the form of Śiva in every respect. He was born for carrying out the tasks of the devotees. He helps Viṣṇu and Brahmā.

## \|\| 8.42.13 \|\|

*anye ca ye samutpannā yathānukramato layam \|*  
*yāṃti naiva tathā rudraḥ śive rudro vilīyate \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Other beings and gods who are born get dissolved in due order but not so Rudra. Rudra gets merged in Śiva.

## \|\| 8.42.14 \|\|

*te vai rudraṃ militvā tu prayānti prakṛtā ime \|*  
*imānrudro militvā tu na yāti śrutiśāsanam \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. This is the directive in the Vedas that all these Prākṛtas (born of Prakṛti) meet Rudra and go but Rudra does not meet these and go.

## \|\| 8.42.15 \|\|

*sarve rudraṃ bhajantyeva rudraḥ kañcidbhajenna hi \|*  
*svātmanā bhaktavātsalyādbhajatyeva kadācana \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. All worship Rudra but Rudra does not worship any one. Yet due to his favourable attitude to his devotees sometimes he worships himself.

## \|\| 8.42.16 \|\|

*anyaṃ bhajanti ye nityaṃ tasmiṃste līnatāṃ gatāḥ \|*  
*tenaiva rudraṃ te prāptāḥ kālena mahatā budhāḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Those who worship others get dissolved in them. That is why they attain Rudra in due course, O learned ones.

## \|\| 8.42.17 \|\|

*rudrabhaktāstu ye kecittatkṣaṇaṃ śivatāṃ gatāḥ \|*  
*anyāpekṣā na vai teṣāṃ śrutireṣā sanātanī \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Those who are Rudra’s devotees attain the state of Śiva in a trice. They need not depend on any one else. This is the eternal statement in the Vedas.

## \|\| 8.42.18 \|\|

*ajñānaṃ vividhaṃ hyetadvijñānaṃ vividhaṃ na hi \|*  
*tatprakāramahaṃ vakṣye śṛṇutādarato dvijāḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. O brahmins, ignorance is of various types. Perfect knowledge is not so. I shall explain their modes. Listen respectively.

## \|\| 8.42.19 \|\|

*brahmādi tṛṇaparyaṃtaṃ yatkiṃciddṛśyate tviha \|*  
*tatsarvaṃ śiva evāsti mithyā nānātvakalpanā \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Whatever is seen here beginning with Brahmā is Śiva himself. The concept of manifoldness is unreal.

## \|\| 8.42.20 \|\|

*sṛṣṭeḥ pūrvaṃ śivaḥ proktaḥ sṛṣṭermadhye śivastathā \|*  
*sṛṣṭerante śivaḥ proktassarvaśūnye tadā śivaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Śiva is said to be before creation. Śiva is there in the midst of creation. Śiva is said to be at the end of creation. When everything is a void, Sadāśiva exists.

## \|\| 8.42.21 \|\|

*tasmāccaturguṇaḥ proktaḥ śiva eva munīśvarāḥ \|*  
*sa eva saguṇo jñeyaḥ śaktimattvāddvidhāpi saḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. O great sages, thus Śiva is fourfold. He alone shall be known as Saguṇa. In view of the possession of Śakti he is twofold.

## \|\| 8.42.22 \|\|

*yenaiva viṣṇave dattāssarve vedāssanātanāḥ \|*  
*varṇā mātrā hyanaikāśca dhyānaṃ svasya ca pūjanam \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. It is by him that the eternal Vedas, syllables, syllabic instants and his meditation and worship were given to Viṣṇu.

## \|\| 8.42.23 \|\|

*īśānaḥ sarvavidyānāṃ śrutireṣā sanātanī \|*  
*vedakartā vedapatistasmācchaṃbhurudāhṛtaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. The eternal Śruti says—“Īśānaḥ Sarvavidyānām”. Hence Śiva is the creator of the Vedas and the lord of the Vedas.

## \|\| 8.42.24 \|\|

*sa eva śaṃkaraḥ sākṣātsarvānugrahakārakaḥ \|*  
*kartā bhartā ca hartā ca sākṣī nirguṇa eva saḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. He alone is Śiva himself who blesses all. He is the creator, sustainer and annihilator. He is the cosmic witness and Nirguṇa.

## \|\| 8.42.25 \|\|

*anyeṣāṃ kālamānaṃ ca kālasya kalanā na hi \|*  
*mahākālassvayaṃ sākṣānmahākālīsamāśritaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Restriction of time is for others and not for Rudra. He is Mahākāla himself associating with Mahākālī.

## \|\| 8.42.26 \|\|

*tathā ca brāhmaṇā rudraṃ tathā kālīṃ pracakṣate \|*  
*sarvaṃ tābhyāntataḥ prāptamicchayā satyalīlayā \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. It is so that Brahmins mention Rudra and Kālī. Thanks to their wish and truthful sports everything is obtained by them.

## \|\| 8.42.27 \|\|

*na tasyotpādakaḥ kaścidbhartā hartā na tasya hi \|*  
*svayaṃ sarvasya hetuste kāryabhūtācyutādayaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. He has no progenitor, no sustainer and no annihilator. He himself is the cause of all Viṣṇu and others are his creations.

## \|\| 8.42.28 \|\|

*svayaṃ ca kāraṇaṃ kāryaṃ svasya naiva kadācana \|*  
*ekovyanekatāṃ yātopyanekopyekatāṃ vrajet \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. He is not the creation of any one. Though single he assumes manifold shapes. Seeming to be many he becomes unified into one.

## \|\| 8.42.29 \|\|

*ekaṃ bījaṃ bahirbhūtvā punarbījaṃ ca jāyate \|*  
*bahutve ca svayaṃ sarvaṃ śivarūpī maheśvaraḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. A single seed grows into a tree and produces many seeds. Even in their multiplicity they are of one tree. Maheśvara in the form of Śiva is verily like this.

## \|\| 8.42.30 \|\|

*etatparaṃ śivajñānaṃ tattvatastadudāhṛtam \|*  
*jānāti jñānavāneva nānyaḥ kaścidṛṣīśvarāḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. This is the great knowledge of Śiva explained factually. O great sages, only the wise know this, none else.

## \|\| 8.42.31 \|\|

*munaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*jñānaṃ salakṣaṇaṃ brūhi yajjñātvā śivatāmvrajet \|*  
*kathaṃ śivaśca tatsarvaṃ sarvaṃ vā śiva eva ca \|\| 31 \|\|*

The sages said:—

31\. Please explain that knowledge with all its characteristics realising which one attains the state of Śiva. How is Śiva all and how is all Śiva?

## \|\| 8.42.32 \|\|

*vyāsa uvāca \|*  
*etadākarṇya vacanaṃ sūtaḥ paurāṇikottamaḥ \|*  
*smṛtvā śivapadāmbhojaṃ munīṃstānabravīdvacaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

Vyāsa said:—

32\. On hearing these words, Sūta the excellent propounder of the Purāṇas remembered the lotus-like feet of Śiva and spoke these words to the sages.

## Chapter 43 - Review of Knowledge

## \|\| 8.43.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*śrūyatāmṛṣayaḥ sarve śivajñānaṃ yathā śrutam \|*  
*kathayāmi mahāguhyaṃ para muktisvarūpakam \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. O ye sages, may this be heard. I shall explain the perfect knowledge of Śiva in the manner I have heard. It is a great secret as it is the form of the highest salvation.

## \|\| 8.43.2 \|\|

*kanāradakumārāṇāṃ vyāsasya kapilasya ca \|*  
*eteṣāṃ ca samāje tairniścitya samudāhṛtam \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. In the assembly of Brahma, Nārada, Kumāra, Vyāsa and Kapila they had discussed this and come to this conclusion.

## \|\| 8.43.3 \|\|

*iti jñānaṃ sadā jñeyaṃ sarvaṃ śivamayaṃ jagat \|*  
*śivaḥ sarvamayo jñeyassarvajñena vipaścitā \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. It shall be known that the entire universe is wholly Śiva. That Śiva is in everything must be known by the learned scholar.

## \|\| 8.43.4 \|\|

*ābrahmatṛṇaparyantaṃ yatkiṃciddṛśyate jagat \|*  
*tatsarvaṃ śiva evāsti sa devaḥ śiva ucyate \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Beginning with Brahmā and ending with a blade of grass whatever is seen as constituting the universe is Śiva himself. That deity is called Śiva.

## \|\| 8.43.5 \|\|

*yadecchā tasya jāyeta tadā ca kriyate tvidam \|*  
*sarvaṃ sa eva jānāti taṃ na jānāti kaścana \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. When he wishes, this is created. He alone knows all. No one knows him.

## \|\| 8.43.6 \|\|

*racayitvā svayaṃ tacca praviśya dūrataḥ sthitaḥ \|*  
*na tatra ca praviṣṭosau nirliptaścitsvarūpavān \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. He himself creates it and enters it but stands far off. The Citsvarūpa (knowledge-formed) being who is pure docs not really enter it.

## \|\| 8.43.7 \|\|

*yathā ca jyotiṣaścaiva jalādau pratibiṃbatā \|*  
*vastuto na praveśo vai tathaiva ca śivaḥ svayam \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Luminary beings are reflected in water etc. They do not actually enter them. Similarly Śiva too appears entering other things.

## \|\| 8.43.8 \|\|

*vastutastu svayaṃ sarvaṃ kramo hi bhāsate śubhaḥ \|*  
*ajñānaṃ ca materbhedo nāstyanyacca dvayampunaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. Really Śiva alone, the auspicious being, manifests himself. The ignorance of the same is a defect of the mind. In fact there is no second entity.

## \|\| 8.43.9 \|\|

*darśaneṣu ca sarveṣu matibhedaḥ pradarśyate \|*  
*paraṃ vedāntino nityamadvaitaṃ praticakṣate \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. In all philosophical systems the concept of duality is evident. But the Vedāntins call him eternal and non-dualistic.

## \|\| 8.43.10 \|\|

*svasyāpyaṃśasya jīvāṃśo hyavidyāmohito vaśaḥ \|*  
*anyo'hamiti jānāti tayā mukto bhavecchivaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. The individual soul, though it is a part of his becomes deluded by Avidyā. He then thinks that he is different. If he is released from Avidyā he becomes Śiva.

## \|\| 8.43.11 \|\|

*sarvaṃ vyāpya śivaḥ sākṣād vyāpakaḥ sarvajantuṣu \|*  
*cetanācetaneśopi sarvatra śaṃkarassvayam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Śiva pervades all creatures. He is the lord of the sentient and the insentient. He is the benefactor.

## \|\| 8.43.12 \|\|

*upāyaṃ yaḥ karotyasya darśanārthaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*vedāntamārgamāśritya taddarśanaphalaṃ labhet \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. He who cleverly tries means of realising him after resorting to the Vedāntic path attains the fruit of his sight.

## \|\| 8.43.13 \|\|

*yathāgnirvyāpakaścaiva kāṣṭhekāṣṭhe ca tiṣṭhati \|*  
*yo vai maṃthati tatkāṣṭhaṃ sa vai paśyatyasaṃśayam \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Just as the pervading fire is latent in every block of wood but only he who churns it sees it manifested, to be sure.

## \|\| 8.43.14 \|\|

*bhaktyādisādhanānīha yaḥ karoti vicakṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*sa vai paśyatyavaśyaṃ hi taṃ śivaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. So also, the clever devotee who makes use of the expedients of devotion etc. certainly reaches Śiva. This is undoubtedly true.

## \|\| 8.43.15 \|\|

*śivaḥśivaḥśivaścaiva nānyadastīti kiṃcana \|*  
*bhrāntyā nānāsvarūpo hi bhāsate śaṅkarassadā \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Lord Śiva is everywhere. There is nothing else. Śiva appears in different forms always due to our illusion.

## \|\| 8.43.16 \|\|

*yathā samudro mṛccaiva suvarṇamathavā punaḥ \|*  
*upādhito hi nānātvaṃ labhate śaṃkarastathā \|\| 16 \|\|*
